PSALMS — LATIN–ENGLISH (CLEMENTINE VULGATE)
Captured from the Saints.Bible staging Parallel Reader, 2 November 2026.
Editorial revisions dated 9 and 14 November 2026. The capture date above identifies the source snapshot.
Verse numbers follow the Saints (Greek/Hebrew) column; 'vulgate_reference' in verses.json gives the Vulgate's own number where it differs.

PSALMS 1

1:1  LA: Beatus vir qui non abiit in consilio impiorum, et in via peccatorum non stetit, et in cathedra pestilentiæ non sedit ;
     EN: Blessed is the man who has not followed the counsel of the ungodly, stood on the path of sinners, or sat in the seat of pestilence,

1:2  LA: sed in lege Domini voluntas ejus, et in lege ejus meditabitur die ac nocte.
     EN: but whose delight is in the Lord's law, and who meditates on His law day and night.

1:3  LA: Et erit tamquam lignum quod plantatum est secus decursus aquarum, quod fructum suum dabit in tempore suo : et folium ejus non defluet ; et omnia quæcumque faciet prosperabuntur.
     EN: He will be like a tree planted beside flowing waters, which will yield its fruit in its season. Its leaf will not fall, and everything he does will prosper.

1:4  LA: Non sic impii, non sic ; sed tamquam pulvis quem projicit ventus a facie terræ.
     EN: Not so the ungodly, not so! They are like dust the wind drives from the face of the earth.

1:5  LA: Ideo non resurgent impii in judicio, neque peccatores in concilio justorum :
     EN: Therefore the ungodly will not rise again in judgment, nor sinners in the assembly of the just,

1:6  LA: quoniam novit Dominus viam justorum, et iter impiorum peribit.]
     EN: for the Lord knows the way of the just, but the path of the ungodly will perish.

PSALMS 2

2:1  LA: Quare fremuerunt gentes, et populi meditati sunt inania ?
     EN: Why have the nations raged and the peoples devised empty things?

2:2  LA: Astiterunt reges terræ, et principes convenerunt in unum adversus Dominum, et adversus christum ejus.
     EN: The kings of the earth took their stand, and the rulers gathered together against the Lord and against His anointed.

2:3  LA: Dirumpamus vincula eorum, et projiciamus a nobis jugum ipsorum.
     EN: “Let us break their bonds and throw their yoke away from us.”

2:4  LA: Qui habitat in cælis irridebit eos, et Dominus subsannabit eos.
     EN: He who dwells in heaven will laugh at them, and the Lord will mock them.

2:5  LA: Tunc loquetur ad eos in ira sua, et in furore suo conturbabit eos.
     EN: Then He will speak to them in His anger and trouble them in His fury.

2:6  LA: Ego autem constitutus sum rex ab eo super Sion, montem sanctum ejus, prædicans præceptum ejus.
     EN: But I have been established as king by Him over Zion, His holy mountain, proclaiming His command.

2:7  LA: Dominus dixit ad me : Filius meus es tu ; ego hodie genui te.
     EN: The Lord said to me, “You are My son; today I have begotten you.

2:8  LA: Postula a me, et dabo tibi gentes hæreditatem tuam, et possessionem tuam terminos terræ.
     EN: Ask of Me, and I will give you the nations as your inheritance and the ends of the earth as your possession.

2:9  LA: Reges eos in virga ferrea, et tamquam vas figuli confringes eos.
     EN: You will rule them with an iron rod and shatter them like a potter's vessel.”

2:10  LA: Et nunc, reges, intelligite ; erudimini, qui judicatis terram.
      EN: And now, kings, understand; receive instruction, you who judge the earth.

2:11  LA: Servite Domino in timore, et exsultate ei cum tremore.
      EN: Serve the Lord in fear, and rejoice before Him with trembling.

2:12  LA: Apprehendite disciplinam, nequando irascatur Dominus, et pereatis de via justa.
      EN: Take hold of discipline, lest the Lord become angry and you perish from the just way. When His anger quickly blazes up, blessed are all who trust in Him.

PSALMS 3

3:1  LA: Psalmus David, cum fugeret a facie Absalom filii sui.
     EN: A psalm of David, when he fled from his son Absalom.

3:2  LA: [Domine, quid multiplicati sunt qui tribulant me ? Multi insurgunt adversum me ;
     EN: Lord, why have those who afflict me multiplied? Many rise against me;

3:3  LA: multi dicunt animæ meæ : Non est salus ipsi in Deo ejus.
     EN: many say of my soul, “There is no salvation for him in his God.”

3:4  LA: Tu autem Domine, susceptor meus es, gloria mea, et exaltans caput meum.
     EN: But You, Lord, are my protector, my glory, and the One who lifts my head.

3:5  LA: Voce mea ad Dominum clamavi ; et exaudivit me de monte sancto suo.
     EN: With my voice I cried to the Lord, and He answered me from His holy mountain.

3:6  LA: Ego dormivi, et soporatus sum ; et exsurrexi, quia Dominus suscepit me.
     EN: I lay down and slept, and I arose, for the Lord sustained me.

3:7  LA: Non timebo millia populi circumdantis me. Exsurge, Domine ; salvum me fac, Deus meus.
     EN: I will not fear the thousands of people surrounding me. Arise, Lord; save me, my God. For You have struck all who oppose me without cause; You have crushed the teeth of sinners.

3:8  LA: Non timebo millia populi circumdantis me. Exsurge, Domine ; salvum me fac, Deus meus.
     EN: I will not fear the thousands of people surrounding me. Arise, Lord; save me, my God. For You have struck all who oppose me without cause; You have crushed the teeth of sinners.

3:9  LA: Domini est salus ; et super populum tuum benedictio tua.]
     EN: Salvation belongs to the Lord, and Your blessing is upon Your people.

PSALMS 4

4:1  LA: In finem, in carminibus. Psalmus David.
     EN: For the end, with songs. A psalm of David.

4:2  LA: [Cum invocarem exaudivit me Deus justitiæ meæ, in tribulatione dilatasti mihi. Miserere mei, et exaudi orationem meam.
     EN: When I called, the God of my righteousness answered me. In distress You made room for me. Have mercy on me and hear my prayer.

4:3  LA: Filii hominum, usquequo gravi corde ? ut quid diligitis vanitatem, et quæritis mendacium ?
     EN: Children of men, how long will your hearts be heavy? Why do you love emptiness and seek a lie?

4:4  LA: Et scitote quoniam mirificavit Dominus sanctum suum ; Dominus exaudiet me cum clamavero ad eum.
     EN: Know that the Lord has made His holy one wonderful; the Lord will hear me when I cry to Him.

4:5  LA: Irascimini, et nolite peccare ; quæ dicitis in cordibus vestris, in cubilibus vestris compungimini.
     EN: Be angry, but do not sin. Be pierced with remorse upon your beds for what you say in your hearts.

4:6  LA: Sacrificate sacrificium justitiæ, et sperate in Domino. Multi dicunt : Quis ostendit nobis bona ?
     EN: Offer a sacrifice of righteousness, and hope in the Lord. Many say, “Who shows us good things?”

4:7  LA: Signatum est super nos lumen vultus tui, Domine : dedisti lætitiam in corde meo.
     EN: The light of Your face has been marked upon us, Lord; You have put gladness in my heart. They have increased through the yield of their grain, wine, and oil.

4:8  LA: Signatum est super nos lumen vultus tui, Domine : dedisti lætitiam in corde meo.
     EN: The light of Your face has been marked upon us, Lord; You have put gladness in my heart. They have increased through the yield of their grain, wine, and oil.

4:9  LA: In pace in idipsum dormiam, et requiescam ;
     EN: In peace I will lie down and rest together, for You, Lord, have established me uniquely in hope.

PSALMS 5

5:1  LA: In finem, pro ea quæ hæreditatem consequitur. Psalmus David.
     EN: For the end, for her who receives the inheritance. A psalm of David.

5:2  LA: [Verba mea auribus percipe, Domine ; intellige clamorem meum.
     EN: Listen to my words, Lord; understand my cry.

5:3  LA: Intende voci orationis meæ, rex meus et Deus meus.
     EN: Attend to the voice of my prayer, my King and my God.

5:4  LA: Quoniam ad te orabo, Domine : mane exaudies vocem meam.
     EN: For I will pray to You, Lord; in the morning You will hear my voice. In the morning I will stand before You and see that You are no God who desires iniquity.

5:5  LA: Quoniam ad te orabo, Domine : mane exaudies vocem meam.
     EN: For I will pray to You, Lord; in the morning You will hear my voice. In the morning I will stand before You and see that You are no God who desires iniquity.

5:6  LA: Neque habitabit juxta te malignus, neque permanebunt injusti ante oculos tuos.
     EN: An evildoer will not dwell beside You, nor will the unjust remain before Your eyes.

5:7  LA: Odisti omnes qui operantur iniquitatem ; perdes omnes qui loquuntur mendacium. Virum sanguinum et dolosum abominabitur Dominus.
     EN: You hate all who work iniquity; You will destroy all who speak lies. The Lord will abhor the bloodthirsty and deceitful man.

5:8  LA: Ego autem in multitudine misericordiæ tuæ introibo in domum tuam ; adorabo ad templum sanctum tuum in timore tuo.
     EN: But in the abundance of Your mercy I will enter Your house; in fear of You I will worship toward Your holy temple.

5:9  LA: Domine, deduc me in justitia tua : propter inimicos meos dirige in conspectu tuo viam meam.
     EN: Lord, lead me in Your righteousness; because of my enemies, make my way straight in Your sight.

5:10  LA: Quoniam non est in ore eorum veritas ; cor eorum vanum est.
      EN: For there is no truth in their mouths; their heart is empty.

5:11  LA: Sepulchrum patens est guttur eorum ; linguis suis dolose agebant : judica illos, Deus. Decidant a cogitationibus suis ; secundum multitudinem impietatum eorum expelle eos, quoniam irritaverunt te, Domine.
      EN: Their throat is an open tomb; with their tongues they acted deceitfully. Judge them, God. Let them fall from their schemes; cast them out for their many ungodly deeds, for they have provoked You, Lord.

5:12  LA: Et lætentur omnes qui sperant in te ; in æternum exsultabunt, et habitabis in eis. Et gloriabuntur in te omnes qui diligunt nomen tuum,
      EN: Let all who hope in You be glad; they will rejoice forever, and You will dwell in them. All who love Your name will glory in You,

5:13  LA: quoniam tu benedices justo. Domine, ut scuto bonæ voluntatis tuæ coronasti nos.]
      EN: for You will bless the just. Lord, You have crowned us with Your goodwill as with a shield.

PSALMS 6

6:1  LA: In finem, in carminibus. Psalmus David. Pro octava.
     EN: For the end, with songs. A psalm of David. For the eighth.

6:2  LA: [Domine, ne in furore tuo arguas me, neque in ira tua corripias me.
     EN: Lord, do not rebuke me in Your fury or chastise me in Your anger.

6:3  LA: Miserere mei, Domine, quoniam infirmus sum ; sana me, Domine, quoniam conturbata sunt ossa mea.
     EN: Have mercy on me, Lord, for I am weak; heal me, Lord, for my bones are troubled.

6:4  LA: Et anima mea turbata est valde ; sed tu, Domine, usquequo ?
     EN: My soul too is greatly troubled; but You, Lord—how long?

6:5  LA: Convertere, Domine, et eripe animam meam ; salvum me fac propter misericordiam tuam.
     EN: Turn back, Lord, and rescue my soul; save me for the sake of Your mercy.

6:6  LA: Quoniam non est in morte qui memor sit tui ; in inferno autem quis confitebitur tibi ?
     EN: For in death no one remembers You; in the underworld, who will give You praise?

6:7  LA: Laboravi in gemitu meo ; lavabo per singulas noctes lectum meum : lacrimis meis stratum meum rigabo.
     EN: I have grown weary with my groaning. Every night I will wash my bed; with my tears I will drench my couch.

6:8  LA: Turbatus est a furore oculus meus ; inveteravi inter omnes inimicos meos.
     EN: My eye is troubled by fury; I have grown old among all my enemies.

6:9  LA: Discedite a me omnes qui operamini iniquitatem, quoniam exaudivit Dominus vocem fletus mei.
     EN: Depart from me, all who work iniquity, for the Lord has heard the sound of my weeping.

6:10  LA: Exaudivit Dominus deprecationem meam ; Dominus orationem meam suscepit.
      EN: The Lord has heard my plea; the Lord has received my prayer.

6:11  LA: Erubescant, et conturbentur vehementer, omnes inimici mei ; convertantur, et erubescant valde velociter.]
      EN: Let all my enemies be ashamed and greatly troubled; let them turn back and be put to shame very quickly.

PSALMS 7

7:1  LA: Psalmus David, quem cantavit Domino pro verbis Chusi, filii Jemini.
     EN: A psalm of David, which he sang to the Lord concerning the words of Chusi, son of Jemini.

7:2  LA: [Domine Deus meus, in te speravi ; salvum me fac ex omnibus persequentibus me, et libera me :
     EN: Lord my God, in You I have hoped; save me from all who pursue me, and deliver me,

7:3  LA: nequando rapiat ut leo animam meam, dum non est qui redimat, neque qui salvum faciat.
     EN: lest he seize my soul like a lion when there is no one to redeem or save.

7:4  LA: Domine Deus meus, si feci istud, si est iniquitas in manibus meis,
     EN: Lord my God, if I have done this, if there is iniquity in my hands,

7:5  LA: si reddidi retribuentibus mihi mala, decidam merito ab inimicis meis inanis.
     EN: if I have repaid those who repay me with evil, let me deservedly fall helpless before my enemies.

7:6  LA: Persequatur inimicus animam meam, et comprehendat ; et conculcet in terra vitam meam, et gloriam meam in pulverem deducat.
     EN: Let the enemy pursue my soul and seize it; let him trample my life upon the earth and bring my glory down to dust.

7:7  LA: Exsurge, Domine, in ira tua, et exaltare in finibus inimicorum meorum : et exsurge, Domine Deus meus, in præcepto quod mandasti,
     EN: Arise, Lord, in Your anger, and be exalted within my enemies' borders. Arise, Lord my God, in the command You have given,

7:8  LA: et synagoga populorum circumdabit te : et propter hanc in altum regredere :
     EN: and an assembly of peoples will surround You; for its sake, return on high.

7:9  LA: Dominus judicat populos. Judica me, Domine, secundum justitiam meam, et secundum innocentiam meam super me.
     EN: The Lord judges the peoples. Judge me, Lord, according to my righteousness and according to the innocence that is in me.

7:10  LA: Consumetur nequitia peccatorum, et diriges justum, scrutans corda et renes, Deus.
      EN: The wickedness of sinners will come to an end, and You will guide the just, God, who searches hearts and kidneys.

7:11  LA: Justum adjutorium meum a Domino, qui salvos facit rectos corde.
      EN: My righteous help comes from the Lord, who saves the upright of heart.

7:12  LA: Deus judex justus, fortis, et patiens ; numquid irascitur per singulos dies ?
      EN: God is a just judge, strong and patient; does He become angry every day?

7:13  LA: Nisi conversi fueritis, gladium suum vibrabit ; arcum suum tetendit, et paravit illum.
      EN: Unless you turn back, He will brandish His sword; He has drawn His bow and made it ready.

7:14  LA: Et in eo paravit vasa mortis, sagittas suas ardentibus effecit.
      EN: Upon it He has prepared instruments of death; He has made His arrows for those who burn.

7:15  LA: Ecce parturiit injustitiam ; concepit dolorem, et peperit iniquitatem.
      EN: See, he has been in labor with injustice; he has conceived trouble and given birth to iniquity.

7:16  LA: Lacum aperuit, et effodit eum ; et incidit in foveam quam fecit.
      EN: He opened a pit and dug it out, and he fell into the hole he made.

7:17  LA: Convertetur dolor ejus in caput ejus, et in verticem ipsius iniquitas ejus descendet.
      EN: His trouble will return upon his head, and his iniquity will come down upon his crown.

7:18  LA: Confitebor Domino secundum justitiam ejus, et psallam nomini Domini altissimi.]
      EN: I will praise the Lord according to His righteousness and sing to the name of the Lord Most High.

PSALMS 8

8:1  LA: In finem, pro torcularibus. Psalmus David.
     EN: For the end, for the winepresses. A psalm of David.

8:2  LA: [Domine, Dominus noster, quam admirabile est nomen tuum in universa terra ! quoniam elevata est magnificentia tua super cælos.
     EN: Lord, our Lord, how wonderful is Your name throughout the earth! For Your majesty has been raised above the heavens.

8:3  LA: Ex ore infantium et lactentium perfecisti laudem propter inimicos tuos, ut destruas inimicum et ultorem.
     EN: From the mouths of infants and nursing children You have perfected praise because of Your enemies, to destroy the enemy and the avenger.

8:4  LA: Quoniam videbo cælos tuos, opera digitorum tuorum, lunam et stellas quæ tu fundasti.
     EN: For I will look upon Your heavens, the works of Your fingers, the moon and the stars You have established.

8:5  LA: Quid est homo, quod memor es ejus ? aut filius hominis, quoniam visitas eum ?
     EN: What is man, that You remember him, or the son of man, that You visit him?

8:6  LA: Minuisti eum paulominus ab angelis ; gloria et honore coronasti eum ;
     EN: You have made him a little lower than the angels; You have crowned him with glory and honor,

8:7  LA: et constituisti eum super opera manuum tuarum.
     EN: and You have set him over the works of Your hands. You have subjected all things beneath his feet: all sheep and cattle, and also the beasts of the field,

8:8  LA: et constituisti eum super opera manuum tuarum.
     EN: and You have set him over the works of Your hands. You have subjected all things beneath his feet: all sheep and cattle, and also the beasts of the field,

8:9  LA: volucres cæli, et pisces maris qui perambulant semitas maris.
     EN: the birds of the sky and the fish of the sea that travel the paths of the seas.

8:10  LA: Domine, Dominus noster, quam admirabile est nomen tuum in universa terra !]
      EN: Lord, our Lord, how wonderful is Your name throughout the earth!

PSALMS 9

9:1  LA: In finem, pro occultis filii. Psalmus David.
     EN: For the end, concerning the hidden things of the son. A psalm of David.

9:2  LA: [Confitebor tibi, Domine, in toto corde meo ; narrabo omnia mirabilia tua.
     EN: I will praise You, Lord, with all my heart; I will tell of all Your wonders.

9:3  LA: Lætabor et exsultabo in te ; psallam nomini tuo, Altissime.
     EN: I will be glad and rejoice in You; I will sing to Your name, Most High.

9:4  LA: In convertendo inimicum meum retrorsum ; infirmabuntur, et peribunt a facie tua.
     EN: When my enemy turns back, they will weaken and perish before You.

9:5  LA: Quoniam fecisti judicium meum et causam meam ; sedisti super thronum, qui judicas justitiam.
     EN: For You have upheld my judgment and my cause; You sat upon the throne, judging righteousness.

9:6  LA: Increpasti gentes, et periit impius : nomen eorum delesti in æternum, et in sæculum sæculi.
     EN: You rebuked the nations, and the ungodly perished; You erased their name forever and for ages of ages.

9:7  LA: Inimici defecerunt frameæ in finem, et civitates eorum destruxisti. Periit memoria eorum cum sonitu ;
     EN: The enemy's spears have failed completely, and You have destroyed their cities. Their memory perished with a crash,

9:8  LA: et Dominus in æternum permanet. Paravit in judicio thronum suum,
     EN: but the Lord remains forever. He has prepared His throne for judgment,

9:9  LA: et ipse judicabit orbem terræ in æquitate : judicabit populos in justitia.
     EN: and He will judge the world with equity; He will judge the peoples with righteousness.

9:10  LA: Et factus est Dominus refugium pauperi ; adjutor in opportunitatibus, in tribulatione.
      EN: The Lord has become a refuge for the poor, a helper at the right times, in distress.

9:11  LA: Et sperent in te qui noverunt nomen tuum, quoniam non dereliquisti quærentes te, Domine.
      EN: Let those who know Your name hope in You, for You have not abandoned those who seek You, Lord.

9:12  LA: Psallite Domino qui habitat in Sion ; annuntiate inter gentes studia ejus :
      EN: Sing to the Lord who dwells in Zion; proclaim His endeavors among the nations,

9:13  LA: quoniam requirens sanguinem eorum recordatus est ; non est oblitus clamorem pauperum.
      EN: for He who calls bloodshed to account has remembered them; He has not forgotten the cry of the poor.

9:14  LA: Miserere mei, Domine : vide humilitatem meam de inimicis meis,
      EN: Have mercy on me, Lord; see how my enemies have brought me low, You who lift me up from the gates of death, so that I may proclaim all Your praises in the gates of the daughter of Zion.

9:15  LA: Miserere mei, Domine : vide humilitatem meam de inimicis meis,
      EN: Have mercy on me, Lord; see how my enemies have brought me low, You who lift me up from the gates of death, so that I may proclaim all Your praises in the gates of the daughter of Zion.

9:16  LA: exultabo in salutari tuo. Infixæ sunt gentes in interitu quem fecerunt ; in laqueo isto quem absconderunt comprehensus est pes eorum.
      EN: I will rejoice in Your salvation. The nations have sunk into the ruin they made; their foot has been caught in the snare they hid.

9:17  LA: Cognoscetur Dominus judicia faciens ; in operibus manuum suarum comprehensus est peccator.
      EN: The Lord will be known through the judgments He carries out; the sinner has been caught in the works of his own hands.

9:18  LA: Convertantur peccatores in infernum, omnes gentes quæ obliviscuntur Deum.
      EN: Let sinners return to the underworld, all the nations that forget God.

9:19  LA: Quoniam non in finem oblivio erit pauperis ; patientia pauperum non peribit in finem.
      EN: For the poor will not be forgotten forever; the endurance of the poor will not perish forever.

9:20  LA: Exsurge, Domine ; non confortetur homo : judicentur gentes in conspectu tuo.
      EN: Arise, Lord; let man not grow strong. Let the nations be judged in Your sight.

9:21  LA: Constitue, Domine, legislatorem super eos, ut sciant gentes quoniam homines sunt.
      EN: Appoint a lawgiver over them, Lord, so that the nations may know that they are human.

9:22  LA: Ut quid, Domine, recessisti longe ; despicis in opportunitatibus, in tribulatione ?
      EN: Why, Lord, have You withdrawn far away? Why do You look away at the needed times, in distress?

9:23  LA: Dum superbit impius, incenditur pauper : comprehenduntur in consiliis quibus cogitant.
      EN: While the ungodly man grows proud, the poor man is set on fire; they are caught in the schemes they devise.

9:24  LA: Quoniam laudatur peccator in desideriis animæ suæ, et iniquus benedicitur.
      EN: For the sinner is praised for his soul's desires, and the unjust man is blessed.

9:25  LA: Exacerbavit Dominum peccator : secundum multitudinem iræ suæ, non quæret.
      EN: The sinner has provoked the Lord; in the abundance of his anger, he will not seek Him.

9:26  LA: Non est Deus in conspectu ejus ; inquinatæ sunt viæ illius in omni tempore. Auferuntur judicia tua a facie ejus ; omnium inimicorum suorum dominabitur.
      EN: God is not in his sight; his ways are defiled at all times. Your judgments are removed from his sight; he will rule over all his enemies.

9:27  LA: Dixit enim in corde suo : Non movebor a generatione in generationem, sine malo.
      EN: For he has said in his heart, “I will not be shaken from generation to generation; I will be without evil.”

9:28  LA: Cujus maledictione os plenum est, et amaritudine, et dolo ; sub lingua ejus labor et dolor.
      EN: His mouth is full of cursing, bitterness, and deceit; beneath his tongue are toil and trouble.

9:29  LA: Sedet in insidiis cum divitibus in occultis, ut interficiat innocentem.
      EN: He sits in ambush with the rich in hidden places, to kill the innocent.

9:30  LA: Oculi ejus in pauperem respiciunt ; insidiatur in abscondito, quasi leo in spelunca sua. Insidiatur ut rapiat pauperem ; rapere pauperem dum attrahit eum.
      EN: His eyes watch the poor; he lies hidden like a lion in its den. He lies in wait to seize the poor, to seize the poor man as he draws him in.

9:31  LA: In laqueo suo humiliabit eum ; inclinabit se, et cadet cum dominatus fuerit pauperum.
      EN: In his snare he will bring him low; he will bend down and fall when he has gained mastery over the poor.

9:32  LA: Dixit enim in corde suo : Oblitus est Deus ; avertit faciem suam, ne videat in finem.
      EN: For he has said in his heart, “God has forgotten; He has turned His face away, never to see.”

9:33  LA: Exsurge, Domine Deus, exaltetur manus tua ; ne obliviscaris pauperum.
      EN: Arise, Lord God; let Your hand be lifted high. Do not forget the poor.

9:34  LA: Propter quid irritavit impius Deum ? dixit enim in corde suo : Non requiret.
      EN: Why has the ungodly man provoked God? For he has said in his heart, “He will not call it to account.”

9:35  LA: Vides, quoniam tu laborem et dolorem consideras, ut tradas eos in manus tuas. Tibi derelictus est pauper ; orphano tu eris adjutor.
      EN: You see, for You consider toil and trouble, to take them into Your hands. The poor man is left to You; You will be a helper to the orphan.

9:36  LA: Contere brachium peccatoris et maligni ; quæretur peccatum illius, et non invenietur.
      EN: Break the arm of the sinner and evildoer; his sin will be sought and will not be found.

9:37  LA: Dominus regnabit in æternum, et in sæculum sæculi ; peribitis, gentes, de terra illius.
      EN: The Lord will reign forever and for ages of ages; you nations will perish from His land.

9:38  LA: Desiderium pauperum exaudivit Dominus ; præparationem cordis eorum audivit auris tua :
      EN: The Lord has heard the desire of the poor; Your ear has heard their heart's readiness,

9:39  LA: judicare pupillo et humili, ut non apponat ultra magnificare se homo super terram.]
      EN: to judge for the orphan and the lowly, so that man may no longer exalt himself upon the earth.

PSALMS 10

10:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David.
      EN: For the end. A psalm of David. In the Lord I trust. How can you say to my soul, “Flee to the mountain like a sparrow”?

10:2  LA: Quoniam ecce peccatores intenderunt arcum ; paraverunt sagittas suas in pharetra, ut sagittent in obscuro rectos corde :
      EN: For see, sinners have drawn the bow; they have prepared their arrows in the quiver to shoot the upright of heart in darkness.

10:3  LA: quoniam quæ perfecisti destruxerunt ; justus autem, quid fecit ?
      EN: For they have destroyed what You perfected; but what has the just man done?

10:4  LA: Dominus in templo sancto suo ; Dominus in cælo sedes ejus. Oculi ejus in pauperem respiciunt ; palpebræ ejus interrogant filios hominum.
      EN: The Lord is in His holy temple; the Lord's throne is in heaven. His eyes look upon the poor; His eyelids examine the children of men.

10:5  LA: Dominus interrogat justum et impium ; qui autem diligit iniquitatem, odit animam suam.
      EN: The Lord examines the just and the ungodly; but the one who loves iniquity hates his own soul.

10:6  LA: Pluet super peccatores laqueos ; ignis et sulphur, et spiritus procellarum, pars calicis eorum.
      EN: He will rain snares upon sinners; fire, sulfur, and a stormy wind will be the portion of their cup.

10:7  LA: Quoniam justus Dominus, et justitias dilexit : æquitatem vidit vultus ejus.]
      EN: For the Lord is just and has loved righteous deeds; His face has looked upon equity.

PSALMS 11

11:1  LA: In finem, pro octava. Psalmus David.
      EN: For the end, for the eighth. A psalm of David.

11:2  LA: [Salvum me fac, Domine, quoniam defecit sanctus, quoniam diminutæ sunt veritates a filiis hominum.
      EN: Save me, Lord, for the holy man has failed, for truths have diminished among the children of men.

11:3  LA: Vana locuti sunt unusquisque ad proximum suum ; labia dolosa, in corde et corde locuti sunt.
      EN: Each has spoken emptiness to his neighbor; with deceitful lips and a double heart they have spoken.

11:4  LA: Disperdat Dominus universa labia dolosa, et linguam magniloquam.
      EN: May the Lord destroy all deceitful lips and the boastful tongue,

11:5  LA: Qui dixerunt : Linguam nostram magnificabimus ; labia nostra a nobis sunt. Quis noster dominus est ?
      EN: those who have said, “We will magnify our tongue; our lips are our own. Who is our master?”

11:6  LA: Propter miseriam inopum, et gemitum pauperum, nunc exsurgam, dicit Dominus. Ponam in salutari ; fiducialiter agam in eo.
      EN: “Because of the misery of the needy and the groaning of the poor, now I will arise,” says the Lord. “I will place him in safety; I will act confidently in him.”

11:7  LA: Eloquia Domini, eloquia casta ; argentum igne examinatum, probatum terræ, purgatum septuplum.
      EN: The Lord's words are pure words: silver examined by fire, tested for the earth, purified sevenfold.

11:8  LA: Tu, Domine, servabis nos, et custodies nos a generatione hac in æternum.
      EN: You, Lord, will preserve us and guard us from this generation forever.

11:9  LA: In circuitu impii ambulant : secundum altitudinem tuam multiplicasti filios hominum.]
      EN: The ungodly walk all around; according to Your exalted greatness You have multiplied the children of men.

PSALMS 12

12:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David. [Usquequo, Domine, oblivisceris me in finem ? usquequo avertis faciem tuam a me ?
      EN: For the end. A psalm of David. How long, Lord, will You forget me completely? How long will You turn Your face away from me?

12:2  LA: In finem. Psalmus David. [Usquequo, Domine, oblivisceris me in finem ? usquequo avertis faciem tuam a me ?
      EN: For the end. A psalm of David. How long, Lord, will You forget me completely? How long will You turn Your face away from me?

12:3  LA: quamdiu ponam consilia in anima mea ; dolorem in corde meo per diem ?
      EN: How long will I lay plans within my soul, with sorrow in my heart through the day? How long will my enemy be exalted above me?

12:4  LA: Respice, et exaudi me, Domine Deus meus. Illumina oculos meos, ne umquam obdormiam in morte ;
      EN: Look upon me and hear me, Lord my God. Give light to my eyes, lest I ever fall asleep in death,

12:5  LA: nequando dicat inimicus meus : Prævalui adversus eum. Qui tribulant me exsultabunt si motus fuero ;
      EN: lest my enemy say, “I have prevailed against him.” Those who afflict me will rejoice if I am shaken.

12:6  LA: ego autem in misericordia tua speravi. Exsultabit cor meum in salutari tuo. Cantabo Domino qui bona tribuit mihi ; et psallam nomini Domini altissimi.]
      EN: But I have hoped in Your mercy. My heart will rejoice in Your salvation. I will sing to the Lord who has bestowed good things on me, and I will sing to the name of the Lord Most High.

PSALMS 13

13:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David. [Dixit insipiens in corde suo : Non est Deus. Corrupti sunt, et abominabiles facti sunt in studiis suis ; non est qui faciat bonum, non est usque ad unum.
      EN: For the end. A psalm of David. The fool has said in his heart, “There is no God.” They are corrupted and have become abominable in their pursuits; no one does good, not even one.

13:2  LA: Dominus de cælo prospexit super filios hominum, ut videat si est intelligens, aut requirens Deum.
      EN: The Lord looked down from heaven upon the children of men to see whether anyone understands or seeks God.

13:3  LA: Omnes declinaverunt, simul inutiles facti sunt. Non est qui faciat bonum, non est usque ad unum. Sepulchrum patens est guttur eorum ; linguis suis dolose agebant. Venenum aspidum sub labiis eorum, quorum os maledictione et amaritudine plenum est ; veloces pedes eorum ad effundendum sanguinem. Contritio et infelicitas in viis eorum, et viam pacis non cognoverunt ; non est timor Dei ante oculos eorum.
      EN: All have turned aside; together they have become useless. No one does good, not even one. Their throat is an open tomb; with their tongues they acted deceitfully. The venom of asps is beneath their lips, and their mouth is full of cursing and bitterness; their feet are swift to shed blood. Ruin and misery are in their ways, and they have not known the way of peace; there is no fear of God before their eyes.

13:4  LA: Nonne cognoscent omnes qui operantur iniquitatem, qui devorant plebem meam sicut escam panis ?
      EN: Will they not come to know, all who work iniquity, who devour my people as they eat bread?

13:5  LA: Dominum non invocaverunt ; illic trepidaverunt timore, ubi non erat timor.
      EN: They have not called upon the Lord; there they trembled with fear where there was no fear.

13:6  LA: Quoniam Dominus in generatione justa est : consilium inopis confudistis, quoniam Dominus spes ejus est.
      EN: For the Lord is among the just generation. You have shamed the counsel of the needy, for the Lord is his hope.

13:7  LA: Quis dabit ex Sion salutare Israël ? Cum averterit Dominus captivitatem plebis suæ, exsultabit Jacob, et lætabitur Israël.]
      EN: Who will bring Israel's salvation from Zion? When the Lord turns back His people's captivity, Jacob will rejoice and Israel will be glad.

PSALMS 14

14:1  LA: Psalmus David. [Domine, quis habitabit in tabernaculo tuo ? aut quis requiescet in monte sancto tuo ?
      EN: A psalm of David. Lord, who will dwell in Your tent, or who will rest upon Your holy mountain?

14:2  LA: Qui ingreditur sine macula, et operatur justitiam ;
      EN: The one who walks without stain and practices righteousness,

14:3  LA: qui loquitur veritatem in corde suo : qui non egit dolum in lingua sua, nec fecit proximo suo malum, et opprobrium non accepit adversus proximos suos.
      EN: who speaks truth in his heart, who has not deceived with his tongue, done evil to his neighbor, or accepted a reproach against his neighbors.

14:4  LA: Ad nihilum deductus est in conspectu ejus malignus ; timentes autem Dominum glorificat. Qui jurat proximo suo, et non decipit ;
      EN: The evildoer is reckoned as nothing in his sight, but he glorifies those who fear the Lord; he swears to his neighbor and does not deceive,

14:5  LA: qui pecuniam suam non dedit ad usuram, et munera super innocentem non accepit : qui facit hæc non movebitur in æternum.]
      EN: he has not lent his money at interest or accepted gifts against the innocent. Whoever does these things will never be shaken.

PSALMS 15

15:1  LA: Tituli inscriptio, ipsi David. [Conserva me, Domine, quoniam speravi in te.
      EN: An inscription for a title, for David himself. Preserve me, Lord, for I have hoped in You.

15:2  LA: Dixi Domino : Deus meus es tu, quoniam bonorum meorum non eges.
      EN: I said to the Lord, “You are my God, for You have no need of my good things.”

15:3  LA: Sanctis qui sunt in terra ejus, mirificavit omnes voluntates meas in eis.
      EN: For the holy ones who are in His land, He has made all my desires in them wonderful.

15:4  LA: Multiplicatæ sunt infirmitates eorum : postea acceleraverunt. Non congregabo conventicula eorum de sanguinibus, nec memor ero nominum eorum per labia mea.
      EN: Their weaknesses have multiplied; afterward they hastened. I will not gather their gatherings of blood, nor will I remember their names upon my lips.

15:5  LA: Dominus pars hæreditatis meæ, et calicis mei : tu es qui restitues hæreditatem meam mihi.
      EN: The Lord is the portion of my inheritance and my cup; You are the One who will restore my inheritance to me.

15:6  LA: Funes ceciderunt mihi in præclaris ; etenim hæreditas mea præclara est mihi.
      EN: The measuring cords have fallen for me in splendid places; indeed, my inheritance is splendid to me.

15:7  LA: Benedicam Dominum qui tribuit mihi intellectum ; insuper et usque ad noctem increpuerunt me renes mei.
      EN: I will bless the Lord who has given me understanding; even into the night my kidneys have admonished me.

15:8  LA: Providebam Dominum in conspectu meo semper : quoniam a dextris est mihi, ne commovear.
      EN: I kept the Lord always before my sight, for He is at my right hand, so that I will not be shaken.

15:9  LA: Propter hoc lætatum est cor meum, et exsultavit lingua mea ; insuper et caro mea requiescet in spe.
      EN: Therefore my heart has been glad and my tongue has rejoiced; even my flesh will rest in hope.

15:10  LA: Quoniam non derelinques animam meam in inferno, nec dabis sanctum tuum videre corruptionem. Notas mihi fecisti vias vitæ ; adimplebis me lætitia cum vultu tuo : delectationes in dextera tua usque in finem.]
       EN: For You will not abandon my soul in the underworld or allow Your holy one to see corruption. You have made the ways of life known to me; You will fill me with gladness in Your presence. At Your right hand are delights forever.

15:11  LA: Quoniam non derelinques animam meam in inferno, nec dabis sanctum tuum videre corruptionem. Notas mihi fecisti vias vitæ ; adimplebis me lætitia cum vultu tuo : delectationes in dextera tua usque in finem.]
       EN: For You will not abandon my soul in the underworld or allow Your holy one to see corruption. You have made the ways of life known to me; You will fill me with gladness in Your presence. At Your right hand are delights forever.

PSALMS 16

16:1  LA: Oratio David. [Exaudi, Domine, justitiam meam ; intende deprecationem meam. Auribus percipe orationem meam, non in labiis dolosis.
      EN: A prayer of David. Hear my righteousness, Lord; attend to my plea. Listen to my prayer, which comes from no deceitful lips.

16:2  LA: De vultu tuo judicium meum prodeat ; oculi tui videant æquitates.
      EN: Let my judgment come forth from Your presence; let Your eyes look upon fairness.

16:3  LA: Probasti cor meum, et visitasti nocte ; igne me examinasti, et non est inventa in me iniquitas.
      EN: You have tested my heart and visited it at night; You have examined me by fire, and no iniquity has been found in me.

16:4  LA: Ut non loquatur os meum opera hominum : propter verba labiorum tuorum, ego custodivi vias duras.
      EN: So that my mouth would not speak of human deeds, because of the words of Your lips I have kept to hard paths.

16:5  LA: Perfice gressus meos in semitis tuis, ut non moveantur vestigia mea.
      EN: Make my steps firm in Your paths, so that my footsteps may not waver.

16:6  LA: Ego clamavi, quoniam exaudisti me, Deus ; inclina aurem tuam mihi, et exaudi verba mea.
      EN: I have cried out, for You have heard me, God; incline Your ear to me and hear my words.

16:7  LA: Mirifica misericordias tuas, qui salvos facis sperantes in te.
      EN: Make Your mercies wonderful, You who save those who hope in You.

16:8  LA: A resistentibus dexteræ tuæ custodi me ut pupillam oculi. Sub umbra alarum tuarum protege me
      EN: From those who resist Your right hand, guard me like the pupil of an eye. Beneath the shadow of Your wings protect me

16:9  LA: a facie impiorum qui me afflixerunt. Inimici mei animam meam circumdederunt ;
      EN: from the ungodly who have afflicted me. My enemies have surrounded my soul;

16:10  LA: adipem suum concluserunt : os eorum locutum est superbiam.
       EN: they have enclosed their fat; their mouth has spoken arrogance.

16:11  LA: Projicientes me nunc circumdederunt me ; oculos suos statuerunt declinare in terram.
       EN: Casting me out, they have now surrounded me; they have fixed their eyes to bow down to the earth.

16:12  LA: Susceperunt me sicut leo paratus ad prædam, et sicut catulus leonis habitans in abditis.
       EN: They have taken me like a lion ready for prey, like a young lion dwelling in hiding.

16:13  LA: Exsurge, Domine : præveni eum, et supplanta eum : eripe animam meam ab impio ; frameam tuam
       EN: Arise, Lord; confront him and trip him up. Rescue my soul from the ungodly, Your spear

16:14  LA: ab inimicis manus tuæ. Domine, a paucis de terra divide eos in vita eorum ; de absconditis tuis adimpletus est venter eorum. Saturati sunt filiis, et dimiserunt reliquias suas parvulis suis.
       EN: from the enemies of Your hand. Lord, separate them in their lifetime from the few upon the earth; their belly is filled with Your hidden things. They are satisfied with children and have left their remains to their little ones.

16:15  LA: Ego autem in justitia apparebo conspectui tuo ; satiabor cum apparuerit gloria tua.]
       EN: But in righteousness I will appear in Your sight; I will be satisfied when Your glory appears.

PSALMS 17

17:1  LA: In finem. Puero Domini David, qui locutus est Domino verba cantici hujus, in die qua eripuit eum Dominus de manu omnium inimicorum ejus, et de manu Saul, et dixit :
      EN: For the end. For the Lord's servant David, who spoke the words of this song to the Lord on the day when the Lord rescued him from the hand of all his enemies and from the hand of Saul. He said:

17:2  LA: [Diligam te, Domine, fortitudo mea.
      EN: I will love You, Lord, my strength.

17:3  LA: Dominus firmamentum meum, et refugium meum, et liberator meus. Deus meus adjutor meus, et sperabo in eum ; protector meus, et cornu salutis meæ, et susceptor meus.
      EN: The Lord is my firm support, my refuge, and my deliverer. My God is my helper, and I will hope in Him; He is my protector, the horn of my salvation, and my defender.

17:4  LA: Laudans invocabo Dominum, et ab inimicis meis salvus ero.
      EN: With praise I will call upon the Lord, and I will be saved from my enemies.

17:5  LA: Circumdederunt me dolores mortis, et torrentes iniquitatis conturbaverunt me.
      EN: The pains of death surrounded me, and torrents of iniquity troubled me.

17:6  LA: Dolores inferni circumdederunt me ; præoccupaverunt me laquei mortis.
      EN: The pains of the underworld surrounded me; the snares of death overtook me.

17:7  LA: In tribulatione mea invocavi Dominum, et ad Deum meum clamavi : et exaudivit de templo sancto suo vocem meam ; et clamor meus in conspectu ejus introivit in aures ejus.
      EN: In my distress I called upon the Lord and cried to my God. From His holy temple He heard my voice, and my cry before Him entered His ears.

17:8  LA: Commota est, et contremuit terra ; fundamenta montium conturbata sunt, et commota sunt : quoniam iratus est eis.
      EN: The earth shook and trembled; the foundations of the mountains were troubled and shaken, for He was angry with them.

17:9  LA: Ascendit fumus in ira ejus, et ignis a facie ejus exarsit ; carbones succensi sunt ab eo.
      EN: Smoke rose in His anger, and fire blazed from His presence; coals were kindled by Him.

17:10  LA: Inclinavit cælos, et descendit, et caligo sub pedibus ejus.
       EN: He bent the heavens and came down, with darkness beneath His feet.

17:11  LA: Et ascendit super cherubim, et volavit ; volavit super pennas ventorum.
       EN: He mounted upon cherubim and flew; He flew upon the wings of the winds.

17:12  LA: Et posuit tenebras latibulum suum ; in circuitu ejus tabernaculum ejus, tenebrosa aqua in nubibus aëris.
       EN: He made darkness His hiding place; His tent surrounded Him, dark water in the clouds of the air.

17:13  LA: Præ fulgore in conspectu ejus nubes transierunt ; grando et carbones ignis.
       EN: Before the brightness in His presence, clouds passed: hail and coals of fire.

17:14  LA: Et intonuit de cælo Dominus, et Altissimus dedit vocem suam : grando et carbones ignis.
       EN: The Lord thundered from heaven, and the Most High uttered His voice: hail and coals of fire.

17:15  LA: Et misit sagittas suas, et dissipavit eos ; fulgura multiplicavit, et conturbavit eos.
       EN: He sent His arrows and scattered them; He multiplied lightning flashes and troubled them.

17:16  LA: Et apparuerunt fontes aquarum, et revelata sunt fundamenta orbis terrarum, ab increpatione tua, Domine, ab inspiratione spiritus iræ tuæ.
       EN: The springs of waters appeared, and the foundations of the world were uncovered at Your rebuke, Lord, at the blast of the breath of Your anger.

17:17  LA: Misit de summo, et accepit me ; et assumpsit me de aquis multis.
       EN: He sent from on high and took me; He drew me out of many waters.

17:18  LA: Eripuit me de inimicis meis fortissimis, et ab his qui oderunt me. Quoniam confortati sunt super me ;
       EN: He rescued me from my strongest enemies and from those who hated me, for they had grown stronger than I.

17:19  LA: prævenerunt me in die afflictionis meæ : et factus est Dominus protector meus.
       EN: They confronted me on the day of my affliction, but the Lord became my protector.

17:20  LA: Et eduxit me in latitudinem ; salvum me fecit, quoniam voluit me,
       EN: He led me out into a broad place; He saved me because He delighted in me.

17:21  LA: et retribuet mihi Dominus secundum justitiam meam, et secundum puritatem manuum mearum retribuet mihi :
       EN: The Lord will repay me according to my righteousness; according to the cleanness of my hands He will repay me,

17:22  LA: quia custodivi vias Domini, nec impie gessi a Deo meo ;
       EN: because I have kept the Lord's ways and have not acted wickedly against my God,

17:23  LA: quoniam omnia judicia ejus in conspectu meo, et justitias ejus non repuli a me.
       EN: for all His judgments are in my sight, and I have not pushed His righteous ordinances away from me.

17:24  LA: Et ero immaculatus cum eo ; et observabo me ab iniquitate mea.
       EN: I will be without stain with Him, and I will guard myself from my iniquity.

17:25  LA: Et retribuet mihi Dominus secundum justitiam meam, et secundum puritatem manuum mearum in conspectu oculorum ejus.
       EN: The Lord will repay me according to my righteousness and according to the cleanness of my hands in His eyes.

17:26  LA: Cum sancto sanctus eris, et cum viro innocente innocens eris,
       EN: With the holy You will be holy, and with the innocent man You will be innocent;

17:27  LA: et cum electo electus eris, et cum perverso perverteris.
       EN: with the chosen You will be chosen, and with the crooked You will turn crooked.

17:28  LA: Quoniam tu populum humilem salvum facies, et oculos superborum humiliabis.
       EN: For You will save the lowly people and bring down the eyes of the proud.

17:29  LA: Quoniam tu illuminas lucernam meam, Domine ; Deus meus, illumina tenebras meas.
       EN: For You light my lamp, Lord; my God, give light to my darkness.

17:30  LA: Quoniam in te eripiar a tentatione ; et in Deo meo transgrediar murum.
       EN: For in You I will be rescued from temptation, and in my God I will cross a wall.

17:31  LA: Deus meus, impolluta via ejus ; eloquia Domini igne examinata : protector est omnium sperantium in se.
       EN: My God—His way is undefiled; the Lord's words have been examined by fire. He is the protector of all who hope in Him.

17:32  LA: Quoniam quis deus præter Dominum ? aut quis deus præter Deum nostrum ?
       EN: For who is God besides the Lord? Or who is God besides our God?

17:33  LA: Deus qui præcinxit me virtute, et posuit immaculatam viam meam ;
       EN: It is God who has girded me with strength and made my way without stain,

17:34  LA: qui perfecit pedes meos tamquam cervorum, et super excelsa statuens me ;
       EN: who has made my feet sure like those of deer and established me upon the heights,

17:35  LA: qui docet manus meas ad prælium. Et posuisti, ut arcum æreum, brachia mea,
       EN: who teaches my hands for battle. You have made my arms like a bronze bow,

17:36  LA: et dedisti mihi protectionem salutis tuæ : et dextera tua suscepit me, et disciplina tua correxit me in finem, et disciplina tua ipsa me docebit.
       EN: and You have given me the protection of Your salvation. Your right hand has sustained me, and Your discipline has corrected me completely; Your discipline itself will teach me.

17:37  LA: Dilatasti gressus meos subtus me, et non sunt infirmata vestigia mea.
       EN: You have widened my steps beneath me, and my footsteps have not weakened.

17:38  LA: Persequar inimicos meos, et comprehendam illos ; et non convertar donec deficiant.
       EN: I will pursue my enemies and seize them; I will not turn back until they fail.

17:39  LA: Confringam illos, nec poterunt stare ; cadent subtus pedes meos.
       EN: I will crush them, and they will be unable to stand; they will fall beneath my feet.

17:40  LA: Et præcinxisti me virtute ad bellum, et supplantasti insurgentes in me subtus me.
       EN: You have girded me with strength for war and brought down beneath me those who rise against me.

17:41  LA: Et inimicos meos dedisti mihi dorsum, et odientes me disperdidisti.
       EN: You have made my enemies turn their backs to me, and You have destroyed those who hate me.

17:42  LA: Clamaverunt, nec erat qui salvos faceret ; ad Dominum, nec exaudivit eos.
       EN: They cried out, but there was no one to save them; they cried to the Lord, but He did not hear them.

17:43  LA: Et comminuam eos ut pulverem ante faciem venti ; ut lutum platearum delebo eos.
       EN: I will grind them like dust before the wind; I will wipe them away like mud in the streets.

17:44  LA: Eripies me de contradictionibus populi ; constitues me in caput gentium.
       EN: You will rescue me from the people's disputes; You will establish me as head of the nations.

17:45  LA: Populus quem non cognovi servivit mihi ; in auditu auris obedivit mihi.
       EN: A people I had not known served me; at the hearing of the ear it obeyed me.

17:46  LA: Filii alieni mentiti sunt mihi, filii alieni inveterati sunt, et claudicaverunt a semitis suis.
       EN: Foreign children have lied to me; foreign children have grown old and limped away from their paths.

17:47  LA: Vivit Dominus, et benedictus Deus meus, et exaltetur Deus salutis meæ.
       EN: The Lord lives, and blessed is my God; exalted be the God of my salvation.

17:48  LA: Deus qui das vindictas mihi, et subdis populos sub me ; liberator meus de inimicis meis iracundis.
       EN: God, You give me vengeance and subject peoples beneath me, my deliverer from my angry enemies.

17:49  LA: Et ab insurgentibus in me exaltabis me ; a viro iniquo eripies me.
       EN: You will lift me above those who rise against me; You will rescue me from the unjust man.

17:50  LA: Propterea confitebor tibi in nationibus, Domine, et nomini tuo psalmum dicam ;
       EN: Therefore I will praise You among the nations, Lord, and sing a psalm to Your name.

17:51  LA: magnificans salutes regis ejus, et faciens misericordiam christo suo David, et semini ejus usque in sæculum.]
       EN: He magnifies the deliverances of His king and shows mercy to His anointed, David, and to his descendants forever.

PSALMS 18

18:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David.
      EN: For the end. A psalm of David.

18:2  LA: [Cæli enarrant gloriam Dei, et opera manuum ejus annuntiat firmamentum.
      EN: The heavens recount the glory of God, and the firmament proclaims the works of His hands.

18:3  LA: Dies diei eructat verbum, et nox nocti indicat scientiam.
      EN: Day pours out a word to day, and night reveals knowledge to night.

18:4  LA: Non sunt loquelæ, neque sermones, quorum non audiantur voces eorum.
      EN: There are no languages or words in which their voices are not heard.

18:5  LA: In omnem terram exivit sonus eorum, et in fines orbis terræ verba eorum.
      EN: Their sound has gone out through all the earth, and their words to the ends of the world.

18:6  LA: In sole posuit tabernaculum suum ; et ipse tamquam sponsus procedens de thalamo suo. Exsultavit ut gigas ad currendam viam ;
      EN: In the sun He has placed His tent, and he is like a bridegroom coming from his chamber. He has rejoiced like a giant to run his course;

18:7  LA: a summo cælo egressio ejus. Et occursus ejus usque ad summum ejus ; nec est qui se abscondat a calore ejus.
      EN: his departure is from the height of heaven, and his circuit reaches to its height. No one can hide from his heat.

18:8  LA: Lex Domini immaculata, convertens animas ; testimonium Domini fidele, sapientiam præstans parvulis.
      EN: The Lord's law is without stain, turning souls back; the Lord's testimony is faithful, giving wisdom to little ones.

18:9  LA: Justitiæ Domini rectæ, lætificantes corda ; præceptum Domini lucidum, illuminans oculos.
      EN: The Lord's righteous ordinances are upright, gladdening hearts; the Lord's command is clear, enlightening eyes.

18:10  LA: Timor Domini sanctus, permanens in sæculum sæculi ; judicia Domini vera, justificata in semetipsa,
       EN: The fear of the Lord is holy, remaining for ages of ages; the Lord's judgments are true, justified in themselves,

18:11  LA: desiderabilia super aurum et lapidem pretiosum multum, et dulciora super mel et favum.
       EN: more desirable than gold and much precious stone, and sweeter than honey and the honeycomb.

18:12  LA: Etenim servus tuus custodit ea ; in custodiendis illis retributio multa.
       EN: Indeed, Your servant keeps them; in keeping them there is great reward.

18:13  LA: Delicta quis intelligit ? ab occultis meis munda me ;
       EN: Who understands his offenses? Cleanse me from my hidden faults,

18:14  LA: et ab alienis parce servo tuo. Si mei non fuerint dominati, tunc immaculatus ero, et emundabor a delicto maximo.
       EN: and spare Your servant from those of others. If they do not rule over me, then I will be without stain and cleansed of the greatest offense.

18:15  LA: Et erunt ut complaceant eloquia oris mei, et meditatio cordis mei in conspectu tuo semper. Domine, adjutor meus, et redemptor meus.]
       EN: The words of my mouth and the meditation of my heart will always be pleasing in Your sight, Lord, my helper and my redeemer.

PSALMS 19

19:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David.
      EN: For the end. A psalm of David.

19:2  LA: [Exaudiat te Dominus in die tribulationis ; protegat te nomen Dei Jacob.
      EN: May the Lord hear you on the day of distress; may the name of the God of Jacob protect you.

19:3  LA: Mittat tibi auxilium de sancto, et de Sion tueatur te.
      EN: May He send you help from the holy place and guard you from Zion.

19:4  LA: Memor sit omnis sacrificii tui, et holocaustum tuum pingue fiat.
      EN: May He remember every sacrifice of yours, and may your burnt offering be rich.

19:5  LA: Tribuat tibi secundum cor tuum, et omne consilium tuum confirmet.
      EN: May He grant you your heart's desire and confirm your every plan.

19:6  LA: Lætabimur in salutari tuo ; et in nomine Dei nostri magnificabimur.
      EN: We will rejoice in your salvation and be exalted in the name of our God.

19:7  LA: Impleat Dominus omnes petitiones tuas ; nunc cognovi quoniam salvum fecit Dominus christum suum. Exaudiet illum de cælo sancto suo, in potentatibus salus dexteræ ejus.
      EN: May the Lord fulfill all your petitions. Now I know that the Lord has saved His anointed. He will hear him from His holy heaven; the salvation of His right hand is mighty.

19:8  LA: Hi in curribus, et hi in equis ; nos autem in nomine Domini Dei nostri invocabimus.
      EN: Some rely on chariots and some on horses, but we will call upon the name of the Lord our God.

19:9  LA: Ipsi obligati sunt, et ceciderunt ; nos autem surreximus, et erecti sumus.
      EN: They have been bound and have fallen, but we have risen and stand upright.

19:10  LA: Domine, salvum fac regem, et exaudi nos in die qua invocaverimus te.]
       EN: Lord, save the king, and hear us on the day when we call upon You.

PSALMS 20

20:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David.
      EN: For the end. A psalm of David.

20:2  LA: [Domine, in virtute tua lætabitur rex, et super salutare tuum exsultabit vehementer.
      EN: Lord, in Your strength the king will be glad, and in Your salvation he will greatly rejoice.

20:3  LA: Desiderium cordis ejus tribuisti ei, et voluntate labiorum ejus non fraudasti eum.
      EN: You have granted him his heart's desire and have not denied him the wish of his lips.

20:4  LA: Quoniam prævenisti eum in benedictionibus dulcedinis ; posuisti in capite ejus coronam de lapide pretioso.
      EN: For You met him with blessings of sweetness; You placed upon his head a crown of precious stone.

20:5  LA: Vitam petiit a te, et tribuisti ei longitudinem dierum, in sæculum, et in sæculum sæculi.
      EN: He asked You for life, and You granted him length of days, forever and for ages of ages.

20:6  LA: Magna est gloria ejus in salutari tuo ; gloriam et magnum decorem impones super eum.
      EN: Great is his glory in Your salvation; You will place glory and great splendor upon him.

20:7  LA: Quoniam dabis eum in benedictionem in sæculum sæculi ; lætificabis eum in gaudio cum vultu tuo.
      EN: For You will make him a blessing for ages of ages; You will gladden him with joy in Your presence.

20:8  LA: Quoniam rex sperat in Domino, et in misericordia Altissimi non commovebitur.
      EN: For the king hopes in the Lord, and through the mercy of the Most High he will not be shaken.

20:9  LA: Inveniatur manus tua omnibus inimicis tuis ; dextera tua inveniat omnes qui te oderunt.
      EN: May your hand be found by all your enemies; may your right hand find all who hate you.

20:10  LA: Pones eos ut clibanum ignis in tempore vultus tui : Dominus in ira sua conturbabit eos, et devorabit eos ignis.
       EN: You will make them like a fiery oven at the time of your appearing. The Lord will trouble them in His anger, and fire will devour them.

20:11  LA: Fructum eorum de terra perdes, et semen eorum a filiis hominum,
       EN: You will destroy their fruit from the earth and their descendants from among the children of men,

20:12  LA: quoniam declinaverunt in te mala ; cogitaverunt consilia quæ non potuerunt stabilire.
       EN: for they have turned evil against you; they have devised plans they could not establish.

20:13  LA: Quoniam pones eos dorsum ; in reliquiis tuis præparabis vultum eorum.
       EN: For you will make them turn their backs; among those you leave, you will make ready their faces.

20:14  LA: Exaltare, Domine, in virtute tua ; cantabimus et psallemus virtutes tuas.]
       EN: Be exalted, Lord, in Your strength; we will sing and chant of Your mighty deeds.

PSALMS 21

21:1  LA: In finem, pro susceptione matutina. Psalmus David.
      EN: For the end, for the morning support. A psalm of David.

21:2  LA: [Deus, Deus meus, respice in me : quare me dereliquisti ? longe a salute mea verba delictorum meorum.
      EN: God, my God, look upon me: why have You abandoned me? Far from my salvation are the words of my offenses.

21:3  LA: Deus meus, clamabo per diem, et non exaudies ; et nocte, et non ad insipientiam mihi.
      EN: My God, I will cry out by day, and You will not hear; and by night, and it will not be folly for me.

21:4  LA: Tu autem in sancto habitas, laus Israël.
      EN: But You dwell in the holy place, the praise of Israel.

21:5  LA: In te speraverunt patres nostri ; speraverunt, et liberasti eos.
      EN: In You our fathers hoped; they hoped, and You delivered them.

21:6  LA: Ad te clamaverunt, et salvi facti sunt ; in te speraverunt, et non sunt confusi.
      EN: To You they cried and were saved; in You they hoped and were not put to shame.

21:7  LA: Ego autem sum vermis, et non homo ; opprobrium hominum, et abjectio plebis.
      EN: But I am a worm and no man, a reproach among men and an outcast of the people.

21:8  LA: Omnes videntes me deriserunt me ; locuti sunt labiis, et moverunt caput.
      EN: All who saw me mocked me; they spoke with their lips and shook their heads.

21:9  LA: Speravit in Domino, eripiat eum : salvum faciat eum, quoniam vult eum.
      EN: “He hoped in the Lord; let Him rescue him. Let Him save him, since He delights in him.”

21:10  LA: Quoniam tu es qui extraxisti me de ventre, spes mea ab uberibus matris meæ.
       EN: For You are the One who drew me from the womb, my hope from my mother's breasts.

21:11  LA: In te projectus sum ex utero ; de ventre matris meæ Deus meus es tu :
       EN: Upon You I was cast from the womb; from my mother's womb You are my God.

21:12  LA: ne discesseris a me, quoniam tribulatio proxima est, quoniam non est qui adjuvet.
       EN: Do not withdraw from me, for distress is near and there is no one to help.

21:13  LA: Circumdederunt me vituli multi ; tauri pingues obsederunt me.
       EN: Many calves surrounded me; fat bulls besieged me.

21:14  LA: Aperuerunt super me os suum, sicut leo rapiens et rugiens.
       EN: They opened their mouths against me like a ravening, roaring lion.

21:15  LA: Sicut aqua effusus sum, et dispersa sunt omnia ossa mea : factum est cor meum tamquam cera liquescens in medio ventris mei.
       EN: I have been poured out like water, and all my bones have been scattered. My heart has become like wax melting within my belly.

21:16  LA: Aruit tamquam testa virtus mea, et lingua mea adhæsit faucibus meis : et in pulverem mortis deduxisti me.
       EN: My strength has dried up like a potsherd, and my tongue has clung to my throat; You have brought me down to the dust of death.

21:17  LA: Quoniam circumdederunt me canes multi ; concilium malignantium obsedit me. Foderunt manus meas et pedes meos ;
       EN: For many dogs surrounded me; an assembly of evildoers besieged me. They pierced my hands and my feet;

21:18  LA: dinumeraverunt omnia ossa mea. Ipsi vero consideraverunt et inspexerunt me.
       EN: they counted all my bones. They looked me over and stared at me.

21:19  LA: Diviserunt sibi vestimenta mea, et super vestem meam miserunt sortem.
       EN: They divided my garments among themselves and cast lots over my clothing.

21:20  LA: Tu autem, Domine, ne elongaveris auxilium tuum a me ; ad defensionem meam conspice.
       EN: But You, Lord, do not withdraw Your help far from me; look to my defense.

21:21  LA: Erue a framea, Deus, animam meam, et de manu canis unicam meam.
       EN: Rescue my soul from the spear, God, and my only life from the hand of the dog.

21:22  LA: Salva me ex ore leonis, et a cornibus unicornium humilitatem meam.
       EN: Save me from the lion's mouth, and my lowliness from the horns of one-horned beasts.

21:23  LA: Narrabo nomen tuum fratribus meis ; in medio ecclesiæ laudabo te.
       EN: I will recount Your name to my brothers; in the midst of the congregation I will praise You.

21:24  LA: Qui timetis Dominum, laudate eum ; universum semen Jacob, glorificate eum.
       EN: You who fear the Lord, praise Him; all you descendants of Jacob, glorify Him.

21:25  LA: Timeat eum omne semen Israël, quoniam non sprevit, neque despexit deprecationem pauperis, nec avertit faciem suam a me : et cum clamarem ad eum, exaudivit me.
       EN: Let all the descendants of Israel fear Him, for He has not scorned or despised the plea of the poor, nor has He turned His face away from me; when I cried to Him, He heard me.

21:26  LA: Apud te laus mea in ecclesia magna ; vota mea reddam in conspectu timentium eum.
       EN: My praise is with You in the great congregation; I will fulfill my vows before those who fear Him.

21:27  LA: Edent pauperes, et saturabuntur, et laudabunt Dominum qui requirunt eum : vivent corda eorum in sæculum sæculi.
       EN: The poor will eat and be satisfied, and those who seek Him will praise the Lord; their hearts will live for ages of ages.

21:28  LA: Reminiscentur et convertentur ad Dominum universi fines terræ ; et adorabunt in conspectu ejus universæ familiæ gentium :
       EN: All the ends of the earth will remember and turn to the Lord, and all the families of the nations will worship in His sight,

21:29  LA: quoniam Domini est regnum, et ipse dominabitur gentium.
       EN: for the kingdom belongs to the Lord, and He will rule the nations.

21:30  LA: Manducaverunt et adoraverunt omnes pingues terræ ; in conspectu ejus cadent omnes qui descendunt in terram.
       EN: All the prosperous of the earth have eaten and worshiped; all who descend into the earth will fall before Him.

21:31  LA: Et anima mea illi vivet ; et semen meum serviet ipsi.
       EN: My soul will live for Him, and my descendants will serve Him. The coming generation will be announced to the Lord, and the heavens will proclaim His righteousness to a people yet to be born, whom the Lord has made.

21:32  LA: Et anima mea illi vivet ; et semen meum serviet ipsi.
       EN: My soul will live for Him, and my descendants will serve Him. The coming generation will be announced to the Lord, and the heavens will proclaim His righteousness to a people yet to be born, whom the Lord has made.

PSALMS 22

22:1  LA: Psalmus David. [Dominus regit me, et nihil mihi deerit :
      EN: A psalm of David. The Lord guides me, and I will lack nothing.

22:2  LA: in loco pascuæ, ibi me collocavit. Super aquam refectionis educavit me ;
      EN: He has settled me in a place of pasture. He has nourished me beside refreshing water;

22:3  LA: animam meam convertit. Deduxit me super semitas justitiæ propter nomen suum.
      EN: He has restored my soul. He has led me along paths of righteousness for His name’s sake.

22:4  LA: Nam etsi ambulavero in medio umbræ mortis, non timebo mala, quoniam tu mecum es. Virga tua, et baculus tuus, ipsa me consolata sunt.
      EN: For even if I walk in the midst of the shadow of death, I will fear no evil, for You are with me. Your rod and Your staff have comforted me.

22:5  LA: Parasti in conspectu meo mensam adversus eos qui tribulant me ; impinguasti in oleo caput meum : et calix meus inebrians, quam præclarus est !
      EN: You have prepared a table before me against those who afflict me; You have enriched my head with oil. And my intoxicating cup—how splendid it is!

22:6  LA: Et misericordia tua subsequetur me omnibus diebus vitæ meæ ; et ut inhabitem in domo Domini in longitudinem dierum.]
      EN: Your mercy will follow me all the days of my life, so that I may dwell in the Lord's house for length of days.

PSALMS 23

23:1  LA: Prima sabbati. Psalmus David. [Domini est terra, et plenitudo ejus ; orbis terrarum, et universi qui habitant in eo.
      EN: For the first day of the week. A psalm of David. The earth belongs to the Lord, and all that fills it; the world and all who dwell in it.

23:2  LA: Quia ipse super maria fundavit eum, et super flumina præparavit eum.
      EN: For He founded it upon the seas and established it upon the rivers.

23:3  LA: Quis ascendet in montem Domini ? aut quis stabit in loco sancto ejus ?
      EN: Who will ascend the Lord's mountain, or who will stand in His holy place?

23:4  LA: Innocens manibus et mundo corde, qui non accepit in vano animam suam, nec juravit in dolo proximo suo :
      EN: One innocent of hands and pure of heart, who has not received his soul in vain or sworn deceitfully to his neighbor:

23:5  LA: hic accipiet benedictionem a Domino, et misericordiam a Deo salutari suo.
      EN: he will receive a blessing from the Lord and mercy from God his savior.

23:6  LA: Hæc est generatio quærentium eum, quærentium faciem Dei Jacob.
      EN: This is the generation of those who seek Him, who seek the face of the God of Jacob.

23:7  LA: Attollite portas, principes, vestras, et elevamini, portæ æternales, et introibit rex gloriæ.
      EN: Lift up your gates, rulers, and be lifted high, everlasting gates, and the King of glory will enter.

23:8  LA: Quis est iste rex gloriæ ? Dominus fortis et potens, Dominus potens in prælio.
      EN: Who is this King of glory? The Lord, strong and mighty, the Lord mighty in battle.

23:9  LA: Attollite portas, principes, vestras, et elevamini, portæ æternales, et introibit rex gloriæ.
      EN: Lift up your gates, rulers, and be lifted high, everlasting gates, and the King of glory will enter.

23:10  LA: Quis est iste rex gloriæ ? Dominus virtutum ipse est rex gloriæ.]
       EN: Who is this King of glory? The Lord of hosts: He is the King of glory.

PSALMS 24

24:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David. [Ad te, Domine, levavi animam meam :
      EN: For the end. A psalm of David. To You, Lord, I have lifted my soul;

24:2  LA: Deus meus, in te confido ; non erubescam.
      EN: my God, in You I trust. Let me not be ashamed,

24:3  LA: Neque irrideant me inimici mei : etenim universi qui sustinent te, non confundentur.
      EN: and let my enemies not mock me, for all who wait for You will not be put to shame. Let all who act unjustly without cause be put to shame. Show me Your ways, Lord, and teach me Your paths.

24:4  LA: Neque irrideant me inimici mei : etenim universi qui sustinent te, non confundentur.
      EN: and let my enemies not mock me, for all who wait for You will not be put to shame. Let all who act unjustly without cause be put to shame. Show me Your ways, Lord, and teach me Your paths.

24:5  LA: Dirige me in veritate tua, et doce me, quia tu es Deus salvator meus, et te sustinui tota die.
      EN: Guide me in Your truth and teach me, for You are God my savior, and I have waited for You all day.

24:6  LA: Reminiscere miserationum tuarum, Domine, et misericordiarum tuarum quæ a sæculo sunt.
      EN: Remember Your compassion, Lord, and Your mercies, which are from of old.

24:7  LA: Delicta juventutis meæ, et ignorantias meas, ne memineris. Secundum misericordiam tuam memento mei tu, propter bonitatem tuam, Domine.
      EN: Do not remember the offenses of my youth or my acts of ignorance. According to Your mercy remember me, for the sake of Your goodness, Lord.

24:8  LA: Dulcis et rectus Dominus ; propter hoc legem dabit delinquentibus in via.
      EN: The Lord is kind and upright; therefore He will give a law to those who go astray upon the way.

24:9  LA: Diriget mansuetos in judicio ; docebit mites vias suas.
      EN: He will guide the gentle in judgment; He will teach the meek His ways.

24:10  LA: Universæ viæ Domini, misericordia et veritas, requirentibus testamentum ejus et testimonia ejus.
       EN: All the Lord's ways are mercy and truth for those who seek His covenant and His testimonies.

24:11  LA: Propter nomen tuum, Domine, propitiaberis peccato meo ; multum est enim.
       EN: For Your name's sake, Lord, You will forgive my sin, for it is great.

24:12  LA: Quis est homo qui timet Dominum ? legem statuit ei in via quam elegit.
       EN: Who is the man who fears the Lord? He has appointed a law for him in the way he has chosen.

24:13  LA: Anima ejus in bonis demorabitur, et semen ejus hæreditabit terram.
       EN: His soul will abide among good things, and his descendants will inherit the land.

24:14  LA: Firmamentum est Dominus timentibus eum ; et testamentum ipsius ut manifestetur illis.
       EN: The Lord is a firm support for those who fear Him, and His covenant is to be made known to them.

24:15  LA: Oculi mei semper ad Dominum, quoniam ipse evellet de laqueo pedes meos.
       EN: My eyes are always toward the Lord, for He will draw my feet from the snare.

24:16  LA: Respice in me, et miserere mei, quia unicus et pauper sum ego.
       EN: Look upon me and have mercy on me, for I am alone and poor.

24:17  LA: Tribulationes cordis mei multiplicatæ sunt : de necessitatibus meis erue me.
       EN: The distresses of my heart have multiplied; rescue me from my hardships.

24:18  LA: Vide humilitatem meam et laborem meum, et dimitte universa delicta mea.
       EN: See my lowliness and my toil, and forgive all my offenses.

24:19  LA: Respice inimicos meos, quoniam multiplicati sunt, et odio iniquo oderunt me.
       EN: Look upon my enemies, for they have multiplied and have hated me with an unjust hatred.

24:20  LA: Custodi animam meam, et erue me : non erubescam, quoniam speravi in te.
       EN: Guard my soul and rescue me; let me not be ashamed, for I have hoped in You.

24:21  LA: Innocentes et recti adhæserunt mihi, quia sustinui te.
       EN: The innocent and upright have clung to me, for I have waited for You.

24:22  LA: Libera, Deus, Israël ex omnibus tribulationibus suis.]
       EN: Deliver Israel, God, from all his distresses.

PSALMS 25

25:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David. [Judica me, Domine, quoniam ego in innocentia mea ingressus sum, et in Domino sperans non infirmabor.
      EN: For the end. A psalm of David. Judge me, Lord, for I have walked in my innocence, and hoping in the Lord, I will not weaken.

25:2  LA: Proba me, Domine, et tenta me ; ure renes meos et cor meum.
      EN: Examine me, Lord, and test me; burn my kidneys and my heart.

25:3  LA: Quoniam misericordia tua ante oculos meos est, et complacui in veritate tua.
      EN: For Your mercy is before my eyes, and I have found delight in Your truth.

25:4  LA: Non sedi cum concilio vanitatis, et cum iniqua gerentibus non introibo.
      EN: I have not sat with an assembly of emptiness, and I will not enter with those who act unjustly.

25:5  LA: Odivi ecclesiam malignantium, et cum impiis non sedebo.
      EN: I have hated the congregation of evildoers, and I will not sit with the ungodly.

25:6  LA: Lavabo inter innocentes manus meas, et circumdabo altare tuum, Domine :
      EN: I will wash my hands among the innocent and go around Your altar, Lord,

25:7  LA: ut audiam vocem laudis, et enarrem universa mirabilia tua.
      EN: to hear the voice of praise and recount all Your wonders.

25:8  LA: Domine, dilexi decorem domus tuæ, et locum habitationis gloriæ tuæ.
      EN: Lord, I have loved the beauty of Your house and the dwelling place of Your glory.

25:9  LA: Ne perdas cum impiis, Deus, animam meam, et cum viris sanguinum vitam meam :
      EN: Do not destroy my soul with the ungodly, God, or my life with bloodthirsty men,

25:10  LA: in quorum manibus iniquitates sunt ; dextera eorum repleta est muneribus.
       EN: in whose hands are iniquities; their right hand is filled with bribes.

25:11  LA: Ego autem in innocentia mea ingressus sum ; redime me, et miserere mei.
       EN: But I have walked in my innocence; redeem me and have mercy on me.

25:12  LA: Pes meus stetit in directo ; in ecclesiis benedicam te, Domine.]
       EN: My foot has stood on level ground; in the congregations I will bless You, Lord.

PSALMS 26

26:1  LA: Psalmus David, priusquam liniretur. [Dominus illuminatio mea et salus mea : quem timebo ? Dominus protector vitæ meæ : a quo trepidabo ?
      EN: A psalm of David, before he was anointed. The Lord is my light and my salvation; whom will I fear? The Lord is the protector of my life; before whom will I tremble?

26:2  LA: Dum appropiant super me nocentes ut edant carnes meas, qui tribulant me inimici mei, ipsi infirmati sunt et ceciderunt.
      EN: When evildoers approach me to eat my flesh, my enemies who afflict me, they themselves have weakened and fallen.

26:3  LA: Si consistant adversum me castra, non timebit cor meum ; si exsurgat adversum me prælium, in hoc ego sperabo.
      EN: If an army camps against me, my heart will not fear; if battle rises against me, in this I will hope.

26:4  LA: Unam petii a Domino, hanc requiram, ut inhabitem in domo Domini omnibus diebus vitæ meæ ; ut videam voluptatem Domini, et visitem templum ejus.
      EN: One thing I have asked of the Lord, and this I will seek: to dwell in the Lord's house all the days of my life, to see the Lord's delightfulness and visit His temple.

26:5  LA: Quoniam abscondit me in tabernaculo suo ; in die malorum protexit me in abscondito tabernaculi sui.
      EN: For He has hidden me in His tent; in the day of evils He protected me in the hidden place of His tent.

26:6  LA: In petra exaltavit me, et nunc exaltavit caput meum super inimicos meos. Circuivi, et immolavi in tabernaculo ejus hostiam vociferationis ; cantabo, et psalmum dicam Domino.
      EN: He has lifted me onto a rock, and now He has raised my head above my enemies. I have gone around and offered a sacrifice of loud acclaim in His tent; I will sing and chant a psalm to the Lord.

26:7  LA: Exaudi, Domine, vocem meam, qua clamavi ad te ; miserere mei, et exaudi me.
      EN: Hear my voice, Lord, with which I have cried to You; have mercy on me and hear me.

26:8  LA: Tibi dixit cor meum : Exquisivit te facies mea ; faciem tuam, Domine, requiram.
      EN: My heart has said to You, “My face has sought You; Your face, Lord, I will seek.”

26:9  LA: Ne avertas faciem tuam a me ; ne declines in ira a servo tuo. Adjutor meus esto ; ne derelinquas me, neque despicias me, Deus salutaris meus.
      EN: Do not turn Your face away from me; do not turn aside in anger from Your servant. Be my helper; do not abandon or despise me, God my savior.

26:10  LA: Quoniam pater meus et mater mea dereliquerunt me ; Dominus autem assumpsit me.
       EN: For my father and my mother have abandoned me, but the Lord has taken me in.

26:11  LA: Legem pone mihi, Domine, in via tua, et dirige me in semitam rectam, propter inimicos meos.
       EN: Set a law for me, Lord, in Your way, and guide me on a straight path because of my enemies.

26:12  LA: Ne tradideris me in animas tribulantium me, quoniam insurrexerunt in me testes iniqui, et mentita est iniquitas sibi.
       EN: Do not hand me over to the desires of those who afflict me, for unjust witnesses have risen against me, and iniquity has lied to itself.

26:13  LA: Credo videre bona Domini in terra viventium.
       EN: I believe that I will see the Lord's good things in the land of the living.

26:14  LA: Expecta Dominum, viriliter age : et confortetur cor tuum, et sustine Dominum.]
       EN: Wait for the Lord; act courageously, and let your heart be strengthened. Wait for the Lord.

PSALMS 27

27:1  LA: Psalmus ipsi David. [Ad te, Domine, clamabo ; Deus meus, ne sileas a me : nequando taceas a me, et assimilabor descendentibus in lacum.
      EN: A psalm for David himself. To You, Lord, I will cry. My God, do not be silent toward me, lest You say nothing to me and I become like those who descend into the pit.

27:2  LA: Exaudi, Domine, vocem deprecationis meæ dum oro ad te ; dum extollo manus meas ad templum sanctum tuum.
      EN: Hear the voice of my plea, Lord, as I pray to You, as I lift my hands toward Your holy temple.

27:3  LA: Ne simul trahas me cum peccatoribus, et cum operantibus iniquitatem ne perdas me ; qui loquuntur pacem cum proximo suo, mala autem in cordibus eorum.
      EN: Do not drag me away with sinners or destroy me with those who work iniquity, who speak peace with their neighbor while evil is in their hearts.

27:4  LA: Da illis secundum opera eorum, et secundum nequitiam adinventionum ipsorum. Secundum opera manuum eorum tribue illis ; redde retributionem eorum ipsis.
      EN: Give them according to their works and according to the wickedness of their devices. Grant them according to the works of their hands; return their recompense to them.

27:5  LA: Quoniam non intellexerunt opera Domini, et in opera manuum ejus destrues illos, et non ædificabis eos.
      EN: For they have not understood the Lord's works and the works of His hands; You will destroy them and not build them up.

27:6  LA: Benedictus Dominus, quoniam exaudivit vocem deprecationis meæ.
      EN: Blessed is the Lord, for He has heard the voice of my plea.

27:7  LA: Dominus adjutor meus et protector meus ; in ipso speravit cor meum, et adjutus sum : et refloruit caro mea, et ex voluntate mea confitebor ei.
      EN: The Lord is my helper and my protector; my heart has hoped in Him, and I have been helped. My flesh has blossomed again, and willingly I will praise Him.

27:8  LA: Dominus fortitudo plebis suæ, et protector salvationum christi sui est.
      EN: The Lord is the strength of His people and the protector of the deliverances of His anointed.

27:9  LA: Salvum fac populum tuum, Domine, et benedic hæreditati tuæ ; et rege eos, et extolle illos usque in æternum.]
      EN: Save Your people, Lord, and bless Your inheritance; govern them and lift them up forever.

PSALMS 28

28:1  LA: Psalmus David, in consummatione tabernaculi. [Afferte Domino, filii Dei, afferte Domino filios arietum.
      EN: A psalm of David, at the completion of the tent. Bring to the Lord, children of God; bring to the Lord the offspring of rams.
