28:2  LA: Afferte Domino gloriam et honorem ; afferte Domino gloriam nomini ejus ; adorate Dominum in atrio sancto ejus.
      EN: Bring to the Lord glory and honor; bring to the Lord the glory due His name. Worship the Lord in His holy court.

28:3  LA: Vox Domini super aquas ; Deus majestatis intonuit : Dominus super aquas multas.
      EN: The Lord's voice is over the waters; the God of majesty has thundered, the Lord over many waters.

28:4  LA: Vox Domini in virtute ; vox Domini in magnificentia.
      EN: The Lord's voice is in strength; the Lord's voice is in magnificence.

28:5  LA: Vox Domini confringentis cedros, et confringet Dominus cedros Libani :
      EN: The Lord's voice breaks cedars; the Lord will break the cedars of Lebanon.

28:6  LA: et comminuet eas, tamquam vitulum Libani, et dilectus quemadmodum filius unicornium.
      EN: He will crush them like a calf of Lebanon, and the beloved one like the offspring of one-horned beasts.

28:7  LA: Vox Domini intercidentis flammam ignis ;
      EN: The Lord's voice divides the flame of fire;

28:8  LA: vox Domini concutientis desertum : et commovebit Dominus desertum Cades.
      EN: the Lord's voice shakes the wilderness, and the Lord will shake the wilderness of Kadesh.

28:9  LA: Vox Domini præparantis cervos : et revelabit condensa, et in templo ejus omnes dicent gloriam.
      EN: The Lord's voice makes the deer ready, and He will uncover the thickets; in His temple all will speak of glory.

28:10  LA: Dominus diluvium inhabitare facit, et sedebit Dominus rex in æternum.
       EN: The Lord makes the flood dwell, and the Lord will sit as King forever.

28:11  LA: Dominus virtutem populo suo dabit ; Dominus benedicet populo suo in pace.]
       EN: The Lord will give strength to His people; the Lord will bless His people with peace.

PSALMS 29

29:1  LA: Psalmus cantici, in dedicatione domus David.
      EN: A psalm of song, at the dedication of David's house.

29:2  LA: [Exaltabo te, Domine, quoniam suscepisti me, nec delectasti inimicos meos super me.
      EN: I will exalt You, Lord, for You have lifted me up and have not let my enemies rejoice over me.

29:3  LA: Domine Deus meus, clamavi ad te, et sanasti me.
      EN: Lord my God, I cried to You, and You healed me.

29:4  LA: Domine, eduxisti ab inferno animam meam ; salvasti me a descendentibus in lacum.
      EN: Lord, You brought my soul up from the underworld; You saved me from those descending into the pit.

29:5  LA: Psallite Domino, sancti ejus ; et confitemini memoriæ sanctitatis ejus.
      EN: Sing to the Lord, His holy ones, and praise the remembrance of His holiness.

29:6  LA: Quoniam ira in indignatione ejus, et vita in voluntate ejus : ad vesperum demorabitur fletus, et ad matutinum lætitia.
      EN: For anger is in His indignation, and life in His favor; weeping will stay for the evening, but gladness comes in the morning.

29:7  LA: Ego autem dixi in abundantia mea : Non movebor in æternum.
      EN: But I said in my abundance, “I will never be shaken.”

29:8  LA: Domine, in voluntate tua præstitisti decori meo virtutem ; avertisti faciem tuam a me, et factus sum conturbatus.
      EN: Lord, in Your favor You gave strength to my splendor; You turned Your face away from me, and I became troubled.

29:9  LA: Ad te, Domine, clamabo, et ad Deum meum deprecabor.
      EN: To You, Lord, I will cry, and to my God I will plead.

29:10  LA: Quæ utilitas in sanguine meo, dum descendo in corruptionem ? numquid confitebitur tibi pulvis, aut annuntiabit veritatem tuam ?
       EN: What profit is there in my blood as I descend into corruption? Will dust praise You or proclaim Your truth?

29:11  LA: Audivit Dominus, et misertus est mei ; Dominus factus est adjutor meus.
       EN: The Lord heard and had mercy on me; the Lord became my helper.

29:12  LA: Convertisti planctum meum in gaudium mihi ; conscidisti saccum meum, et circumdedisti me lætitia :
       EN: You turned my mourning into joy for me; You tore away my sackcloth and surrounded me with gladness,

29:13  LA: ut cantet tibi gloria mea, et non compungar. Domine Deus meus, in æternum confitebor tibi.]
       EN: so that my glory may sing to You and I may not be pierced with sorrow. Lord my God, I will praise You forever.

PSALMS 30

30:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David, pro extasi.
      EN: For the end. A psalm of David, for ecstasy.

30:2  LA: [In te, Domine, speravi ; non confundar in æternum : in justitia tua libera me.
      EN: In You, Lord, I have hoped; let me never be put to shame. In Your righteousness deliver me.

30:3  LA: Inclina ad me aurem tuam ; accelera ut eruas me. Esto mihi in Deum protectorem, et in domum refugii, ut salvum me facias :
      EN: Incline Your ear to me; hurry to rescue me. Be to me a protecting God and a house of refuge, to save me,

30:4  LA: quoniam fortitudo mea et refugium meum es tu ; et propter nomen tuum deduces me et enutries me.
      EN: for You are my strength and my refuge. For Your name's sake You will guide and nourish me.

30:5  LA: Educes me de laqueo hoc quem absconderunt mihi, quoniam tu es protector meus.
      EN: You will lead me out of the snare they have hidden for me, for You are my protector.

30:6  LA: In manus tuas commendo spiritum meum ; redemisti me, Domine Deus veritatis.
      EN: Into Your hands I entrust my spirit; You have redeemed me, Lord, God of truth.

30:7  LA: Odisti observantes vanitates supervacue ; ego autem in Domino speravi.
      EN: You have hated those who heed vain things to no purpose, but I have hoped in the Lord.

30:8  LA: Exsultabo, et lætabor in misericordia tua, quoniam respexisti humilitatem meam ; salvasti de necessitatibus animam meam.
      EN: I will rejoice and be glad in Your mercy, for You have looked upon my lowliness; You have saved my soul from hardships.

30:9  LA: Nec conclusisti me in manibus inimici : statuisti in loco spatioso pedes meos.
      EN: You have not enclosed me in the enemy's hands; You have set my feet in a spacious place.

30:10  LA: Miserere mei, Domine, quoniam tribulor ; conturbatus est in ira oculus meus, anima mea, et venter meus.
       EN: Have mercy on me, Lord, for I am in distress. My eye is troubled with anger, along with my soul and my belly.

30:11  LA: Quoniam defecit in dolore vita mea, et anni mei in gemitibus. Infirmata est in paupertate virtus mea, et ossa mea conturbata sunt.
       EN: For my life has failed in sorrow and my years in groaning. My strength has weakened in poverty, and my bones are troubled.

30:12  LA: Super omnes inimicos meos factus sum opprobrium, et vicinis meis valde, et timor notis meis ; qui videbant me foras fugerunt a me.
       EN: More than all my enemies I have become a reproach, especially to my neighbors, and a terror to those who know me; those who saw me outside fled from me.

30:13  LA: Oblivioni datus sum, tamquam mortuus a corde ; factus sum tamquam vas perditum :
       EN: I have been given over to oblivion, like a dead man forgotten by the heart; I have become like a broken vessel,

30:14  LA: quoniam audivi vituperationem multorum commorantium in circuitu. In eo dum convenirent simul adversum me, accipere animam meam consiliati sunt.
       EN: for I have heard the reproach of many who dwell around me. When they gathered together against me, they planned to take my soul.

30:15  LA: Ego autem in te speravi, Domine ; dixi : Deus meus es tu ;
       EN: But I have hoped in You, Lord; I said, “You are my God;

30:16  LA: in manibus tuis sortes meæ : eripe me de manu inimicorum meorum, et a persequentibus me.
       EN: my fortunes are in Your hands. Rescue me from the hand of my enemies and from those who pursue me.”

30:17  LA: Illustra faciem tuam super servum tuum ; salvum me fac in misericordia tua.
       EN: Make Your face shine upon Your servant; save me in Your mercy.

30:18  LA: Domine, non confundar, quoniam invocavi te. Erubescant impii, et deducantur in infernum ;
       EN: Lord, let me not be put to shame, for I have called upon You. Let the ungodly be ashamed and be led down to the underworld;

30:19  LA: muta fiant labia dolosa, quæ loquuntur adversus justum iniquitatem, in superbia, et in abusione.
       EN: let deceitful lips become mute, those that speak iniquity against the just with pride and contempt.

30:20  LA: Quam magna multitudo dulcedinis tuæ, Domine, quam abscondisti timentibus te ; perfecisti eis qui sperant in te in conspectu filiorum hominum !
       EN: How great is the abundance of Your sweetness, Lord, which You have hidden for those who fear You, which You have accomplished for those who hope in You before the children of men!

30:21  LA: Abscondes eos in abscondito faciei tuæ a conturbatione hominum ; proteges eos in tabernaculo tuo, a contradictione linguarum.
       EN: You will hide them in the hidden place of Your presence from human turmoil; You will protect them in Your tent from the strife of tongues.

30:22  LA: Benedictus Dominus, quoniam mirificavit misericordiam suam mihi in civitate munita.
       EN: Blessed is the Lord, for He has made His mercy wonderful to me in a fortified city.

30:23  LA: Ego autem dixi in excessu mentis meæ : Projectus sum a facie oculorum tuorum : ideo exaudisti vocem orationis meæ, dum clamarem ad te.
       EN: But I said in my distraction, “I have been cast away from Your sight.” Therefore You heard the voice of my prayer when I cried to You.

30:24  LA: Diligite Dominum, omnes sancti ejus, quoniam veritatem requiret Dominus, et retribuet abundanter facientibus superbiam.
       EN: Love the Lord, all His holy ones, for the Lord will require truth and repay abundantly those who act proudly.

30:25  LA: Viriliter agite, et confortetur cor vestrum, omnes qui speratis in Domino.]
       EN: Act courageously, and let your hearts be strengthened, all who hope in the Lord.

PSALMS 31

31:1  LA: Ipsi David intellectus. [Beati quorum remissæ sunt iniquitates, et quorum tecta sunt peccata.
      EN: For David himself, for understanding. Blessed are those whose iniquities have been forgiven and whose sins have been covered.

31:2  LA: Beatus vir cui non imputavit Dominus peccatum, nec est in spiritu ejus dolus.
      EN: Blessed is the man to whom the Lord has not charged sin, and in whose spirit there is no deceit.

31:3  LA: Quoniam tacui, inveteraverunt ossa mea, dum clamarem tota die.
      EN: Because I kept silent, my bones grew old while I cried out all day.

31:4  LA: Quoniam die ac nocte gravata est super me manus tua, conversus sum in ærumna mea, dum configitur spina.
      EN: For day and night Your hand weighed heavily upon me; I turned in my misery while the thorn was driven in.

31:5  LA: Delictum meum cognitum tibi feci, et injustitiam meam non abscondi. Dixi : Confitebor adversum me injustitiam meam Domino ; et tu remisisti impietatem peccati mei.
      EN: I made my offense known to You and did not hide my injustice. I said, “I will confess my injustice against myself to the Lord,” and You forgave the ungodliness of my sin.

31:6  LA: Pro hac orabit ad te omnis sanctus in tempore opportuno. Verumtamen in diluvio aquarum multarum, ad eum non approximabunt.
      EN: For this every holy one will pray to You at the proper time. Yet in a flood of many waters, they will not approach him.

31:7  LA: Tu es refugium meum a tribulatione quæ circumdedit me ; exsultatio mea, erue me a circumdantibus me.
      EN: You are my refuge from the distress that has surrounded me; my joy, rescue me from those who surround me.

31:8  LA: Intellectum tibi dabo, et instruam te in via hac qua gradieris ; firmabo super te oculos meos.
      EN: I will give you understanding and instruct you in this way in which you will walk; I will fix my eyes upon you.

31:9  LA: Nolite fieri sicut equus et mulus, quibus non est intellectus. In camo et freno maxillas eorum constringe, qui non approximant ad te.
      EN: Do not become like a horse or mule, which have no understanding. With bit and bridle constrain the jaws of those who do not approach you.

31:10  LA: Multa flagella peccatoris ; sperantem autem in Domino misericordia circumdabit.
       EN: Many are the scourges of the sinner, but mercy will surround the one who hopes in the Lord.

31:11  LA: Lætamini in Domino, et exsultate, justi ; et gloriamini, omnes recti corde.]
       EN: Be glad in the Lord and rejoice, you just; glory, all you upright of heart.

PSALMS 32

32:1  LA: Psalmus David. [Exsultate, justi, in Domino ; rectos decet collaudatio.
      EN: A psalm of David. Rejoice in the Lord, you just; praise befits the upright.

32:2  LA: Confitemini Domino in cithara ; in psalterio decem chordarum psallite illi.
      EN: Praise the Lord with the lyre; sing to Him with a ten-stringed harp.

32:3  LA: Cantate ei canticum novum ; bene psallite ei in vociferatione.
      EN: Sing Him a new song; play well for Him with loud acclaim.

32:4  LA: Quia rectum est verbum Domini, et omnia opera ejus in fide.
      EN: For the Lord's word is upright, and all His works are faithful.

32:5  LA: Diligit misericordiam et judicium ; misericordia Domini plena est terra.
      EN: He loves mercy and judgment; the earth is full of the Lord's mercy.

32:6  LA: Verbo Domini cæli firmati sunt, et spiritu oris ejus omnis virtus eorum.
      EN: By the Lord's word the heavens were made firm, and by the breath of His mouth all their host.

32:7  LA: Congregans sicut in utre aquas maris ; ponens in thesauris abyssos.
      EN: He gathers the waters of the sea as in a skin, placing the depths in storehouses.

32:8  LA: Timeat Dominum omnis terra ; ab eo autem commoveantur omnes inhabitantes orbem.
      EN: Let all the earth fear the Lord; let all who inhabit the world tremble before Him.

32:9  LA: Quoniam ipse dixit, et facta sunt ; ipse mandavit et creata sunt.
      EN: For He spoke, and they came to be; He commanded, and they were created.

32:10  LA: Dominus dissipat consilia gentium ; reprobat autem cogitationes populorum, et reprobat consilia principum.
       EN: The Lord scatters the counsels of nations; He rejects the thoughts of peoples and rejects the counsels of rulers.

32:11  LA: Consilium autem Domini in æternum manet ; cogitationes cordis ejus in generatione et generationem.
       EN: But the Lord's counsel remains forever, the thoughts of His heart from generation to generation.

32:12  LA: Beata gens cujus est Dominus Deus ejus ; populus quem elegit in hæreditatem sibi.
       EN: Blessed is the nation whose God is the Lord, the people He has chosen as His inheritance.

32:13  LA: De cælo respexit Dominus ; vidit omnes filios hominum.
       EN: The Lord looked down from heaven; He saw all the children of men.

32:14  LA: De præparato habitaculo suo respexit super omnes qui habitant terram :
       EN: From His prepared dwelling He looked upon all who inhabit the earth,

32:15  LA: qui finxit sigillatim corda eorum ; qui intelligit omnia opera eorum.
       EN: He who formed their hearts one by one, who understands all their works.

32:16  LA: Non salvatur rex per multam virtutem, et gigas non salvabitur in multitudine virtutis suæ.
       EN: A king is not saved by great strength, nor will a giant be saved by his abundant might.

32:17  LA: Fallax equus ad salutem ; in abundantia autem virtutis suæ non salvabitur.
       EN: A horse is deceptive as a means of safety; even with its abundant strength it will not be saved.

32:18  LA: Ecce oculi Domini super metuentes eum, et in eis qui sperant super misericordia ejus :
       EN: See, the Lord's eyes are upon those who fear Him and upon those who hope in His mercy,

32:19  LA: ut eruat a morte animas eorum, et alat eos in fame.
       EN: to rescue their souls from death and nourish them in famine.

32:20  LA: Anima nostra sustinet Dominum, quoniam adjutor et protector noster est.
       EN: Our soul waits for the Lord, for He is our helper and protector.

32:21  LA: Quia in eo lætabitur cor nostrum, et in nomine sancto ejus speravimus.
       EN: For our heart will be glad in Him, and we have hoped in His holy name.

32:22  LA: Fiat misericordia tua, Domine, super nos, quemadmodum speravimus in te.]
       EN: May Your mercy be upon us, Lord, just as we have hoped in You.

PSALMS 33

33:1  LA: Davidi , cum immutavit vultum suum coram Achimelech, et dimisit eum, et abiit.
      EN: For David, when he changed his appearance before Ahimelech, who dismissed him, and he went away.

33:2  LA: [Benedicam Dominum in omni tempore ; semper laus ejus in ore meo.
      EN: I will bless the Lord at all times; His praise will always be in my mouth.

33:3  LA: In Domino laudabitur anima mea : audiant mansueti, et lætentur.
      EN: My soul will be praised in the Lord; let the gentle hear and be glad.

33:4  LA: Magnificate Dominum mecum, et exaltemus nomen ejus in idipsum.
      EN: Magnify the Lord with me, and let us exalt His name together.

33:5  LA: Exquisivi Dominum, et exaudivit me ; et ex omnibus tribulationibus meis eripuit me.
      EN: I sought the Lord, and He heard me; He rescued me from all my distresses.

33:6  LA: Accedite ad eum, et illuminamini ; et facies vestræ non confundentur.
      EN: Approach Him and be enlightened, and your faces will not be put to shame.

33:7  LA: Iste pauper clamavit, et Dominus exaudivit eum, et de omnibus tribulationibus ejus salvavit eum.
      EN: This poor man cried out, and the Lord heard him and saved him from all his distresses.

33:8  LA: Immittet angelus Domini in circuitu timentium eum, et eripiet eos.
      EN: The Lord's angel will press in around those who fear Him and rescue them.

33:9  LA: Gustate et videte quoniam suavis est Dominus ; beatus vir qui sperat in eo.
      EN: Taste and see that the Lord is sweet; blessed is the man who hopes in Him.

33:10  LA: Timete Dominum, omnes sancti ejus, quoniam non est inopia timentibus eum.
       EN: Fear the Lord, all His holy ones, for those who fear Him lack nothing.

33:11  LA: Divites eguerunt, et esurierunt ; inquirentes autem Dominum non minuentur omni bono.
       EN: The rich have been needy and hungry, but those who seek the Lord will lack no good thing.

33:12  LA: Venite, filii ; audite me : timorem Domini docebo vos.
       EN: Come, children, listen to me; I will teach you the fear of the Lord.

33:13  LA: Quis est homo qui vult vitam ; diligit dies videre bonos ?
       EN: Who is the man who desires life, who loves to see good days?

33:14  LA: Prohibe linguam tuam a malo, et labia tua ne loquantur dolum.
       EN: Keep your tongue from evil and your lips from speaking deceit.

33:15  LA: Diverte a malo, et fac bonum ; inquire pacem, et persequere eam.
       EN: Turn aside from evil and do good; seek peace and pursue it.

33:16  LA: Oculi Domini super justos, et aures ejus in preces eorum.
       EN: The Lord's eyes are upon the just, and His ears attend to their prayers.

33:17  LA: Vultus autem Domini super facientes mala, ut perdat de terra memoriam eorum.
       EN: But the Lord's face is against those who do evil, to destroy their memory from the earth.

33:18  LA: Clamaverunt justi, et Dominus exaudivit eos ; et ex omnibus tribulationibus eorum liberavit eos.
       EN: The just cried out, and the Lord heard them; He delivered them from all their distresses.

33:19  LA: Juxta est Dominus iis qui tribulato sunt corde, et humiles spiritu salvabit.
       EN: The Lord is near those whose hearts are troubled, and He will save the lowly in spirit.

33:20  LA: Multæ tribulationes justorum ; et de omnibus his liberabit eos Dominus.
       EN: Many are the distresses of the just, but the Lord will deliver them from all of them.

33:21  LA: Custodit Dominus omnia ossa eorum : unum ex his non conteretur.
       EN: The Lord guards all their bones; not one of them will be broken.

33:22  LA: Mors peccatorum pessima ; et qui oderunt justum delinquent.
       EN: The death of sinners is most evil, and those who hate the just will incur guilt.

33:23  LA: Redimet Dominus animas servorum suorum, et non delinquent omnes qui sperant in eo.]
       EN: The Lord will redeem the souls of His servants, and all who hope in Him will not incur guilt.

PSALMS 34

34:1  LA: Ipsi David. [Judica, Domine, nocentes me ; expugna impugnantes me.
      EN: For David himself. Judge those who harm me, Lord; fight those who fight me.

34:2  LA: Apprehende arma et scutum, et exsurge in adjutorium mihi.
      EN: Take hold of weapons and shield, and arise to help me.

34:3  LA: Effunde frameam, et conclude adversus eos qui persequuntur me ; dic animæ meæ : Salus tua ego sum.
      EN: Draw out the spear and block the way against those who pursue me. Say to my soul, “I am your salvation.”

34:4  LA: Confundantur et revereantur quærentes animam meam ; avertantur retrorsum et confundantur cogitantes mihi mala.
      EN: Let those who seek my soul be confounded and ashamed; let those who devise evil for me turn back and be put to shame.

34:5  LA: Fiant tamquam pulvis ante faciem venti, et angelus Domini coarctans eos.
      EN: Let them become like dust before the wind, with the Lord's angel pressing them hard.

34:6  LA: Fiat via illorum tenebræ et lubricum, et angelus Domini persequens eos.
      EN: Let their way be darkness and slippery ground, with the Lord's angel pursuing them.

34:7  LA: Quoniam gratis absconderunt mihi interitum laquei sui ; supervacue exprobraverunt animam meam.
      EN: For without cause they hid the ruin of their snare for me; to no purpose they reproached my soul.

34:8  LA: Veniat illi laqueus quem ignorat, et captio quam abscondit apprehendat eum, et in laqueum cadat in ipsum.
      EN: Let a snare he does not know come upon him; let the trap he hid seize him, and let him fall into that very snare.

34:9  LA: Anima autem mea exsultabit in Domino, et delectabitur super salutari suo.
      EN: But my soul will rejoice in the Lord and delight in His salvation.

34:10  LA: Omnia ossa mea dicent : Domine, quis similis tibi ? eripiens inopem de manu fortiorum ejus ; egenum et pauperem a diripientibus eum.
       EN: All my bones will say, “Lord, who is like You, rescuing the needy from the hand of those stronger than he, the destitute and poor from those who plunder him?”

34:11  LA: Surgentes testes iniqui, quæ ignorabam interrogabant me.
       EN: Unjust witnesses rose up; they questioned me about things I did not know.

34:12  LA: Retribuebant mihi mala pro bonis, sterilitatem animæ meæ.
       EN: They repaid me evil for good, barrenness for my soul.

34:13  LA: Ego autem, cum mihi molesti essent, induebar cilicio ; humiliabam in jejunio animam meam, et oratio mea in sinu meo convertetur.
       EN: But when they troubled me, I clothed myself in sackcloth; I humbled my soul with fasting, and my prayer will return into my bosom.

34:14  LA: Quasi proximum et quasi fratrem nostrum sic complacebam ; quasi lugens et contristatus sic humiliabar.
       EN: As for a neighbor and as for our brother, so I sought to please; as one mourning and sorrowful, so I humbled myself.

34:15  LA: Et adversum me lætati sunt, et convenerunt ; congregata sunt super me flagella, et ignoravi.
       EN: They rejoiced against me and gathered together; scourges were gathered against me, and I did not know.

34:16  LA: Dissipati sunt, nec compuncti ; tentaverunt me, subsannaverunt me subsannatione ; frenduerunt super me dentibus suis.
       EN: They were scattered but not remorseful; they tested me and mocked me with mockery; they gnashed their teeth against me.

34:17  LA: Domine, quando respicies ? Restitue animam meam a malignitate eorum ; a leonibus unicam meam.
       EN: Lord, when will You look? Restore my soul from their malice, my only life from the lions.

34:18  LA: Confitebor tibi in ecclesia magna ; in populo gravi laudabo te.
       EN: I will praise You in the great congregation; among a weighty people I will praise You.

34:19  LA: Non supergaudeant mihi qui adversantur mihi inique, qui oderunt me gratis, et annuunt oculis.
       EN: Let those who oppose me unjustly not rejoice over me, those who hate me without cause and wink with their eyes.

34:20  LA: Quoniam mihi quidem pacifice loquebantur ; et in iracundia terræ loquentes, dolos cogitabant.
       EN: For they spoke peaceably to me, but speaking in the earth's anger, they devised deceit.

34:21  LA: Et dilataverunt super me os suum ; dixerunt : Euge, euge ! viderunt oculi nostri.
       EN: They opened their mouths wide against me; they said, “Aha, aha! Our eyes have seen it.”

34:22  LA: Vidisti, Domine : ne sileas ; Domine, ne discedas a me.
       EN: You have seen, Lord; do not be silent. Lord, do not withdraw from me.

34:23  LA: Exsurge et intende judicio meo, Deus meus ; et Dominus meus, in causam meam.
       EN: Arise and attend to my judgment, my God, and to my cause, my Lord.

34:24  LA: Judica me secundum justitiam tuam, Domine Deus meus, et non supergaudeant mihi.
       EN: Judge me according to Your righteousness, Lord my God, and let them not rejoice over me.

34:25  LA: Non dicant in cordibus suis : Euge, euge, animæ nostræ ; nec dicant : Devoravimus eum.
       EN: Let them not say in their hearts, “Aha, aha, for our soul!” Nor let them say, “We have devoured him.”

34:26  LA: Erubescant et revereantur simul qui gratulantur malis meis ; induantur confusione et reverentia qui magna loquuntur super me.
       EN: Let those who rejoice at my misfortunes be confounded and ashamed together; let those who boast against me be clothed with confusion and shame.

34:27  LA: Exsultent et lætentur qui volunt justitiam meam ; et dicant semper : Magnificetur Dominus, qui volunt pacem servi ejus.
       EN: Let those who desire my righteousness rejoice and be glad, and let those who desire His servant's peace always say, “May the Lord be magnified.”

34:28  LA: Et lingua mea meditabitur justitiam tuam ; tota die laudem tuam.]
       EN: My tongue will meditate on Your righteousness, on Your praise all day.

PSALMS 35

35:1  LA: In finem. Servo Domini ipsi David.
      EN: For the end. For the Lord's servant David himself.

35:2  LA: [Dixit injustus ut delinquat in semetipso : non est timor Dei ante oculos ejus.
      EN: The unjust man has spoken within himself to commit sin; there is no fear of God before his eyes.

35:3  LA: Quoniam dolose egit in conspectu ejus, ut inveniatur iniquitas ejus ad odium.
      EN: For he has acted deceitfully in His sight, so that his iniquity may be found hateful.

35:4  LA: Verba oris ejus iniquitas, et dolus ; noluit intelligere ut bene ageret.
      EN: The words of his mouth are iniquity and deceit; he has refused to understand so as to act well.

35:5  LA: Iniquitatem meditatus est in cubili suo ; astitit omni viæ non bonæ : malitiam autem non odivit.
      EN: He has devised iniquity upon his bed; he has stood upon every way that is not good, and he has not hated malice.

35:6  LA: Domine, in cælo misericordia tua, et veritas tua usque ad nubes.
      EN: Lord, Your mercy is in heaven, and Your truth reaches to the clouds.

35:7  LA: Justitia tua sicut montes Dei ; judicia tua abyssus multa. Homines et jumenta salvabis, Domine,
      EN: Your righteousness is like the mountains of God; Your judgments are a vast deep. You will save humans and beasts, Lord,

35:8  LA: quemadmodum multiplicasti misericordiam tuam, Deus. Filii autem hominum in tegmine alarum tuarum sperabunt.
      EN: just as You have multiplied Your mercy, God. The children of men will hope beneath the covering of Your wings.

35:9  LA: Inebriabuntur ab ubertate domus tuæ, et torrente voluptatis tuæ potabis eos :
      EN: They will be intoxicated with the richness of Your house, and You will give them drink from the torrent of Your delight,

35:10  LA: quoniam apud te est fons vitæ, et in lumine tuo videbimus lumen.
       EN: for with You is the fountain of life, and in Your light we will see light.

35:11  LA: Prætende misericordiam tuam scientibus te, et justitiam tuam his qui recto sunt corde.
       EN: Extend Your mercy to those who know You and Your righteousness to the upright of heart.

35:12  LA: Non veniat mihi pes superbiæ, et manus peccatoris non moveat me.
       EN: Let the foot of pride not come upon me, and let the sinner's hand not move me.

35:13  LA: Ibi ceciderunt qui operantur iniquitatem ; expulsi sunt, nec potuerunt stare.]
       EN: There those who work iniquity have fallen; they have been driven out and could not stand.

PSALMS 36

36:1  LA: Psalmus ipsi David. [Noli æmulari in malignantibus, neque zelaveris facientes iniquitatem :
      EN: A psalm for David himself. Do not be jealous of evildoers or envy those who practice iniquity,

36:2  LA: quoniam tamquam fœnum velociter arescent, et quemadmodum olera herbarum cito decident.
      EN: for they will quickly wither like grass and soon fall like green plants.

36:3  LA: Spera in Domino, et fac bonitatem ; et inhabita terram, et pasceris in divitiis ejus.
      EN: Hope in the Lord and do good; inhabit the land, and you will be fed with its riches.

36:4  LA: Delectare in Domino, et dabit tibi petitiones cordis tui.
      EN: Delight in the Lord, and He will grant the requests of your heart.

36:5  LA: Revela Domino viam tuam, et spera in eo, et ipse faciet.
      EN: Reveal your way to the Lord and hope in Him, and He will act.

36:6  LA: Et educet quasi lumen justitiam tuam, et judicium tuum tamquam meridiem.
      EN: He will bring out your righteousness like light and your judgment like midday.

36:7  LA: Subditus esto Domino, et ora eum. Noli æmulari in eo qui prosperatur in via sua ; in homine faciente injustitias.
      EN: Be subject to the Lord and pray to Him. Do not be jealous of one who prospers in his way, of a man who practices injustice.

36:8  LA: Desine ab ira, et derelinque furorem ; noli æmulari ut maligneris.
      EN: Cease from anger and abandon fury; do not become jealous so as to act wickedly.

36:9  LA: Quoniam qui malignantur exterminabuntur ; sustinentes autem Dominum, ipsi hæreditabunt terram.
      EN: For evildoers will be uprooted, but those who wait for the Lord will inherit the land.

36:10  LA: Et adhuc pusillum, et non erit peccator ; et quæres locum ejus, et non invenies.
       EN: A little while longer, and the sinner will be no more; you will seek his place and not find it.

36:11  LA: Mansueti autem hæreditabunt terram, et delectabuntur in multitudine pacis.
       EN: But the gentle will inherit the land and delight in abundant peace.

36:12  LA: Observabit peccator justum, et stridebit super eum dentibus suis.
       EN: The sinner will watch the just man and gnash his teeth against him.

36:13  LA: Dominus autem irridebit eum, quoniam prospicit quod veniet dies ejus.
       EN: But the Lord will laugh at him, for He foresees that his day will come.

36:14  LA: Gladium evaginaverunt peccatores ; intenderunt arcum suum : ut dejiciant pauperem et inopem, ut trucident rectos corde.
       EN: Sinners have drawn their sword and bent their bow to bring down the poor and needy, to slaughter the upright of heart.

36:15  LA: Gladius eorum intret in corda ipsorum, et arcus eorum confringatur.
       EN: Let their sword enter their own hearts, and let their bow be broken.

36:16  LA: Melius est modicum justo, super divitias peccatorum multas :
       EN: A little for the just man is better than the great riches of sinners,

36:17  LA: quoniam brachia peccatorum conterentur : confirmat autem justos Dominus.
       EN: for the arms of sinners will be crushed, but the Lord strengthens the just.

36:18  LA: Novit Dominus dies immaculatorum, et hæreditas eorum in æternum erit.
       EN: The Lord knows the days of the unstained, and their inheritance will last forever.

36:19  LA: Non confundentur in tempore malo, et in diebus famis saturabuntur :
       EN: They will not be put to shame in an evil time, and in days of famine they will be satisfied,

36:20  LA: quia peccatores peribunt. Inimici vero Domini mox ut honorificati fuerint et exaltati, deficientes quemadmodum fumus deficient.
       EN: for sinners will perish. The Lord's enemies, as soon as they have been honored and exalted, will vanish, fading like smoke.

36:21  LA: Mutuabitur peccator, et non solvet ; justus autem miseretur et tribuet :
       EN: The sinner will borrow and not repay, but the just man shows mercy and gives,

36:22  LA: quia benedicentes ei hæreditabunt terram ; maledicentes autem ei disperibunt.
       EN: for those who bless him will inherit the land, but those who curse him will perish completely.

36:23  LA: Apud Dominum gressus hominis dirigentur, et viam ejus volet.
       EN: By the Lord a man's steps will be guided, and He will delight in his way.

36:24  LA: Cum ceciderit, non collidetur, quia Dominus supponit manum suam.
       EN: When he falls, he will not be dashed to pieces, for the Lord places His hand beneath him.

36:25  LA: Junior fui, etenim senui ; et non vidi justum derelictum, nec semen ejus quærens panem.
       EN: I was young and indeed have grown old, but I have not seen the just man abandoned or his descendants seeking bread.

36:26  LA: Tota die miseretur et commodat ; et semen illius in benedictione erit.
       EN: All day he shows mercy and lends, and his descendants will be blessed.

36:27  LA: Declina a malo, et fac bonum, et inhabita in sæculum sæculi :
       EN: Turn aside from evil and do good, and dwell for ages of ages,

36:28  LA: quia Dominus amat judicium, et non derelinquet sanctos suos : in æternum conservabuntur. Injusti punientur, et semen impiorum peribit.
       EN: for the Lord loves judgment and will not abandon His holy ones; they will be preserved forever. The unjust will be punished, and the descendants of the ungodly will perish.

36:29  LA: Justi autem hæreditabunt terram, et inhabitabunt in sæculum sæculi super eam.
       EN: But the just will inherit the land and dwell upon it for ages of ages.

36:30  LA: Os justi meditabitur sapientiam, et lingua ejus loquetur judicium.
       EN: The mouth of the just will meditate on wisdom, and his tongue will speak judgment.

36:31  LA: Lex Dei ejus in corde ipsius, et non supplantabuntur gressus ejus.
       EN: The law of his God is in his heart, and his steps will not be tripped up.

36:32  LA: Considerat peccator justum, et quærit mortificare eum.
       EN: The sinner watches the just man and seeks to put him to death.

36:33  LA: Dominus autem non derelinquet eum in manibus ejus, nec damnabit eum cum judicabitur illi.
       EN: But the Lord will not abandon him in his hands or condemn him when he is judged.

36:34  LA: Exspecta Dominum, et custodi viam ejus, et exaltabit te ut hæreditate capias terram : cum perierint peccatores, videbis.
       EN: Wait for the Lord and keep His way, and He will exalt you to inherit the land. When sinners perish, you will see.

36:35  LA: Vidi impium superexaltatum, et elevatum sicut cedros Libani :
       EN: I saw the ungodly man greatly exalted and raised high like the cedars of Lebanon.

36:36  LA: et transivi, et ecce non erat ; et quæsivi eum, et non est inventus locus ejus.
       EN: I passed by, and see, he was no more; I sought him, and his place was not found.

36:37  LA: Custodi innocentiam, et vide æquitatem, quoniam sunt reliquiæ homini pacifico.
       EN: Guard innocence and look upon equity, for a remnant remains for the peaceful man.

36:38  LA: Injusti autem disperibunt simul ; reliquiæ impiorum interibunt.
       EN: But the unjust will perish together; the remnant of the ungodly will be destroyed.

36:39  LA: Salus autem justorum a Domino ; et protector eorum in tempore tribulationis.
       EN: The salvation of the just comes from the Lord, and He is their protector in a time of distress.

36:40  LA: Et adjuvabit eos Dominus, et liberabit eos ; et eruet eos a peccatoribus, et salvabit eos, quia speraverunt in eo.]
       EN: The Lord will help and deliver them; He will rescue them from sinners and save them, because they have hoped in Him.

PSALMS 37

37:1  LA: Psalmus David, in rememorationem de sabbato.
      EN: A psalm of David, for remembrance concerning the Sabbath.

37:2  LA: [Domine, ne in furore tuo arguas me, neque in ira tua corripias me :
      EN: Lord, do not rebuke me in Your fury or chastise me in Your anger,

37:3  LA: quoniam sagittæ tuæ infixæ sunt mihi, et confirmasti super me manum tuam.
      EN: for Your arrows have been driven into me, and You have pressed Your hand upon me.

37:4  LA: Non est sanitas in carne mea, a facie iræ tuæ ; non est pax ossibus meis, a facie peccatorum meorum :
      EN: There is no health in my flesh because of Your anger; there is no peace in my bones because of my sins,

37:5  LA: quoniam iniquitates meæ supergressæ sunt caput meum, et sicut onus grave gravatæ sunt super me.
      EN: for my iniquities have risen over my head, and like a heavy load they weigh upon me.

37:6  LA: Putruerunt et corruptæ sunt cicatrices meæ, a facie insipientiæ meæ.
      EN: My wounds have festered and become corrupt because of my folly.

37:7  LA: Miser factus sum et curvatus sum usque in finem ; tota die contristatus ingrediebar.
      EN: I have become miserable and bent down completely; all day I walked in sorrow.

37:8  LA: Quoniam lumbi mei impleti sunt illusionibus, et non est sanitas in carne mea.
      EN: For my loins are filled with delusions, and there is no health in my flesh.

37:9  LA: Afflictus sum, et humiliatus sum nimis ; rugiebam a gemitu cordis mei.
      EN: I have been afflicted and greatly humbled; I roared from the groaning of my heart.

37:10  LA: Domine, ante te omne desiderium meum, et gemitus meus a te non est absconditus.
       EN: Lord, all my desire is before You, and my groaning is not hidden from You.

37:11  LA: Cor meum conturbatum est ; dereliquit me virtus mea, et lumen oculorum meorum, et ipsum non est mecum.
       EN: My heart is troubled; my strength has abandoned me, and the light of my eyes itself is no longer with me.

37:12  LA: Amici mei et proximi mei adversum me appropinquaverunt, et steterunt ; et qui juxta me erant, de longe steterunt : et vim faciebant qui quærebant animam meam.
       EN: My friends and neighbors approached and stood against me, and those near me stood far off; those who sought my soul used violence.

37:13  LA: Et qui inquirebant mala mihi, locuti sunt vanitates, et dolos tota die meditabantur.
       EN: Those who sought evil for me spoke empty things and devised deceit all day.

37:14  LA: Ego autem, tamquam surdus, non audiebam ; et sicut mutus non aperiens os suum.
       EN: But like a deaf man I did not hear, and I was like a mute who does not open his mouth.

37:15  LA: Et factus sum sicut homo non audiens, et non habens in ore suo redargutiones.
       EN: I became like a man who does not hear and has no rebukes in his mouth.

37:16  LA: Quoniam in te, Domine, speravi ; tu exaudies me, Domine Deus meus.
       EN: For in You, Lord, I have hoped; You will hear me, Lord my God.

37:17  LA: Quia dixi : Nequando supergaudeant mihi inimici mei ; et dum commoventur pedes mei, super me magna locuti sunt.
       EN: For I said, “Let my enemies never rejoice over me,” and when my feet are shaken, they have boasted against me.

37:18  LA: Quoniam ego in flagella paratus sum, et dolor meus in conspectu meo semper.
       EN: For I am ready for scourges, and my sorrow is always before me.

37:19  LA: Quoniam iniquitatem meam annuntiabo, et cogitabo pro peccato meo.
       EN: For I will declare my iniquity and take thought for my sin.

37:20  LA: Inimici autem mei vivunt, et confirmati sunt super me : et multiplicati sunt qui oderunt me inique.
       EN: But my enemies live and have grown strong against me, and those who hate me unjustly have multiplied.

37:21  LA: Qui retribuunt mala pro bonis detrahebant mihi, quoniam sequebar bonitatem.
       EN: Those who repay evil for good slandered me because I followed goodness.

37:22  LA: Ne derelinquas me, Domine Deus meus ; ne discesseris a me.
       EN: Do not abandon me, Lord my God; do not withdraw from me.

37:23  LA: Intende in adjutorium meum, Domine Deus salutis meæ.]
       EN: Attend to my help, Lord, God of my salvation.

PSALMS 38

38:1  LA: In finem, ipsi Idithun. Canticum David.
      EN: For the end, for Jeduthun himself. A song of David.

38:2  LA: [Dixi : Custodiam vias meas : ut non delinquam in lingua mea. Posui ori meo custodiam, cum consisteret peccator adversum me.
      EN: I said, “I will guard my ways, so that I do not sin with my tongue.” I placed a guard over my mouth when the sinner stood against me.

38:3  LA: Obmutui, et humiliatus sum, et silui a bonis ; et dolor meus renovatus est.
      EN: I became mute and was humbled; I was silent even from good things, and my sorrow was renewed.

38:4  LA: Concaluit cor meum intra me ; et in meditatione mea exardescet ignis.
      EN: My heart grew hot within me, and in my meditation fire will blaze up.

38:5  LA: Locutus sum in lingua mea : Notum fac mihi, Domine, finem meum, et numerum dierum meorum quis est, ut sciam quid desit mihi.
      EN: I spoke with my tongue: “Make my end known to me, Lord, and what the number of my days is, so that I may know what is lacking to me.

38:6  LA: Ecce mensurabiles posuisti dies meos, et substantia mea tamquam nihilum ante te. Verumtamen universa vanitas, omnis homo vivens.
      EN: See, You have made my days measurable, and my existence is as nothing before You. Truly every living human is utter emptiness.

38:7  LA: Verumtamen in imagine pertransit homo ; sed et frustra conturbatur : thesaurizat, et ignorat cui congregabit ea.
      EN: Truly a person passes by like an image, yet is troubled to no purpose; he stores up treasure and does not know for whom he will gather it.

38:8  LA: Et nunc quæ est exspectatio mea : nonne Dominus ? et substantia mea apud te est.
      EN: And now, what is my expectation? Is it not the Lord? My existence is with You.

38:9  LA: Ab omnibus iniquitatibus meis erue me : opprobrium insipienti dedisti me.
      EN: Rescue me from all my iniquities; You have made me a reproach to the fool.

38:10  LA: Obmutui, et non aperui os meum, quoniam tu fecisti ;
       EN: I became mute and did not open my mouth, for You have acted.

38:11  LA: amove a me plagas tuas.
       EN: Remove Your blows from me.

38:12  LA: A fortitudine manus tuæ ego defeci in increpationibus : propter iniquitatem corripuisti hominem. Et tabescere fecisti sicut araneam animam ejus : verumtamen vane conturbatur omnis homo.
       EN: Under the strength of Your hand I have failed amid rebukes; because of iniquity You have chastised man. You have made his soul waste away like a spider; truly everyone is troubled to no purpose.

38:13  LA: Exaudi orationem meam, Domine, et deprecationem meam ; auribus percipe lacrimas meas. Ne sileas, quoniam advena ego sum apud te, et peregrinus sicut omnes patres mei.
       EN: Hear my prayer, Lord, and my plea; listen to my tears. Do not be silent, for I am a stranger with You and a sojourner like all my fathers.

38:14  LA: Remitte mihi, ut refrigerer priusquam abeam et amplius non ero.]
       EN: Relent toward me so that I may be refreshed before I depart and am no more.”

PSALMS 39

39:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus ipsi David.
      EN: For the end. A psalm for David himself.

39:2  LA: [Exspectans exspectavi Dominum, et intendit mihi.
      EN: Waiting, I waited for the Lord, and He attended to me.

39:3  LA: Et exaudivit preces meas, et eduxit me de lacu miseriæ et de luto fæcis. Et statuit super petram pedes meos, et direxit gressus meos.
      EN: He heard my prayers and brought me out of the pit of misery and the filthy mud. He set my feet upon a rock and guided my steps.

39:4  LA: Et immisit in os meum canticum novum, carmen Deo nostro. Videbunt multi, et timebunt, et sperabunt in Domino.
      EN: He put a new song into my mouth, a song to our God. Many will see and fear and hope in the Lord.

39:5  LA: Beatus vir cujus est nomen Domini spes ejus, et non respexit in vanitates et insanias falsas.
      EN: Blessed is the man whose hope is the Lord's name, who has not looked to empty things and lying follies.

39:6  LA: Multa fecisti tu, Domine Deus meus, mirabilia tua ; et cogitationibus tuis non est qui similis sit tibi. Annuntiavi et locutus sum : multiplicati sunt super numerum.
      EN: Many are the wonders You have done, Lord my God, and in Your thoughts no one is like You. I have proclaimed and spoken; they have multiplied beyond number.

39:7  LA: Sacrificium et oblationem noluisti ; aures autem perfecisti mihi. Holocaustum et pro peccato non postulasti ;
      EN: You did not desire sacrifice and offering, but You made ears for me. You did not ask for a burnt offering or an offering for sin;

39:8  LA: tunc dixi : Ecce venio. In capite libri scriptum est de me,
      EN: then I said, “See, I come. At the head of the book it is written of me,

39:9  LA: ut facerem voluntatem tuam. Deus meus, volui, et legem tuam in medio cordis mei.
      EN: that I should do Your will.” My God, I have desired it, and Your law is in the midst of my heart.

39:10  LA: Annuntiavi justitiam tuam in ecclesia magna ; ecce labia mea non prohibebo : Domine, tu scisti.
       EN: I have proclaimed Your righteousness in the great congregation; see, I will not restrain my lips. Lord, You have known.

39:11  LA: Justitiam tuam non abscondi in corde meo ; veritatem tuam et salutare tuum dixi ; non abscondi misericordiam tuam et veritatem tuam a concilio multo.
       EN: I have not hidden Your righteousness within my heart; I have spoken of Your truth and Your salvation. I have not hidden Your mercy and Your truth from a great assembly.

39:12  LA: Tu autem, Domine, ne longe facias miserationes tuas a me ; misericordia tua et veritas tua semper susceperunt me.
       EN: But You, Lord, do not keep Your compassion far from me; Your mercy and Your truth have always sustained me.

39:13  LA: Quoniam circumdederunt me mala quorum non est numerus ; comprehenderunt me iniquitates meæ, et non potui ut viderem. Multiplicatæ sunt super capillos capitis mei, et cor meum dereliquit me.
       EN: For evils beyond number have surrounded me; my iniquities have overtaken me, and I have been unable to see. They have multiplied beyond the hairs of my head, and my heart has abandoned me.

39:14  LA: Complaceat tibi, Domine, ut eruas me ; Domine, ad adjuvandum me respice.
       EN: May it please You, Lord, to rescue me; Lord, look to helping me.

39:15  LA: Confundantur et revereantur simul, qui quærunt animam meam ut auferant eam ; convertantur retrorsum et revereantur, qui volunt mihi mala.
       EN: Let those who seek my soul to take it away be confounded and ashamed together; let those who desire evil for me turn back and be ashamed.

39:16  LA: Ferant confestim confusionem suam, qui dicunt mihi : Euge, euge !
       EN: Let those who say to me, “Aha, aha!” immediately bear their shame.

39:17  LA: Exsultent et lætentur super te omnes quærentes te ; et dicant semper : Magnificetur Dominus, qui diligunt salutare tuum.
       EN: Let all who seek You rejoice and be glad in You, and let those who love Your salvation always say, “May the Lord be magnified.”

39:18  LA: Ego autem mendicus sum et pauper ; Dominus sollicitus est mei. Adjutor meus et protector meus tu es ; Deus meus, ne tardaveris.]
       EN: But I am a beggar and poor; the Lord is concerned for me. You are my helper and protector; my God, do not delay.

PSALMS 40

40:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus ipsi David.
      EN: For the end. A psalm for David himself.

40:2  LA: [Beatus qui intelligit super egenum et pauperem : in die mala liberabit eum Dominus.
      EN: Blessed is the one who understands the needy and poor; on an evil day the Lord will deliver him.

40:3  LA: Dominus conservet eum, et vivificet eum, et beatum faciat eum in terra, et non tradat eum in animam inimicorum ejus.
      EN: May the Lord preserve him and give him life, make him blessed upon the earth, and not hand him over to his enemies' desire.

40:4  LA: Dominus opem ferat illi super lectum doloris ejus ; universum stratum ejus versasti in infirmitate ejus.
      EN: May the Lord bring him help upon his bed of sorrow; You have turned all his bedding in his weakness.

40:5  LA: Ego dixi : Domine, miserere mei ; sana animam meam, quia peccavi tibi.
      EN: I said, “Lord, have mercy on me; heal my soul, for I have sinned against You.”

40:6  LA: Inimici mei dixerunt mala mihi : Quando morietur, et peribit nomen ejus ?
      EN: My enemies spoke evil of me: “When will he die and his name perish?”

40:7  LA: Et si ingrediebatur ut videret, vana loquebatur ; cor ejus congregavit iniquitatem sibi. Egrediebatur foras et loquebatur.
      EN: If one came in to see me, he spoke empty things; his heart gathered iniquity to itself. He went outside and spoke.

40:8  LA: In idipsum adversum me susurrabant omnes inimici mei ; adversum me cogitabant mala mihi.
      EN: All my enemies whispered together against me; against me they devised evil for me.

40:9  LA: Verbum iniquum constituerunt adversum me : Numquid qui dormit non adjiciet ut resurgat ?
      EN: They established an unjust word against me: “Will the one who sleeps not rise again?”

40:10  LA: Etenim homo pacis meæ in quo speravi, qui edebat panes meos, magnificavit super me supplantationem.
       EN: Even the man at peace with me, in whom I trusted, who ate my bread, has greatly tripped me up.

40:11  LA: Tu autem, Domine, miserere mei, et resuscita me ; et retribuam eis.
       EN: But You, Lord, have mercy on me and raise me up, and I will repay them.

40:12  LA: In hoc cognovi quoniam voluisti me, quoniam non gaudebit inimicus meus super me.
       EN: By this I have known that You delight in me: my enemy will not rejoice over me.

40:13  LA: Me autem propter innocentiam suscepisti ; et confirmasti me in conspectu tuo in æternum.
       EN: But You have sustained me because of my innocence and established me in Your sight forever.

40:14  LA: Benedictus Dominus Deus Israël a sæculo et usque in sæculum. Fiat, fiat.]
       EN: Blessed is the Lord, the God of Israel, from age to age. Let it be, let it be.

PSALMS 41

41:1  LA: In finem. Intellectus filiis Core.
      EN: For the end. For understanding, for the sons of Korah.

41:2  LA: [Quemadmodum desiderat cervus ad fontes aquarum, ita desiderat anima mea ad te, Deus.
      EN: As the deer longs for springs of water, so my soul longs for You, God.

41:3  LA: Sitivit anima mea ad Deum fortem, vivum ; quando veniam, et apparebo ante faciem Dei ?
      EN: My soul has thirsted for the strong, living God. When will I come and appear before the face of God?

41:4  LA: Fuerunt mihi lacrimæ meæ panes die ac nocte, dum dicitur mihi quotidie : Ubi est Deus tuus ?
      EN: My tears have been my bread by day and night, while every day I am asked, “Where is your God?”

41:5  LA: Hæc recordatus sum, et effudi in me animam meam, quoniam transibo in locum tabernaculi admirabilis, usque ad domum Dei, in voce exsultationis et confessionis, sonus epulantis.
      EN: I remembered these things and poured out my soul within me, for I will pass into the place of the wonderful tent, as far as the house of God, amid a voice of rejoicing and praise, the sound of one feasting.

41:6  LA: Quare tristis es, anima mea ? et quare conturbas me ? Spera in Deo, quoniam adhuc confitebor illi, salutare vultus mei,
      EN: Why are you sorrowful, my soul, and why do you trouble me? Hope in God, for I will still praise Him, the salvation of my countenance

41:7  LA: et Deus meus. Ad meipsum anima mea conturbata est : propterea memor ero tui de terra Jordanis et Hermoniim a monte modico.
      EN: and my God. My soul is troubled within me; therefore I will remember You from the land of the Jordan and the Hermons, from the little mountain.

41:8  LA: Abyssus abyssum invocat, in voce cataractarum tuarum ; omnia excelsa tua, et fluctus tui super me transierunt.
      EN: Deep calls to deep at the sound of Your waterfalls; all Your heights and Your waves have passed over me.

41:9  LA: In die mandavit Dominus misericordiam suam, et nocte canticum ejus ; apud me oratio Deo vitæ meæ.
      EN: By day the Lord commanded His mercy, and by night His song; with me is a prayer to the God of my life.

41:10  LA: Dicam Deo : Susceptor meus es ; quare oblitus es mei ? et quare contristatus incedo, dum affligit me inimicus ?
       EN: I will say to God, “You are my defender; why have You forgotten me? Why do I walk in sorrow while the enemy afflicts me?”

41:11  LA: Dum confringuntur ossa mea, exprobraverunt mihi qui tribulant me inimici mei, dum dicunt mihi per singulos dies : Ubi est Deus tuus ?
       EN: While my bones are being broken, my enemies who afflict me reproached me, saying to me every day, “Where is your God?”

41:12  LA: Quare tristis es, anima mea ? et quare conturbas me ? Spera in Deo, quoniam adhuc confitebor illi, salutare vultus mei, et Deus meus.]
       EN: Why are you sorrowful, my soul, and why do you trouble me? Hope in God, for I will still praise Him, the salvation of my countenance and my God.

PSALMS 42

42:1  LA: Psalmus David. [Judica me, Deus, et discerne causam meam de gente non sancta : ab homine iniquo et doloso erue me.
      EN: A psalm of David. Judge me, God, and distinguish my cause from an unholy nation; rescue me from an unjust and deceitful man.

42:2  LA: Quia tu es, Deus, fortitudo mea : quare me repulisti ? et quare tristis incedo, dum affligit me inimicus ?
      EN: For You, God, are my strength. Why have You rejected me? Why do I walk in sorrow while the enemy afflicts me?

42:3  LA: Emitte lucem tuam et veritatem tuam : ipsa me deduxerunt, et adduxerunt in montem sanctum tuum, et in tabernacula tua.
      EN: Send forth Your light and Your truth; they have guided me and brought me to Your holy mountain and to Your tents.

42:4  LA: Et introibo ad altare Dei, ad Deum qui lætificat juventutem meam. Confitebor tibi in cithara, Deus, Deus meus.
      EN: I will go in to the altar of God, to God who gladdens my youth. I will praise You with the lyre, God, my God.

42:5  LA: Quare tristis es, anima mea ? et quare conturbas me ? Spera in Deo, quoniam adhuc confitebor illi, salutare vultus mei, et Deus meus.]
      EN: Why are you sorrowful, my soul, and why do you trouble me? Hope in God, for I will still praise Him, the salvation of my countenance and my God.

PSALMS 43

43:1  LA: In finem. Filiis Core ad intellectum.
      EN: For the end. For the sons of Korah, for understanding.

43:2  LA: [Deus, auribus nostris audivimus, patres nostri annuntiaverunt nobis, opus quod operatus es in diebus eorum, et in diebus antiquis.
      EN: God, we have heard with our ears; our fathers have told us the work You did in their days and in ancient days.

43:3  LA: Manus tua gentes disperdidit, et plantasti eos ; afflixisti populos, et expulisti eos.
      EN: Your hand destroyed nations, and You planted them; You afflicted peoples and drove them out.

43:4  LA: Nec enim in gladio suo possederunt terram, et brachium eorum non salvavit eos : sed dextera tua et brachium tuum, et illuminatio vultus tui, quoniam complacuisti in eis.
      EN: For they did not possess the land by their sword, nor did their arm save them, but Your right hand, Your arm, and the light of Your face, for You delighted in them.

43:5  LA: Tu es ipse rex meus et Deus meus, qui mandas salutes Jacob.
      EN: You are my King and my God, who commands deliverances for Jacob.

43:6  LA: In te inimicos nostros ventilabimus cornu, et in nomine tuo spernemus insurgentes in nobis.
      EN: In You we will toss our enemies with the horn, and in Your name we will scorn those who rise against us.

43:7  LA: Non enim in arcu meo sperabo, et gladius meus non salvabit me :
      EN: For I will not hope in my bow, and my sword will not save me,

43:8  LA: salvasti enim nos de affligentibus nos, et odientes nos confudisti.
      EN: for You have saved us from those who afflict us and put those who hate us to shame.

43:9  LA: In Deo laudabimur tota die, et in nomine tuo confitebimur in sæculum.
      EN: In God we will be praised all day, and in Your name we will give praise forever.

43:10  LA: Nunc autem repulisti et confudisti nos, et non egredieris, Deus, in virtutibus nostris.
       EN: But now You have rejected and confounded us, and You will not go out, God, with our armies.

43:11  LA: Avertisti nos retrorsum post inimicos nostros, et qui oderunt nos diripiebant sibi.
       EN: You have turned us back behind our enemies, and those who hate us plundered for themselves.

43:12  LA: Dedisti nos tamquam oves escarum, et in gentibus dispersisti nos.
       EN: You have given us up like sheep for food and scattered us among the nations.

43:13  LA: Vendidisti populum tuum sine pretio, et non fuit multitudo in commutationibus eorum.
       EN: You sold Your people for no price, and there was no abundance in what was exchanged for them.

43:14  LA: Posuisti nos opprobrium vicinis nostris ; subsannationem et derisum his qui sunt in circuitu nostro.
       EN: You have made us a reproach to our neighbors, a mockery and laughingstock to those around us.

43:15  LA: Posuisti nos in similitudinem gentibus ; commotionem capitis in populis.
       EN: You have made us a byword among the nations, a shaking of the head among the peoples.

43:16  LA: Tota die verecundia mea contra me est, et confusio faciei meæ cooperuit me :
       EN: All day my shame is before me, and the confusion of my face has covered me,

43:17  LA: a voce exprobrantis et obloquentis, a facie inimici et persequentis.
       EN: at the voice of the reproacher and slanderer, before the enemy and pursuer.

43:18  LA: Hæc omnia venerunt super nos ; nec obliti sumus te, et inique non egimus in testamento tuo.
       EN: All these things have come upon us, yet we have not forgotten You or acted unjustly toward Your covenant.

43:19  LA: Et non recessit retro cor nostrum ; et declinasti semitas nostras a via tua :
       EN: Our heart has not turned backward, and You have turned our paths aside from Your way,

43:20  LA: quoniam humiliasti nos in loco afflictionis, et cooperuit nos umbra mortis.
       EN: for You have humbled us in a place of affliction, and the shadow of death has covered us.

43:21  LA: Si obliti sumus nomen Dei nostri, et si expandimus manus nostras ad deum alienum,
       EN: If we have forgotten the name of our God, and if we have stretched out our hands to a foreign god,

43:22  LA: nonne Deus requiret ista ? ipse enim novit abscondita cordis. Quoniam propter te mortificamur tota die ; æstimati sumus sicut oves occisionis.
       EN: will God not inquire into these things? For He knows the hidden things of the heart. For Your sake we are put to death all day; we have been reckoned as sheep for slaughter.

43:23  LA: nonne Deus requiret ista ? ipse enim novit abscondita cordis. Quoniam propter te mortificamur tota die ; æstimati sumus sicut oves occisionis.
       EN: will God not inquire into these things? For He knows the hidden things of the heart. For Your sake we are put to death all day; we have been reckoned as sheep for slaughter.

43:24  LA: Exsurge ; quare obdormis, Domine ? exsurge, et ne repellas in finem.
       EN: Arise! Why do You sleep, Lord? Arise, and do not reject us forever.

43:25  LA: Quare faciem tuam avertis ? oblivisceris inopiæ nostræ et tribulationis nostræ ?
       EN: Why do You turn Your face away? Do You forget our need and our distress?

43:26  LA: Quoniam humiliata est in pulvere anima nostra ; conglutinatus est in terra venter noster.
       EN: For our soul has been humbled into the dust; our belly has clung to the earth.

43:27  LA: Exsurge, Domine, adjuva nos, et redime nos propter nomen tuum.]
       EN: Arise, Lord, help us and redeem us for Your name's sake.

PSALMS 44

44:1  LA: In finem, pro iis qui commutabuntur. Filiis Core, ad intellectum. Canticum pro dilecto.
      EN: For the end, for those who will be changed. For the sons of Korah, for understanding. A song for the beloved.

44:2  LA: [Eructavit cor meum verbum bonum : dico ego opera mea regi. Lingua mea calamus scribæ velociter scribentis.
      EN: My heart has poured out a good word; I speak my works to the king. My tongue is the pen of a swiftly writing scribe.

44:3  LA: Speciosus forma præ filiis hominum, diffusa est gratia in labiis tuis : propterea benedixit te Deus in æternum.
      EN: You are beautiful in form beyond the children of men; grace has been poured upon your lips. Therefore God has blessed you forever.

44:4  LA: Accingere gladio tuo super femur tuum, potentissime.
      EN: Gird your sword upon your thigh, most mighty one.

44:5  LA: Specie tua et pulchritudine tua intende, prospere procede, et regna, propter veritatem, et mansuetudinem, et justitiam ; et deducet te mirabiliter dextera tua.
      EN: In your splendor and your beauty draw your bow, advance prosperously, and reign for the sake of truth, gentleness, and righteousness; your right hand will guide you wonderfully.

44:6  LA: Sagittæ tuæ acutæ : populi sub te cadent, in corda inimicorum regis.
      EN: Your arrows are sharp in the hearts of the king's enemies; peoples will fall beneath you.

44:7  LA: Sedes tua, Deus, in sæculum sæculi ; virga directionis virga regni tui.
      EN: Your throne, God, is for ages of ages; a rod of uprightness is the rod of Your kingdom.

44:8  LA: Dilexisti justitiam, et odisti iniquitatem ; propterea unxit te Deus, Deus tuus, oleo lætitiæ, præ consortibus tuis.
      EN: You have loved righteousness and hated iniquity; therefore God, your God, has anointed you with the oil of gladness above your companions.

44:9  LA: Myrrha, et gutta, et casia a vestimentis tuis, a domibus eburneis ; ex quibus delectaverunt te
      EN: Myrrh, resin, and cassia come from your garments, from ivory houses, from which you have been delighted

44:10  LA: filiæ regum in honore tuo. Astitit regina a dextris tuis in vestitu deaurato, circumdata varietate.
       EN: by kings' daughters in your honor. The queen stood at your right hand in clothing worked with gold, surrounded with varied adornment.

44:11  LA: Audi, filia, et vide, et inclina aurem tuam ; et obliviscere populum tuum, et domum patris tui.
       EN: Listen, daughter, and see, and incline your ear; forget your people and your father's house.

44:12  LA: Et concupiscet rex decorem tuum, quoniam ipse est Dominus Deus tuus, et adorabunt eum.
       EN: The king will desire your beauty, for He is the Lord your God, and they will worship Him.

44:13  LA: Et filiæ Tyri in muneribus vultum tuum deprecabuntur ; omnes divites plebis.
       EN: The daughters of Tyre will entreat your favor with gifts, all the rich among the people.

44:14  LA: Omnis gloria ejus filiæ regis ab intus, in fimbriis aureis,
       EN: All the glory of the king’s daughter is within, in golden fringes,

44:15  LA: circumamicta varietatibus. Adducentur regi virgines post eam ; proximæ ejus afferentur tibi.
       EN: clothed all around in varied colors. Virgins will be led to the king after her; her companions will be brought to you.

44:16  LA: Afferentur in lætitia et exsultatione ; adducentur in templum regis.
       EN: They will be brought with gladness and rejoicing; they will be led into the king's temple.

44:17  LA: Pro patribus tuis nati sunt tibi filii ; constitues eos principes super omnem terram.
       EN: In place of your fathers, sons have been born to you; you will establish them as rulers over all the earth.

44:18  LA: Memores erunt nominis tui in omni generatione et generationem : propterea populi confitebuntur tibi in æternum, et in sæculum sæculi.]
       EN: They will remember your name from generation to generation; therefore the peoples will praise you forever and for ages of ages.

PSALMS 45

45:1  LA: In finem, filiis Core, pro arcanis. Psalmus.
      EN: For the end, for the sons of Korah, concerning hidden things. A psalm.

45:2  LA: [Deus noster refugium et virtus ; adjutor in tribulationibus quæ invenerunt nos nimis.
      EN: Our God is refuge and strength, a helper in the distresses that have greatly overtaken us.

45:3  LA: Propterea non timebimus dum turbabitur terra, et transferentur montes in cor maris.
      EN: Therefore we will not fear when the earth is troubled and mountains are moved into the heart of the sea.

45:4  LA: Sonuerunt, et turbatæ sunt aquæ eorum ; conturbati sunt montes in fortitudine ejus.
      EN: Their waters have roared and been troubled; the mountains have been shaken by its strength.

45:5  LA: Fluminis impetus lætificat civitatem Dei : sanctificavit tabernaculum suum Altissimus.
      EN: The rush of a river gladdens the city of God; the Most High has sanctified His tent.

45:6  LA: Deus in medio ejus, non commovebitur ; adjuvabit eam Deus mane diluculo.
      EN: God is in its midst; it will not be shaken. God will help it in the morning, at daybreak.

45:7  LA: Conturbatæ sunt gentes, et inclinata sunt regna : dedit vocem suam, mota est terra.
      EN: Nations have been troubled and kingdoms brought low; He uttered His voice, and the earth was shaken.

45:8  LA: Dominus virtutum nobiscum ; susceptor noster Deus Jacob.
      EN: The Lord of hosts is with us; the God of Jacob is our defender.

45:9  LA: Venite, et videte opera Domini, quæ posuit prodigia super terram,
      EN: Come and see the Lord's works, the wonders He has placed upon the earth,

45:10  LA: auferens bella usque ad finem terræ. Arcum conteret, et confringet arma, et scuta comburet igni.
       EN: removing wars to the ends of the earth. He will crush the bow and break weapons, and He will burn shields with fire.

45:11  LA: Vacate, et videte quoniam ego sum Deus ; exaltabor in gentibus, et exaltabor in terra.
       EN: Be still and see that I am God; I will be exalted among the nations and exalted upon the earth.

45:12  LA: Dominus virtutum nobiscum ; susceptor noster Deus Jacob.]
       EN: The Lord of hosts is with us; the God of Jacob is our defender.

PSALMS 46

46:1  LA: In finem, pro filiis Core. Psalmus.
      EN: For the end, for the sons of Korah. A psalm.

46:2  LA: [Omnes gentes, plaudite manibus ; jubilate Deo in voce exsultationis :
      EN: All nations, clap your hands; shout to God with a voice of rejoicing,

46:3  LA: quoniam Dominus excelsus, terribilis, rex magnus super omnem terram.
      EN: for the Lord is most high and fearsome, a great King over all the earth.

46:4  LA: Subjecit populos nobis, et gentes sub pedibus nostris.
      EN: He has subjected peoples to us and nations beneath our feet.

46:5  LA: Elegit nobis hæreditatem suam ; speciem Jacob quam dilexit.
      EN: He has chosen His inheritance for us, the beauty of Jacob, which He loved.

46:6  LA: Ascendit Deus in jubilo, et Dominus in voce tubæ.
      EN: God has ascended with a shout, and the Lord with the sound of a trumpet.

46:7  LA: Psallite Deo nostro, psallite ; psallite regi nostro, psallite :
      EN: Sing to our God, sing; sing to our King, sing,

46:8  LA: quoniam rex omnis terræ Deus, psallite sapienter.
      EN: for God is King of all the earth; sing with understanding.

46:9  LA: Regnabit Deus super gentes ; Deus sedet super sedem sanctam suam.
      EN: God will reign over the nations; God sits upon His holy throne.

46:10  LA: Principes populorum congregati sunt cum Deo Abraham, quoniam dii fortes terræ vehementer elevati sunt.]
       EN: The rulers of the peoples have gathered with the God of Abraham, for the mighty gods of the earth have been greatly exalted.

PSALMS 47

47:1  LA: Psalmus cantici. Filiis Core, secunda sabbati.
      EN: A psalm of song. For the sons of Korah, for the second day of the week.

47:2  LA: [Magnus Dominus et laudabilis nimis, in civitate Dei nostri, in monte sancto ejus.
      EN: Great is the Lord and greatly to be praised in the city of our God, upon His holy mountain.

47:3  LA: Fundatur exsultatione universæ terræ mons Sion ; latera aquilonis, civitas regis magni.
      EN: Mount Zion is founded with the rejoicing of the whole earth; the sides of the north, the city of the great King.

47:4  LA: Deus in domibus ejus cognoscetur cum suscipiet eam.
      EN: God will be known in its houses when He defends it.

47:5  LA: Quoniam ecce reges terræ congregati sunt ; convenerunt in unum.
      EN: For see, the kings of the earth have assembled; they have gathered together.

47:6  LA: Ipsi videntes, sic admirati sunt, conturbati sunt, commoti sunt.
      EN: They saw it and so were amazed; they were troubled, they were shaken.

47:7  LA: Tremor apprehendit eos ; ibi dolores ut parturientis :
      EN: Trembling seized them there, pains like those of a woman in labor.

47:8  LA: in spiritu vehementi conteres naves Tharsis.
      EN: With a violent wind You will crush the ships of Tarshish.

47:9  LA: Sicut audivimus, sic vidimus, in civitate Domini virtutum, in civitate Dei nostri : Deus fundavit eam in æternum.
      EN: As we have heard, so we have seen in the city of the Lord of hosts, in the city of our God; God has founded it forever.

47:10  LA: Suscepimus, Deus, misericordiam tuam in medio templi tui.
       EN: We have received Your mercy, God, in the midst of Your temple.

47:11  LA: Secundum nomen tuum, Deus, sic et laus tua in fines terræ ; justitia plena est dextera tua.
       EN: As is Your name, God, so is Your praise to the ends of the earth; Your right hand is full of righteousness.

47:12  LA: Lætetur mons Sion, et exsultent filiæ Judæ, propter judicia tua, Domine.
       EN: Let Mount Zion be glad, and let the daughters of Judah rejoice because of Your judgments, Lord.

47:13  LA: Circumdate Sion, et complectimini eam ; narrate in turribus ejus.
       EN: Go around Zion and encircle it; tell in its towers.

47:14  LA: Ponite corda vestra in virtute ejus, et distribuite domos ejus, ut enarretis in progenie altera.
       EN: Set your hearts upon its strength and distribute its houses, so that you may recount it to another generation.

47:15  LA: Quoniam hic est Deus, Deus noster in æternum, et in sæculum sæculi : ipse reget nos in sæcula.]
       EN: For this is God, our God forever and for ages of ages; He will guide us through the ages.

PSALMS 48

48:1  LA: In finem, filiis Core. Psalmus.
      EN: For the end, for the sons of Korah. A psalm.

48:2  LA: [Audite hæc, omnes gentes ; auribus percipite, omnes qui habitatis orbem :
      EN: Hear these things, all nations; listen, all who inhabit the world,

48:3  LA: quique terrigenæ et filii hominum, simul in unum dives et pauper.
      EN: both those born of earth and the children of men, rich and poor together.

48:4  LA: Os meum loquetur sapientiam, et meditatio cordis mei prudentiam.
      EN: My mouth will speak wisdom, and my heart's meditation will speak understanding.

48:5  LA: Inclinabo in parabolam aurem meam ; aperiam in psalterio propositionem meam.
      EN: I will incline my ear to a parable; I will open my riddle upon the harp.

48:6  LA: Cur timebo in die mala ? iniquitas calcanei mei circumdabit me.
      EN: Why will I fear on an evil day? The iniquity of my heel will surround me.

48:7  LA: Qui confidunt in virtute sua, et in multitudine divitiarum suarum, gloriantur.
      EN: Those who trust in their strength and in the abundance of their riches boast.

48:8  LA: Frater non redimit, redimet homo : non dabit Deo placationem suam,
      EN: A brother does not redeem; a man will redeem. He will not give God his appeasement

48:9  LA: et pretium redemptionis animæ suæ. Et laborabit in æternum ;
      EN: or the price of his soul's redemption, and he will labor forever,

48:10  LA: et vivet adhuc in finem.
       EN: and will still live to the end. He will not see destruction when he sees the wise dying; the senseless and the fool will perish together. They will leave their riches to strangers,

48:11  LA: et vivet adhuc in finem.
       EN: and will still live to the end. He will not see destruction when he sees the wise dying; the senseless and the fool will perish together. They will leave their riches to strangers,

48:12  LA: et sepulchra eorum domus illorum in æternum ; tabernacula eorum in progenie et progenie : vocaverunt nomina sua in terris suis.
       EN: and their tombs will be their houses forever, their tents from generation to generation; they have called their lands by their own names.

48:13  LA: Et homo, cum in honore esset, non intellexit. Comparatus est jumentis insipientibus, et similis factus est illis.
       EN: Man, when he was in honor, did not understand; he was compared with senseless beasts and became like them.

48:14  LA: Hæc via illorum scandalum ipsis ; et postea in ore suo complacebunt.
       EN: This way of theirs is a stumbling block to them, yet afterward they will take pleasure in their own mouth.

48:15  LA: Sicut oves in inferno positi sunt : mors depascet eos. Et dominabuntur eorum justi in matutino ; et auxilium eorum veterascet in inferno a gloria eorum.
       EN: They have been placed like sheep in the underworld; death will pasture them. The just will rule over them in the morning, and their help will grow old in the underworld, away from their glory.

48:16  LA: Verumtamen Deus redimet animam meam de manu inferi, cum acceperit me.
       EN: Yet God will redeem my soul from the underworld's hand when He receives me.

48:17  LA: Ne timueris cum dives factus fuerit homo, et cum multiplicata fuerit gloria domus ejus :
       EN: Do not fear when a man becomes rich and the glory of his house multiplies,

48:18  LA: quoniam, cum interierit, non sumet omnia, neque descendet cum eo gloria ejus.
       EN: for when he dies he will not take everything, nor will his glory descend with him.

48:19  LA: Quia anima ejus in vita ipsius benedicetur ; confitebitur tibi cum benefeceris ei.
       EN: For his soul will be blessed during his lifetime; he will praise you when you do him good.

48:20  LA: Introibit usque in progenies patrum suorum ; et usque in æternum non videbit lumen.
       EN: He will enter among the generations of his fathers and will never see light.

48:21  LA: Homo, cum in honore esset, non intellexit. Comparatus est jumentis insipientibus, et similis factus est illis.]
       EN: Man, when he was in honor, did not understand; he was compared with senseless beasts and became like them.

PSALMS 49

49:1  LA: Psalmus Asaph. [Deus deorum Dominus locutus est, et vocavit terram a solis ortu usque ad occasum.
      EN: A psalm of Asaph. The God of gods, the Lord, has spoken and called the earth from sunrise to sunset.

49:2  LA: Ex Sion species decoris ejus :
      EN: From Zion comes the appearance of His beauty.

49:3  LA: Deus manifeste veniet ; Deus noster, et non silebit. Ignis in conspectu ejus exardescet ; et in circuitu ejus tempestas valida.
      EN: God will come openly, our God, and will not keep silent. Fire will blaze before Him, and a mighty storm will surround Him.

49:4  LA: Advocabit cælum desursum, et terram, discernere populum suum.
      EN: He will summon heaven from above, and the earth, to judge His people.

49:5  LA: Congregate illi sanctos ejus, qui ordinant testamentum ejus super sacrificia.
      EN: Gather His holy ones to Him, those who establish His covenant over sacrifices.

49:6  LA: Et annuntiabunt cæli justitiam ejus, quoniam Deus judex est.
      EN: The heavens will proclaim His righteousness, for God is judge.

49:7  LA: Audi, populus meus, et loquar ; Israël, et testificabor tibi : Deus, Deus tuus ego sum.
      EN: Hear, My people, and I will speak; Israel, and I will testify to you. I am God, your God.

49:8  LA: Non in sacrificiis tuis arguam te ; holocausta autem tua in conspectu meo sunt semper.
      EN: I will not rebuke you for your sacrifices; your burnt offerings are always before Me.

49:9  LA: Non accipiam de domo tua vitulos, neque de gregibus tuis hircos :
      EN: I will not accept calves from your house or goats from your flocks,

49:10  LA: quoniam meæ sunt omnes feræ silvarum, jumenta in montibus, et boves.
       EN: for all the beasts of the forests are Mine, the animals upon the mountains and the cattle.

49:11  LA: Cognovi omnia volatilia cæli, et pulchritudo agri mecum est.
       EN: I know all the birds of the sky, and the beauty of the field is with Me.

49:12  LA: Si esuriero, non dicam tibi : meus est enim orbis terræ et plenitudo ejus.
       EN: If I were hungry, I would not tell you, for the world and all that fills it are Mine.

49:13  LA: Numquid manducabo carnes taurorum ? aut sanguinem hircorum potabo ?
       EN: Will I eat the flesh of bulls or drink the blood of goats?

49:14  LA: Immola Deo sacrificium laudis, et redde Altissimo vota tua.
       EN: Offer God a sacrifice of praise and fulfill your vows to the Most High.

49:15  LA: Et invoca me in die tribulationis : eruam te, et honorificabis me.
       EN: Call upon Me on the day of distress; I will rescue you, and you will honor Me.

49:16  LA: Peccatori autem dixit Deus : Quare tu enarras justitias meas ? et assumis testamentum meum per os tuum ?
       EN: But to the sinner God said, “Why do you recount My righteous ordinances and take My covenant upon your lips?

49:17  LA: Tu vero odisti disciplinam, et projecisti sermones meos retrorsum.
       EN: Yet you have hated discipline and cast My words behind you.

49:18  LA: Si videbas furem, currebas cum eo ; et cum adulteris portionem tuam ponebas.
       EN: If you saw a thief, you ran with him, and with adulterers you placed your portion.

49:19  LA: Os tuum abundavit malitia, et lingua tua concinnabat dolos.
       EN: Your mouth has abounded in malice, and your tongue wove deceit.

49:20  LA: Sedens adversus fratrem tuum loquebaris, et adversus filium matris tuæ ponebas scandalum.
       EN: Sitting, you spoke against your brother and placed a stumbling block against your mother's son.

49:21  LA: Hæc fecisti, et tacui. Existimasti inique quod ero tui similis : arguam te, et statuam contra faciem tuam.
       EN: These things you did, and I kept silent. You wrongly supposed that I would be like you; I will rebuke you and set it before your face.”

49:22  LA: Intelligite hæc, qui obliviscimini Deum, nequando rapiat, et non sit qui eripiat.
       EN: Understand these things, you who forget God, lest He seize you and there be no one to rescue.

49:23  LA: Sacrificium laudis honorificabit me, et illic iter quo ostendam illi salutare Dei.]
       EN: A sacrifice of praise will honor Me, and there is the way by which I will show him the salvation of God.

PSALMS 50

50:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David,
      EN: For the end. A psalm of David,

50:2  LA: cum venit ad eum Nathan propheta, quando intravit ad Bethsabee.
      EN: when Nathan the prophet came to him, after he had gone in to Bathsheba.

50:3  LA: [Miserere mei, Deus, secundum magnam misericordiam tuam ; et secundum multitudinem miserationum tuarum, dele iniquitatem meam.
      EN: Have mercy on me, God, according to Your great mercy, and according to the abundance of Your compassion erase my iniquity.

50:4  LA: Amplius lava me ab iniquitate mea, et a peccato meo munda me.
      EN: Wash me more thoroughly of my iniquity, and cleanse me of my sin.

50:5  LA: Quoniam iniquitatem meam ego cognosco, et peccatum meum contra me est semper.
      EN: For I know my iniquity, and my sin is always before me.

50:6  LA: Tibi soli peccavi, et malum coram te feci ; ut justificeris in sermonibus tuis, et vincas cum judicaris.
