      EN: Against You alone I have sinned and done evil before You, so that You may be justified in Your words and prevail when You are judged.

50:7  LA: Ecce enim in iniquitatibus conceptus sum, et in peccatis concepit me mater mea.
      EN: For see, I was conceived in iniquities, and in sins my mother conceived me.

50:8  LA: Ecce enim veritatem dilexisti ; incerta et occulta sapientiæ tuæ manifestasti mihi.
      EN: For see, You have loved truth; You have made the uncertain and hidden things of Your wisdom known to me.

50:9  LA: Asperges me hyssopo, et mundabor ; lavabis me, et super nivem dealbabor.
      EN: You will sprinkle me with hyssop, and I will be clean; You will wash me, and I will be whiter than snow.

50:10  LA: Auditui meo dabis gaudium et lætitiam, et exsultabunt ossa humiliata.
       EN: You will let me hear joy and gladness, and the humbled bones will rejoice.

50:11  LA: Averte faciem tuam a peccatis meis, et omnes iniquitates meas dele.
       EN: Turn Your face away from my sins and erase all my iniquities.

50:12  LA: Cor mundum crea in me, Deus, et spiritum rectum innova in visceribus meis.
       EN: Create a clean heart in me, God, and renew an upright spirit deep within me.

50:13  LA: Ne projicias me a facie tua, et spiritum sanctum tuum ne auferas a me.
       EN: Do not cast me away from Your presence or take Your holy spirit from me.

50:14  LA: Redde mihi lætitiam salutaris tui, et spiritu principali confirma me.
       EN: Restore to me the gladness of Your salvation, and strengthen me with a governing spirit.

50:15  LA: Docebo iniquos vias tuas, et impii ad te convertentur.
       EN: I will teach the unjust Your ways, and the ungodly will turn back to You.

50:16  LA: Libera me de sanguinibus, Deus, Deus salutis meæ, et exsultabit lingua mea justitiam tuam.
       EN: Deliver me from bloodshed, God, God of my salvation, and my tongue will rejoice in Your righteousness.

50:17  LA: Domine, labia mea aperies, et os meum annuntiabit laudem tuam.
       EN: Lord, You will open my lips, and my mouth will proclaim Your praise.

50:18  LA: Quoniam si voluisses sacrificium, dedissem utique ; holocaustis non delectaberis.
       EN: For if You had desired sacrifice, I would certainly have given it; You will not delight in burnt offerings.

50:19  LA: Sacrificium Deo spiritus contribulatus ; cor contritum et humiliatum, Deus, non despicies.
       EN: A troubled spirit is a sacrifice to God; a contrite and humbled heart, God, You will not despise.

50:20  LA: Benigne fac, Domine, in bona voluntate tua Sion, ut ædificentur muri Jerusalem.
       EN: Deal kindly, Lord, with Zion in Your goodwill, so that Jerusalem's walls may be built.

50:21  LA: Tunc acceptabis sacrificium justitiæ, oblationes et holocausta ; tunc imponent super altare tuum vitulos.]
       EN: Then You will accept a sacrifice of righteousness, offerings and burnt offerings; then they will place calves upon Your altar.

PSALMS 51

51:1  LA: In finem. Intellectus David,
      EN: For the end. For understanding, of David,

51:2  LA: cum venit Doëg Idumæus, et nuntiavit Sauli : Venit David in domum Achimelech.
      EN: when Doeg the Edomite came and told Saul, “David has come into Ahimelech's house.”

51:3  LA: [Quid gloriaris in malitia, qui potens es in iniquitate ?
      EN: Why do you glory in malice, you who are mighty in iniquity?

51:4  LA: Tota die injustitiam cogitavit lingua tua ; sicut novacula acuta fecisti dolum.
      EN: All day your tongue has devised injustice; like a sharp razor you have practiced deceit.

51:5  LA: Dilexisti malitiam super benignitatem ; iniquitatem magis quam loqui æquitatem.
      EN: You have loved malice more than kindness, iniquity more than speaking what is fair.

51:6  LA: Dilexisti omnia verba præcipitationis ; lingua dolosa.
      EN: You have loved every word of ruin, deceitful tongue.

51:7  LA: Propterea Deus destruet te in finem ; evellet te, et emigrabit te de tabernaculo tuo, et radicem tuam de terra viventium.
      EN: Therefore God will destroy you completely; He will uproot you and remove you from your tent, and your root from the land of the living.

51:8  LA: Videbunt justi, et timebunt ; et super eum ridebunt, et dicent :
      EN: The just will see and fear; they will laugh at him and say,

51:9  LA: Ecce homo qui non posuit Deum adjutorem suum ; sed speravit in multitudine divitiarum suarum, et prævaluit in vanitate sua.
      EN: “See the man who did not make God his helper, but hoped in the abundance of his riches and grew strong in his emptiness.”

51:10  LA: Ego autem, sicut oliva fructifera in domo Dei ; speravi in misericordia Dei, in æternum et in sæculum sæculi.
       EN: But I am like a fruitful olive tree in God's house; I have hoped in God's mercy forever and for ages of ages.

51:11  LA: Confitebor tibi in sæculum, quia fecisti ; et exspectabo nomen tuum, quoniam bonum est in conspectu sanctorum tuorum.]
       EN: I will praise You forever, for You have acted, and I will wait for Your name, for it is good in the sight of Your holy ones.

PSALMS 52

52:1  LA: In finem, pro Maëleth intelligentiæ David. [Dixit insipiens in corde suo : Non est Deus.
      EN: For the end, for Mahalath, for understanding, of David. The fool has said in his heart, “There is no God.” They are corrupted and have become abominable in their iniquities; no one does good.

52:2  LA: In finem, pro Maëleth intelligentiæ David. [Dixit insipiens in corde suo : Non est Deus.
      EN: For the end, for Mahalath, for understanding, of David. The fool has said in his heart, “There is no God.” They are corrupted and have become abominable in their iniquities; no one does good.

52:3  LA: Deus de cælo prospexit super filios hominum, ut videat si est intelligens, aut requirens Deum.
      EN: God looked down from heaven upon the children of men to see whether anyone understands or seeks God.

52:4  LA: Omnes declinaverunt ; simul inutiles facti sunt : non est qui faciat bonum, non est usque ad unum.
      EN: All have turned aside; together they have become useless. No one does good, not even one.

52:5  LA: Nonne scient omnes qui operantur iniquitatem, qui devorant plebem meam ut cibum panis ?
      EN: Will they not know, all who work iniquity, who devour my people as they eat bread?

52:6  LA: Deum non invocaverunt ; illic trepidaverunt timore, ubi non erat timor. Quoniam Deus dissipavit ossa eorum qui hominibus placent : confusi sunt, quoniam Deus sprevit eos.
      EN: They have not called upon God; there they trembled with fear where there was no fear. For God has scattered the bones of those who please men; they have been put to shame, for God has despised them.

52:7  LA: Quis dabit ex Sion salutare Israël ? cum converterit Deus captivitatem plebis suæ, exsultabit Jacob, et lætabitur Israël.]
      EN: Who will bring Israel's salvation from Zion? When God turns back His people's captivity, Jacob will rejoice and Israel will be glad.

PSALMS 53

53:1  LA: In finem, in carminibus. Intellectus David,
      EN: For the end, with songs. For understanding, of David,

53:2  LA: cum venissent Ziphæi, et dixissent ad Saul : Nonne David absconditus est apud nos ?
      EN: when the Ziphites came and said to Saul, “Is David not hidden among us?”

53:3  LA: [Deus, in nomine tuo salvum me fac, et in virtute tua judica me.
      EN: God, save me in Your name and judge me in Your strength.

53:4  LA: Deus, exaudi orationem meam ; auribus percipe verba oris mei.
      EN: God, hear my prayer; listen to the words of my mouth.

53:5  LA: Quoniam alieni insurrexerunt adversum me, et fortes quæsierunt animam meam, et non proposuerunt Deum ante conspectum suum.
      EN: For strangers have risen against me, and the mighty have sought my soul; they have not set God before their sight.

53:6  LA: Ecce enim Deus adjuvat me, et Dominus susceptor est animæ meæ.
      EN: For see, God helps me, and the Lord is the defender of my soul.

53:7  LA: Averte mala inimicis meis ; et in veritate tua disperde illos.
      EN: Turn evils back upon my enemies, and in Your truth destroy them.

53:8  LA: Voluntarie sacrificabo tibi, et confitebor nomini tuo, Domine, quoniam bonum est.
      EN: Willingly I will sacrifice to You and praise Your name, Lord, for it is good.

53:9  LA: Quoniam ex omni tribulatione eripuisti me, et super inimicos meos despexit oculus meus.]
      EN: For You have rescued me from every distress, and my eye has looked down upon my enemies.

PSALMS 54

54:1  LA: In finem, in carminibus. Intellectus David.
      EN: For the end, with songs. For understanding, of David.

54:2  LA: [Exaudi, Deus, orationem meam, et ne despexeris deprecationem meam :
      EN: Hear my prayer, God, and do not despise my plea;

54:3  LA: intende mihi, et exaudi me. Contristatus sum in exercitatione mea, et conturbatus sum
      EN: attend to me and hear me. I have become sorrowful in my exercise and have been troubled

54:4  LA: a voce inimici, et a tribulatione peccatoris. Quoniam declinaverunt in me iniquitates, et in ira molesti erant mihi.
      EN: by the enemy's voice and the sinner's affliction. For they have turned iniquities against me, and in anger they troubled me.

54:5  LA: Cor meum conturbatum est in me, et formido mortis cecidit super me.
      EN: My heart is troubled within me, and the terror of death has fallen upon me.

54:6  LA: Timor et tremor venerunt super me, et contexerunt me tenebræ.
      EN: Fear and trembling have come upon me, and darkness has covered me.

54:7  LA: Et dixi : Quis dabit mihi pennas sicut columbæ, et volabo, et requiescam ?
      EN: I said, “Who will give me wings like a dove, so that I may fly and rest?”

54:8  LA: Ecce elongavi fugiens, et mansi in solitudine.
      EN: See, I fled far away and remained in the wilderness.

54:9  LA: Exspectabam eum qui salvum me fecit a pusillanimitate spiritus, et tempestate.
      EN: I waited for Him who saved me from faintness of spirit and from the storm.

54:10  LA: Præcipita, Domine ; divide linguas eorum : quoniam vidi iniquitatem et contradictionem in civitate.
       EN: Cast them down, Lord; divide their tongues, for I have seen iniquity and strife in the city.

54:11  LA: Die ac nocte circumdabit eam super muros ejus iniquitas ; et labor in medio ejus,
       EN: Day and night iniquity will surround it upon its walls, and toil is in its midst,

54:12  LA: et injustitia : et non defecit de plateis ejus usura et dolus.
       EN: and injustice; usury and deceit have not departed from its streets.

54:13  LA: Quoniam si inimicus meus maledixisset mihi, sustinuissem utique. Et si is qui oderat me super me magna locutus fuisset, abscondissem me forsitan ab eo.
       EN: For if my enemy had cursed me, I would certainly have endured it; if one who hated me had boasted against me, I might have hidden from him.

54:14  LA: Tu vero homo unanimis, dux meus, et notus meus :
       EN: But you, a man of one mind with me, my guide and my acquaintance,

54:15  LA: qui simul mecum dulces capiebas cibos ; in domo Dei ambulavimus cum consensu.
       EN: who took sweet food together with me; we walked in God's house in harmony.

54:16  LA: Veniat mors super illos, et descendant in infernum viventes : quoniam nequitiæ in habitaculis eorum, in medio eorum.
       EN: Let death come upon them, and let them descend alive into the underworld, for wickedness is in their dwellings, in their midst.

54:17  LA: Ego autem ad Deum clamavi, et Dominus salvabit me.
       EN: But I have cried to God, and the Lord will save me.

54:18  LA: Vespere, et mane, et meridie, narrabo, et annuntiabo ; et exaudiet vocem meam.
       EN: In the evening, in the morning, and at midday I will recount and proclaim, and He will hear my voice.

54:19  LA: Redimet in pace animam meam ab his qui appropinquant mihi : quoniam inter multos erant mecum.
       EN: He will redeem my soul in peace from those who approach me, for among many they were with me.

54:20  LA: Exaudiet Deus, et humiliabit illos, qui est ante sæcula. Non enim est illis commutatio, et non timuerunt Deum.
       EN: God, who is before the ages, will hear and humble them, for there is no change in them, and they have not feared God.

54:21  LA: Extendit manum suam in retribuendo ; contaminaverunt testamentum ejus :
       EN: He has stretched out His hand in repayment; they have defiled His covenant.

54:22  LA: divisi sunt ab ira vultus ejus, et appropinquavit cor illius. Molliti sunt sermones ejus super oleum ; et ipsi sunt jacula.
       EN: They have been divided by the anger of His face, and his heart has drawn near. His words have become softer than oil, yet they are javelins.

54:23  LA: Jacta super Dominum curam tuam, et ipse te enutriet ; non dabit in æternum fluctuationem justo.
       EN: Cast your care upon the Lord, and He will nourish you; He will never allow the just man to waver.

54:24  LA: Tu vero, Deus, deduces eos in puteum interitus. Viri sanguinum et dolosi non dimidiabunt dies suos ; ego autem sperabo in te, Domine.]
       EN: But You, God, will lead them down into the pit of destruction. Bloodthirsty and deceitful men will not live out half their days, but I will hope in You, Lord.

PSALMS 55

55:1  LA: In finem, pro populo qui a sanctis longe factus est. David in tituli inscriptionem, cum tenuerunt eum Allophyli in Geth.
      EN: For the end, for the people who have been taken far from holy things. For David, as an inscription for a title, when the Philistines held him in Gath.

55:2  LA: [Miserere mei, Deus, quoniam conculcavit me homo ; tota die impugnans, tribulavit me.
      EN: Have mercy on me, God, for man has trampled me; fighting all day, he has afflicted me.

55:3  LA: Conculcaverunt me inimici mei tota die, quoniam multi bellantes adversum me.
      EN: My enemies have trampled me all day, for many fight against me.

55:4  LA: Ab altitudine diei timebo : ego vero in te sperabo.
      EN: At the height of the day I will fear, but I will hope in You.

55:5  LA: In Deo laudabo sermones meos ; in Deo speravi : non timebo quid faciat mihi caro.
      EN: In God I will praise my words; in God I have hoped. I will not fear what flesh may do to me.

55:6  LA: Tota die verba mea execrabantur ; adversum me omnes cogitationes eorum in malum.
      EN: All day they cursed my words; all their thoughts against me are for evil.

55:7  LA: Inhabitabunt, et abscondent ; ipsi calcaneum meum observabunt. Sicut sustinuerunt animam meam,
      EN: They will dwell and hide; they themselves will watch my heel. As they have waited for my soul,

55:8  LA: pro nihilo salvos facies illos ; in ira populos confringes.
      EN: You will save them for nothing; in anger You will crush peoples.

55:9  LA: Deus, vitam meam annuntiavi tibi ; posuisti lacrimas meas in conspectu tuo, sicut et in promissione tua :
      EN: God, I have told You my life; You have placed my tears before Your sight, as also in Your promise.

55:10  LA: tunc convertentur inimici mei retrorsum. In quacumque die invocavero te, ecce cognovi quoniam Deus meus es.
       EN: Then my enemies will turn back. On whatever day I call upon You, see, I have known that You are my God.

55:11  LA: In Deo laudabo verbum ; in Domino laudabo sermonem. In Deo speravi : non timebo quid faciat mihi homo.
       EN: In God I will praise the word; in the Lord I will praise the saying. In God I have hoped; I will not fear what man may do to me.

55:12  LA: In Deo laudabo verbum ; in Domino laudabo sermonem. In Deo speravi : non timebo quid faciat mihi homo.
       EN: In God I will praise the word; in the Lord I will praise the saying. In God I have hoped; I will not fear what man may do to me.

55:13  LA: In me sunt, Deus, vota tua, quæ reddam, laudationes tibi :
       EN: The vows to You that I will fulfill are within me, God, praises to You,

55:14  LA: quoniam eripuisti animam meam de morte, et pedes meos de lapsu, ut placeam coram Deo in lumine viventium.]
       EN: for You have rescued my soul from death and my feet from falling, so that I may be pleasing before God in the light of the living.

PSALMS 56

56:1  LA: In finem, ne disperdas. David in tituli inscriptionem, cum fugeret a facie Saul in speluncam.
      EN: For the end, do not destroy. For David, as an inscription for a title, when he fled from Saul into the cave.

56:2  LA: [Miserere mei, Deus, miserere mei, quoniam in te confidit anima mea. Et in umbra alarum tuarum sperabo, donec transeat iniquitas.
      EN: Have mercy on me, God, have mercy on me, for my soul trusts in You. In the shadow of Your wings I will hope until iniquity passes.

56:3  LA: Clamabo ad Deum altissimum, Deum qui benefecit mihi.
      EN: I will cry to God Most High, to God who has done good to me.

56:4  LA: Misit de cælo, et liberavit me ; dedit in opprobrium conculcantes me. Misit Deus misericordiam suam et veritatem suam,
      EN: He sent from heaven and delivered me; He gave those who trampled me over to reproach. God sent His mercy and His truth,

56:5  LA: et eripuit animam meam de medio catulorum leonum. Dormivi conturbatus. Filii hominum dentes eorum arma et sagittæ, et lingua eorum gladius acutus.
      EN: and rescued my soul from among young lions. I slept in turmoil. The children of men—their teeth are weapons and arrows, and their tongue a sharp sword.

56:6  LA: Exaltare super cælos, Deus, et in omnem terram gloria tua.
      EN: Be exalted above the heavens, God, and let Your glory be throughout all the earth.

56:7  LA: Laqueum paraverunt pedibus meis, et incurvaverunt animam meam. Foderunt ante faciem meam foveam, et inciderunt in eam.
      EN: They prepared a snare for my feet and bowed down my soul. They dug a pit before me and fell into it.

56:8  LA: Paratum cor meum, Deus, paratum cor meum ; cantabo, et psalmum dicam.
      EN: My heart is ready, God, my heart is ready; I will sing and chant a psalm.

56:9  LA: Exsurge, gloria mea ; exsurge, psalterium et cithara : exsurgam diluculo.
      EN: Arise, my glory; arise, harp and lyre. I will arise at daybreak.

56:10  LA: Confitebor tibi in populis, Domine, et psalmum dicam tibi in gentibus :
       EN: I will praise You among the peoples, Lord, and sing a psalm to You among the nations,

56:11  LA: quoniam magnificata est usque ad cælos misericordia tua, et usque ad nubes veritas tua.
       EN: for Your mercy has been magnified to the heavens and Your truth to the clouds.

56:12  LA: Exaltare super cælos, Deus, et super omnem terram gloria tua.]
       EN: Be exalted above the heavens, God, and let Your glory be above all the earth.

PSALMS 57

57:1  LA: In finem, ne disperdas. David in tituli inscriptionem.
      EN: For the end, do not destroy. For David, as an inscription for a title.

57:2  LA: [Si vere utique justitiam loquimini, recta judicate, filii hominum.
      EN: If you truly speak righteousness, judge uprightly, children of men.

57:3  LA: Etenim in corde iniquitates operamini ; in terra injustitias manus vestræ concinnant.
      EN: For in your heart you practice iniquities; upon the earth your hands weave injustices.

57:4  LA: Alienati sunt peccatores a vulva ; erraverunt ab utero : locuti sunt falsa.
      EN: Sinners have been estranged from birth; they have strayed from the womb and spoken falsehoods.

57:5  LA: Furor illis secundum similitudinem serpentis, sicut aspidis surdæ et obturantis aures suas,
      EN: Their fury is like that of a serpent, like a deaf asp that closes its ears,

57:6  LA: quæ non exaudiet vocem incantantium, et venefici incantantis sapienter.
      EN: which will not hear the voice of charmers or of the sorcerer who charms skillfully.

57:7  LA: Deus conteret dentes eorum in ore ipsorum ; molas leonum confringet Dominus.
      EN: God will crush their teeth within their mouths; the Lord will break the lions' grinders.

57:8  LA: Ad nihilum devenient tamquam aqua decurrens ; intendit arcum suum donec infirmentur.
      EN: They will come to nothing like running water; He has drawn His bow until they grow weak.

57:9  LA: Sicut cera quæ fluit auferentur ; supercecidit ignis, et non viderunt solem.
      EN: They will be swept away like wax that melts; fire has fallen upon them, and they have not seen the sun.

57:10  LA: Priusquam intelligerent spinæ vestræ rhamnum, sicut viventes sic in ira absorbet eos.
       EN: Before your thorns could recognize the buckthorn, He will swallow them alive in anger.

57:11  LA: Lætabitur justus cum viderit vindictam ; manus suas lavabit in sanguine peccatoris.
       EN: The just man will be glad when he sees vengeance; he will wash his hands in the sinner's blood.

57:12  LA: Et dicet homo : Si utique est fructus justo, utique est Deus judicans eos in terra.]
       EN: A person will say, “If the just do bear fruit, then surely there is a God who judges them on the earth.”

PSALMS 58

58:1  LA: In finem, ne disperdas. David in tituli inscriptionem, quando misit Saul et custodivit domum ejus ut eum interficeret.
      EN: For the end, do not destroy. For David, as an inscription for a title, when Saul sent men and kept watch over his house to kill him.

58:2  LA: [Eripe me de inimicis meis, Deus meus, et ab insurgentibus in me libera me.
      EN: Rescue me from my enemies, my God, and deliver me from those who rise against me.

58:3  LA: Eripe me de operantibus iniquitatem, et de viris sanguinum salva me.
      EN: Rescue me from those who work iniquity and save me from bloodthirsty men.

58:4  LA: Quia ecce ceperunt animam meam ; irruerunt in me fortes.
      EN: For see, they have seized my soul; the mighty have rushed against me. It is neither my iniquity nor my sin, Lord; without iniquity I have run and kept straight.

58:5  LA: Quia ecce ceperunt animam meam ; irruerunt in me fortes.
      EN: For see, they have seized my soul; the mighty have rushed against me. It is neither my iniquity nor my sin, Lord; without iniquity I have run and kept straight.

58:6  LA: Exsurge in occursum meum, et vide : et tu, Domine Deus virtutum, Deus Israël, intende ad visitandas omnes gentes : non miserearis omnibus qui operantur iniquitatem.
      EN: Arise to meet me and see; You, Lord God of hosts, God of Israel, attend to visiting all nations. Have no mercy on all who work iniquity.

58:7  LA: Convertentur ad vesperam, et famem patientur ut canes : et circuibunt civitatem.
      EN: They will return at evening and go hungry like dogs, and they will go around the city.

58:8  LA: Ecce loquentur in ore suo, et gladius in labiis eorum : quoniam quis audivit ?
      EN: See, they will speak with their mouth, and a sword is on their lips, for who has heard?

58:9  LA: Et tu, Domine, deridebis eos ; ad nihilum deduces omnes gentes.
      EN: But You, Lord, will laugh at them; You will bring all nations to nothing.

58:10  LA: Fortitudinem meam ad te custodiam, quia, Deus, susceptor meus es :
       EN: I will keep my strength for You, for You, God, are my defender.

58:11  LA: Deus meus misericordia ejus præveniet me.
       EN: My God—His mercy will meet me.

58:12  LA: Deus ostendet mihi super inimicos meos : ne occidas eos, nequando obliviscantur populi mei. Disperge illos in virtute tua, et depone eos, protector meus, Domine :
       EN: God will show me over my enemies. Do not kill them, lest my peoples forget. Scatter them in Your strength and bring them down, my protector, Lord:

58:13  LA: delictum oris eorum, sermonem labiorum ipsorum ; et comprehendantur in superbia sua. Et de execratione et mendacio annuntiabuntur
       EN: the offense of their mouth, the speech of their lips. Let them be caught in their pride, and for their cursing and lying they will be proclaimed

58:14  LA: in consummatione : in ira consummationis, et non erunt. Et scient quia Deus dominabitur Jacob, et finium terræ.
       EN: in their destruction, in the anger of destruction, and they will be no more. They will know that God rules Jacob and the ends of the earth.

58:15  LA: Convertentur ad vesperam, et famem patientur ut canes : et circuibunt civitatem.
       EN: They will return at evening and go hungry like dogs, and they will go around the city.

58:16  LA: Ipsi dispergentur ad manducandum ; si vero non fuerint saturati, et murmurabunt.
       EN: They will scatter to eat, and if they are not satisfied, they will grumble.

58:17  LA: Ego autem cantabo fortitudinem tuam, et exsultabo mane misericordiam tuam : quia factus es susceptor meus, et refugium meum in die tribulationis meæ.
       EN: But I will sing of Your strength and rejoice in Your mercy in the morning, for You have become my defender and my refuge on the day of my distress.

58:18  LA: Adjutor meus, tibi psallam, quia Deus susceptor meus es ; Deus meus, misericordia mea.]
       EN: My helper, to You I will sing, for You, God, are my defender, my God, my mercy.

PSALMS 59

59:1  LA: In finem, pro his qui immutabuntur, in tituli inscriptionem ipsi David, in doctrinam,
      EN: For the end, for those who will be changed, as an inscription for a title for David himself, for teaching,

59:2  LA: cum succendit Mesopotamiam Syriæ et Sobal, et convertit Joab, et percussit Idumæam in valle Salinarum duodecim millia.
      EN: when he set fire to Syrian Mesopotamia and Zobah, and Joab turned back and struck twelve thousand of Edom in the Valley of Salt.

59:3  LA: [Deus, repulisti nos, et destruxisti nos ; iratus es, et misertus es nobis.
      EN: God, You have rejected us and destroyed us; You have been angry and have had mercy on us.

59:4  LA: Commovisti terram, et conturbasti eam ; sana contritiones ejus, quia commota est.
      EN: You have shaken the earth and troubled it; heal its fractures, for it has been shaken.

59:5  LA: Ostendisti populo tuo dura ; potasti nos vino compunctionis.
      EN: You have shown Your people hard things; You have given us the wine of piercing sorrow to drink.

59:6  LA: Dedisti metuentibus te significationem, ut fugiant a facie arcus ; ut liberentur dilecti tui.
      EN: You have given a sign to those who fear You, so that they may flee from the bow, so that Your beloved may be delivered. Save with Your right hand and hear me.

59:7  LA: Dedisti metuentibus te significationem, ut fugiant a facie arcus ; ut liberentur dilecti tui.
      EN: You have given a sign to those who fear You, so that they may flee from the bow, so that Your beloved may be delivered. Save with Your right hand and hear me.

59:8  LA: Deus locutus est in sancto suo : lætabor, et partibor Sichimam ; et convallem tabernaculorum metibor.
      EN: God has spoken in His holy place: “I will be glad and divide Shechem, and I will measure the Valley of Tents.

59:9  LA: Meus est Galaad, et meus est Manasses ; et Ephraim fortitudo capitis mei. Juda rex meus ;
      EN: Gilead is Mine, and Manasseh is Mine; Ephraim is the strength of My head. Judah is My king;

59:10  LA: Moab olla spei meæ. In Idumæam extendam calceamentum meum : mihi alienigenæ subditi sunt.
       EN: Moab is the pot of My hope. Over Edom I will stretch My sandal; foreigners have been subjected to Me.”

59:11  LA: Quis deducet me in civitatem munitam ? quis deducet me usque in Idumæam ?
       EN: Who will lead me into the fortified city? Who will lead me as far as Edom?

59:12  LA: nonne tu, Deus, qui repulisti nos ? et non egredieris, Deus, in virtutibus nostris ?
       EN: Will it not be You, God, who have rejected us? And will You not go out, God, with our armies?

59:13  LA: Da nobis auxilium de tribulatione, quia vana salus hominis.
       EN: Give us help out of distress, for human salvation is vain.

59:14  LA: In Deo faciemus virtutem ; et ipse ad nihilum deducet tribulantes nos.]
       EN: In God we will act with strength, and He will bring to nothing those who afflict us.

PSALMS 60

60:1  LA: In finem. In hymnis David.
      EN: For the end. Among the hymns of David.

60:2  LA: [Exaudi, Deus, deprecationem meam ; intende orationi meæ.
      EN: Hear my plea, God; attend to my prayer.

60:3  LA: A finibus terræ ad te clamavi, dum anxiaretur cor meum ; in petra exaltasti me. Deduxisti me,
      EN: From the ends of the earth I cried to You when my heart was anxious; You lifted me onto a rock. You guided me,

60:4  LA: quia factus es spes mea : turris fortitudinis a facie inimici.
      EN: for You became my hope, a tower of strength against the enemy.

60:5  LA: Inhabitabo in tabernaculo tuo in sæcula ; protegar in velamento alarum tuarum.
      EN: I will dwell in Your tent through the ages; I will be protected beneath the covering of Your wings.

60:6  LA: Quoniam tu, Deus meus, exaudisti orationem meam ; dedisti hæreditatem timentibus nomen tuum.
      EN: For You, my God, have heard my prayer; You have given an inheritance to those who fear Your name.

60:7  LA: Dies super dies regis adjicies ; annos ejus usque in diem generationis et generationis.
      EN: You will add days to the king's days, his years as far as the day of generation upon generation.

60:8  LA: Permanet in æternum in conspectu Dei : misericordiam et veritatem ejus quis requiret ?
      EN: He remains forever in the sight of God; who will seek His mercy and truth?

60:9  LA: Sic psalmum dicam nomini tuo in sæculum sæculi, ut reddam vota mea de die in diem.]
      EN: Thus I will sing a psalm to Your name for ages of ages, to fulfill my vows day by day.

PSALMS 61

61:1  LA: In finem, pro Idithun. Psalmus David.
      EN: For the end, for Jeduthun. A psalm of David.

61:2  LA: [Nonne Deo subjecta erit anima mea ? ab ipso enim salutare meum.
      EN: Will my soul not be subject to God? For my salvation comes from Him.

61:3  LA: Nam et ipse Deus meus et salutaris meus ; susceptor meus, non movebor amplius.
      EN: For He is my God and my savior, my defender; I will not be shaken anymore.

61:4  LA: Quousque irruitis in hominem ? interficitis universi vos, tamquam parieti inclinato et maceriæ depulsæ.
      EN: How long do you rush against a man? All of you are killing him, as though attacking a leaning wall and a broken-down fence.

61:5  LA: Verumtamen pretium meum cogitaverunt repellere ; cucurri in siti : ore suo benedicebant, et corde suo maledicebant.
      EN: Yet they planned to reject my worth; I ran in thirst. With their mouths they blessed, and in their hearts they cursed.

61:6  LA: Verumtamen Deo subjecta esto, anima mea, quoniam ab ipso patientia mea :
      EN: Yet be subject to God, my soul, for my endurance comes from Him,

61:7  LA: quia ipse Deus meus et salvator meus, adjutor meus, non emigrabo.
      EN: for He is my God and my savior, my helper; I will not be displaced.

61:8  LA: In Deo salutare meum et gloria mea ; Deus auxilii mei, et spes mea in Deo est.
      EN: In God are my salvation and my glory; He is the God of my help, and my hope is in God.

61:9  LA: Sperate in eo, omnis congregatio populi ; effundite coram illo corda vestra : Deus adjutor noster in æternum.
      EN: Hope in Him, all the assembly of the people; pour out your hearts before Him. God is our helper forever.

61:10  LA: Verumtamen vani filii hominum, mendaces filii hominum in stateris, ut decipiant ipsi de vanitate in idipsum.
       EN: Yet the children of men are empty, the children of men are liars in the balances, so that together they may deceive with emptiness.

61:11  LA: Nolite sperare in iniquitate, et rapinas nolite concupiscere ; divitiæ si affluant, nolite cor apponere.
       EN: Do not hope in iniquity or desire plunder; if riches flow in, do not set your heart upon them.

61:12  LA: Semel locutus est Deus ; duo hæc audivi : quia potestas Dei est,
       EN: Once God has spoken; these two things I have heard: that power belongs to God, and mercy to You, Lord, for You will repay each one according to his works.

61:13  LA: Semel locutus est Deus ; duo hæc audivi : quia potestas Dei est,
       EN: Once God has spoken; these two things I have heard: that power belongs to God, and mercy to You, Lord, for You will repay each one according to his works.

PSALMS 62

62:1  LA: Psalmus David, cum esset in deserto Idumææ.
      EN: A psalm of David, when he was in the wilderness of Edom.

62:2  LA: [Deus, Deus meus, ad te de luce vigilo. Sitivit in te anima mea ; quam multipliciter tibi caro mea !
      EN: God, my God, for You I keep watch from dawn. My soul has thirsted for You; in how many ways my flesh longs for You!

62:3  LA: In terra deserta, et invia, et inaquosa, sic in sancto apparui tibi, ut viderem virtutem tuam et gloriam tuam.
      EN: In a deserted, pathless, waterless land, thus I appeared to You in the holy place, to see Your strength and Your glory.

62:4  LA: Quoniam melior est misericordia tua super vitas, labia mea laudabunt te.
      EN: For Your mercy is better than lives; my lips will praise You.

62:5  LA: Sic benedicam te in vita mea, et in nomine tuo levabo manus meas.
      EN: Thus I will bless You in my life, and in Your name I will lift my hands.

62:6  LA: Sicut adipe et pinguedine repleatur anima mea, et labiis exsultationis laudabit os meum.
      EN: May my soul be filled as with fat and rich food, and with rejoicing lips my mouth will praise You.

62:7  LA: Si memor fui tui super stratum meum, in matutinis meditabor in te.
      EN: If I have remembered You upon my bed, at dawn I will meditate upon You,

62:8  LA: Quia fuisti adjutor meus, et in velamento alarum tuarum exsultabo.
      EN: for You have been my helper, and beneath the covering of Your wings I will rejoice.

62:9  LA: Adhæsit anima mea post te ; me suscepit dextera tua.
      EN: My soul has clung close behind You; Your right hand has sustained me.

62:10  LA: Ipsi vero in vanum quæsierunt animam meam : introibunt in inferiora terræ ;
       EN: But they have sought my soul in vain; they will enter the lower parts of the earth.

62:11  LA: tradentur in manus gladii : partes vulpium erunt.
       EN: They will be handed over to the sword's power; they will be portions for foxes.

62:12  LA: Rex vero lætabitur in Deo ; laudabuntur omnes qui jurant in eo : quia obstructum est os loquentium iniqua.]
       EN: But the king will be glad in God; all who swear by Him will be praised, for the mouth of those who speak unjust things has been stopped.

PSALMS 63

63:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David.
      EN: For the end. A psalm of David.

63:2  LA: [Exaudi, Deus, orationem meam cum deprecor ; a timore inimici eripe animam meam.
      EN: Hear my prayer, God, when I plead; rescue my soul from fear of the enemy.

63:3  LA: Protexisti me a conventu malignantium, a multitudine operantium iniquitatem.
      EN: You have protected me from the assembly of evildoers, from the multitude of those who work iniquity.

63:4  LA: Quia exacuerunt ut gladium linguas suas ; intenderunt arcum rem amaram,
      EN: For they have sharpened their tongues like a sword; they have drawn the bow, a bitter thing,

63:5  LA: ut sagittent in occultis immaculatum.
      EN: to shoot the unstained man in hidden places. Suddenly they will shoot him and will not fear; they have confirmed an evil word among themselves. They have talked of hiding snares; they have said, “Who will see them?”

63:6  LA: ut sagittent in occultis immaculatum.
      EN: to shoot the unstained man in hidden places. Suddenly they will shoot him and will not fear; they have confirmed an evil word among themselves. They have talked of hiding snares; they have said, “Who will see them?”

63:7  LA: Scrutati sunt iniquitates ; defecerunt scrutantes scrutinio. Accedet homo ad cor altum,
      EN: They have searched out iniquities; searching deeply, they have failed. A man will approach a deep heart,

63:8  LA: et exaltabitur Deus. Sagittæ parvulorum factæ sunt plagæ eorum,
      EN: and God will be exalted. Their blows have become the arrows of little children,

63:9  LA: et infirmatæ sunt contra eos linguæ eorum. Conturbati sunt omnes qui videbant eos,
      EN: and their tongues have weakened against them. All who saw them were troubled,

63:10  LA: et timuit omnis homo. Et annuntiaverunt opera Dei, et facta ejus intellexerunt.
       EN: and every person feared. They proclaimed God's works and understood His deeds.

63:11  LA: Lætabitur justus in Domino, et sperabit in eo, et laudabuntur omnes recti corde.]
       EN: The just man will be glad in the Lord and hope in Him, and all the upright of heart will be praised.

PSALMS 64

64:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David, canticum Jeremiæ et Ezechielis populo transmigrationis, cum inciperent exire.
      EN: For the end. A psalm of David, a song of Jeremiah and Ezekiel for the people of the exile, when they were beginning to depart.

64:2  LA: [Te decet hymnus, Deus, in Sion, et tibi reddetur votum in Jerusalem.
      EN: A hymn befits You, God, in Zion, and to You a vow will be fulfilled in Jerusalem.

64:3  LA: Exaudi orationem meam ; ad te omnis caro veniet.
      EN: Hear my prayer; all flesh will come to You.

64:4  LA: Verba iniquorum prævaluerunt super nos, et impietatibus nostris tu propitiaberis.
      EN: The words of the unjust have prevailed against us, and You will forgive our ungodly deeds.

64:5  LA: Beatus quem elegisti et assumpsisti : inhabitabit in atriis tuis. Replebimur in bonis domus tuæ ; sanctum est templum tuum,
      EN: Blessed is the one You have chosen and taken to Yourself; he will dwell in Your courts. We will be filled with the good things of Your house; Your temple is holy,

64:6  LA: mirabile in æquitate. Exaudi nos, Deus, salutaris noster, spes omnium finium terræ, et in mari longe.
      EN: wonderful in equity. Hear us, God our savior, the hope of all the ends of the earth and of those far away at sea,

64:7  LA: Præparans montes in virtute tua, accinctus potentia ;
      EN: You who establish the mountains in Your strength, girded with power,

64:8  LA: qui conturbas profundum maris, sonum fluctuum ejus. Turbabuntur gentes,
      EN: who trouble the depth of the sea, the sound of its waves. The nations will be troubled,

64:9  LA: et timebunt qui habitant terminos a signis tuis ; exitus matutini et vespere delectabis.
      EN: and those who inhabit the farthest reaches will fear because of Your signs; You will gladden the outgoings of morning and evening.

64:10  LA: Visitasti terram, et inebriasti eam ; multiplicasti locupletare eam. Flumen Dei repletum est aquis ; parasti cibum illorum : quoniam ita est præparatio ejus.
       EN: You have visited the earth and drenched it; You have greatly enriched it. God's river is full of water; You have prepared their food, for thus is its preparation.

64:11  LA: Rivos ejus inebria ; multiplica genimina ejus : in stillicidiis ejus lætabitur germinans.
       EN: Drench its channels and multiply its growth; it will be glad as it sprouts beneath its showers.

64:12  LA: Benedices coronæ anni benignitatis tuæ, et campi tui replebuntur ubertate.
       EN: You will bless the crown of the year of Your kindness, and Your fields will be filled with richness.

64:13  LA: Pinguescent speciosa deserti, et exsultatione colles accingentur.
       EN: The beautiful places of the wilderness will grow rich, and the hills will be girded with rejoicing.

64:14  LA: Induti sunt arietes ovium, et valles abundabunt frumento ; clamabunt, etenim hymnum dicent.]
       EN: The rams of the flocks have been clothed, and the valleys will abound in grain; they will shout and indeed sing a hymn.

PSALMS 65

65:1  LA: In finem. Canticum psalmi resurrectionis. [Jubilate Deo, omnis terra ;
      EN: For the end. A song of a psalm of resurrection. Shout to God, all the earth;

65:2  LA: psalmum dicite nomini ejus ; date gloriam laudi ejus.
      EN: sing a psalm to His name; give glory to His praise.

65:3  LA: Dicite Deo : Quam terribilia sunt opera tua, Domine ! in multitudine virtutis tuæ mentientur tibi inimici tui.
      EN: Say to God, “How fearsome are Your works, Lord! In the greatness of Your strength Your enemies will lie to You.

65:4  LA: Omnis terra adoret te, et psallat tibi ; psalmum dicat nomini tuo.
      EN: Let all the earth worship You and sing to You; let it sing a psalm to Your name.”

65:5  LA: Venite, et videte opera Dei : terribilis in consiliis super filios hominum.
      EN: Come and see God's works; He is fearsome in His counsels concerning the children of men.

65:6  LA: Qui convertit mare in aridam ; in flumine pertransibunt pede : ibi lætabimur in ipso.
      EN: He turned the sea into dry land; through the river they will pass on foot. There we will be glad in Him,

65:7  LA: Qui dominatur in virtute sua in æternum ; oculi ejus super gentes respiciunt : qui exasperant non exaltentur in semetipsis.
      EN: who rules in His strength forever. His eyes look upon the nations; let those who provoke Him not exalt themselves.

65:8  LA: Benedicite, gentes, Deum nostrum, et auditam facite vocem laudis ejus :
      EN: Bless our God, you nations, and make the sound of His praise heard,

65:9  LA: qui posuit animam meam ad vitam, et non dedit in commotionem pedes meos.
      EN: He who has set my soul in life and has not allowed my feet to be shaken.

65:10  LA: Quoniam probasti nos, Deus ; igne nos examinasti, sicut examinatur argentum.
       EN: For You have tested us, God; You have examined us by fire as silver is examined.

65:11  LA: Induxisti nos in laqueum ; posuisti tribulationes in dorso nostro ;
       EN: You brought us into a snare; You laid distresses upon our backs.

65:12  LA: imposuisti homines super capita nostra. Transivimus per ignem et aquam, et eduxisti nos in refrigerium.
       EN: You placed men over our heads. We passed through fire and water, and You led us out into refreshment.

65:13  LA: Introibo in domum tuam in holocaustis ; reddam tibi vota mea
       EN: I will enter Your house with burnt offerings; I will fulfill to You my vows,

65:14  LA: quæ distinxerunt labia mea : et locutum est os meum in tribulatione mea.
       EN: which my lips distinctly uttered and my mouth spoke in my distress.

65:15  LA: Holocausta medullata offeram tibi, cum incenso arietum ; offeram tibi boves cum hircis.
       EN: I will offer You rich burnt offerings with the smoke of rams; I will offer You cattle with goats.

65:16  LA: Venite, audite, et narrabo, omnes qui timetis Deum, quanta fecit animæ meæ.
       EN: Come, listen, all who fear God, and I will recount how much He has done for my soul.

65:17  LA: Ad ipsum ore meo clamavi, et exaltavi sub lingua mea.
       EN: To Him I cried with my mouth, and I exalted Him beneath my tongue.

65:18  LA: Iniquitatem si aspexi in corde meo, non exaudiet Dominus.
       EN: If I have looked upon iniquity in my heart, the Lord will not hear.

65:19  LA: Propterea exaudivit Deus, et attendit voci deprecationis meæ.
       EN: Therefore God has heard and attended to the voice of my plea.

65:20  LA: Benedictus Deus, qui non amovit orationem meam, et misericordiam suam a me.]
       EN: Blessed is God, who has not removed my prayer or His mercy from me.

PSALMS 66

66:1  LA: In finem, in hymnis. Psalmus cantici David.
      EN: For the end, among hymns. A psalm of song, of David.

66:2  LA: [Deus misereatur nostri, et benedicat nobis ; illuminet vultum suum super nos, et misereatur nostri :
      EN: May God have mercy on us and bless us; may He make His face shine upon us and have mercy on us,

66:3  LA: ut cognoscamus in terra viam tuam, in omnibus gentibus salutare tuum.
      EN: so that we may know Your way upon the earth, Your salvation among all nations.

66:4  LA: Confiteantur tibi populi, Deus : confiteantur tibi populi omnes.
      EN: Let the peoples praise You, God; let all the peoples praise You.

66:5  LA: Lætentur et exsultent gentes, quoniam judicas populos in æquitate, et gentes in terra dirigis.
      EN: Let the nations be glad and rejoice, for You judge the peoples with equity and guide the nations upon the earth.

66:6  LA: Confiteantur tibi populi, Deus : confiteantur tibi populi omnes.
      EN: Let the peoples praise You, God; let all the peoples praise You.

66:7  LA: Terra dedit fructum suum : benedicat nos Deus, Deus noster !
      EN: The earth has yielded its fruit; may God, our God, bless us!

66:8  LA: Benedicat nos Deus, et metuant eum omnes fines terræ.]
      EN: May God bless us, and may all the ends of the earth fear Him.

PSALMS 67

67:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus cantici ipsi David.
      EN: For the end. A psalm of song for David himself.

67:2  LA: [Exsurgat Deus, et dissipentur inimici ejus ; et fugiant qui oderunt eum a facie ejus.
      EN: Let God arise, and let His enemies be scattered; let those who hate Him flee from His presence.

67:3  LA: Sicut deficit fumus, deficiant ; sicut fluit cera a facie ignis, sic pereant peccatores a facie Dei.
      EN: As smoke vanishes, let them vanish; as wax melts before fire, so let sinners perish before God.

67:4  LA: Et justi epulentur, et exsultent in conspectu Dei, et delectentur in lætitia.
      EN: Let the just feast and rejoice in God's sight and delight in gladness.

67:5  LA: Cantate Deo ; psalmum dicite nomini ejus : iter facite ei qui ascendit super occasum. Dominus nomen illi ; exsultate in conspectu ejus. Turbabuntur a facie ejus,
      EN: Sing to God; sing a psalm to His name. Make a way for Him who ascends above the sunset. The Lord is His name; rejoice before Him. They will be troubled before Him,

67:6  LA: patris orphanorum, et judicis viduarum ; Deus in loco sancto suo.
      EN: the father of orphans and the judge of widows, God in His holy place.

67:7  LA: Deus qui inhabitare facit unius moris in domo ; qui educit vinctos in fortitudine, similiter eos qui exasperant, qui habitant in sepulchris.
      EN: God makes those of one disposition dwell in a house; He leads prisoners out with strength, likewise those who provoke Him, who dwell in tombs.

67:8  LA: Deus, cum egredereris in conspectu populi tui, cum pertransires in deserto,
      EN: God, when You went out before Your people, when You passed through the wilderness,

67:9  LA: terra mota est, etenim cæli distillaverunt, a facie Dei Sinai, a facie Dei Israël.
      EN: the earth shook, and indeed the heavens dripped before the God of Sinai, before the God of Israel.

67:10  LA: Pluviam voluntariam segregabis, Deus, hæreditati tuæ ; et infirmata est, tu vero perfecisti eam.
       EN: You will set apart a willing rain, God, for Your inheritance. It grew weak, but You made it firm.

67:11  LA: Animalia tua habitabunt in ea ; parasti in dulcedine tua pauperi, Deus.
       EN: Your creatures will dwell in it; in Your sweetness You have made provision for the poor, God.

67:12  LA: Dominus dabit verbum evangelizantibus, virtute multa.
       EN: The Lord will give a word to those who proclaim good news, with great strength.

67:13  LA: Rex virtutum dilecti, dilecti ; et speciei domus dividere spolia.
       EN: The king of the hosts of the beloved, the beloved; and for the beauty of the house, the dividing of spoils.

67:14  LA: Si dormiatis inter medios cleros, pennæ columbæ deargentatæ, et posteriora dorsi ejus in pallore auri.
       EN: If you sleep between the allotted portions, the dove's wings are silvered, and the back of her body is in the pallor of gold.

67:15  LA: Dum discernit cælestis reges super eam, nive dealbabuntur in Selmon.
       EN: When the heavenly One distinguishes kings over it, they will be whitened with snow in Zalmon.

67:16  LA: Mons Dei, mons pinguis : mons coagulatus, mons pinguis.
       EN: A mountain of God, a rich mountain; a curdled mountain, a rich mountain.

67:17  LA: Ut quid suspicamini, montes coagulatos ? mons in quo beneplacitum est Deo habitare in eo ; etenim Dominus habitabit in finem.
       EN: Why do you regard curdled mountains? This is the mountain in which God has been pleased to dwell; indeed, the Lord will dwell there forever.

67:18  LA: Currus Dei decem millibus multiplex, millia lætantium ; Dominus in eis in Sina, in sancto.
       EN: God's chariot is multiplied by tens of thousands, thousands rejoicing; the Lord is among them at Sinai, in the holy place.

67:19  LA: Ascendisti in altum, cepisti captivitatem, accepisti dona in hominibus ; etenim non credentes inhabitare Dominum Deum.
       EN: You have ascended on high, taken captivity captive, and received gifts among men, even among those who do not believe that the Lord God dwells there.

67:20  LA: Benedictus Dominus die quotidie : prosperum iter faciet nobis Deus salutarium nostrorum.
       EN: Blessed is the Lord day by day; the God of our salvation will make our journey prosperous.

67:21  LA: Deus noster, Deus salvos faciendi ; et Domini, Domini exitus mortis.
       EN: Our God is a God who saves, and to the Lord, the Lord, belong the ways out of death.

67:22  LA: Verumtamen Deus confringet capita inimicorum suorum, verticem capilli perambulantium in delictis suis.
       EN: Yet God will crush His enemies' heads, the hairy crown of those who walk in their offenses.

67:23  LA: Dixit Dominus : Ex Basan convertam, convertam in profundum maris :
       EN: The Lord said, “I will turn them from Bashan; I will turn them into the depth of the sea,

67:24  LA: ut intingatur pes tuus in sanguine ; lingua canum tuorum ex inimicis, ab ipso.
       EN: so that your foot may be dipped in blood, the tongue of your dogs in that of your enemies, from him.”

67:25  LA: Viderunt ingressus tuos, Deus, ingressus Dei mei, regis mei, qui est in sancto.
       EN: They have seen Your processions, God, the processions of my God, my King, who is in the holy place.

67:26  LA: Prævenerunt principes conjuncti psallentibus, in medio juvencularum tympanistriarum.
       EN: Rulers went before, joined with singers, in the midst of young women beating tambourines.

67:27  LA: In ecclesiis benedicite Deo Domino de fontibus Israël.
       EN: In the congregations bless God the Lord, from the springs of Israel.

67:28  LA: Ibi Benjamin adolescentulus, in mentis excessu ; principes Juda, duces eorum ; principes Zabulon, principes Nephthali.
       EN: There is young Benjamin, in ecstasy; the rulers of Judah, their leaders; the rulers of Zebulun, the rulers of Naphtali.

67:29  LA: Manda, Deus, virtuti tuæ ; confirma hoc, Deus, quod operatus es in nobis.
       EN: Command Your strength, God; strengthen, God, what You have accomplished in us.

67:30  LA: A templo tuo in Jerusalem, tibi offerent reges munera.
       EN: From Your temple in Jerusalem, kings will offer You gifts.

67:31  LA: Increpa feras arundinis ; congregatio taurorum in vaccis populorum : ut excludant eos qui probati sunt argento. Dissipa gentes quæ bella volunt.
       EN: Rebuke the beasts of the reeds, the assembly of bulls among the cows of the peoples, so that they may exclude those tested with silver. Scatter the nations that desire wars.

67:32  LA: Venient legati ex Ægypto ; Æthiopia præveniet manus ejus Deo.
       EN: Envoys will come from Egypt; Ethiopia will hasten to offer its hands to God.

67:33  LA: Regna terræ, cantate Deo ; psallite Domino ; psallite Deo.
       EN: Kingdoms of the earth, sing to God; sing to the Lord; sing to God,

67:34  LA: Qui ascendit super cælum cæli, ad orientem : ecce dabit voci suæ vocem virtutis.
       EN: who ascends above the heaven of heavens, toward the east. See, He will give to His voice a voice of power.

67:35  LA: Date gloriam Deo super Israël ; magnificentia ejus et virtus ejus in nubibus.
       EN: Give glory to God over Israel; His magnificence and His strength are in the clouds.

67:36  LA: Mirabilis Deus in sanctis suis ; Deus Israël ipse dabit virtutem et fortitudinem plebi suæ. Benedictus Deus !]
       EN: God is wonderful in His holy ones; the God of Israel Himself will give strength and might to His people. Blessed is God!

PSALMS 68

68:1  LA: In finem, pro iis qui commutabuntur. David.
      EN: For the end, for those who will be changed. Of David.

68:2  LA: [Salvum me fac, Deus, quoniam intraverunt aquæ usque ad animam meam.
      EN: Save me, God, for the waters have entered as far as my soul.

68:3  LA: Infixus sum in limo profundi et non est substantia. Veni in altitudinem maris, et tempestas demersit me.
      EN: I am stuck in the mud of the deep, and there is no firm footing. I have come into the depth of the sea, and the storm has submerged me.

68:4  LA: Laboravi clamans, raucæ factæ sunt fauces meæ ; defecerunt oculi mei, dum spero in Deum meum.
      EN: I have grown weary with crying; my throat has become hoarse. My eyes have failed while I hope in my God.

68:5  LA: Multiplicati sunt super capillos capitis mei qui oderunt me gratis. Confortati sunt qui persecuti sunt me inimici mei injuste ; quæ non rapui, tunc exsolvebam.
      EN: Those who hate me without cause have multiplied beyond the hairs of my head. My enemies who unjustly pursued me have grown strong; then I repaid what I had not stolen.

68:6  LA: Deus, tu scis insipientiam meam ; et delicta mea a te non sunt abscondita.
      EN: God, You know my folly, and my offenses are not hidden from You.

68:7  LA: Non erubescant in me qui exspectant te, Domine, Domine virtutum ; non confundantur super me qui quærunt te, Deus Israël.
      EN: Let those who wait for You not be ashamed because of me, Lord, Lord of hosts; let those who seek You not be confounded over me, God of Israel.

68:8  LA: Quoniam propter te sustinui opprobrium ; operuit confusio faciem meam.
      EN: For Your sake I have borne reproach; confusion has covered my face.

68:9  LA: Extraneus factus sum fratribus meis, et peregrinus filiis matris meæ.
      EN: I have become a stranger to my brothers and a foreigner to my mother's sons,

68:10  LA: Quoniam zelus domus tuæ comedit me, et opprobria exprobrantium tibi ceciderunt super me.
       EN: for zeal for Your house has consumed me, and the reproaches of those who reproach You have fallen upon me.

68:11  LA: Et operui in jejunio animam meam, et factum est in opprobrium mihi.
       EN: I covered my soul with fasting, and it became a reproach to me.

68:12  LA: Et posui vestimentum meum cilicium ; et factus sum illis in parabolam.
       EN: I made sackcloth my clothing, and I became a byword to them.

68:13  LA: Adversum me loquebantur qui sedebant in porta, et in me psallebant qui bibebant vinum.
       EN: Those who sat at the gate spoke against me, and those who drank wine sang of me.

68:14  LA: Ego vero orationem meam ad te, Domine ; tempus beneplaciti, Deus. In multitudine misericordiæ tuæ, exaudi me in veritate salutis tuæ.
       EN: But my prayer is to You, Lord, at a time of favor, God. In the abundance of Your mercy, hear me in the truth of Your salvation.

68:15  LA: Eripe me de luto, ut non infigar ; libera me ab iis qui oderunt me, et de profundis aquarum.
       EN: Rescue me from the mud, so that I do not become stuck; deliver me from those who hate me and from the depths of the waters.

68:16  LA: Non me demergat tempestas aquæ, neque absorbeat me profundum, neque urgeat super me puteus os suum.
       EN: Let the storm of water not submerge me or the deep swallow me; let the pit not close its mouth over me.

68:17  LA: Exaudi me, Domine, quoniam benigna est misericordia tua ; secundum multitudinem miserationum tuarum respice in me.
       EN: Hear me, Lord, for Your mercy is kind; according to the abundance of Your compassion, look upon me.

68:18  LA: Et ne avertas faciem tuam a puero tuo ; quoniam tribulor, velociter exaudi me.
       EN: Do not turn Your face away from Your servant; for I am in distress, hear me quickly.

68:19  LA: Intende animæ meæ, et libera eam ; propter inimicos meos, eripe me.
       EN: Attend to my soul and deliver it; because of my enemies, rescue me.

68:20  LA: Tu scis improperium meum, et confusionem meam, et reverentiam meam ;
       EN: You know my reproach, my confusion, and my shame;

68:21  LA: in conspectu tuo sunt omnes qui tribulant me. Improperium exspectavit cor meum et miseriam : et sustinui qui simul contristaretur, et non fuit ; et qui consolaretur, et non inveni.
       EN: all who afflict me are before Your sight. My heart expected reproach and misery; I waited for someone to share my sorrow, but there was no one, and for someone to comfort me, but I found none.

68:22  LA: Et dederunt in escam meam fel, et in siti mea potaverunt me aceto.
       EN: They gave me gall for my food, and in my thirst they gave me vinegar to drink.

68:23  LA: Fiat mensa eorum coram ipsis in laqueum, et in retributiones, et in scandalum.
       EN: Let their table before them become a snare, a repayment, and a stumbling block.

68:24  LA: Obscurentur oculi eorum, ne videant, et dorsum eorum semper incurva.
       EN: Let their eyes be darkened so that they do not see, and always bend their back.

68:25  LA: Effunde super eos iram tuam, et furor iræ tuæ comprehendat eos.
       EN: Pour out Your anger upon them, and let the fury of Your anger overtake them.

68:26  LA: Fiat habitatio eorum deserta, et in tabernaculis eorum non sit qui inhabitet.
       EN: Let their dwelling become desolate, and let there be no one to dwell in their tents.

68:27  LA: Quoniam quem tu percussisti persecuti sunt, et super dolorem vulnerum meorum addiderunt.
       EN: For they have pursued the one You struck, and they have added to the pain of my wounds.

68:28  LA: Appone iniquitatem super iniquitatem eorum, et non intrent in justitiam tuam.
       EN: Add iniquity to their iniquity, and let them not enter into Your righteousness.

68:29  LA: Deleantur de libro viventium, et cum justis non scribantur.
       EN: Let them be erased from the book of the living, and let them not be written with the just.

68:30  LA: Ego sum pauper et dolens ; salus tua, Deus, suscepit me.
       EN: I am poor and sorrowful; Your salvation, God, has sustained me.

68:31  LA: Laudabo nomen Dei cum cantico, et magnificabo eum in laude :
       EN: I will praise God's name with a song and magnify Him with praise,

68:32  LA: et placebit Deo super vitulum novellum, cornua producentem et ungulas.
       EN: and it will please God more than a young calf growing horns and hooves.

68:33  LA: Videant pauperes, et lætentur ; quærite Deum, et vivet anima vestra :
       EN: Let the poor see and be glad; seek God, and your soul will live,

68:34  LA: quoniam exaudivit pauperes Dominus, et vinctos suos non despexit.
       EN: for the Lord has heard the poor and has not despised His prisoners.

68:35  LA: Laudent illum cæli et terra ; mare, et omnia reptilia in eis.
       EN: Let heaven and earth praise Him, the sea and all that crawls in them.

68:36  LA: Quoniam Deus salvam faciet Sion, et ædificabuntur civitates Juda, et inhabitabunt ibi, et hæreditate acquirent eam.
       EN: For God will save Zion, and the cities of Judah will be built; they will dwell there and acquire it as an inheritance.

68:37  LA: Et semen servorum ejus possidebit eam ; et qui diligunt nomen ejus habitabunt in ea.]
       EN: The descendants of His servants will possess it, and those who love His name will dwell in it.

PSALMS 69

69:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David in rememorationem, quod salvum fecerit eum Dominus.
      EN: For the end. A psalm of David, in remembrance that the Lord saved him.

69:2  LA: [Deus, in adjutorium meum intende ; Domine, ad adjuvandum me festina.
      EN: God, attend to my help; Lord, hurry to help me.

69:3  LA: Confundantur, et revereantur, qui quærunt animam meam.
      EN: Let those who seek my soul be confounded and ashamed.

69:4  LA: Avertantur retrorsum, et erubescant, qui volunt mihi mala ; avertantur statim erubescentes qui dicunt mihi : Euge, euge !
      EN: Let those who desire evil for me turn back and be ashamed; let those who say to me, “Aha, aha!” immediately turn away in shame.

69:5  LA: Exsultent et lætentur in te omnes qui quærunt te ; et dicant semper : Magnificetur Dominus, qui diligunt salutare tuum.
      EN: Let all who seek You rejoice and be glad in You, and let those who love Your salvation always say, “May the Lord be magnified.”

69:6  LA: Ego vero egenus et pauper sum ; Deus, adjuva me. Adjutor meus et liberator meus es tu ; Domine, ne moreris.]
      EN: But I am needy and poor; God, help me. You are my helper and my deliverer; Lord, do not delay.

PSALMS 70

70:1  LA: Psalmus David, filiorum Jonadab, et priorum captivorum. [In te, Domine, speravi ; non confundar in æternum.
      EN: A psalm of David, of the sons of Jonadab and of the first captives. In You, Lord, I have hoped; let me never be put to shame.

70:2  LA: In justitia tua libera me, et eripe me : inclina ad me aurem tuam, et salva me.
      EN: In Your righteousness deliver and rescue me; incline Your ear to me and save me.

70:3  LA: Esto mihi in Deum protectorem, et in locum munitum, ut salvum me facias : quoniam firmamentum meum et refugium meum es tu.
      EN: Be to me a protecting God and a fortified place, to save me, for You are my firm support and my refuge.

70:4  LA: Deus meus, eripe me de manu peccatoris, et de manu contra legem agentis, et iniqui :
      EN: My God, rescue me from the sinner's hand, from the hand of the lawbreaker and the unjust,

70:5  LA: quoniam tu es patientia mea, Domine ; Domine, spes mea a juventute mea.
      EN: for You are my endurance, Lord; Lord, my hope from my youth.

70:6  LA: In te confirmatus sum ex utero ; de ventre matris meæ tu es protector meus ; in te cantatio mea semper.
      EN: Upon You I have been upheld from the womb; from my mother's womb You are my protector. My song is always of You.

70:7  LA: Tamquam prodigium factus sum multis ; et tu adjutor fortis.
      EN: I have become like a portent to many, but You are a strong helper.

70:8  LA: Repleatur os meum laude, ut cantem gloriam tuam, tota die magnitudinem tuam.
      EN: Let my mouth be filled with praise, so that I may sing Your glory, Your greatness all day.

70:9  LA: Ne projicias me in tempore senectutis ; cum defecerit virtus mea, ne derelinquas me.
      EN: Do not cast me away in the time of old age; when my strength fails, do not abandon me.

70:10  LA: Quia dixerunt inimici mei mihi, et qui custodiebant animam meam consilium fecerunt in unum,
       EN: For my enemies have spoken of me, and those who watched for my soul have taken counsel together,

70:11  LA: dicentes : Deus dereliquit eum : persequimini et comprehendite eum, quia non est qui eripiat.
       EN: saying, “God has abandoned him; pursue him and seize him, for there is no one to rescue.”

70:12  LA: Deus, ne elongeris a me ; Deus meus, in auxilium meum respice.
       EN: God, do not be far from me; my God, look to my help.

70:13  LA: Confundantur et deficiant detrahentes animæ meæ ; operiantur confusione et pudore qui quærunt mala mihi.
       EN: Let those who slander my soul be confounded and fail; let those who seek evil for me be covered with confusion and shame.

70:14  LA: Ego autem semper sperabo, et adjiciam super omnem laudem tuam.
       EN: But I will always hope and add to all Your praise.

70:15  LA: Os meum annuntiabit justitiam tuam, tota die salutare tuum. Quoniam non cognovi litteraturam,
       EN: My mouth will proclaim Your righteousness, Your salvation all day. For I have not known learning,

70:16  LA: introibo in potentias Domini ; Domine, memorabor justitiæ tuæ solius.
       EN: I will enter into the mighty deeds of the Lord; Lord, I will remember Your righteousness alone.

70:17  LA: Deus, docuisti me a juventute mea ; et usque nunc pronuntiabo mirabilia tua.
       EN: God, You have taught me from my youth, and until now I will proclaim Your wonders.

70:18  LA: Et usque in senectam et senium, Deus, ne derelinquas me, donec annuntiem brachium tuum generationi omni quæ ventura est, potentiam tuam,
       EN: Even into old age and gray years, God, do not abandon me until I proclaim Your arm to every coming generation, Your power

70:19  LA: et justitiam tuam, Deus, usque in altissima ; quæ fecisti magnalia, Deus : quis similis tibi ?
       EN: and Your righteousness, God, to the highest heights. The great things You have done, God—who is like You?

70:20  LA: Quantas ostendisti mihi tribulationes multas et malas ! et conversus vivificasti me, et de abyssis terræ iterum reduxisti me.
       EN: How many great and evil distresses You have shown me! Yet You turned back and gave me life, and from the depths of the earth You brought me back again.

70:21  LA: Multiplicasti magnificentiam tuam ; et conversus consolatus es me.
       EN: You have multiplied Your magnificence, and turning back, You have comforted me.

70:22  LA: Nam et ego confitebor tibi in vasis psalmi veritatem tuam, Deus ; psallam tibi in cithara, sanctus Israël.
       EN: I too will praise You with instruments of song for Your truth, God; I will sing to You with the lyre, Holy One of Israel.

70:23  LA: Exsultabunt labia mea cum cantavero tibi ; et anima mea quam redemisti.
       EN: My lips will rejoice when I sing to You, and so will my soul, which You have redeemed.

70:24  LA: Sed et lingua mea tota die meditabitur justitiam tuam, cum confusi et reveriti fuerint qui quærunt mala mihi.]
       EN: My tongue too will meditate on Your righteousness all day, when those who seek evil for me have been confounded and ashamed.

PSALMS 71

71:1  LA: Psalmus , in Salomonem.
      EN: A psalm concerning Solomon. God, give Your judgment to the king and Your righteousness to the king's son, to judge Your people with righteousness and Your poor with judgment.

71:2  LA: Psalmus , in Salomonem.
      EN: A psalm concerning Solomon. God, give Your judgment to the king and Your righteousness to the king's son, to judge Your people with righteousness and Your poor with judgment.

71:3  LA: Suscipiant montes pacem populo, et colles justitiam.
      EN: Let the mountains receive peace for the people, and the hills righteousness.

71:4  LA: Judicabit pauperes populi, et salvos faciet filios pauperum, et humiliabit calumniatorem.
      EN: He will judge the poor of the people and save the children of the poor, and he will humble the slanderer.

71:5  LA: Et permanebit cum sole, et ante lunam, in generatione et generationem.
      EN: He will remain with the sun and before the moon, from generation to generation.

71:6  LA: Descendet sicut pluvia in vellus, et sicut stillicidia stillantia super terram.
      EN: He will descend like rain upon fleece, like showers dripping upon the earth.

71:7  LA: Orietur in diebus ejus justitia, et abundantia pacis, donec auferatur luna.
      EN: In his days righteousness will arise, and abundant peace, until the moon is taken away.

71:8  LA: Et dominabitur a mari usque ad mare, et a flumine usque ad terminos orbis terrarum.
      EN: He will rule from sea to sea and from the river to the ends of the world.

71:9  LA: Coram illo procident Æthiopes, et inimici ejus terram lingent.
      EN: Before him Ethiopians will fall, and his enemies will lick the earth.

71:10  LA: Reges Tharsis et insulæ munera offerent ; reges Arabum et Saba dona adducent :
       EN: The kings of Tarshish and the islands will offer gifts; the kings of the Arabs and Sheba will bring presents.

71:11  LA: et adorabunt eum omnes reges terræ ; omnes gentes servient ei.
       EN: All the kings of the earth will worship him; all nations will serve him.

71:12  LA: Quia liberabit pauperem a potente, et pauperem cui non erat adjutor.
       EN: For he will deliver the poor from the powerful, and the poor man who had no helper.

71:13  LA: Parcet pauperi et inopi, et animas pauperum salvas faciet.
       EN: He will spare the poor and needy, and he will save the souls of the poor.

71:14  LA: Ex usuris et iniquitate redimet animas eorum, et honorabile nomen eorum coram illo.
       EN: From usury and iniquity he will redeem their souls, and their name will be honored before him.

71:15  LA: Et vivet, et dabitur ei de auro Arabiæ ; et adorabunt de ipso semper, tota die benedicent ei.
       EN: He will live, and gold from Arabia will be given to him; they will pray for him always and bless him all day.

71:16  LA: Et erit firmamentum in terra in summis montium ; superextolletur super Libanum fructus ejus, et florebunt de civitate sicut fœnum terræ.
       EN: There will be a firm support in the land upon the mountain tops; its fruit will be raised above Lebanon, and they will flourish from the city like the grass of the earth.

71:17  LA: Sit nomen ejus benedictum in sæcula ; ante solem permanet nomen ejus. Et benedicentur in ipso omnes tribus terræ ; omnes gentes magnificabunt eum.
       EN: May his name be blessed through the ages; before the sun his name remains. All the tribes of the earth will be blessed in him; all nations will magnify him.

71:18  LA: Benedictus Dominus Deus Israël, qui facit mirabilia solus.
       EN: Blessed is the Lord, the God of Israel, who alone does wonders.

71:19  LA: Et benedictum nomen majestatis ejus in æternum, et replebitur majestate ejus omnis terra. Fiat, fiat.]
       EN: Blessed is the name of His majesty forever, and all the earth will be filled with His majesty. Let it be, let it be.

71:20  LA: Defecerunt laudes David, filii Jesse.
       EN: The praises of David, son of Jesse, have ended.

PSALMS 72

72:1  LA: Psalmus Asaph. [Quam bonus Israël Deus, his qui recto sunt corde !
      EN: A psalm of Asaph. How good God is to Israel, to those who are upright of heart!

72:2  LA: Mei autem pene moti sunt pedes, pene effusi sunt gressus mei :
      EN: But my feet were almost shaken; my steps almost slipped away,

72:3  LA: quia zelavi super iniquos, pacem peccatorum videns.
      EN: for I envied the unjust when I saw the peace of sinners.

72:4  LA: Quia non est respectus morti eorum, et firmamentum in plaga eorum.
      EN: For there is no thought of their death, and there is strength in their affliction.

72:5  LA: In labore hominum non sunt, et cum hominibus non flagellabuntur.
      EN: They do not share human toil, and they will not be scourged with other people.

72:6  LA: Ideo tenuit eos superbia ; operti sunt iniquitate et impietate sua.
      EN: Therefore pride has held them; they are covered with their iniquity and ungodliness.

72:7  LA: Prodiit quasi ex adipe iniquitas eorum ; transierunt in affectum cordis.
      EN: Their iniquity has come out as from fat; they have passed into the heart's desires.

72:8  LA: Cogitaverunt et locuti sunt nequitiam ; iniquitatem in excelso locuti sunt.
      EN: They have thought and spoken wickedness; they have spoken iniquity from on high.

72:9  LA: Posuerunt in cælum os suum, et lingua eorum transivit in terra.
      EN: They have set their mouth against heaven, and their tongue has passed over the earth.

72:10  LA: Ideo convertetur populus meus hic, et dies pleni invenientur in eis.
       EN: Therefore my people will turn back here, and full days will be found among them.

72:11  LA: Et dixerunt : Quomodo scit Deus, et si est scientia in excelso ?
       EN: They have said, “How does God know? Is there knowledge on high?”

72:12  LA: Ecce ipsi peccatores, et abundantes in sæculo obtinuerunt divitias.
       EN: See, these are sinners, and prospering in the world, they have obtained riches.

72:13  LA: Et dixi : Ergo sine causa justificavi cor meum, et lavi inter innocentes manus meas,
       EN: I said, “Then without cause I have made my heart just and washed my hands among the innocent,

72:14  LA: et fui flagellatus tota die, et castigatio mea in matutinis.
       EN: and I have been scourged all day, with chastisement every morning.”

72:15  LA: Si dicebam : Narrabo sic ; ecce nationem filiorum tuorum reprobavi.
       EN: If I said, “I will speak this way,” see, I would have rejected the generation of Your children.

72:16  LA: Existimabam ut cognoscerem hoc ; labor est ante me :
       EN: I considered how to understand this; toil lies before me,

72:17  LA: donec intrem in sanctuarium Dei, et intelligam in novissimis eorum.
       EN: until I enter God's sanctuary and understand their last things.

72:18  LA: Verumtamen propter dolos posuisti eis ; dejecisti eos dum allevarentur.
       EN: Yet because of their deceit You have set it for them; You cast them down while they were being raised up.

72:19  LA: Quomodo facti sunt in desolationem ? subito defecerunt : perierunt propter iniquitatem suam.
       EN: How have they become desolate! Suddenly they have failed; they have perished because of their iniquity.

72:20  LA: Velut somnium surgentium, Domine, in civitate tua imaginem ipsorum ad nihilum rediges.
       EN: Like the dream of those who awaken, Lord, in Your city You will bring their image to nothing.

72:21  LA: Quia inflammatum est cor meum, et renes mei commutati sunt ;
       EN: For my heart was inflamed and my kidneys were changed,

72:22  LA: et ego ad nihilum redactus sum, et nescivi :
       EN: and I was brought to nothing and did not know.

72:23  LA: ut jumentum factus sum apud te, et ego semper tecum.
       EN: I became like a beast before You, yet I am always with You.

72:24  LA: Tenuisti manum dexteram meam, et in voluntate tua deduxisti me, et cum gloria suscepisti me.
       EN: You have held my right hand, guided me in Your will, and received me with glory.

72:25  LA: Quid enim mihi est in cælo ? et a te quid volui super terram ?
       EN: For what do I have in heaven, and what have I desired from You upon the earth?

72:26  LA: Defecit caro mea et cor meum ; Deus cordis mei, et pars mea, Deus in æternum.
       EN: My flesh and my heart have failed; God is the God of my heart and my portion forever.

72:27  LA: Quia ecce qui elongant se a te peribunt ; perdidisti omnes qui fornicantur abs te.
       EN: For see, those who withdraw far from You will perish; You have destroyed all who stray from You in fornication.

72:28  LA: Mihi autem adhærere Deo bonum est ; ponere in Domino Deo spem meam : ut annuntiem omnes prædicationes tuas in portis filiæ Sion.]
       EN: But for me it is good to cling to God, to place my hope in the Lord God, so that I may proclaim all Your praises in the gates of the daughter of Zion.

PSALMS 73

73:1  LA: Intellectus Asaph. [Ut quid, Deus, repulisti in finem, iratus est furor tuus super oves pascuæ tuæ ?
      EN: For understanding, of Asaph. Why, God, have You rejected us forever? Why has Your furious anger burned against the sheep of Your pasture?

73:2  LA: Memor esto congregationis tuæ, quam possedisti ab initio. Redemisti virgam hæreditatis tuæ, mons Sion, in quo habitasti in eo.
      EN: Remember Your congregation, which You possessed from the beginning. You redeemed the rod of Your inheritance, Mount Zion in which You dwelt.

73:3  LA: Leva manus tuas in superbias eorum in finem : quanta malignatus est inimicus in sancto !
      EN: Lift Your hands against their pride to the end. How much evil the enemy has done in the holy place!

73:4  LA: Et gloriati sunt qui oderunt te in medio solemnitatis tuæ ; posuerunt signa sua, signa :
      EN: Those who hate You have boasted in the midst of Your solemn feast; they have set up their signs as signs,

73:5  LA: et non cognoverunt sicut in exitu super summum. Quasi in silva lignorum securibus
      EN: and they did not recognize it, as at the departure upon the height. As in a forest of trees, with axes they cut down its doors together; with axe and adze they cast it down.

73:6  LA: et non cognoverunt sicut in exitu super summum. Quasi in silva lignorum securibus
      EN: and they did not recognize it, as at the departure upon the height. As in a forest of trees, with axes they cut down its doors together; with axe and adze they cast it down.

73:7  LA: Incenderunt igni sanctuarium tuum ; in terra polluerunt tabernaculum nominis tui.
      EN: They burned Your sanctuary with fire; upon the earth they defiled the tent of Your name.

73:8  LA: Dixerunt in corde suo cognatio eorum simul : Quiescere faciamus omnes dies festos Dei a terra.
      EN: Their kindred together said in their hearts, “Let us make all God's feast days cease from the earth.”

73:9  LA: Signa nostra non vidimus ; jam non est propheta ; et nos non cognoscet amplius.
      EN: We have not seen our signs; there is no longer a prophet, and he will no longer know us.

73:10  LA: Usquequo, Deus, improperabit inimicus ? irritat adversarius nomen tuum in finem ?
       EN: How long, God, will the enemy reproach You? Will the adversary provoke Your name forever?

73:11  LA: Ut quid avertis manum tuam, et dexteram tuam de medio sinu tuo in finem ?
       EN: Why do You turn Your hand away, and Your right hand from within Your bosom, forever?

73:12  LA: Deus autem rex noster ante sæcula : operatus est salutem in medio terræ.
       EN: But God is our King from before the ages; He has accomplished salvation in the midst of the earth.

73:13  LA: Tu confirmasti in virtute tua mare ; contribulasti capita draconum in aquis.
       EN: You made the sea firm in Your strength; You crushed the heads of dragons in the waters.

73:14  LA: Tu confregisti capita draconis ; dedisti eum escam populis Æthiopum.
       EN: You broke the heads of the dragon; You gave him as food to the peoples of Ethiopia.

73:15  LA: Tu dirupisti fontes et torrentes ; tu siccasti fluvios Ethan.
       EN: You broke open springs and torrents; You dried up the rivers of Ethan.

73:16  LA: Tuus est dies, et tua est nox ; tu fabricatus es auroram et solem.
       EN: The day is Yours and the night is Yours; You fashioned the dawn and the sun.

73:17  LA: Tu fecisti omnes terminos terræ ; æstatem et ver tu plasmasti ea.
       EN: You made all the boundaries of the earth; summer and spring, You shaped them.

73:18  LA: Memor esto hujus : inimicus improperavit Domino, et populus insipiens incitavit nomen tuum.
       EN: Remember this: the enemy has reproached the Lord, and a foolish people has provoked Your name.

73:19  LA: Ne tradas bestiis animas confitentes tibi, et animas pauperum tuorum ne obliviscaris in finem.
       EN: Do not hand souls that praise You over to beasts, and do not forget the souls of Your poor forever.

73:20  LA: Respice in testamentum tuum, quia repleti sunt qui obscurati sunt terræ domibus iniquitatum.
       EN: Look upon Your covenant, for the darkened ones of the earth have been filled with houses of iniquities.

73:21  LA: Ne avertatur humilis factus confusus ; pauper et inops laudabunt nomen tuum.
       EN: Let the lowly not turn back in shame; the poor and needy will praise Your name.

73:22  LA: Exsurge, Deus, judica causam tuam ; memor esto improperiorum tuorum, eorum quæ ab insipiente sunt tota die.
       EN: Arise, God, judge Your cause; remember the reproaches against You, those that come from the fool all day.

73:23  LA: Ne obliviscaris voces inimicorum tuorum : superbia eorum qui te oderunt ascendit semper.]
       EN: Do not forget the voices of Your enemies; the pride of those who hate You rises continually.

PSALMS 74

74:1  LA: In finem, ne corrumpas. Psalmus cantici Asaph.
      EN: For the end, do not corrupt. A psalm of song, of Asaph.

74:2  LA: [Confitebimur tibi, Deus, confitebimur, et invocabimus nomen tuum ; narrabimus mirabilia tua.
      EN: We will praise You, God, we will praise You and call upon Your name; we will recount Your wonders.

74:3  LA: Cum accepero tempus, ego justitias judicabo.
      EN: “When I receive the time, I will judge with righteousness.

74:4  LA: Liquefacta est terra et omnes qui habitant in ea : ego confirmavi columnas ejus.
      EN: The earth and all who dwell in it have melted away; I have made its pillars firm.

74:5  LA: Dixi iniquis : Nolite inique agere : et delinquentibus : Nolite exaltare cornu :
