      EN: I said to the unjust, ‘Do not act unjustly,’ and to offenders, ‘Do not exalt your horn;

74:6  LA: nolite extollere in altum cornu vestrum ; nolite loqui adversus Deum iniquitatem.
      EN: do not lift your horn on high; do not speak iniquity against God.’”

74:7  LA: Quia neque ab oriente, neque ab occidente, neque a desertis montibus :
      EN: For it comes neither from the east nor from the west nor from the deserted mountains,

74:8  LA: quoniam Deus judex est. Hunc humiliat, et hunc exaltat :
      EN: for God is judge. He humbles this one and exalts that one,

74:9  LA: quia calix in manu Domini vini meri, plenus misto. Et inclinavit ex hoc in hoc ; verumtamen fæx ejus non est exinanita : bibent omnes peccatores terræ.
      EN: for in the Lord's hand is a cup of pure wine, full of a mixture. He has poured from this into that, yet its dregs have not been emptied; all the sinners of the earth will drink.

74:10  LA: Ego autem annuntiabo in sæculum ; cantabo Deo Jacob :
       EN: But I will proclaim forever; I will sing to the God of Jacob.

74:11  LA: et omnia cornua peccatorum confringam, et exaltabuntur cornua justi.]
       EN: I will break all the horns of sinners, and the horns of the just will be exalted.

PSALMS 75

75:1  LA: In finem, in laudibus. Psalmus Asaph, canticum ad Assyrios.
      EN: For the end, among praises. A psalm of Asaph, a song to the Assyrians.

75:2  LA: [Notus in Judæa Deus ; in Israël magnum nomen ejus.
      EN: God is known in Judah; His name is great in Israel.

75:3  LA: Et factus est in pace locus ejus, et habitatio ejus in Sion.
      EN: His place has been established in peace, and His dwelling in Zion.

75:4  LA: Ibi confregit potentias arcuum, scutum, gladium, et bellum.
      EN: There He broke the powers of bows, shield, sword, and war.

75:5  LA: Illuminans tu mirabiliter a montibus æternis ;
      EN: You give light wonderfully from the everlasting mountains;

75:6  LA: turbati sunt omnes insipientes corde. Dormierunt somnum suum, et nihil invenerunt omnes viri divitiarum in manibus suis.
      EN: all the foolish of heart were troubled. They slept their sleep, and all the men of riches found nothing in their hands.

75:7  LA: Ab increpatione tua, Deus Jacob, dormitaverunt qui ascenderunt equos.
      EN: At Your rebuke, God of Jacob, those who mounted horses fell asleep.

75:8  LA: Tu terribilis es ; et quis resistet tibi ? ex tunc ira tua.
      EN: You are fearsome, and who will resist You? From that time is Your anger.

75:9  LA: De cælo auditum fecisti judicium : terra tremuit et quievit
      EN: From heaven You made judgment heard; the earth trembled and grew still

75:10  LA: cum exsurgeret in judicium Deus, ut salvos faceret omnes mansuetos terræ.
       EN: when God arose in judgment to save all the gentle of the earth.

75:11  LA: Quoniam cogitatio hominis confitebitur tibi, et reliquiæ cogitationis diem festum agent tibi.
       EN: For human thought will praise You, and the remainder of thought will keep a feast for You.

75:12  LA: Vovete et reddite Domino Deo vestro, omnes qui in circuitu ejus affertis munera : terribili,
       EN: Make vows and fulfill them to the Lord your God, all you around Him who bring gifts to the fearsome One,

75:13  LA: et ei qui aufert spiritum principum : terribili apud reges terræ.]
       EN: to Him who takes away the spirit of rulers, who is fearsome among the kings of the earth.

PSALMS 76

76:1  LA: In finem, pro Idithun. Psalmus Asaph.
      EN: For the end. For Jeduthun. A psalm of Asaph.

76:2  LA: [Voce mea ad Dominum clamavi ; voce mea ad Deum, et intendit mihi.
      EN: With my voice I cried to the Lord; with my voice to God, and He attended to me.

76:3  LA: In die tribulationis meæ Deum exquisivi ; manibus meis nocte contra eum, et non sum deceptus. Renuit consolari anima mea ;
      EN: On the day of my distress I sought God, with my hands stretched toward Him at night, and I was not deceived. My soul refused to be comforted.

76:4  LA: memor fui Dei, et delectatus sum, et exercitatus sum, et defecit spiritus meus.
      EN: I remembered God and delighted; I pondered, and my spirit failed.

76:5  LA: Anticipaverunt vigilias oculi mei ; turbatus sum, et non sum locutus.
      EN: My eyes anticipated the watches; I was troubled and did not speak.

76:6  LA: Cogitavi dies antiquos, et annos æternos in mente habui.
      EN: I thought of ancient days and held the everlasting years in mind.

76:7  LA: Et meditatus sum nocte cum corde meo, et exercitabar, et scopebam spiritum meum.
      EN: At night I meditated with my heart; I pondered and searched my spirit.

76:8  LA: Numquid in æternum projiciet Deus ? aut non apponet ut complacitior sit adhuc ?
      EN: Will God cast away forever? Will He not again become more favorable?

76:9  LA: aut in finem misericordiam suam abscindet, a generatione in generationem ?
      EN: Or will He cut off His mercy completely, from generation to generation?

76:10  LA: aut obliviscetur misereri Deus ? aut continebit in ira sua misericordias suas ?
       EN: Or will God forget to show mercy? Will He restrain His mercies in His anger?

76:11  LA: Et dixi : Nunc cœpi ; hæc mutatio dexteræ Excelsi.
       EN: And I said, “Now I have begun; this is the change of the Most High's right hand.”

76:12  LA: Memor fui operum Domini, quia memor ero ab initio mirabilium tuorum :
       EN: I remembered the Lord's works, for I will remember Your wonders from the beginning.

76:13  LA: et meditabor in omnibus operibus tuis, et in adinventionibus tuis exercebor.
       EN: I will meditate on all Your works and ponder Your deeds.

76:14  LA: Deus, in sancto via tua : quis deus magnus sicut Deus noster ?
       EN: God, Your way is in the holy place. What god is great like our God?

76:15  LA: Tu es Deus qui facis mirabilia : notam fecisti in populis virtutem tuam.
       EN: You are the God who does wonders; You have made Your might known among the peoples.

76:16  LA: Redemisti in brachio tuo populum tuum, filios Jacob et Joseph.
       EN: With Your arm You redeemed Your people, the children of Jacob and Joseph.

76:17  LA: Viderunt te aquæ, Deus ; viderunt te aquæ, et timuerunt : et turbatæ sunt abyssi.
       EN: The waters saw You, God; the waters saw You and were afraid, and the depths were troubled.

76:18  LA: Multitudo sonitus aquarum ; vocem dederunt nubes. Etenim sagittæ tuæ transeunt ;
       EN: Great was the sound of the waters; the clouds gave voice. Indeed, Your arrows pass through.

76:19  LA: vox tonitrui tui in rota. Illuxerunt coruscationes tuæ orbi terræ ; commota est, et contremuit terra.
       EN: The voice of Your thunder was in the wheel. Your lightning lit up the world; the earth shook and trembled.

76:20  LA: In mari via tua, et semitæ tuæ in aquis multis, et vestigia tua non cognoscentur.
       EN: Your way is in the sea and Your paths in many waters, and Your footprints will not be known.

76:21  LA: Deduxisti sicut oves populum tuum, in manu Moysi et Aaron.]
       EN: You led Your people like sheep by the hand of Moses and Aaron.

PSALMS 77

77:1  LA: Intellectus Asaph. [Attendite, popule meus, legem meam ; inclinate aurem vestram in verba oris mei.
      EN: For understanding. Of Asaph. Attend, my people, to my law; incline your ear to the words of my mouth.

77:2  LA: Aperiam in parabolis os meum ; loquar propositiones ab initio.
      EN: I will open my mouth in parables; I will speak propositions from the beginning.

77:3  LA: Quanta audivimus, et cognovimus ea, et patres nostri narraverunt nobis.
      EN: How much we have heard and known, and our fathers have recounted to us!

77:4  LA: Non sunt occultata a filiis eorum in generatione altera, narrantes laudes Domini et virtutes ejus, et mirabilia ejus quæ fecit.
      EN: These things were not hidden from their children in another generation, as they recounted the Lord's praises and His mighty deeds and the wonders He had done.

77:5  LA: Et suscitavit testimonium in Jacob, et legem posuit in Israël, quanta mandavit patribus nostris nota facere ea filiis suis :
      EN: He raised up a testimony in Jacob and established a law in Israel, the things He commanded our fathers to make known to their children,

77:6  LA: ut cognoscat generatio altera : filii qui nascentur et exsurgent, et narrabunt filiis suis,
      EN: so that another generation might know: the children who would be born and arise and recount them to their children,

77:7  LA: ut ponant in Deo spem suam, et non obliviscantur operum Dei, et mandata ejus exquirant :
      EN: so that they might place their hope in God and not forget God's works, but seek out His commandments,

77:8  LA: ne fiant, sicut patres eorum, generatio prava et exasperans ; generatio quæ non direxit cor suum, et non est creditus cum Deo spiritus ejus.
      EN: and not become like their fathers, a perverse and provoking generation, a generation that did not set its heart right and whose spirit was not faithful with God.

77:9  LA: Filii Ephrem, intendentes et mittentes arcum, conversi sunt in die belli.
      EN: The children of Ephraim, drawing and shooting the bow, turned back on the day of battle.

77:10  LA: Non custodierunt testamentum Dei, et in lege ejus noluerunt ambulare.
       EN: They did not keep God's covenant and were unwilling to walk in His law.

77:11  LA: Et obliti sunt benefactorum ejus, et mirabilium ejus quæ ostendit eis.
       EN: They forgot His benefits and the wonders He had shown them.

77:12  LA: Coram patribus eorum fecit mirabilia in terra Ægypti, in campo Taneos.
       EN: Before their fathers He did wonders in the land of Egypt, in the plain of Tanis.

77:13  LA: Interrupit mare, et perduxit eos, et statuit aquas quasi in utre :
       EN: He broke apart the sea and led them through, and made the waters stand as though in a skin bottle.

77:14  LA: et deduxit eos in nube diei, et tota nocte in illuminatione ignis.
       EN: He led them in a cloud by day and all night in the light of fire.

77:15  LA: Interrupit petram in eremo, et adaquavit eos velut in abysso multa.
       EN: He split the rock in the wilderness and gave them water as from a great deep.

77:16  LA: Et eduxit aquam de petra, et deduxit tamquam flumina aquas.
       EN: He brought water out of the rock and led the waters like rivers.

77:17  LA: Et apposuerunt adhuc peccare ei ; in iram excitaverunt Excelsum in inaquoso.
       EN: They still went on sinning against Him; they stirred the Most High to anger in the waterless place.

77:18  LA: Et tentaverunt Deum in cordibus suis, ut peterent escas animabus suis.
       EN: They tested God in their hearts by asking for food for their souls.

77:19  LA: Et male locuti sunt de Deo ; dixerunt : Numquid poterit Deus parare mensam in deserto ?
       EN: They spoke evil of God and said, “Can God prepare a table in the wilderness?

77:20  LA: quoniam percussit petram, et fluxerunt aquæ, et torrentes inundaverunt. Numquid et panem poterit dare, aut parare mensam populo suo ?
       EN: For He struck the rock, and waters flowed and torrents overflowed. Can He also give bread or prepare a table for His people?”

77:21  LA: Ideo audivit Dominus et distulit ; et ignis accensus est in Jacob, et ira ascendit in Israël :
       EN: Therefore the Lord heard and delayed; fire was kindled in Jacob, and anger rose in Israel,

77:22  LA: quia non crediderunt in Deo, nec speraverunt in salutari ejus.
       EN: because they did not believe in God or hope in His salvation.

77:23  LA: Et mandavit nubibus desuper, et januas cæli aperuit.
       EN: He commanded the clouds above and opened the doors of heaven.

77:24  LA: Et pluit illis manna ad manducandum, et panem cæli dedit eis.
       EN: He rained manna upon them to eat and gave them the bread of heaven.

77:25  LA: Panem angelorum manducavit homo ; cibaria misit eis in abundantia.
       EN: A human being ate the bread of angels; He sent them food in abundance.

77:26  LA: Transtulit austrum de cælo, et induxit in virtute sua africum.
       EN: He moved the south wind from heaven and brought in the southwest wind by His might.

77:27  LA: Et pluit super eos sicut pulverem carnes, et sicut arenam maris volatilia pennata.
       EN: He rained flesh upon them like dust, and winged birds like the sand of the sea.

77:28  LA: Et ceciderunt in medio castrorum eorum, circa tabernacula eorum.
       EN: They fell in the middle of their camp, around their tents.

77:29  LA: Et manducaverunt, et saturati sunt nimis, et desiderium eorum attulit eis :
       EN: They ate and were filled exceedingly, and He brought them what they desired.

77:30  LA: non sunt fraudati a desiderio suo. Adhuc escæ eorum erant in ore ipsorum,
       EN: They were not deprived of their desire. Their food was still in their mouths,

77:31  LA: et ira Dei ascendit super eos : et occidit pingues eorum, et electos Israël impedivit.
       EN: and God's anger rose against them. He killed their stout ones and brought down the chosen men of Israel.

77:32  LA: In omnibus his peccaverunt adhuc, et non crediderunt in mirabilibus ejus.
       EN: In all this they still sinned and did not believe in His wonders.

77:33  LA: Et defecerunt in vanitate dies eorum, et anni eorum cum festinatione.
       EN: Their days dwindled in futility, and their years in haste.

77:34  LA: Cum occideret eos, quærebant eum et revertebantur, et diluculo veniebant ad eum.
       EN: When He killed them, they sought Him and returned, and came to Him at daybreak.

77:35  LA: Et rememorati sunt quia Deus adjutor est eorum, et Deus excelsus redemptor eorum est.
       EN: They remembered that God was their helper and God Most High their redeemer.

77:36  LA: Et dilexerunt eum in ore suo, et lingua sua mentiti sunt ei ;
       EN: They loved Him with their mouths and lied to Him with their tongues,

77:37  LA: cor autem eorum non erat rectum cum eo, nec fideles habiti sunt in testamento ejus.
       EN: but their heart was not right with Him, nor were they found faithful in His covenant.

77:38  LA: Ipse autem est misericors, et propitius fiet peccatis eorum, et non disperdet eos. Et abundavit ut averteret iram suam, et non accendit omnem iram suam.
       EN: Yet He is merciful and will forgive their sins and not destroy them. He repeatedly turned His anger away and did not kindle all His wrath.

77:39  LA: Et recordatus est quia caro sunt, spiritus vadens et non rediens.
       EN: He remembered that they are flesh, a spirit that goes and does not return.

77:40  LA: Quoties exacerbaverunt eum in deserto ; in iram concitaverunt eum in inaquoso ?
       EN: How often they provoked Him in the wilderness and stirred Him to anger in the waterless place!

77:41  LA: Et conversi sunt, et tentaverunt Deum, et sanctum Israël exacerbaverunt.
       EN: They turned back and tested God and provoked the Holy One of Israel.

77:42  LA: Non sunt recordati manus ejus, die qua redemit eos de manu tribulantis :
       EN: They did not remember His hand on the day He redeemed them from the hand of the oppressor,

77:43  LA: sicut posuit in Ægypto signa sua, et prodigia sua in campo Taneos ;
       EN: when He set His signs in Egypt and His portents in the plain of Tanis,

77:44  LA: et convertit in sanguinem flumina eorum, et imbres eorum, ne biberent.
       EN: and turned their rivers and their rainwater into blood, so that they could not drink.

77:45  LA: Misit in eos cœnomyiam, et comedit eos, et ranam, et disperdidit eos ;
       EN: He sent swarms of flies among them, which devoured them, and frogs, which destroyed them.

77:46  LA: et dedit ærugini fructus eorum, et labores eorum locustæ ;
       EN: He gave their crops to blight and their labors to the locust.

77:47  LA: et occidit in grandine vineas eorum, et moros eorum in pruina ;
       EN: He killed their vines with hail and their mulberry trees with frost.

77:48  LA: et tradidit grandini jumenta eorum, et possessionem eorum igni ;
       EN: He delivered their beasts of burden to hail and their possessions to fire.

77:49  LA: misit in eos iram indignationis suæ, indignationem, et iram, et tribulationem, immissiones per angelos malos.
       EN: He sent upon them the anger of His indignation, indignation and anger and distress, assaults through evil angels.

77:50  LA: Viam fecit semitæ iræ suæ : non pepercit a morte animabus eorum, et jumenta eorum in morte conclusit :
       EN: He made a way for the path of His anger. He did not spare their souls from death and shut up their beasts of burden in death.

77:51  LA: et percussit omne primogenitum in terra Ægypti ; primitias omnis laboris eorum in tabernaculis Cham :
       EN: He struck every firstborn in the land of Egypt, the firstfruits of all their labor in the tents of Ham.

77:52  LA: et abstulit sicut oves populum suum, et perduxit eos tamquam gregem in deserto :
       EN: He took away His people like sheep and led them like a flock in the wilderness.

77:53  LA: et deduxit eos in spe, et non timuerunt, et inimicos eorum operuit mare.
       EN: He led them in hope, and they did not fear; the sea covered their enemies.

77:54  LA: Et induxit eos in montem sanctificationis suæ, montem quem acquisivit dextera ejus ; et ejecit a facie eorum gentes, et sorte divisit eis terram in funiculo distributionis ;
       EN: He brought them to the mountain of His sanctification, the mountain His right hand acquired. He drove out nations before them and divided the land to them by lot, with a measuring line for distribution.

77:55  LA: et habitare fecit in tabernaculis eorum tribus Israël.
       EN: He made the tribes of Israel dwell in their tents.

77:56  LA: Et tentaverunt, et exacerbaverunt Deum excelsum, et testimonia ejus non custodierunt.
       EN: They tested and provoked God Most High and did not keep His testimonies.

77:57  LA: Et averterunt se, et non servaverunt pactum : quemadmodum patres eorum, conversi sunt in arcum pravum.
       EN: They turned away and did not keep the covenant; like their fathers they turned into a crooked bow.

77:58  LA: In iram concitaverunt eum in collibus suis, et in sculptilibus suis ad æmulationem eum provocaverunt.
       EN: They stirred Him to anger on their hills and provoked Him to jealousy with their carved images.

77:59  LA: Audivit Deus, et sprevit, et ad nihilum redegit valde Israël.
       EN: God heard and despised them and brought Israel utterly to nothing.

77:60  LA: Et repulit tabernaculum Silo, tabernaculum suum, ubi habitavit in hominibus.
       EN: He rejected the tabernacle of Shiloh, His tabernacle where He had dwelt among human beings.

77:61  LA: Et tradidit in captivitatem virtutem eorum, et pulchritudinem eorum in manus inimici.
       EN: He delivered their strength into captivity and their beauty into the enemy's hands.

77:62  LA: Et conclusit in gladio populum suum, et hæreditatem suam sprevit.
       EN: He shut up His people under the sword and despised His inheritance.

77:63  LA: Juvenes eorum comedit ignis, et virgines eorum non sunt lamentatæ.
       EN: Fire devoured their young men, and their young women were not mourned.

77:64  LA: Sacerdotes eorum in gladio ceciderunt, et viduæ eorum non plorabantur.
       EN: Their priests fell by the sword, and their widows were not wept for.

77:65  LA: Et excitatus est tamquam dormiens Dominus, tamquam potens crapulatus a vino.
       EN: The Lord awoke as though from sleep, like a mighty man overcome with wine.

77:66  LA: Et percussit inimicos suos in posteriora ; opprobrium sempiternum dedit illis.
       EN: He struck His enemies in their hindquarters and gave them everlasting disgrace.

77:67  LA: Et repulit tabernaculum Joseph, et tribum Ephraim non elegit :
       EN: He rejected the tabernacle of Joseph and did not choose the tribe of Ephraim,

77:68  LA: sed elegit tribum Juda, montem Sion, quem dilexit.
       EN: but chose the tribe of Judah, Mount Zion, which He loved.

77:69  LA: Et ædificavit sicut unicornium sanctificium suum, in terra quam fundavit in sæcula.
       EN: He built His sanctuary like the one-horned beasts, in the land He founded forever.

77:70  LA: Et elegit David, servum suum, et sustulit eum de gregibus ovium ; de post fœtantes accepit eum :
       EN: He chose David His servant and took him from the flocks of sheep; from following the ewes with young He took him,

77:71  LA: pascere Jacob servum suum, et Israël hæreditatem suam.
       EN: to shepherd Jacob His servant and Israel His inheritance.

77:72  LA: Et pavit eos in innocentia cordis sui, et in intellectibus manuum suarum deduxit eos.]
       EN: He shepherded them in the innocence of his heart and guided them with the understanding of his hands.

PSALMS 78

78:1  LA: Psalmus Asaph. [Deus, venerunt gentes in hæreditatem tuam ; polluerunt templum sanctum tuum ; posuerunt Jerusalem in pomorum custodiam.
      EN: A psalm of Asaph. God, nations have entered Your inheritance; they have defiled Your holy temple and made Jerusalem an orchard watchpost.

78:2  LA: Posuerunt morticina servorum tuorum escas volatilibus cæli ; carnes sanctorum tuorum bestiis terræ.
      EN: They have made the corpses of Your servants food for the birds of heaven, the flesh of Your holy ones food for the beasts of the earth.

78:3  LA: Effuderunt sanguinem eorum tamquam aquam in circuitu Jerusalem, et non erat qui sepeliret.
      EN: They have poured out their blood like water around Jerusalem, and there was no one to bury them.

78:4  LA: Facti sumus opprobrium vicinis nostris ; subsannatio et illusio his qui in circuitu nostro sunt.
      EN: We have become a reproach to our neighbors, mockery and derision to those around us.

78:5  LA: Usquequo, Domine, irasceris in finem ? accendetur velut ignis zelus tuus ?
      EN: How long, Lord? Will You be angry forever? Will Your jealousy burn like fire?

78:6  LA: Effunde iram tuam in gentes quæ te non noverunt, et in regna quæ nomen tuum non invocaverunt :
      EN: Pour out Your anger on the nations that have not known You and on the kingdoms that have not called upon Your name,

78:7  LA: quia comederunt Jacob, et locum ejus desolaverunt.
      EN: because they have devoured Jacob and laid his dwelling waste.

78:8  LA: Ne memineris iniquitatum nostrarum antiquarum ; cito anticipent nos misericordiæ tuæ, quia pauperes facti sumus nimis.
      EN: Do not remember our ancient iniquities. Let Your mercies quickly come to meet us, because we have become exceedingly poor.

78:9  LA: Adjuva nos, Deus salutaris noster, et propter gloriam nominis tui, Domine, libera nos : et propitius esto peccatis nostris, propter nomen tuum.
      EN: Help us, God our savior, and for the glory of Your name, Lord, deliver us. Forgive our sins for Your name's sake.

78:10  LA: Ne forte dicant in gentibus : Ubi est Deus eorum ? et innotescat in nationibus coram oculis nostris ultio sanguinis servorum tuorum qui effusus est.
       EN: Lest they say among the nations, “Where is their God?” Let vengeance for the outpoured blood of Your servants become known among the nations before our eyes.

78:11  LA: Introëat in conspectu tuo gemitus compeditorum ; secundum magnitudinem brachii tui posside filios mortificatorum :
       EN: Let the groaning of those in chains enter Your presence. According to the greatness of Your arm, take possession of the children of those put to death.

78:12  LA: et redde vicinis nostris septuplum in sinu eorum ; improperium ipsorum quod exprobraverunt tibi, Domine.
       EN: Repay our neighbors sevenfold into their bosom the reproach with which they have reproached You, Lord.

78:13  LA: Nos autem populus tuus, et oves pascuæ tuæ, confitebimur tibi in sæculum ; in generationem et generationem annuntiabimus laudem tuam.]
       EN: But we, Your people and the sheep of Your pasture, will give You thanks forever; from generation to generation we will proclaim Your praise.

PSALMS 79

79:1  LA: In finem, pro iis qui commutabuntur. Testimonium Asaph, psalmus.
      EN: For the end. For those who will be changed. A testimony of Asaph. A psalm.

79:2  LA: [Qui regis Israël, intende ; qui deducis velut ovem Joseph. Qui sedes super cherubim, manifestare
      EN: Attend, You who rule Israel, who lead Joseph like a sheep. You who sit above the cherubim, reveal Yourself

79:3  LA: coram Ephraim, Benjamin, et Manasse. Excita potentiam tuam, et veni, ut salvos facias nos.
      EN: before Ephraim, Benjamin, and Manasseh. Stir up Your power and come to save us.

79:4  LA: Deus, converte nos, et ostende faciem tuam, et salvi erimus.
      EN: God, turn us back and show Your face, and we shall be saved.

79:5  LA: Domine Deus virtutum, quousque irasceris super orationem servi tui ?
      EN: Lord God of hosts, how long will You be angry at Your servant's prayer?

79:6  LA: cibabis nos pane lacrimarum, et potum dabis nobis in lacrimis in mensura ?
      EN: Will You feed us with the bread of tears and give us tears to drink in measure?

79:7  LA: Posuisti nos in contradictionem vicinis nostris, et inimici nostri subsannaverunt nos.
      EN: You have made us an object of contention to our neighbors, and our enemies have mocked us.

79:8  LA: Deus virtutum, converte nos, et ostende faciem tuam, et salvi erimus.
      EN: God of hosts, turn us back and show Your face, and we shall be saved.

79:9  LA: Vineam de Ægypto transtulisti : ejecisti gentes, et plantasti eam.
      EN: You transplanted a vine from Egypt; You drove out nations and planted it.

79:10  LA: Dux itineris fuisti in conspectu ejus ; plantasti radices ejus, et implevit terram.
       EN: You went before it as its guide. You planted its roots, and it filled the land.

79:11  LA: Operuit montes umbra ejus, et arbusta ejus cedros Dei.
       EN: Its shade covered the mountains, and its branches covered the cedars of God.

79:12  LA: Extendit palmites suos usque ad mare, et usque ad flumen propagines ejus.
       EN: It stretched its shoots as far as the sea and its tendrils as far as the river.

79:13  LA: Ut quid destruxisti maceriam ejus, et vindemiant eam omnes qui prætergrediuntur viam ?
       EN: Why have You broken down its enclosure, so that all who pass along the road gather its grapes?

79:14  LA: Exterminavit eam aper de silva, et singularis ferus depastus est eam.
       EN: The boar from the forest has ravaged it, and the solitary wild beast has fed on it.

79:15  LA: Deus virtutum, convertere, respice de cælo, et vide, et visita vineam istam :
       EN: God of hosts, turn back; look from heaven and see, and visit this vine,

79:16  LA: et perfice eam quam plantavit dextera tua, et super filium hominis quem confirmasti tibi.
       EN: and perfect what Your right hand planted, and look upon the son of man whom You strengthened for Yourself.

79:17  LA: Incensa igni et suffossa, ab increpatione vultus tui peribunt.
       EN: Burned with fire and dug up, they will perish at the rebuke of Your face.

79:18  LA: Fiat manus tua super virum dexteræ tuæ, et super filium hominis quem confirmasti tibi.
       EN: Let Your hand be upon the man of Your right hand and upon the son of man whom You strengthened for Yourself.

79:19  LA: Et non discedimus a te : vivificabis nos, et nomen tuum invocabimus.
       EN: We do not depart from You. You will give us life, and we will call upon Your name.

79:20  LA: Domine Deus virtutum, converte nos, et ostende faciem tuam, et salvi erimus.]
       EN: Lord God of hosts, turn us back and show Your face, and we shall be saved.

PSALMS 80

80:1  LA: In finem, pro torcularibus. Psalmus ipsi Asaph.
      EN: For the end. For the winepresses. A psalm of Asaph himself.

80:2  LA: [Exsultate Deo adjutori nostro ; jubilate Deo Jacob.
      EN: Rejoice in God our helper; shout for joy to the God of Jacob.

80:3  LA: Sumite psalmum, et date tympanum ; psalterium jucundum cum cithara.
      EN: Take up a psalm and sound the tambourine, the delightful harp with the lyre.

80:4  LA: Buccinate in neomenia tuba, in insigni die solemnitatis vestræ :
      EN: Blow the trumpet at the new moon, on the notable day of your solemn feast,

80:5  LA: quia præceptum in Israël est, et judicium Deo Jacob.
      EN: for it is a commandment in Israel and an ordinance of the God of Jacob.

80:6  LA: Testimonium in Joseph posuit illud, cum exiret de terra Ægypti ; linguam quam non noverat, audivit.
      EN: He established it as a testimony in Joseph when he went out from the land of Egypt; he heard a language he had not known.

80:7  LA: Divertit ab oneribus dorsum ejus ; manus ejus in cophino servierunt.
      EN: He turned his back away from burdens; his hands had served with the basket.

80:8  LA: In tribulatione invocasti me, et liberavi te. Exaudivi te in abscondito tempestatis ; probavi te apud aquam contradictionis.
      EN: In distress you called upon Me, and I delivered you. I answered you in the hidden place of the storm; I tested you at the water of contradiction.

80:9  LA: Audi, populus meus, et contestabor te. Israël, si audieris me,
      EN: Hear, My people, and I will bear witness to you. Israel, if you hear Me,

80:10  LA: non erit in te deus recens, neque adorabis deum alienum.
       EN: there shall be no new god among you, nor shall you worship a foreign god.

80:11  LA: Ego enim sum Dominus Deus tuus, qui eduxi te de terra Ægypti. Dilata os tuum, et implebo illud.
       EN: For I am the Lord your God, who brought you out of the land of Egypt. Open your mouth wide, and I will fill it.

80:12  LA: Et non audivit populus meus vocem meam, et Israël non intendit mihi.
       EN: My people did not hear My voice, and Israel did not attend to Me.

80:13  LA: Et dimisi eos secundum desideria cordis eorum ; ibunt in adinventionibus suis.
       EN: I let them go according to the desires of their hearts; they will walk in their own devices.

80:14  LA: Si populus meus audisset me, Israël si in viis meis ambulasset,
       EN: If My people had heard Me, if Israel had walked in My ways,

80:15  LA: pro nihilo forsitan inimicos eorum humiliassem, et super tribulantes eos misissem manum meam.
       EN: perhaps I would have humbled their enemies with ease and sent My hand against those who troubled them.

80:16  LA: Inimici Domini mentiti sunt ei, et erit tempus eorum in sæcula.
       EN: The Lord's enemies have lied to Him, and their time will be forever.

80:17  LA: Et cibavit eos ex adipe frumenti, et de petra melle saturavit eos.]
       EN: He fed them with the richness of wheat and filled them with honey from the rock.

PSALMS 81

81:1  LA: Psalmus Asaph. [Deus stetit in synagoga deorum ; in medio autem deos dijudicat.
      EN: A psalm of Asaph. God has stood in the assembly of gods; in their midst He judges the gods.

81:2  LA: Usquequo judicatis iniquitatem, et facies peccatorum sumitis ?
      EN: How long will you judge unjustly and favor the persons of sinners?

81:3  LA: Judicate egeno et pupillo ; humilem et pauperem justificate.
      EN: Give judgment for the needy and the orphan; vindicate the lowly and the poor.

81:4  LA: Eripite pauperem, et egenum de manu peccatoris liberate.
      EN: Rescue the poor and deliver the needy from the sinner's hand.

81:5  LA: Nescierunt, neque intellexerunt ; in tenebris ambulant : movebuntur omnia fundamenta terræ.
      EN: They have not known or understood; they walk in darkness. All the foundations of the earth will be shaken.

81:6  LA: Ego dixi : Dii estis, et filii Excelsi omnes.
      EN: I said, “You are gods, and all of you children of the Most High.”

81:7  LA: Vos autem sicut homines moriemini, et sicut unus de principibus cadetis.
      EN: Yet you will die like human beings and fall like one of the rulers.

81:8  LA: Surge, Deus, judica terram, quoniam tu hæreditabis in omnibus gentibus.]
      EN: Rise, God, judge the earth, for You will inherit among all nations.

PSALMS 82

82:1  LA: Canticum Psalmi Asaph.
      EN: A song. A psalm of Asaph.

82:2  LA: [Deus, quis similis erit tibi ? ne taceas, neque compescaris, Deus :
      EN: God, who will be like You? Do not be silent or hold back, God,

82:3  LA: quoniam ecce inimici tui sonuerunt, et qui oderunt te extulerunt caput.
      EN: for look, Your enemies have made an uproar, and those who hate You have raised their heads.

82:4  LA: Super populum tuum malignaverunt consilium, et cogitaverunt adversus sanctos tuos.
      EN: They have devised an evil plan against Your people and plotted against Your holy ones.

82:5  LA: Dixerunt : Venite, et disperdamus eos de gente, et non memoretur nomen Israël ultra.
      EN: They said, “Come, let us destroy them as a nation, so that the name of Israel will be remembered no more.”

82:6  LA: Quoniam cogitaverunt unanimiter ; simul adversum te testamentum disposuerunt :
      EN: For they have plotted with one mind; together they have established a covenant against You:

82:7  LA: tabernacula Idumæorum et Ismahelitæ, Moab et Agareni,
      EN: the tents of the Edomites and the Ishmaelites, Moab and the Hagrites,

82:8  LA: Gebal, et Ammon, et Amalec ; alienigenæ cum habitantibus Tyrum.
      EN: Gebal, Ammon, and Amalek, the foreigners with the inhabitants of Tyre.

82:9  LA: Etenim Assur venit cum illis : facti sunt in adjutorium filiis Lot.
      EN: Indeed, Assyria has come with them; they have become a help to the children of Lot.

82:10  LA: Fac illis sicut Madian et Sisaræ, sicut Jabin in torrente Cisson.
       EN: Deal with them as with Midian and Sisera, as with Jabin at the torrent Kishon.

82:11  LA: Disperierunt in Endor ; facti sunt ut stercus terræ.
       EN: They perished at Endor and became like dung upon the earth.

82:12  LA: Pone principes eorum sicut Oreb, et Zeb, et Zebee, et Salmana : omnes principes eorum,
       EN: Make their rulers like Oreb and Zeeb and Zebah and Zalmunna, all their rulers

82:13  LA: qui dixerunt : Hæreditate possideamus sanctuarium Dei.
       EN: who said, “Let us take God's sanctuary as our inheritance.”

82:14  LA: Deus meus, pone illos ut rotam, et sicut stipulam ante faciem venti.
       EN: My God, make them like a wheel and like stubble before the wind.

82:15  LA: Sicut ignis qui comburit silvam, et sicut flamma comburens montes,
       EN: As fire burns a forest and as a flame burns mountains,

82:16  LA: ita persequeris illos in tempestate tua, et in ira tua turbabis eos.
       EN: so You will pursue them in Your storm and trouble them in Your anger.

82:17  LA: Imple facies eorum ignominia, et quærent nomen tuum, Domine.
       EN: Fill their faces with shame, and they will seek Your name, Lord.

82:18  LA: Erubescant, et conturbentur in sæculum sæculi, et confundantur, et pereant.
       EN: Let them blush and be troubled forever and ever; let them be confounded and perish.

82:19  LA: Et cognoscant quia nomen tibi Dominus : tu solus Altissimus in omni terra.]
       EN: Let them know that Your name is the Lord; You alone are the Most High over all the earth.

PSALMS 83

83:1  LA: In finem, pro torcularibus filiis Core. Psalmus.
      EN: For the end. For the winepresses. For the sons of Korah. A psalm.

83:2  LA: [Quam dilecta tabernacula tua, Domine virtutum !
      EN: How beloved are Your tabernacles, Lord of hosts!

83:3  LA: Concupiscit, et deficit anima mea in atria Domini ; cor meum et caro mea exsultaverunt in Deum vivum.
      EN: My soul longs and faints for the Lord's courts; my heart and my flesh have rejoiced in the living God.

83:4  LA: Etenim passer invenit sibi domum, et turtur nidum sibi, ubi ponat pullos suos : altaria tua, Domine virtutum, rex meus, et Deus meus.
      EN: Even the sparrow has found a home for itself, and the turtledove a nest for itself where it may lay its young: Your altars, Lord of hosts, my King and my God.

83:5  LA: Beati qui habitant in domo tua, Domine ; in sæcula sæculorum laudabunt te.
      EN: Happy are those who dwell in Your house, Lord; they will praise You forever and ever.

83:6  LA: Beatus vir cujus est auxilium abs te : ascensiones in corde suo disposuit,
      EN: Happy is the man whose help is from You; he has arranged ascents in his heart,

83:7  LA: in valle lacrimarum, in loco quem posuit.
      EN: in the valley of tears, in the place he appointed.

83:8  LA: Etenim benedictionem dabit legislator ; ibunt de virtute in virtutem : videbitur Deus deorum in Sion.
      EN: For the lawgiver will give a blessing; they will go from strength to strength. The God of gods will be seen in Zion.

83:9  LA: Domine Deus virtutum, exaudi orationem meam ; auribus percipe, Deus Jacob.
      EN: Lord God of hosts, hear my prayer; give ear, God of Jacob.

83:10  LA: Protector noster, aspice, Deus, et respice in faciem christi tui.
       EN: Look, God our protector, and look upon the face of Your anointed.

83:11  LA: Quia melior est dies una in atriis tuis super millia ; elegi abjectus esse in domo Dei mei magis quam habitare in tabernaculis peccatorum.
       EN: For one day in Your courts is better than thousands. I have chosen to be cast aside in my God's house rather than dwell in the tents of sinners.

83:12  LA: Quia misericordiam et veritatem diligit Deus : gratiam et gloriam dabit Dominus.
       EN: For God loves mercy and truth; the Lord will give grace and glory. He will not deprive those who walk in innocence of good things. Lord of hosts, happy is the person who hopes in You.

83:13  LA: Quia misericordiam et veritatem diligit Deus : gratiam et gloriam dabit Dominus.
       EN: For God loves mercy and truth; the Lord will give grace and glory. He will not deprive those who walk in innocence of good things. Lord of hosts, happy is the person who hopes in You.

PSALMS 84

84:1  LA: In finem, filiis Core. Psalmus.
      EN: For the end. For the sons of Korah. A psalm.

84:2  LA: [Benedixisti, Domine, terram tuam ; avertisti captivitatem Jacob.
      EN: You have blessed Your land, Lord; You have turned back Jacob's captivity.

84:3  LA: Remisisti iniquitatem plebis tuæ ; operuisti omnia peccata eorum.
      EN: You have forgiven Your people's iniquity and covered all their sins.

84:4  LA: Mitigasti omnem iram tuam ; avertisti ab ira indignationis tuæ.
      EN: You have softened all Your anger; You have turned away from Your indignant wrath.

84:5  LA: Converte nos, Deus salutaris noster, et averte iram tuam a nobis.
      EN: Turn us back, God our savior, and turn Your anger away from us.

84:6  LA: Numquid in æternum irasceris nobis ? aut extendes iram tuam a generatione in generationem ?
      EN: Will You be angry with us forever, or extend Your anger from generation to generation?

84:7  LA: Deus, tu conversus vivificabis nos, et plebs tua lætabitur in te.
      EN: God, You will turn back and give us life, and Your people will rejoice in You.

84:8  LA: Ostende nobis, Domine, misericordiam tuam, et salutare tuum da nobis.
      EN: Show us Your mercy, Lord, and give us Your salvation.

84:9  LA: Audiam quid loquatur in me Dominus Deus, quoniam loquetur pacem in plebem suam, et super sanctos suos, et in eos qui convertuntur ad cor.
      EN: I will hear what the Lord God speaks within me, for He will speak peace to His people and to His holy ones and to those who turn back to the heart.

84:10  LA: Verumtamen prope timentes eum salutare ipsius, ut inhabitet gloria in terra nostra.
       EN: Surely His salvation is near those who fear Him, so that glory may dwell in our land.

84:11  LA: Misericordia et veritas obviaverunt sibi ; justitia et pax osculatæ sunt.
       EN: Mercy and truth have met one another; justice and peace have kissed.

84:12  LA: Veritas de terra orta est, et justitia de cælo prospexit.
       EN: Truth has sprung from the earth, and justice has looked down from heaven.

84:13  LA: Etenim Dominus dabit benignitatem, et terra nostra dabit fructum suum.
       EN: Indeed, the Lord will give goodness, and our land will give its fruit.

84:14  LA: Justitia ante eum ambulabit, et ponet in via gressus suos.]
       EN: Justice will walk before Him and set its footsteps on the way.

PSALMS 85

85:1  LA: Oratio ipsi David. [Inclina, Domine, aurem tuam et exaudi me, quoniam inops et pauper sum ego.
      EN: A prayer of David himself. Incline Your ear, Lord, and answer me, for I am needy and poor.

85:2  LA: Custodi animam meam, quoniam sanctus sum ; salvum fac servum tuum, Deus meus, sperantem in te.
      EN: Guard my soul, for I am holy; save Your servant who hopes in You, my God.

85:3  LA: Miserere mei, Domine, quoniam ad te clamavi tota die ;
      EN: Have mercy on me, Lord, for I have cried to You all day.

85:4  LA: lætifica animam servi tui, quoniam ad te, Domine, animam meam levavi.
      EN: Gladden Your servant's soul, for to You, Lord, I have lifted my soul.

85:5  LA: Quoniam tu, Domine, suavis et mitis, et multæ misericordiæ omnibus invocantibus te.
      EN: For You, Lord, are pleasant and gentle, and abundant in mercy to all who call upon You.

85:6  LA: Auribus percipe, Domine, orationem meam, et intende voci deprecationis meæ.
      EN: Give ear, Lord, to my prayer, and attend to the voice of my plea.

85:7  LA: In die tribulationis meæ clamavi ad te, quia exaudisti me.
      EN: On the day of my distress I cried to You, because You answered me.

85:8  LA: Non est similis tui in diis, Domine, et non est secundum opera tua.
      EN: There is none like You among the gods, Lord, and nothing to compare with Your works.

85:9  LA: Omnes gentes quascumque fecisti venient, et adorabunt coram te, Domine, et glorificabunt nomen tuum.
      EN: All the nations You have made will come and worship before You, Lord, and glorify Your name.

85:10  LA: Quoniam magnus es tu, et faciens mirabilia ; tu es Deus solus.
       EN: For You are great and do wonders; You alone are God.

85:11  LA: Deduc me, Domine, in via tua, et ingrediar in veritate tua ; lætetur cor meum, ut timeat nomen tuum.
       EN: Lead me, Lord, in Your way, and I will walk in Your truth. Let my heart rejoice to fear Your name.

85:12  LA: Confitebor tibi, Domine Deus meus, in toto corde meo, et glorificabo nomen tuum in æternum :
       EN: I will give You thanks with all my heart, Lord my God, and glorify Your name forever,

85:13  LA: quia misericordia tua magna est super me, et eruisti animam meam ex inferno inferiori.
       EN: for Your mercy toward me is great, and You have rescued my soul from the lower underworld.

85:14  LA: Deus, iniqui insurrexerunt super me, et synagoga potentium quæsierunt animam meam : et non proposuerunt te in conspectu suo.
       EN: God, the wicked have risen against me, and an assembly of the powerful have sought my soul, and they have not set You before their eyes.

85:15  LA: Et tu, Domine Deus, miserator et misericors ; patiens, et multæ misericordiæ, et verax.
       EN: But You, Lord God, are compassionate and merciful, patient, abundant in mercy, and true.

85:16  LA: Respice in me, et miserere mei ; da imperium tuum puero tuo, et salvum fac filium ancillæ tuæ.
       EN: Look upon me and have mercy on me; give Your authority to Your servant and save the son of Your maidservant.

85:17  LA: Fac mecum signum in bonum, ut videant qui oderunt me, et confundantur : quoniam tu, Domine, adjuvisti me, et consolatus es me.]
       EN: Work a sign for my good, so that those who hate me may see and be confounded, because You, Lord, have helped me and comforted me.

PSALMS 86

86:1  LA: Filiis Core. Psalmus cantici. [Fundamenta ejus in montibus sanctis ;
      EN: For the sons of Korah. A psalm of a song. Its foundations are on the holy mountains;

86:2  LA: diligit Dominus portas Sion super omnia tabernacula Jacob.
      EN: the Lord loves the gates of Zion above all the tabernacles of Jacob.

86:3  LA: Gloriosa dicta sunt de te, civitas Dei !
      EN: Glorious things have been spoken of you, city of God!

86:4  LA: Memor ero Rahab et Babylonis, scientium me ; ecce alienigenæ, et Tyrus, et populus Æthiopum, hi fuerunt illic.
      EN: I will remember Rahab and Babylon, who know Me. Look, foreigners and Tyre and the people of Ethiopia—these were there.

86:5  LA: Numquid Sion dicet : Homo et homo natus est in ea, et ipse fundavit eam Altissimus ?
      EN: Will Zion say, “This man and that man were born in her, and the Most High Himself founded her”?

86:6  LA: Dominus narrabit in scripturis populorum et principum, horum qui fuerunt in ea.
      EN: The Lord will recount in the records of peoples and rulers those who were in her.

86:7  LA: Sicut lætantium omnium habitatio est in te.]
      EN: The dwelling in you is like that of all who rejoice.

PSALMS 87

87:1  LA: Canticum Psalmi, filiis Core, in finem, pro Maheleth ad respondendum. Intellectus Eman Ezrahitæ.
      EN: A song. A psalm for the sons of Korah. For the end. For Mahalath, to respond. For understanding. Of Heman the Ezrahite.

87:2  LA: [Domine, Deus salutis meæ, in die clamavi et nocte coram te.
      EN: Lord, God of my salvation, I have cried by day and by night before You.

87:3  LA: Intret in conspectu tuo oratio mea, inclina aurem tuam ad precem meam.
      EN: Let my prayer enter Your presence; incline Your ear to my plea.

87:4  LA: Quia repleta est malis anima mea, et vita mea inferno appropinquavit.
      EN: For my soul is filled with evils, and my life has drawn near the underworld.

87:5  LA: Æstimatus sum cum descendentibus in lacum, factus sum sicut homo sine adjutorio,
      EN: I have been reckoned with those who descend into the pit; I have become like a person without help,

87:6  LA: inter mortuos liber ; sicut vulnerati dormientes in sepulchris, quorum non es memor amplius, et ipsi de manu tua repulsi sunt.
      EN: free among the dead, like the wounded sleeping in tombs, whom You remember no more and who have been cast away from Your hand.

87:7  LA: Posuerunt me in lacu inferiori, in tenebrosis, et in umbra mortis.
      EN: They have placed me in the lowest pit, in dark places and in the shadow of death.

87:8  LA: Super me confirmatus est furor tuus, et omnes fluctus tuos induxisti super me.
      EN: Your fury has settled upon me, and You have brought all Your waves over me.

87:9  LA: Longe fecisti notos meos a me ; posuerunt me abominationem sibi. Traditus sum, et non egrediebar ;
      EN: You have put my acquaintances far from me; they have made me an abomination to themselves. I was handed over and could not go out;

87:10  LA: oculi mei languerunt præ inopia. Clamavi ad te, Domine, tota die ; expandi ad te manus meas.
       EN: my eyes languished from want. I cried to You, Lord, all day; I spread out my hands to You.

87:11  LA: Numquid mortuis facies mirabilia ? aut medici suscitabunt, et confitebuntur tibi ?
       EN: Will You do wonders for the dead? Or will physicians raise them up, and will they give You thanks?

87:12  LA: Numquid narrabit aliquis in sepulchro misericordiam tuam, et veritatem tuam in perditione ?
       EN: Will anyone recount Your mercy in the tomb and Your truth in destruction?

87:13  LA: Numquid cognoscentur in tenebris mirabilia tua ? et justitia tua in terra oblivionis ?
       EN: Will Your wonders be known in the darkness, and Your justice in the land of forgetfulness?

87:14  LA: Et ego ad te, Domine, clamavi, et mane oratio mea præveniet te.
       EN: But I have cried to You, Lord, and in the morning my prayer will come before You.

87:15  LA: Ut quid, Domine, repellis orationem meam ; avertis faciem tuam a me ?
       EN: Why, Lord, do You reject my prayer and turn Your face away from me?

87:16  LA: Pauper sum ego, et in laboribus a juventute mea ; exaltatus autem, humiliatus sum et conturbatus.
       EN: I am poor and in labors from my youth; though raised up, I have been humbled and troubled.

87:17  LA: In me transierunt iræ tuæ, et terrores tui conturbaverunt me :
       EN: Your wrath has passed over me, and Your terrors have troubled me.

87:18  LA: circumdederunt me sicut aqua tota die ; circumdederunt me simul.
       EN: They have surrounded me like water all day; together they have surrounded me.

87:19  LA: Elongasti a me amicum et proximum, et notos meos a miseria.]
       EN: You have put friend and neighbor far from me, and my acquaintances because of misery.

PSALMS 88

88:1  LA: Intellectus Ethan Ezrahitæ.
      EN: For understanding. Of Ethan the Ezrahite.

88:2  LA: [Misericordias Domini in æternum cantabo ; in generationem et generationem annuntiabo veritatem tuam in ore meo.
      EN: I will sing the Lord's mercies forever; from generation to generation I will proclaim Your truth with my mouth.

88:3  LA: Quoniam dixisti : In æternum misericordia ædificabitur in cælis ; præparabitur veritas tua in eis.
      EN: For You have said: mercy will be built up forever in the heavens; Your truth will be established in them.

88:4  LA: Disposui testamentum electis meis ; juravi David servo meo :
      EN: I have established a covenant with My chosen ones; I have sworn to David My servant:

88:5  LA: Usque in æternum præparabo semen tuum, et ædificabo in generationem et generationem sedem tuam.
      EN: “I will establish your offspring forever and build up your throne from generation to generation.”

88:6  LA: Confitebuntur cæli mirabilia tua, Domine ; etenim veritatem tuam in ecclesia sanctorum.
      EN: The heavens will acknowledge Your wonders, Lord, and Your truth in the assembly of the holy ones.

88:7  LA: Quoniam quis in nubibus æquabitur Domino ; similis erit Deo in filiis Dei ?
      EN: For who among the clouds will equal the Lord or be like God among the children of God?

88:8  LA: Deus, qui glorificatur in consilio sanctorum, magnus et terribilis super omnes qui in circuitu ejus sunt.
      EN: God is glorified in the council of the holy ones, great and fearsome above all who surround Him.

88:9  LA: Domine Deus virtutum, quis similis tibi ? potens es, Domine, et veritas tua in circuitu tuo.
      EN: Lord God of hosts, who is like You? You are mighty, Lord, and Your truth surrounds You.

88:10  LA: Tu dominaris potestati maris ; motum autem fluctuum ejus tu mitigas.
       EN: You rule the sea's power, and You calm the movement of its waves.

88:11  LA: Tu humiliasti, sicut vulneratum, superbum ; in brachio virtutis tuæ dispersisti inimicos tuos.
       EN: You have humbled the proud one like a wounded man; with Your mighty arm You have scattered Your enemies.

88:12  LA: Tui sunt cæli, et tua est terra : orbem terræ, et plenitudinem ejus tu fundasti ;
       EN: The heavens are Yours, and the earth is Yours; You founded the world and all that fills it.

88:13  LA: aquilonem et mare tu creasti. Thabor et Hermon in nomine tuo exsultabunt :
       EN: You created the north and the sea. Tabor and Hermon will rejoice in Your name.

88:14  LA: tuum brachium cum potentia. Firmetur manus tua, et exaltetur dextera tua :
       EN: Your arm has power. Let Your hand be strengthened and Your right hand lifted high.

88:15  LA: justitia et judicium præparatio sedis tuæ : misericordia et veritas præcedent faciem tuam.
       EN: Justice and judgment are the preparation of Your throne; mercy and truth will go before Your face.

88:16  LA: Beatus populus qui scit jubilationem : Domine, in lumine vultus tui ambulabunt,
       EN: Happy is the people who know the shout of joy. Lord, they will walk in the light of Your face,

88:17  LA: et in nomine tuo exsultabunt tota die, et in justitia tua exaltabuntur.
       EN: and in Your name they will rejoice all day, and in Your justice they will be exalted.

88:18  LA: Quoniam gloria virtutis eorum tu es, et in beneplacito tuo exaltabitur cornu nostrum.
       EN: For You are the glory of their strength, and through Your good pleasure our horn will be lifted high.

88:19  LA: Quia Domini est assumptio nostra, et sancti Israël regis nostri.
       EN: For our protection belongs to the Lord and to the Holy One of Israel, our King.

88:20  LA: Tunc locutus es in visione sanctis tuis, et dixisti : Posui adjutorium in potente, et exaltavi electum de plebe mea.
       EN: Then You spoke in a vision to Your holy ones and said, “I have placed help in a mighty man and raised up one chosen from My people.

88:21  LA: Inveni David, servum meum ; oleo sancto meo unxi eum.
       EN: I have found David My servant; with My holy oil I have anointed him.

88:22  LA: Manus enim mea auxiliabitur ei, et brachium meum confortabit eum.
       EN: For My hand will help him, and My arm will strengthen him.

88:23  LA: Nihil proficiet inimicus in eo, et filius iniquitatis non apponet nocere ei.
       EN: The enemy will gain nothing against him, and the son of iniquity will no longer harm him.

88:24  LA: Et concidam a facie ipsius inimicos ejus, et odientes eum in fugam convertam.
       EN: I will cut down his enemies before him and turn those who hate him to flight.

88:25  LA: Et veritas mea et misericordia mea cum ipso, et in nomine meo exaltabitur cornu ejus.
       EN: My truth and My mercy are with him, and in My name his horn will be lifted high.

88:26  LA: Et ponam in mari manum ejus, et in fluminibus dexteram ejus.
       EN: I will set his hand upon the sea and his right hand upon the rivers.

88:27  LA: Ipse invocabit me : Pater meus es tu, Deus meus, et susceptor salutis meæ.
       EN: He will call upon Me, ‘You are my Father, my God, and the protector of my salvation.’

88:28  LA: Et ego primogenitum ponam illum, excelsum præ regibus terræ.
       EN: I will make him the firstborn, exalted above the kings of the earth.

88:29  LA: In æternum servabo illi misericordiam meam, et testamentum meum fidele ipsi.
       EN: I will keep My mercy for him forever, and My covenant will be faithful to him.

88:30  LA: Et ponam in sæculum sæculi semen ejus, et thronum ejus sicut dies cæli.
       EN: I will establish his offspring forever and ever, and his throne like the days of heaven.

88:31  LA: Si autem dereliquerint filii ejus legem meam, et in judiciis meis non ambulaverint ;
       EN: But if his children forsake My law and do not walk in My judgments,

88:32  LA: si justitias meas profanaverint, et mandata mea non custodierint :
       EN: if they profane My just ordinances and do not keep My commandments,

88:33  LA: visitabo in virga iniquitates eorum, et in verberibus peccata eorum ;
       EN: I will visit their iniquities with a rod and their sins with blows.

88:34  LA: misericordiam autem meam non dispergam ab eo, neque nocebo in veritate mea,
       EN: Yet I will not withdraw My mercy from him or prove false to My truth,

88:35  LA: neque profanabo testamentum meum : et quæ procedunt de labiis meis non faciam irrita.
       EN: and I will not profane My covenant or make void what proceeds from My lips.

88:36  LA: Semel juravi in sancto meo, si David mentiar :
       EN: Once I have sworn by My holiness: I will not lie to David.

88:37  LA: semen ejus in æternum manebit. Et thronus ejus sicut sol in conspectu meo,
       EN: His offspring will remain forever, and his throne like the sun before Me,

88:38  LA: et sicut luna perfecta in æternum, et testis in cælo fidelis.
       EN: and like the moon made perfect forever, a faithful witness in heaven.”

88:39  LA: Tu vero repulisti et despexisti ; distulisti christum tuum.
       EN: But You have rejected and despised; You have put off Your anointed.

88:40  LA: Evertisti testamentum servi tui ; profanasti in terra sanctuarium ejus.
       EN: You have overturned Your servant's covenant; You have profaned his sanctuary on the ground.

88:41  LA: Destruxisti omnes sepes ejus ; posuisti firmamentum ejus formidinem.
       EN: You have broken down all his enclosures and made his stronghold a terror.

88:42  LA: Diripuerunt eum omnes transeuntes viam ; factus est opprobrium vicinis suis.
       EN: All who pass along the road have plundered him; he has become a reproach to his neighbors.

88:43  LA: Exaltasti dexteram deprimentium eum ; lætificasti omnes inimicos ejus.
       EN: You have raised the right hand of those who bring him down and gladdened all his enemies.

88:44  LA: Avertisti adjutorium gladii ejus, et non es auxiliatus ei in bello.
       EN: You have turned away the help of his sword and have not helped him in battle.

88:45  LA: Destruxisti eum ab emundatione, et sedem ejus in terram collisisti.
       EN: You have brought him down from purity and dashed his throne to the ground.

88:46  LA: Minorasti dies temporis ejus ; perfudisti eum confusione.
       EN: You have shortened the days of his lifetime and drenched him with disgrace.

88:47  LA: Usquequo, Domine, avertis in finem ? exardescet sicut ignis ira tua ?
       EN: How long, Lord? Will You turn away forever? Will Your anger blaze like fire?

88:48  LA: Memorare quæ mea substantia : numquid enim vane constituisti omnes filios hominum ?
       EN: Remember what my substance is. Have You established all the children of men in vain?

88:49  LA: Quis est homo qui vivet et non videbit mortem ? eruet animam suam de manu inferi ?
       EN: What person will live and not see death? Will he rescue his soul from the hand of the underworld?

88:50  LA: Ubi sunt misericordiæ tuæ antiquæ, Domine, sicut jurasti David in veritate tua ?
       EN: Where are Your ancient mercies, Lord, as You swore to David in Your truth?

88:51  LA: Memor esto, Domine, opprobrii servorum tuorum, quod continui in sinu meo, multarum gentium :
       EN: Remember, Lord, the reproach of Your servants, which I have carried in my bosom from many nations,

88:52  LA: quod exprobraverunt inimici tui, Domine ; quod exprobraverunt commutationem christi tui.
       EN: with which Your enemies have reproached, Lord, with which they have reproached the change of Your anointed.

88:53  LA: Benedictus Dominus in æternum. Fiat, fiat.]
       EN: Blessed be the Lord forever. Let it be, let it be.

PSALMS 89

89:1  LA: Oratio Moysi, hominis Dei. [Domine, refugium factus es nobis a generatione in generationem.
      EN: A prayer of Moses, the man of God. Lord, You have become our refuge from generation to generation.

89:2  LA: Priusquam montes fierent, aut formaretur terra et orbis, a sæculo et usque in sæculum tu es, Deus.
      EN: Before the mountains came to be or the earth and the world were formed, from age to age You are, God.

89:3  LA: Ne avertas hominem in humilitatem : et dixisti : Convertimini, filii hominum.
      EN: Do not turn the human being toward humiliation; and You have said, “Turn back, children of men.”

89:4  LA: Quoniam mille anni ante oculos tuos tamquam dies hesterna quæ præteriit : et custodia in nocte
      EN: For a thousand years before Your eyes are like yesterday, which has passed, and a watch in the night,

89:5  LA: quæ pro nihilo habentur, eorum anni erunt.
      EN: things counted as nothing—such their years will be. In the morning let it pass like grass; in the morning let it bloom and pass; in the evening let it fall, grow hard, and wither.

89:6  LA: quæ pro nihilo habentur, eorum anni erunt.
      EN: things counted as nothing—such their years will be. In the morning let it pass like grass; in the morning let it bloom and pass; in the evening let it fall, grow hard, and wither.

89:7  LA: Quia defecimus in ira tua, et in furore tuo turbati sumus.
      EN: For we have failed in Your anger and have been troubled in Your fury.

89:8  LA: Posuisti iniquitates nostras in conspectu tuo ; sæculum nostrum in illuminatione vultus tui.
      EN: You have set our iniquities before You, our age in the light of Your face.

89:9  LA: Quoniam omnes dies nostri defecerunt, et in ira tua defecimus. Anni nostri sicut aranea meditabuntur ;
      EN: For all our days have dwindled, and we have failed in Your anger. Our years will be reckoned like a spider.

89:10  LA: dies annorum nostrorum in ipsis septuaginta anni. Si autem in potentatibus octoginta anni, et amplius eorum labor et dolor ; quoniam supervenit mansuetudo, et corripiemur.
       EN: The days of our years are seventy years in all, or eighty years with strength, and what lies beyond them is labor and pain; for gentleness has come upon us, and we shall be chastened.

89:11  LA: Quis novit potestatem iræ tuæ, et præ timore tuo iram tuam
       EN: Who knows the power of Your anger and, from fear of You, Your wrath,

89:12  LA: dinumerare ? Dexteram tuam sic notam fac, et eruditos corde in sapientia.
       EN: to count it? In this way make Your right hand known, and those instructed in heart in wisdom.

89:13  LA: Convertere, Domine ; usquequo ? et deprecabilis esto super servos tuos.
       EN: Turn back, Lord—how long?—and be moved by prayer for Your servants.

89:14  LA: Repleti sumus mane misericordia tua ; et exsultavimus, et delectati sumus omnibus diebus nostris.
       EN: In the morning we have been filled with Your mercy, and we have rejoiced and delighted all our days.

89:15  LA: Lætati sumus pro diebus quibus nos humiliasti ; annis quibus vidimus mala.
       EN: We have rejoiced in return for the days You humbled us, the years in which we saw evils.

89:16  LA: Respice in servos tuos et in opera tua, et dirige filios eorum.
       EN: Look upon Your servants and Your works, and guide their children.

89:17  LA: Et sit splendor Domini Dei nostri super nos, et opera manuum nostrarum dirige super nos, et opus manuum nostrarum dirige.]
       EN: Let the splendor of the Lord our God be upon us, and direct the works of our hands over us; direct the work of our hands.

PSALMS 90

90:1  LA: Laus cantici David. [Qui habitat in adjutorio Altissimi, in protectione Dei cæli commorabitur.
      EN: The praise of a song of David. Whoever dwells in the help of the Most High will abide under the protection of the God of heaven.

90:2  LA: Dicet Domino : Susceptor meus es tu, et refugium meum ; Deus meus, sperabo in eum.
      EN: He will say to the Lord, “You are my protector and my refuge, my God; I will hope in Him.”

90:3  LA: Quoniam ipse liberavit me de laqueo venantium, et a verbo aspero.
      EN: For He has delivered me from the hunters' snare and from the harsh word.

90:4  LA: Scapulis suis obumbrabit tibi, et sub pennis ejus sperabis.
      EN: He will overshadow you with His shoulders, and beneath His wings you will hope. His truth will surround you with a shield; you will not fear the terror of night,

90:5  LA: Scapulis suis obumbrabit tibi, et sub pennis ejus sperabis.
      EN: He will overshadow you with His shoulders, and beneath His wings you will hope. His truth will surround you with a shield; you will not fear the terror of night,

90:6  LA: a sagitta volante in die, a negotio perambulante in tenebris, ab incursu, et dæmonio meridiano.
      EN: the arrow flying by day, the trouble that walks about in the darkness, the assault, or the noonday demon.

90:7  LA: Cadent a latere tuo mille, et decem millia a dextris tuis ; ad te autem non appropinquabit.
      EN: A thousand will fall at your side and ten thousand at your right hand, but it will not come near you.

90:8  LA: Verumtamen oculis tuis considerabis, et retributionem peccatorum videbis.
      EN: Yet with your eyes you will look and see the repayment of sinners.

90:9  LA: Quoniam tu es, Domine, spes mea ; Altissimum posuisti refugium tuum.
      EN: For You, Lord, are my hope; you have made the Most High your refuge.

90:10  LA: Non accedet ad te malum, et flagellum non appropinquabit tabernaculo tuo.
       EN: Evil will not approach you, and a scourge will not draw near your tent.

90:11  LA: Quoniam angelis suis mandavit de te, ut custodiant te in omnibus viis tuis.
       EN: For He has commanded His angels concerning you, to guard you in all your ways.

90:12  LA: In manibus portabunt te, ne forte offendas ad lapidem pedem tuum.
       EN: They will carry you in their hands, lest you strike your foot against a stone.

90:13  LA: Super aspidem et basiliscum ambulabis, et conculcabis leonem et draconem.
       EN: You will walk upon the asp and the basilisk, and trample the lion and the dragon.

90:14  LA: Quoniam in me speravit, liberabo eum ; protegam eum, quoniam cognovit nomen meum.
       EN: Because he has hoped in Me, I will deliver him; I will protect him, because he has known My name.

90:15  LA: Clamabit ad me, et ego exaudiam eum ; cum ipso sum in tribulatione : eripiam eum, et glorificabo eum.
       EN: He will cry to Me, and I will answer him. I am with him in distress; I will rescue him and glorify him.

90:16  LA: Longitudine dierum replebo eum, et ostendam illi salutare meum.]
       EN: I will fill him with length of days and show him My salvation.

PSALMS 91

91:1  LA: Psalmus cantici, in die sabbati.
      EN: A psalm of a song, on the sabbath day.

91:2  LA: [Bonum est confiteri Domino, et psallere nomini tuo, Altissime :
      EN: It is good to give thanks to the Lord and sing psalms to Your name, Most High,

91:3  LA: ad annuntiandum mane misericordiam tuam, et veritatem tuam per noctem,
      EN: to proclaim Your mercy in the morning and Your truth through the night,

91:4  LA: in decachordo, psalterio ; cum cantico, in cithara.
      EN: on the ten-stringed harp, with a song upon the lyre.

91:5  LA: Quia delectasti me, Domine, in factura tua ; et in operibus manuum tuarum exsultabo.
      EN: For You have delighted me, Lord, by what You have made, and I will rejoice in the works of Your hands.

91:6  LA: Quam magnificata sunt opera tua, Domine ! nimis profundæ factæ sunt cogitationes tuæ.
      EN: How great Your works have become, Lord! Your thoughts have grown exceedingly deep.

91:7  LA: Vir insipiens non cognoscet, et stultus non intelliget hæc.
      EN: The senseless man will not know, and the fool will not understand these things.

91:8  LA: Cum exorti fuerint peccatores sicut fœnum, et apparuerint omnes qui operantur iniquitatem, ut intereant in sæculum sæculi :
      EN: When sinners spring up like grass and all who work iniquity appear, it is so that they may perish forever and ever.

91:9  LA: tu autem Altissimus in æternum, Domine.
      EN: But You, Lord, are Most High forever.

91:10  LA: Quoniam ecce inimici tui, Domine, quoniam ecce inimici tui peribunt ; et dispergentur omnes qui operantur iniquitatem.
       EN: For look, Your enemies, Lord—for look, Your enemies will perish, and all who work iniquity will be scattered.

91:11  LA: Et exaltabitur sicut unicornis cornu meum, et senectus mea in misericordia uberi.
       EN: My horn will be lifted high like that of a one-horned beast, and my old age will be in abundant mercy.

91:12  LA: Et despexit oculus meus inimicos meos, et in insurgentibus in me malignantibus audiet auris mea.
       EN: My eye has looked down upon my enemies, and my ear will hear of those who rise against me to do evil.

91:13  LA: Justus ut palma florebit ; sicut cedrus Libani multiplicabitur.
       EN: The just man will bloom like a palm tree; he will grow like a cedar of Lebanon.

91:14  LA: Plantati in domo Domini, in atriis domus Dei nostri florebunt.
       EN: Planted in the Lord's house, they will bloom in the courts of our God's house.

91:15  LA: Adhuc multiplicabuntur in senecta uberi, et bene patientes erunt :
       EN: They will still increase in a fruitful old age and will endure well,

91:16  LA: ut annuntient quoniam rectus Dominus Deus noster, et non est iniquitas in eo.]
       EN: to proclaim that the Lord our God is upright and that there is no iniquity in Him.

PSALMS 92

92:1  LA: Laus cantici ipsi David, in die ante sabbatum, quando fundata est terra. [Dominus regnavit, decorem indutus est : indutus est Dominus fortitudinem, et præcinxit se. Etenim firmavit orbem terræ, qui non commovebitur.
      EN: The praise of a song of David himself, on the day before the sabbath, when the earth was founded. The Lord has reigned; He is clothed in beauty. The Lord is clothed in strength and has girded Himself. Indeed, He has established the world, which will not be shaken.

92:2  LA: Parata sedes tua ex tunc ; a sæculo tu es.
      EN: Your throne has been prepared from that time; You are from of old.

92:3  LA: Elevaverunt flumina, Domine, elevaverunt flumina vocem suam ; elevaverunt flumina fluctus suos,
      EN: The rivers have lifted up, Lord, the rivers have lifted up their voice; the rivers have lifted up their waves,

92:4  LA: a vocibus aquarum multarum. Mirabiles elationes maris ; mirabilis in altis Dominus.
      EN: at the voices of many waters. Wondrous are the swellings of the sea; wondrous is the Lord on high.

92:5  LA: Testimonia tua credibilia facta sunt nimis ; domum tuam decet sanctitudo, Domine, in longitudinem dierum.]
      EN: Your testimonies have become exceedingly trustworthy. Holiness befits Your house, Lord, for length of days.

PSALMS 93

93:1  LA: Psalmus ipsi David, quarta sabbati. [Deus ultionum Dominus ; Deus ultionum libere egit.
      EN: A psalm of David himself, on the fourth day of the week. The Lord is the God of vengeance; the God of vengeance has acted freely.

93:2  LA: Exaltare, qui judicas terram ; redde retributionem superbis.
      EN: Rise high, You who judge the earth; give repayment to the proud.

93:3  LA: Usquequo peccatores, Domine, usquequo peccatores gloriabuntur ;
      EN: How long will sinners, Lord, how long will sinners boast,

93:4  LA: effabuntur et loquentur iniquitatem ; loquentur omnes qui operantur injustitiam ?
      EN: pouring out and speaking iniquity? How long will all who work injustice speak?

93:5  LA: Populum tuum, Domine, humiliaverunt, et hæreditatem tuam vexaverunt.
      EN: They have humbled Your people, Lord, and afflicted Your inheritance.

93:6  LA: Viduam et advenam interfecerunt, et pupillos occiderunt.
      EN: They have killed the widow and the foreign resident and slain the orphans.

93:7  LA: Et dixerunt : Non videbit Dominus, nec intelliget Deus Jacob.
      EN: They said, “The Lord will not see, nor will the God of Jacob understand.”

93:8  LA: Intelligite, insipientes in populo ; et stulti, aliquando sapite.
      EN: Understand, you senseless among the people; you fools, at last be wise.

93:9  LA: Qui plantavit aurem non audiet ? aut qui finxit oculum non considerat ?
      EN: Will the One who planted the ear not hear? Does the One who formed the eye not look?

93:10  LA: Qui corripit gentes non arguet, qui docet hominem scientiam ?
       EN: Will the One who chastens nations not rebuke, the One who teaches humanity knowledge?

93:11  LA: Dominus scit cogitationes hominum, quoniam vanæ sunt.
       EN: The Lord knows human thoughts, that they are futile.

93:12  LA: Beatus homo quem tu erudieris, Domine, et de lege tua docueris eum :
       EN: Happy is the person whom You instruct, Lord, and whom You teach from Your law,

93:13  LA: ut mitiges ei a diebus malis, donec fodiatur peccatori fovea.
       EN: to grant him relief from evil days until a pit is dug for the sinner.

93:14  LA: Quia non repellet Dominus plebem suam, et hæreditatem suam non derelinquet,
       EN: For the Lord will not reject His people or forsake His inheritance,

93:15  LA: quoadusque justitia convertatur in judicium : et qui juxta illam, omnes qui recto sunt corde.
       EN: until justice returns to judgment, and all the upright of heart stand beside it.

93:16  LA: Quis consurget mihi adversus malignantes ? aut quis stabit mecum adversus operantes iniquitatem ?
       EN: Who will rise for me against evildoers, or stand with me against those who work iniquity?

93:17  LA: Nisi quia Dominus adjuvit me, paulominus habitasset in inferno anima mea.
       EN: Had the Lord not helped me, my soul would almost have dwelt in the underworld.

93:18  LA: Si dicebam : Motus est pes meus : misericordia tua, Domine, adjuvabat me.
       EN: If I said, “My foot has slipped,” Your mercy, Lord, helped me.

93:19  LA: Secundum multitudinem dolorum meorum in corde meo, consolationes tuæ lætificaverunt animam meam.
       EN: In proportion to the multitude of pains in my heart, Your consolations gladdened my soul.

93:20  LA: Numquid adhæret tibi sedes iniquitatis, qui fingis laborem in præcepto ?
       EN: Can a throne of iniquity cling to You, who fashion toil in a commandment?

93:21  LA: Captabunt in animam justi, et sanguinem innocentem condemnabunt.
       EN: They will hunt the soul of the just man and condemn innocent blood.

93:22  LA: Et factus est mihi Dominus in refugium, et Deus meus in adjutorium spei meæ.
       EN: The Lord has become a refuge for me, and my God a help for my hope.

93:23  LA: Et reddet illis iniquitatem ipsorum, et in malitia eorum disperdet eos : disperdet illos Dominus Deus noster.]
       EN: He will repay them their iniquity and destroy them in their malice; the Lord our God will destroy them.

PSALMS 94

94:1  LA: Laus cantici ipsi David. [Venite, exsultemus Domino ; jubilemus Deo salutari nostro ;
      EN: The praise of a song of David himself. Come, let us rejoice in the Lord; let us shout for joy to God our savior.

94:2  LA: præoccupemus faciem ejus in confessione, et in psalmis jubilemus ei :
      EN: Let us come before His face with thanksgiving and shout for joy to Him in psalms,

94:3  LA: quoniam Deus magnus Dominus, et rex magnus super omnes deos.
      EN: for the Lord is a great God and a great King above all gods.

94:4  LA: Quia in manu ejus sunt omnes fines terræ, et altitudines montium ipsius sunt ;
      EN: For all the ends of the earth are in His hand, and the mountain heights are His.

94:5  LA: quoniam ipsius est mare, et ipse fecit illud, et siccam manus ejus formaverunt.
      EN: For the sea is His, and He made it, and His hands formed the dry land.

94:6  LA: Venite, adoremus, et procidamus, et ploremus ante Dominum qui fecit nos :
      EN: Come, let us worship and fall down and weep before the Lord who made us,

94:7  LA: quia ipse est Dominus Deus noster, et nos populus pascuæ ejus, et oves manus ejus.
      EN: for He is the Lord our God, and we are the people of His pasture and the sheep of His hand.

94:8  LA: Hodie si vocem ejus audieritis, nolite obdurare corda vestra
      EN: Today, if you hear His voice, do not harden your hearts as in the provocation, as on the day of testing in the wilderness, where your fathers tested Me; they tried Me and saw My works.

94:9  LA: Hodie si vocem ejus audieritis, nolite obdurare corda vestra
      EN: Today, if you hear His voice, do not harden your hearts as in the provocation, as on the day of testing in the wilderness, where your fathers tested Me; they tried Me and saw My works.

94:10  LA: Quadraginta annis offensus fui generationi illi, et dixi : Semper hi errant corde.
       EN: For forty years I was offended by that generation and said, “They always wander in heart.”

94:11  LA: Et isti non cognoverunt vias meas : ut juravi in ira mea : Si introibunt in requiem meam.]
       EN: They have not known My ways, so I swore in My anger, “They shall not enter My rest.”

PSALMS 95

95:1  LA: Canticum ipsi David, quando domus ædificabatur post captivitatem. [Cantate Domino canticum novum ; cantate Domino omnis terra.
      EN: A song of David himself, when the house was being built after the captivity. Sing to the Lord a new song; sing to the Lord, all the earth.

95:2  LA: Cantate Domino, et benedicite nomini ejus ; annuntiate de die in diem salutare ejus.
      EN: Sing to the Lord and bless His name; proclaim His salvation from day to day.

95:3  LA: Annuntiate inter gentes gloriam ejus ; in omnibus populis mirabilia ejus.
      EN: Proclaim His glory among the nations, His wonders among all peoples.

95:4  LA: Quoniam magnus Dominus, et laudabilis nimis : terribilis est super omnes deos.
      EN: For the Lord is great and exceedingly worthy of praise; He is fearsome above all gods.

95:5  LA: Quoniam omnes dii gentium dæmonia ; Dominus autem cælos fecit.
      EN: For all the gods of the nations are demons, but the Lord made the heavens.

95:6  LA: Confessio et pulchritudo in conspectu ejus ; sanctimonia et magnificentia in sanctificatione ejus.
      EN: Praise and beauty are before Him; holiness and magnificence are in His sanctuary.

95:7  LA: Afferte Domino, patriæ gentium, afferte Domino gloriam et honorem ;
      EN: Bring to the Lord, families of nations, bring to the Lord glory and honor.

95:8  LA: afferte Domino gloriam nomini ejus. Tollite hostias, et introite in atria ejus ;
      EN: Bring to the Lord glory for His name. Take up offerings and enter His courts;

95:9  LA: adorate Dominum in atrio sancto ejus. Commoveatur a facie ejus universa terra ;
      EN: worship the Lord in His holy court. Let all the earth tremble before Him.

95:10  LA: dicite in gentibus, quia Dominus regnavit. Etenim correxit orbem terræ, qui non commovebitur ; judicabit populos in æquitate.
       EN: Say among the nations that the Lord has reigned. Indeed, He has set the world firm, so that it will not be shaken; He will judge the peoples with equity.

95:11  LA: Lætentur cæli, et exsultet terra ; commoveatur mare et plenitudo ejus ;
       EN: Let the heavens rejoice and the earth exult; let the sea and all that fills it stir.

95:12  LA: gaudebunt campi, et omnia quæ in eis sunt. Tunc exsultabunt omnia ligna silvarum
       EN: The fields and everything in them will rejoice; then all the trees of the forests will exult

95:13  LA: a facie Domini, quia venit, quoniam venit judicare terram. Judicabit orbem terræ in æquitate, et populos in veritate sua.]
       EN: before the Lord, for He comes, for He comes to judge the earth. He will judge the world with equity and the peoples in His truth.

PSALMS 96

96:1  LA: Huic David, quando terra ejus restituta est. [Dominus regnavit : exsultet terra ; lætentur insulæ multæ.
      EN: For this David, when his land was restored. The Lord has reigned; let the earth rejoice, let the many islands be glad.

96:2  LA: Nubes et caligo in circuitu ejus ; justitia et judicium correctio sedis ejus.
      EN: Cloud and darkness surround Him; justice and judgment are the foundation of His throne.

96:3  LA: Ignis ante ipsum præcedet, et inflammabit in circuitu inimicos ejus.
      EN: Fire will go before Him and set His enemies ablaze all around.

96:4  LA: Illuxerunt fulgura ejus orbi terræ ; vidit, et commota est terra.
      EN: His lightning lit up the world; the earth saw and trembled.

96:5  LA: Montes sicut cera fluxerunt a facie Domini ; a facie Domini omnis terra.
      EN: The mountains melted like wax before the Lord, all the earth before the Lord.

96:6  LA: Annuntiaverunt cæli justitiam ejus, et viderunt omnes populi gloriam ejus.
      EN: The heavens proclaimed His justice, and all the peoples saw His glory.

96:7  LA: Confundantur omnes qui adorant sculptilia, et qui gloriantur in simulacris suis. Adorate eum omnes angeli ejus.
      EN: Let all who worship carved images be confounded, all who boast in their idols. Worship Him, all His angels.

96:8  LA: Audivit, et lætata est Sion, et exsultaverunt filiæ Judæ propter judicia tua, Domine.
      EN: Zion heard and was glad, and the daughters of Judah rejoiced because of Your judgments, Lord.

96:9  LA: Quoniam tu Dominus altissimus super omnem terram ; nimis exaltatus es super omnes deos.
      EN: For You, Lord, are Most High over all the earth; You are exalted far above all gods.

96:10  LA: Qui diligitis Dominum, odite malum : custodit Dominus animas sanctorum suorum ; de manu peccatoris liberabit eos.
       EN: You who love the Lord, hate evil. The Lord guards the souls of His holy ones; He will deliver them from the sinner's hand.

96:11  LA: Lux orta est justo, et rectis corde lætitia.
       EN: Light has risen for the just, and gladness for the upright of heart.
