96:12  LA: Lætamini, justi, in Domino, et confitemini memoriæ sanctificationis ejus.]
       EN: Rejoice in the Lord, you just, and give thanks at the remembrance of His holiness.

PSALMS 97

97:1  LA: Psalmus ipsi David. [Cantate Domino canticum novum, quia mirabilia fecit. Salvavit sibi dextera ejus, et brachium sanctum ejus.
      EN: A psalm of David himself. Sing to the Lord a new song, for He has done wonders. His right hand and His holy arm have won salvation for Him.

97:2  LA: Notum fecit Dominus salutare suum ; in conspectu gentium revelavit justitiam suam.
      EN: The Lord has made His salvation known; before the nations He has revealed His justice.

97:3  LA: Recordatus est misericordiæ suæ, et veritatis suæ domui Israël. Viderunt omnes termini terræ salutare Dei nostri.
      EN: He has remembered His mercy and His truth toward the house of Israel. All the ends of the earth have seen the salvation of our God.

97:4  LA: Jubilate Deo, omnis terra ; cantate, et exsultate, et psallite.
      EN: Shout for joy to God, all the earth; sing and rejoice and make music.

97:5  LA: Psallite Domino in cithara ; in cithara et voce psalmi ;
      EN: Sing psalms to the Lord with the lyre, with the lyre and the voice of a psalm,

97:6  LA: in tubis ductilibus, et voce tubæ corneæ. Jubilate in conspectu regis Domini :
      EN: with hammered trumpets and the sound of a horn trumpet. Shout for joy before the King, the Lord.

97:7  LA: moveatur mare, et plenitudo ejus ; orbis terrarum, et qui habitant in eo.
      EN: Let the sea and all that fills it stir, the world and those who dwell in it.

97:8  LA: Flumina plaudent manu ; simul montes exsultabunt
      EN: The rivers will clap their hands; together the mountains will rejoice

97:9  LA: a conspectu Domini : quoniam venit judicare terram. Judicabit orbem terrarum in justitia, et populos in æquitate.]
      EN: before the Lord, for He comes to judge the earth. He will judge the world with justice and the peoples with equity.

PSALMS 98

98:1  LA: Psalmus ipsi David. [Dominus regnavit : irascantur populi ; qui sedet super cherubim : moveatur terra.
      EN: A psalm of David himself. The Lord has reigned; let the peoples be angry. He sits above the cherubim; let the earth be shaken.

98:2  LA: Dominus in Sion magnus, et excelsus super omnes populos.
      EN: The Lord is great in Zion and exalted above all peoples.

98:3  LA: Confiteantur nomini tuo magno, quoniam terribile et sanctum est,
      EN: Let them praise Your great name, for it is fearsome and holy,

98:4  LA: et honor regis judicium diligit. Tu parasti directiones ; judicium et justitiam in Jacob tu fecisti.
      EN: and the king's honor loves judgment. You have established upright ways; You have carried out judgment and justice in Jacob.

98:5  LA: Exaltate Dominum Deum nostrum, et adorate scabellum pedum ejus, quoniam sanctum est.
      EN: Exalt the Lord our God and worship His footstool, for it is holy.

98:6  LA: Moyses et Aaron in sacerdotibus ejus, et Samuel inter eos qui invocant nomen ejus : invocabant Dominum, et ipse exaudiebat eos ;
      EN: Moses and Aaron were among His priests, and Samuel among those who call upon His name. They called upon the Lord, and He answered them;

98:7  LA: in columna nubis loquebatur ad eos. Custodiebant testimonia ejus, et præceptum quod dedit illis.
      EN: He spoke to them in the pillar of cloud. They kept His testimonies and the commandment He gave them.

98:8  LA: Domine Deus noster, tu exaudiebas eos ; Deus, tu propitius fuisti eis, et ulciscens in omnes adinventiones eorum.
      EN: Lord our God, You answered them; God, You were forgiving toward them and took vengeance on all their devices.

98:9  LA: Exaltate Dominum Deum nostrum, et adorate in monte sancto ejus, quoniam sanctus Dominus Deus noster.]
      EN: Exalt the Lord our God and worship on His holy mountain, for the Lord our God is holy.

PSALMS 99

99:1  LA: Psalmus in confessione.
      EN: A psalm of thanksgiving. Shout for joy to God, all the earth; serve the Lord with gladness. Enter His presence with rejoicing.

99:2  LA: Psalmus in confessione.
      EN: A psalm of thanksgiving. Shout for joy to God, all the earth; serve the Lord with gladness. Enter His presence with rejoicing.

99:3  LA: Scitote quoniam Dominus ipse est Deus ; ipse fecit nos, et non ipsi nos : populus ejus, et oves pascuæ ejus.
      EN: Know that the Lord Himself is God. He made us, and we did not make ourselves: His people and the sheep of His pasture.

99:4  LA: Introite portas ejus in confessione ; atria ejus in hymnis : confitemini illi. Laudate nomen ejus,
      EN: Enter His gates with thanksgiving, His courts with hymns; give thanks to Him. Praise His name,

99:5  LA: quoniam suavis est Dominus, in æternum misericordia ejus, et usque in generationem et generationem veritas ejus.]
      EN: for the Lord is pleasant, His mercy is forever, and His truth from generation to generation.

PSALMS 100

100:1  LA: Psalmus ipsi David. [Misericordiam et judicium cantabo tibi, Domine ; psallam,
       EN: A psalm of David himself. I will sing to You of mercy and judgment, Lord; I will sing psalms

100:2  LA: et intelligam in via immaculata : quando venies ad me ? Perambulabam in innocentia cordis mei, in medio domus meæ.
       EN: and gain understanding in the undefiled way. When will You come to me? I walked in the innocence of my heart within my house.

100:3  LA: Non proponebam ante oculos meos rem injustam ; facientes prævaricationes odivi ; non adhæsit mihi
       EN: I did not set an unjust thing before my eyes; I hated those who acted treacherously. There did not cling to me

100:4  LA: cor pravum ; declinantem a me malignum non cognoscebam.
       EN: a perverse heart; I did not know the evildoer who turned away from me.

100:5  LA: Detrahentem secreto proximo suo, hunc persequebar : superbo oculo, et insatiabili corde, cum hoc non edebam.
       EN: The one who secretly slandered his neighbor I pursued; with one of proud eye and insatiable heart I did not eat.

100:6  LA: Oculi mei ad fideles terræ, ut sedeant mecum ; ambulans in via immaculata, hic mihi ministrabat.
       EN: My eyes were upon the faithful of the land, that they might sit with me; the one who walked in an undefiled way ministered to me.

100:7  LA: Non habitabit in medio domus meæ qui facit superbiam ; qui loquitur iniqua non direxit in conspectu oculorum meorum.
       EN: Whoever acts proudly will not dwell within my house; whoever speaks unjust things did not prosper before my eyes.

100:8  LA: In matutino interficiebam omnes peccatores terræ, ut disperderem de civitate Domini omnes operantes iniquitatem.]
       EN: At daybreak I killed all the sinners of the land, to destroy from the Lord's city all who work iniquity.

PSALMS 101

101:1  LA: Oratio pauperis, cum anxius fuerit, et in conspectu Domini effuderit precem suam.
       EN: A prayer of the poor man when he is anxious and pours out his plea before the Lord.

101:2  LA: [Domine, exaudi orationem meam, et clamor meus ad te veniat.
       EN: Lord, hear my prayer, and let my cry come to You.

101:3  LA: Non avertas faciem tuam a me : in quacumque die tribulor, inclina ad me aurem tuam ; in quacumque die invocavero te, velociter exaudi me.
       EN: Do not turn Your face away from me. On whatever day I am distressed, incline Your ear to me; on whatever day I call upon You, answer me quickly.

101:4  LA: Quia defecerunt sicut fumus dies mei, et ossa mea sicut cremium aruerunt.
       EN: For my days have dwindled like smoke, and my bones have dried like kindling.

101:5  LA: Percussus sum ut fœnum, et aruit cor meum, quia oblitus sum comedere panem meum.
       EN: I have been struck like grass, and my heart has withered, because I forgot to eat my bread.

101:6  LA: A voce gemitus mei adhæsit os meum carni meæ.
       EN: At the sound of my groaning my bone has clung to my flesh.

101:7  LA: Similis factus sum pellicano solitudinis ; factus sum sicut nycticorax in domicilio.
       EN: I have become like a pelican of the wilderness, like a night raven in a dwelling.

101:8  LA: Vigilavi, et factus sum sicut passer solitarius in tecto.
       EN: I have kept watch and become like a solitary sparrow on a roof.

101:9  LA: Tota die exprobrabant mihi inimici mei, et qui laudabant me adversum me jurabant :
       EN: All day my enemies reproached me, and those who praised me swore against me,

101:10  LA: quia cinerem tamquam panem manducabam, et potum meum cum fletu miscebam,
        EN: because I ate ashes like bread and mixed my drink with weeping,

101:11  LA: a facie iræ et indignationis tuæ : quia elevans allisisti me.
        EN: before Your anger and indignation, for You lifted me up and dashed me down.

101:12  LA: Dies mei sicut umbra declinaverunt, et ego sicut fœnum arui.
        EN: My days have declined like a shadow, and I have withered like grass.

101:13  LA: Tu autem, Domine, in æternum permanes, et memoriale tuum in generationem et generationem.
        EN: But You, Lord, remain forever, and Your remembrance from generation to generation.

101:14  LA: Tu exsurgens misereberis Sion, quia tempus miserendi ejus, quia venit tempus :
        EN: You will rise and have mercy on Zion, for it is time to have mercy on her, for the time has come.

101:15  LA: quoniam placuerunt servis tuis lapides ejus, et terræ ejus miserebuntur.
        EN: For her stones have pleased Your servants, and they will have pity on her earth.

101:16  LA: Et timebunt gentes nomen tuum, Domine, et omnes reges terræ gloriam tuam :
        EN: The nations will fear Your name, Lord, and all the kings of the earth Your glory,

101:17  LA: quia ædificavit Dominus Sion, et videbitur in gloria sua.
        EN: for the Lord has built Zion and will be seen in His glory.

101:18  LA: Respexit in orationem humilium et non sprevit precem eorum.
        EN: He has looked upon the prayer of the lowly and has not despised their plea.

101:19  LA: Scribantur hæc in generatione altera, et populus qui creabitur laudabit Dominum.
        EN: Let these things be written for another generation, and a people yet to be created will praise the Lord.

101:20  LA: Quia prospexit de excelso sancto suo ; Dominus de cælo in terram aspexit :
        EN: For He has looked down from His holy height; the Lord has looked from heaven upon the earth,

101:21  LA: ut audiret gemitus compeditorum ; ut solveret filios interemptorum :
        EN: to hear the groaning of those in chains, to release the children of those slain,

101:22  LA: ut annuntient in Sion nomen Domini, et laudem ejus in Jerusalem :
        EN: so that they may proclaim the Lord's name in Zion and His praise in Jerusalem,

101:23  LA: in conveniendo populos in unum, et reges, ut serviant Domino.
        EN: when peoples gather together, and kings, to serve the Lord.

101:24  LA: Respondit ei in via virtutis suæ : Paucitatem dierum meorum nuntia mihi :
        EN: He answered him in the way of his strength: “Tell me the fewness of my days.

101:25  LA: ne revoces me in dimidio dierum meorum, in generationem et generationem anni tui.
        EN: Do not recall me in the middle of my days; Your years are from generation to generation.

101:26  LA: Initio tu, Domine, terram fundasti, et opera manuum tuarum sunt cæli.
        EN: In the beginning You, Lord, founded the earth, and the heavens are the works of Your hands.

101:27  LA: Ipsi peribunt, tu autem permanes ; et omnes sicut vestimentum veterascent. Et sicut opertorium mutabis eos, et mutabuntur ;
        EN: They will perish, but You remain, and they will all grow old like a garment. Like a covering You will change them, and they will be changed.

101:28  LA: tu autem idem ipse es, et anni tui non deficient.
        EN: But You are the very same, and Your years will not fail.

101:29  LA: Filii servorum tuorum habitabunt, et semen eorum in sæculum dirigetur.]
        EN: The children of Your servants will dwell, and their offspring will be established forever.”

PSALMS 102

102:1  LA: Ipsi David. [Benedic, anima mea, Domino, et omnia quæ intra me sunt nomini sancto ejus.
       EN: Of David himself. Bless the Lord, my soul, and all that is within me, bless His holy name.

102:2  LA: Benedic, anima mea, Domino, et noli oblivisci omnes retributiones ejus.
       EN: Bless the Lord, my soul, and do not forget all His rewards.

102:3  LA: Qui propitiatur omnibus iniquitatibus tuis ; qui sanat omnes infirmitates tuas :
       EN: He forgives all your iniquities and heals all your infirmities;

102:4  LA: qui redimit de interitu vitam tuam ; qui coronat te in misericordia et miserationibus :
       EN: He redeems your life from destruction and crowns you with mercy and compassion;

102:5  LA: qui replet in bonis desiderium tuum ; renovabitur ut aquilæ juventus tua :
       EN: He fills your desire with good things. Your youth will be renewed like the eagle's.

102:6  LA: faciens misericordias Dominus, et judicium omnibus injuriam patientibus.
       EN: The Lord works mercies and judgment for all who suffer wrong.

102:7  LA: Notas fecit vias suas Moysi ; filiis Israël voluntates suas.
       EN: He made His ways known to Moses, His wishes to the children of Israel.

102:8  LA: Miserator et misericors Dominus : longanimis, et multum misericors.
       EN: The Lord is compassionate and merciful, patient and abundantly merciful.

102:9  LA: Non in perpetuum irascetur, neque in æternum comminabitur.
       EN: He will not be angry perpetually or threaten forever.

102:10  LA: Non secundum peccata nostra fecit nobis, neque secundum iniquitates nostras retribuit nobis.
        EN: He has not dealt with us according to our sins or repaid us according to our iniquities.

102:11  LA: Quoniam secundum altitudinem cæli a terra, corroboravit misericordiam suam super timentes se ;
        EN: For as high as heaven is above the earth, He has strengthened His mercy over those who fear Him.

102:12  LA: quantum distat ortus ab occidente, longe fecit a nobis iniquitates nostras.
        EN: As far as sunrise is from sunset, He has put our iniquities far from us.

102:13  LA: Quomodo miseretur pater filiorum, misertus est Dominus timentibus se.
        EN: As a father has compassion for his children, the Lord has had compassion for those who fear Him.

102:14  LA: Quoniam ipse cognovit figmentum nostrum ; recordatus est quoniam pulvis sumus.
        EN: For He knows how we are formed; He has remembered that we are dust.

102:15  LA: Homo, sicut fœnum dies ejus ; tamquam flos agri, sic efflorebit :
        EN: A person's days are like grass; like a flower of the field he will bloom,

102:16  LA: quoniam spiritus pertransibit in illo, et non subsistet, et non cognoscet amplius locum suum.
        EN: for the wind will pass over him, and he will not remain, nor will he know his place any longer.

102:17  LA: Misericordia autem Domini ab æterno, et usque in æternum super timentes eum. Et justitia illius in filios filiorum,
        EN: But the Lord's mercy is from eternity to eternity upon those who fear Him, and His justice upon children's children,

102:18  LA: his qui servant testamentum ejus, et memores sunt mandatorum ipsius ad faciendum ea.
        EN: upon those who keep His covenant and remember His commandments to do them.

102:19  LA: Dominus in cælo paravit sedem suam, et regnum ipsius omnibus dominabitur.
        EN: The Lord has prepared His throne in heaven, and His kingdom will rule over all.

102:20  LA: Benedicite Domino, omnes angeli ejus : potentes virtute, facientes verbum illius, ad audiendam vocem sermonum ejus.
        EN: Bless the Lord, all His angels, mighty in strength, doing His word in order to hear the voice of His sayings.

102:21  LA: Benedicite Domino, omnes virtutes ejus ; ministri ejus, qui facitis voluntatem ejus.
        EN: Bless the Lord, all His hosts, His ministers who do His will.

102:22  LA: Benedicite Domino, omnia opera ejus : in omni loco dominationis ejus, benedic, anima mea, Domino.]
        EN: Bless the Lord, all His works, in every place of His dominion. Bless the Lord, my soul.

PSALMS 103

103:1  LA: Ipsi David. [Benedic, anima mea, Domino : Domine Deus meus, magnificatus es vehementer. Confessionem et decorem induisti,
       EN: Of David himself. Bless the Lord, my soul. Lord my God, You have become exceedingly great. You have clothed Yourself in praise and beauty,

103:2  LA: amictus lumine sicut vestimento. Extendens cælum sicut pellem,
       EN: wrapped in light as in a garment, stretching out heaven like a skin.

103:3  LA: qui tegis aquis superiora ejus : qui ponis nubem ascensum tuum ; qui ambulas super pennas ventorum :
       EN: You cover its upper parts with waters, make a cloud Your ascent, and walk upon the wings of the winds.

103:4  LA: qui facis angelos tuos spiritus, et ministros tuos ignem urentem.
       EN: You make Your angels spirits and Your ministers burning fire.

103:5  LA: Qui fundasti terram super stabilitatem suam : non inclinabitur in sæculum sæculi.
       EN: You founded the earth upon its stability; it will not be moved forever and ever.

103:6  LA: Abyssus sicut vestimentum amictus ejus ; super montes stabunt aquæ.
       EN: The deep is its clothing, like a garment; the waters will stand above the mountains.

103:7  LA: Ab increpatione tua fugient ; a voce tonitrui tui formidabunt.
       EN: At Your rebuke they will flee; at the voice of Your thunder they will be afraid.

103:8  LA: Ascendunt montes, et descendunt campi, in locum quem fundasti eis.
       EN: Mountains rise and plains descend to the place You founded for them.

103:9  LA: Terminum posuisti quem non transgredientur, neque convertentur operire terram.
       EN: You have set a boundary they will not cross, nor will they return to cover the earth.

103:10  LA: Qui emittis fontes in convallibus ; inter medium montium pertransibunt aquæ.
        EN: You send out springs in the valleys; the waters will pass between the mountains.

103:11  LA: Potabunt omnes bestiæ agri ; expectabunt onagri in siti sua.
        EN: All the beasts of the field will drink; the wild asses will wait in their thirst.

103:12  LA: Super ea volucres cæli habitabunt ; de medio petrarum dabunt voces.
        EN: Above them the birds of heaven will dwell; from among the rocks they will give voice.

103:13  LA: Rigans montes de superioribus suis ; de fructu operum tuorum satiabitur terra :
        EN: You water the mountains from Your upper chambers; the earth will be filled with the fruit of Your works.

103:14  LA: producens fœnum jumentis, et herbam servituti hominum, ut educas panem de terra,
        EN: You bring forth grass for beasts of burden and plants for human service, to bring bread from the earth,

103:15  LA: et vinum lætificet cor hominis : ut exhilaret faciem in oleo, et panis cor hominis confirmet.
        EN: and wine to gladden the human heart, to brighten the face with oil, and bread to strengthen the human heart.

103:16  LA: Saturabuntur ligna campi, et cedri Libani quas plantavit :
        EN: The trees of the field will be filled, and the cedars of Lebanon that He planted.

103:17  LA: illic passeres nidificabunt : herodii domus dux est eorum.
        EN: There sparrows will nest; the heron's house is chief among them.

103:18  LA: Montes excelsi cervis ; petra refugium herinaciis.
        EN: The high mountains are for deer; the rock is a refuge for hedgehogs.

103:19  LA: Fecit lunam in tempora ; sol cognovit occasum suum.
        EN: He made the moon for seasons; the sun knows its setting.

103:20  LA: Posuisti tenebras, et facta est nox ; in ipsa pertransibunt omnes bestiæ silvæ :
        EN: You appointed darkness, and night came; in it all the beasts of the forest will pass through,

103:21  LA: catuli leonum rugientes ut rapiant, et quærant a Deo escam sibi.
        EN: the young lions roaring to seize prey and seek their food from God.

103:22  LA: Ortus est sol, et congregati sunt, et in cubilibus suis collocabuntur.
        EN: The sun has risen, and they have gathered together and will lie down in their dens.

103:23  LA: Exibit homo ad opus suum, et ad operationem suam usque ad vesperum.
        EN: The human being will go out to his work and to his labor until evening.

103:24  LA: Quam magnificata sunt opera tua, Domine ! omnia in sapientia fecisti ; impleta est terra possessione tua.
        EN: How great Your works have become, Lord! You have made everything in wisdom; the earth is filled with Your possessions.

103:25  LA: Hoc mare magnum et spatiosum manibus ; illic reptilia quorum non est numerus : animalia pusilla cum magnis.
        EN: Here is the sea, great and wide in its expanse; there are creeping things without number, small creatures together with great.

103:26  LA: Illic naves pertransibunt ; draco iste quem formasti ad illudendum ei.
        EN: There ships will pass, and that dragon You formed to play with.

103:27  LA: Omnia a te expectant ut des illis escam in tempore.
        EN: All wait for You to give them food in season.

103:28  LA: Dante te illis, colligent ; aperiente te manum tuam, omnia implebuntur bonitate.
        EN: When You give to them, they will gather; when You open Your hand, all will be filled with goodness.

103:29  LA: Avertente autem te faciem, turbabuntur ; auferes spiritum eorum, et deficient, et in pulverem suum revertentur.
        EN: But when You turn Your face away, they will be troubled. You will take away their spirit, and they will fail and return to their dust.

103:30  LA: Emittes spiritum tuum, et creabuntur, et renovabis faciem terræ.
        EN: You will send forth Your spirit, and they will be created, and You will renew the face of the earth.

103:31  LA: Sit gloria Domini in sæculum ; lætabitur Dominus in operibus suis.
        EN: May the Lord's glory endure forever; the Lord will rejoice in His works.

103:32  LA: Qui respicit terram, et facit eam tremere ; qui tangit montes, et fumigant.
        EN: He looks upon the earth and makes it tremble; He touches the mountains, and they smoke.

103:33  LA: Cantabo Domino in vita mea ; psallam Deo meo quamdiu sum.
        EN: I will sing to the Lord in my life; I will sing psalms to my God as long as I am.

103:34  LA: Jucundum sit ei eloquium meum ; ego vero delectabor in Domino.
        EN: May my speech be delightful to Him, and I will delight in the Lord.

103:35  LA: Deficiant peccatores a terra, et iniqui, ita ut non sint. Benedic, anima mea, Domino.]
        EN: Let sinners fail from the earth, and the wicked, so that they are no more. Bless the Lord, my soul.

PSALMS 104

104:1  LA: Alleluja . [Confitemini Domino, et invocate nomen ejus ; annuntiate inter gentes opera ejus.
       EN: Alleluia. Give thanks to the Lord and call upon His name; proclaim His works among the nations.

104:2  LA: Cantate ei, et psallite ei ; narrate omnia mirabilia ejus.
       EN: Sing to Him and sing psalms to Him; recount all His wonders.

104:3  LA: Laudamini in nomine sancto ejus ; lætetur cor quærentium Dominum.
       EN: Glory in His holy name; let the heart of those who seek the Lord rejoice.

104:4  LA: Quærite Dominum, et confirmamini ; quærite faciem ejus semper.
       EN: Seek the Lord and be strengthened; seek His face always.

104:5  LA: Mementote mirabilium ejus quæ fecit ; prodigia ejus, et judicia oris ejus :
       EN: Remember the wonders He has done, His portents and the judgments of His mouth,

104:6  LA: semen Abraham servi ejus ; filii Jacob electi ejus.
       EN: you offspring of Abraham His servant, children of Jacob His chosen one.

104:7  LA: Ipse Dominus Deus noster ; in universa terra judicia ejus.
       EN: He is the Lord our God; His judgments are in all the earth.

104:8  LA: Memor fuit in sæculum testamenti sui ; verbi quod mandavit in mille generationes :
       EN: He has remembered His covenant forever, the word He commanded for a thousand generations,

104:9  LA: quod disposuit ad Abraham, et juramenti sui ad Isaac :
       EN: which He established with Abraham, and His oath to Isaac.

104:10  LA: et statuit illud Jacob in præceptum, et Israël in testamentum æternum,
        EN: He established it as a commandment for Jacob and an everlasting covenant for Israel,

104:11  LA: dicens : Tibi dabo terram Chanaan, funiculum hæreditatis vestræ :
        EN: saying, “To you I will give the land of Canaan, the measured portion of your inheritance,”

104:12  LA: cum essent numero brevi, paucissimi, et incolæ ejus.
        EN: when they were few in number, very few, and foreign residents in it.

104:13  LA: Et pertransierunt de gente in gentem, et de regno ad populum alterum.
        EN: They passed from nation to nation and from a kingdom to another people.

104:14  LA: Non reliquit hominem nocere eis : et corripuit pro eis reges.
        EN: He allowed no one to harm them and rebuked kings on their behalf:

104:15  LA: Nolite tangere christos meos, et in prophetis meis nolite malignari.
        EN: “Do not touch My anointed ones or do evil to My prophets.”

104:16  LA: Et vocavit famem super terram, et omne firmamentum panis contrivit.
        EN: He called famine upon the land and broke every support of bread.

104:17  LA: Misit ante eos virum : in servum venumdatus est, Joseph.
        EN: He sent a man before them: Joseph was sold as a slave.

104:18  LA: Humiliaverunt in compedibus pedes ejus ; ferrum pertransiit animam ejus :
        EN: They humbled his feet in shackles; iron passed through his soul,

104:19  LA: donec veniret verbum ejus. Eloquium Domini inflammavit eum.
        EN: until his word came. The Lord's saying set him aflame.

104:20  LA: Misit rex, et solvit eum ; princeps populorum, et dimisit eum.
        EN: The king sent and released him; the ruler of peoples set him free.

104:21  LA: Constituit eum dominum domus suæ, et principem omnis possessionis suæ :
        EN: He appointed him lord of his house and ruler of all his possessions,

104:22  LA: ut erudiret principes ejus sicut semetipsum, et senes ejus prudentiam doceret.
        EN: to instruct his rulers like himself and teach his elders prudence.

104:23  LA: Et intravit Israël in Ægyptum, et Jacob accola fuit in terra Cham.
        EN: Israel entered Egypt, and Jacob was a foreign resident in the land of Ham.

104:24  LA: Et auxit populum suum vehementer, et firmavit eum super inimicos ejus.
        EN: He increased His people exceedingly and made them stronger than their enemies.

104:25  LA: Convertit cor eorum, ut odirent populum ejus, et dolum facerent in servos ejus.
        EN: He turned their hearts to hate His people and act deceitfully against His servants.

104:26  LA: Misit Moysen servum suum ; Aaron quem elegit ipsum.
        EN: He sent Moses His servant, Aaron whom He Himself had chosen.

104:27  LA: Posuit in eis verba signorum suorum, et prodigiorum in terra Cham.
        EN: He placed in them the words of His signs and portents in the land of Ham.

104:28  LA: Misit tenebras, et obscuravit ; et non exacerbavit sermones suos.
        EN: He sent darkness and made it dark, and did not embitter His words.

104:29  LA: Convertit aquas eorum in sanguinem, et occidit pisces eorum.
        EN: He turned their waters into blood and killed their fish.

104:30  LA: Edidit terra eorum ranas in penetralibus regum ipsorum.
        EN: Their land brought forth frogs in the inner chambers of their kings.

104:31  LA: Dixit, et venit cœnomyia et ciniphes in omnibus finibus eorum.
        EN: He spoke, and swarms of flies and gnats came throughout their borders.

104:32  LA: Posuit pluvias eorum grandinem : ignem comburentem in terra ipsorum.
        EN: He made their rain into hail, burning fire in their land.

104:33  LA: Et percussit vineas eorum, et ficulneas eorum, et contrivit lignum finium eorum.
        EN: He struck their vines and fig trees and broke the trees within their borders.

104:34  LA: Dixit, et venit locusta, et bruchus cujus non erat numerus :
        EN: He spoke, and locusts came, and grasshoppers without number.

104:35  LA: et comedit omne fœnum in terra eorum, et comedit omnem fructum terræ eorum.
        EN: They ate all the grass in their land and ate every fruit of their soil.

104:36  LA: Et percussit omne primogenitum in terra eorum, primitias omnis laboris eorum.
        EN: He struck every firstborn in their land, the firstfruits of all their labor.

104:37  LA: Et eduxit eos cum argento et auro, et non erat in tribubus eorum infirmus.
        EN: He brought them out with silver and gold, and there was no weak person among their tribes.

104:38  LA: Lætata est Ægyptus in profectione eorum, quia incubuit timor eorum super eos.
        EN: Egypt rejoiced at their departure, because fear of them had fallen upon it.

104:39  LA: Expandit nubem in protectionem eorum, et ignem ut luceret eis per noctem.
        EN: He spread a cloud to protect them and fire to give them light through the night.

104:40  LA: Petierunt, et venit coturnix, et pane cæli saturavit eos.
        EN: They asked, and quail came, and He filled them with the bread of heaven.

104:41  LA: Dirupit petram, et fluxerunt aquæ : abierunt in sicco flumina.
        EN: He split the rock, and waters flowed; rivers ran through the dry land.

104:42  LA: Quoniam memor fuit verbi sancti sui, quod habuit ad Abraham puerum suum.
        EN: For He remembered His holy word, which He had spoken to Abraham His servant.

104:43  LA: Et eduxit populum suum in exsultatione, et electos suos in lætitia.
        EN: He brought His people out with exultation and His chosen ones with gladness.

104:44  LA: Et dedit illis regiones gentium, et labores populorum possederunt :
        EN: He gave them the lands of nations, and they possessed the labors of peoples,

104:45  LA: ut custodiant justificationes ejus, et legem ejus requirant.]
        EN: so that they might keep His just ordinances and seek out His law.

PSALMS 105

105:1  LA: Alleluja . [Confitemini Domino, quoniam bonus, quoniam in sæculum misericordia ejus.
       EN: Alleluia. Give thanks to the Lord, for He is good, for His mercy is forever.

105:2  LA: Quis loquetur potentias Domini ; auditas faciet omnes laudes ejus ?
       EN: Who will speak of the Lord's mighty deeds or make all His praises heard?

105:3  LA: Beati qui custodiunt judicium, et faciunt justitiam in omni tempore.
       EN: Happy are those who keep judgment and do justice at all times.

105:4  LA: Memento nostri, Domine, in beneplacito populi tui ; visita nos in salutari tuo :
       EN: Remember us, Lord, in Your favor toward Your people; visit us with Your salvation,

105:5  LA: ad videndum in bonitate electorum tuorum ; ad lætandum in lætitia gentis tuæ : ut lauderis cum hæreditate tua.
       EN: to see the well-being of Your chosen ones, to rejoice in the gladness of Your nation, so that You may be praised with Your inheritance.

105:6  LA: Peccavimus cum patribus nostris : injuste egimus ; iniquitatem fecimus.
       EN: We have sinned with our fathers; we have acted unjustly and done iniquity.

105:7  LA: Patres nostri in Ægypto non intellexerunt mirabilia tua ; non fuerunt memores multitudinis misericordiæ tuæ. Et irritaverunt ascendentes in mare, mare Rubrum ;
       EN: Our fathers in Egypt did not understand Your wonders; they did not remember Your abundant mercy. They provoked as they went up into the sea, the Red Sea.

105:8  LA: et salvavit eos propter nomen suum, ut notam faceret potentiam suam.
       EN: He saved them for His name's sake, to make His power known.

105:9  LA: Et increpuit mare Rubrum et exsiccatum est, et deduxit eos in abyssis sicut in deserto.
       EN: He rebuked the Red Sea, and it dried up; He led them in the depths as in a wilderness.

105:10  LA: Et salvavit eos de manu odientium, et redemit eos de manu inimici.
        EN: He saved them from the hand of those who hated them and redeemed them from the enemy's hand.

105:11  LA: Et operuit aqua tribulantes eos ; unus ex eis non remansit.
        EN: Water covered their oppressors; not one of them remained.

105:12  LA: Et crediderunt verbis ejus, et laudaverunt laudem ejus.
        EN: They believed His words and sang His praise.

105:13  LA: Cito fecerunt ; obliti sunt operum ejus : et non sustinuerunt consilium ejus.
        EN: They acted quickly: they forgot His works and did not wait for His counsel.

105:14  LA: Et concupierunt concupiscentiam in deserto, et tentaverunt Deum in inaquoso.
        EN: They craved a craving in the wilderness and tested God in the waterless place.

105:15  LA: Et dedit eis petitionem ipsorum, et misit saturitatem in animas eorum.
        EN: He gave them what they asked and sent fullness into their souls.

105:16  LA: Et irritaverunt Moysen in castris ; Aaron, sanctum Domini.
        EN: They provoked Moses in the camp, Aaron, the Lord's holy one.

105:17  LA: Aperta est terra, et deglutivit Dathan, et operuit super congregationem Abiron.
        EN: The earth opened and swallowed Dathan and covered the gathering of Abiram.

105:18  LA: Et exarsit ignis in synagoga eorum : flamma combussit peccatores.
        EN: Fire blazed in their assembly; a flame burned the sinners.

105:19  LA: Et fecerunt vitulum in Horeb, et adoraverunt sculptile.
        EN: They made a calf at Horeb and worshiped a carved image.

105:20  LA: Et mutaverunt gloriam suam in similitudinem vituli comedentis fœnum.
        EN: They exchanged their glory for the likeness of a calf eating grass.

105:21  LA: Obliti sunt Deum qui salvavit eos ; qui fecit magnalia in Ægypto,
        EN: They forgot the God who saved them, who had done great things in Egypt,

105:22  LA: mirabilia in terra Cham, terribilia in mari Rubro.
        EN: wonders in the land of Ham, fearsome things at the Red Sea.

105:23  LA: Et dixit ut disperderet eos, si non Moyses, electus ejus, stetisset in confractione in conspectu ejus, ut averteret iram ejus, ne disperderet eos.
        EN: He said that He would destroy them, had not Moses His chosen one stood in the breach before Him, to turn away His anger so that He would not destroy them.

105:24  LA: Et pro nihilo habuerunt terram desiderabilem ; non crediderunt verbo ejus.
        EN: They regarded the desirable land as nothing and did not believe His word.

105:25  LA: Et murmuraverunt in tabernaculis suis ; non exaudierunt vocem Domini.
        EN: They murmured in their tents and did not hear the Lord's voice.

105:26  LA: Et elevavit manum suam super eos ut prosterneret eos in deserto :
        EN: He raised His hand over them to lay them low in the wilderness,

105:27  LA: et ut dejiceret semen eorum in nationibus, et dispergeret eos in regionibus.
        EN: to cast down their offspring among the nations and scatter them in the lands.

105:28  LA: Et initiati sunt Beelphegor, et comederunt sacrificia mortuorum.
        EN: They were initiated into Baal-peor and ate sacrifices offered to the dead.

105:29  LA: Et irritaverunt eum in adinventionibus suis, et multiplicata est in eis ruina.
        EN: They provoked Him with their devices, and destruction multiplied among them.

105:30  LA: Et stetit Phinees, et placavit, et cessavit quassatio.
        EN: Phinehas stood and made appeasement, and the scourge ceased.

105:31  LA: Et reputatum est ei in justitiam, in generationem et generationem usque in sempiternum.
        EN: It was reckoned to him as justice, from generation to generation, forever.

105:32  LA: Et irritaverunt eum ad aquas contradictionis, et vexatus est Moyses propter eos :
        EN: They provoked Him at the waters of contradiction, and Moses was afflicted because of them,

105:33  LA: quia exacerbaverunt spiritum ejus, et distinxit in labiis suis.
        EN: for they embittered his spirit, and he made distinctions with his lips.

105:34  LA: Non disperdiderunt gentes quas dixit Dominus illis :
        EN: They did not destroy the nations of which the Lord had spoken to them,

105:35  LA: et commisti sunt inter gentes, et didicerunt opera eorum ;
        EN: but mingled among the nations and learned their works.

105:36  LA: et servierunt sculptilibus eorum, et factum est illis in scandalum.
        EN: They served their carved images, and this became a stumbling block to them.

105:37  LA: Et immolaverunt filios suos et filias suas dæmoniis.
        EN: They sacrificed their sons and daughters to demons.

105:38  LA: Et effuderunt sanguinem innocentem, sanguinem filiorum suorum et filiarum suarum, quas sacrificaverunt sculptilibus Chanaan. Et infecta est terra in sanguinibus,
        EN: They poured out innocent blood, the blood of their sons and daughters whom they sacrificed to the carved images of Canaan. The land was stained with blood

105:39  LA: et contaminata est in operibus eorum : et fornicati sunt in adinventionibus suis.
        EN: and defiled by their works, and they prostituted themselves through their devices.

105:40  LA: Et iratus est furore Dominus in populum suum, et abominatus est hæreditatem suam.
        EN: The Lord was furiously angry with His people and abhorred His inheritance.

105:41  LA: Et tradidit eos in manus gentium ; et dominati sunt eorum qui oderunt eos.
        EN: He delivered them into the hands of nations, and those who hated them ruled over them.

105:42  LA: Et tribulaverunt eos inimici eorum, et humiliati sunt sub manibus eorum ;
        EN: Their enemies oppressed them, and they were humbled beneath their hands.

105:43  LA: sæpe liberavit eos. Ipsi autem exacerbaverunt eum in consilio suo, et humiliati sunt in iniquitatibus suis.
        EN: Often He delivered them, but they provoked Him with their counsel and were humbled in their iniquities.

105:44  LA: Et vidit cum tribularentur, et audivit orationem eorum.
        EN: He saw when they were oppressed and heard their prayer.

105:45  LA: Et memor fuit testamenti sui, et pœnituit eum secundum multitudinem misericordiæ suæ :
        EN: He remembered His covenant and relented according to His abundant mercy.

105:46  LA: et dedit eos in misericordias, in conspectu omnium qui ceperant eos.
        EN: He granted them compassion before all who had taken them captive.

105:47  LA: Salvos nos fac, Domine Deus noster, et congrega nos de nationibus : ut confiteamur nomini sancto tuo, et gloriemur in laude tua.
        EN: Save us, Lord our God, and gather us from the nations, so that we may give thanks to Your holy name and glory in Your praise.

105:48  LA: Benedictus Dominus Deus Israël, a sæculo et usque in sæculum ; et dicet omnis populus : Fiat, fiat.]
        EN: Blessed be the Lord, the God of Israel, from age to age, and all the people will say, “Let it be, let it be.”

PSALMS 106

106:1  LA: Alleluja . [Confitemini Domino, quoniam bonus, quoniam in sæculum misericordia ejus.
       EN: Alleluia. Give thanks to the Lord, for He is good, for His mercy is forever.

106:2  LA: Dicant qui redempti sunt a Domino, quos redemit de manu inimici, et de regionibus congregavit eos,
       EN: Let those redeemed by the Lord say so, those He redeemed from the enemy's hand and gathered from the lands,

106:3  LA: a solis ortu, et occasu, ab aquilone, et mari.
       EN: from sunrise and sunset, from the north and the sea.

106:4  LA: Erraverunt in solitudine, in inaquoso ; viam civitatis habitaculi non invenerunt.
       EN: They wandered in the wilderness, in a waterless place; they found no way to a city to dwell in.

106:5  LA: Esurientes et sitientes, anima eorum in ipsis defecit.
       EN: Hungry and thirsty, their soul failed within them.

106:6  LA: Et clamaverunt ad Dominum cum tribularentur, et de necessitatibus eorum eripuit eos ;
       EN: They cried to the Lord when they were distressed, and He rescued them from their hardships.

106:7  LA: et deduxit eos in viam rectam, ut irent in civitatem habitationis.
       EN: He led them on a straight way, so that they might go to a city to dwell in.

106:8  LA: Confiteantur Domino misericordiæ ejus, et mirabilia ejus filiis hominum.
       EN: Let His mercies give thanks to the Lord, and His wonders to the children of men.

106:9  LA: Quia satiavit animam inanem, et animam esurientem satiavit bonis.
       EN: For He filled the empty soul and filled the hungry soul with good things.

106:10  LA: Sedentes in tenebris et umbra mortis ; vinctos in mendicitate et ferro.
        EN: They sat in darkness and the shadow of death, bound in poverty and iron,

106:11  LA: Quia exacerbaverunt eloquia Dei, et consilium Altissimi irritaverunt.
        EN: because they had embittered God's sayings and provoked the counsel of the Most High.

106:12  LA: Et humiliatum est in laboribus cor eorum ; infirmati sunt, nec fuit qui adjuvaret.
        EN: Their heart was humbled in labors; they grew weak, and there was no one to help.

106:13  LA: Et clamaverunt ad Dominum cum tribularentur ; et de necessitatibus eorum liberavit eos.
        EN: They cried to the Lord when they were distressed, and He delivered them from their hardships.

106:14  LA: Et eduxit eos de tenebris et umbra mortis, et vincula eorum dirupit.
        EN: He brought them out of darkness and the shadow of death and broke their bonds apart.

106:15  LA: Confiteantur Domino misericordiæ ejus, et mirabilia ejus filiis hominum.
        EN: Let His mercies give thanks to the Lord, and His wonders to the children of men.

106:16  LA: Quia contrivit portas æreas, et vectes ferreos confregit.
        EN: For He shattered the bronze gates and broke the iron bars.

106:17  LA: Suscepit eos de via iniquitatis eorum ; propter injustitias enim suas humiliati sunt.
        EN: He took them up from the way of their iniquity, for they were humbled because of their injustices.

106:18  LA: Omnem escam abominata est anima eorum, et appropinquaverunt usque ad portas mortis.
        EN: Their soul abhorred every food, and they drew near to the gates of death.

106:19  LA: Et clamaverunt ad Dominum cum tribularentur, et de necessitatibus eorum liberavit eos.
        EN: They cried to the Lord when they were distressed, and He delivered them from their hardships.

106:20  LA: Misit verbum suum, et sanavit eos, et eripuit eos de interitionibus eorum.
        EN: He sent His word and healed them and rescued them from their destructions.

106:21  LA: Confiteantur Domino misericordiæ ejus, et mirabilia ejus filiis hominum.
        EN: Let His mercies give thanks to the Lord, and His wonders to the children of men.

106:22  LA: Et sacrificent sacrificium laudis, et annuntient opera ejus in exsultatione.
        EN: Let them offer a sacrifice of praise and proclaim His works with rejoicing.

106:23  LA: Qui descendunt mare in navibus, facientes operationem in aquis multis :
        EN: Those who go down to the sea in ships, doing work upon many waters,

106:24  LA: ipsi viderunt opera Domini, et mirabilia ejus in profundo.
        EN: have seen the Lord's works and His wonders in the deep.

106:25  LA: Dixit, et stetit spiritus procellæ, et exaltati sunt fluctus ejus.
        EN: He spoke, and the storm wind arose, and its waves were lifted high.

106:26  LA: Ascendunt usque ad cælos, et descendunt usque ad abyssos ; anima eorum in malis tabescebat.
        EN: They rise to the heavens and descend to the depths; their soul wasted away in evils.

106:27  LA: Turbati sunt, et moti sunt sicut ebrius, et omnis sapientia eorum devorata est.
        EN: They were troubled and staggered like a drunkard, and all their wisdom was swallowed up.

106:28  LA: Et clamaverunt ad Dominum cum tribularentur ; et de necessitatibus eorum eduxit eos.
        EN: They cried to the Lord when they were distressed, and He brought them out of their hardships.

106:29  LA: Et statuit procellam ejus in auram, et siluerunt fluctus ejus.
        EN: He turned its storm into a breeze, and its waves fell silent.

106:30  LA: Et lætati sunt quia siluerunt ; et deduxit eos in portum voluntatis eorum.
        EN: They rejoiced because the waves were silent, and He led them to the harbor they desired.

106:31  LA: Confiteantur Domino misericordiæ ejus, et mirabilia ejus filiis hominum.
        EN: Let His mercies give thanks to the Lord, and His wonders to the children of men.

106:32  LA: Et exaltent eum in ecclesia plebis, et in cathedra seniorum laudent eum.
        EN: Let them exalt Him in the assembly of the people and praise Him where the elders sit.

106:33  LA: Posuit flumina in desertum, et exitus aquarum in sitim ;
        EN: He made rivers into a wilderness and springs of water into thirsty ground,

106:34  LA: terram fructiferam in salsuginem, a malitia inhabitantium in ea.
        EN: fruitful land into salt land because of the wickedness of its inhabitants.

106:35  LA: Posuit desertum in stagna aquarum, et terram sine aqua in exitus aquarum.
        EN: He made a wilderness into pools of water and waterless land into springs of water.

106:36  LA: Et collocavit illic esurientes, et constituerunt civitatem habitationis :
        EN: He settled the hungry there, and they established a city to dwell in.

106:37  LA: et seminaverunt agros et plantaverunt vineas, et fecerunt fructum nativitatis.
        EN: They sowed fields and planted vineyards and produced the fruit of their growth.

106:38  LA: Et benedixit eis, et multiplicati sunt nimis ; et jumenta eorum non minoravit.
        EN: He blessed them, and they multiplied exceedingly, and He did not diminish their beasts of burden.

106:39  LA: Et pauci facti sunt et vexati sunt, a tribulatione malorum et dolore.
        EN: They became few and were afflicted through the distress of evils and pain.

106:40  LA: Effusa est contemptio super principes : et errare fecit eos in invio, et non in via.
        EN: Contempt was poured upon rulers, and He made them wander in trackless ground, with no road.

106:41  LA: Et adjuvit pauperem de inopia, et posuit sicut oves familias.
        EN: He helped the poor out of want and made families like flocks of sheep.

106:42  LA: Videbunt recti, et lætabuntur ; et omnis iniquitas oppilabit os suum.
        EN: The upright will see and rejoice, and all iniquity will stop its mouth.

106:43  LA: Quis sapiens, et custodiet hæc, et intelliget misericordias Domini ?]
        EN: Who is wise and will keep these things and understand the Lord's mercies?

PSALMS 107

107:1  LA: Canticum Psalmi, ipsi David.
       EN: A song. A psalm of David himself.

107:2  LA: [Paratum cor meum, Deus, paratum cor meum ; cantabo, et psallam in gloria mea.
       EN: My heart is ready, God, my heart is ready; I will sing and make music in my glory.

107:3  LA: Exsurge, gloria mea ; exsurge, psalterium et cithara ; exsurgam diluculo.
       EN: Rise, my glory; rise, harp and lyre. I will rise at daybreak.

107:4  LA: Confitebor tibi in populis, Domine, et psallam tibi in nationibus :
       EN: I will give You thanks among the peoples, Lord, and sing psalms to You among the nations,

107:5  LA: quia magna est super cælos misericordia tua, et usque ad nubes veritas tua.
       EN: for Your mercy is great above the heavens, and Your truth reaches the clouds.

107:6  LA: Exaltare super cælos, Deus, et super omnem terram gloria tua :
       EN: Rise high above the heavens, God, and let Your glory be above all the earth,

107:7  LA: ut liberentur dilecti tui. Salvum fac dextera tua, et exaudi me.
       EN: so that Your beloved may be delivered. Save with Your right hand and answer me.

107:8  LA: Deus locutus est in sancto suo : Exsultabo, et dividam Sichimam ; et convallem tabernaculorum dimetiar.
       EN: God has spoken in His holy place: “I will rejoice and divide Shechem and measure the valley of tents.

107:9  LA: Meus est Galaad, et meus est Manasses, et Ephraim susceptio capitis mei. Juda rex meus ;
       EN: Gilead is Mine, and Manasseh is Mine, and Ephraim is the protection of My head. Judah is My king;

107:10  LA: Moab lebes spei meæ : in Idumæam extendam calceamentum meum ; mihi alienigenæ amici facti sunt.
        EN: Moab is the pot of My hope. Over Edom I will stretch My sandal; foreigners have become My friends.”

107:11  LA: Quis deducet me in civitatem munitam ? quis deducet me usque in Idumæam ?
        EN: Who will lead me into the fortified city? Who will lead me as far as Edom?

107:12  LA: nonne tu, Deus, qui repulisti nos ? et non exibis, Deus, in virtutibus nostris ?
        EN: Will it not be You, God, who have rejected us? And will You not go out, God, with our armies?

107:13  LA: Da nobis auxilium de tribulatione, quia vana salus hominis.
        EN: Give us help from distress, for human salvation is futile.

107:14  LA: In Deo faciemus virtutem ; et ipse ad nihilum deducet inimicos nostros.]
        EN: In God we will do mighty deeds, and He Himself will bring our enemies to nothing.

PSALMS 108

108:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David.
       EN: For the end. A psalm of David. God, do not be silent about my praise, for the sinner's mouth and the deceitful mouth have opened against me.

108:2  LA: In finem. Psalmus David.
       EN: For the end. A psalm of David. God, do not be silent about my praise, for the sinner's mouth and the deceitful mouth have opened against me.

108:3  LA: Locuti sunt adversum me lingua dolosa, et sermonibus odii circumdederunt me : et expugnaverunt me gratis.
       EN: They have spoken against me with a deceitful tongue and surrounded me with words of hatred; they have fought against me without cause.

108:4  LA: Pro eo ut me diligerent, detrahebant mihi ; ego autem orabam.
       EN: Instead of loving me, they slandered me, but I prayed.

108:5  LA: Et posuerunt adversum me mala pro bonis, et odium pro dilectione mea.
       EN: They have repaid me with evil for good and hatred for my love.

108:6  LA: Constitue super eum peccatorem, et diabolus stet a dextris ejus.
       EN: Appoint a sinner over him, and let the devil stand at his right hand.

108:7  LA: Cum judicatur, exeat condemnatus ; et oratio ejus fiat in peccatum.
       EN: When he is judged, let him come out condemned, and let his prayer become sin.

108:8  LA: Fiant dies ejus pauci, et episcopatum ejus accipiat alter.
       EN: Let his days be few, and let another take his office of oversight.

108:9  LA: Fiant filii ejus orphani, et uxor ejus vidua.
       EN: Let his children become orphans and his wife a widow.

108:10  LA: Nutantes transferantur filii ejus et mendicent, et ejiciantur de habitationibus suis.
        EN: Let his children be driven about unsteadily and beg, and be cast out of their dwellings.

108:11  LA: Scrutetur fœnerator omnem substantiam ejus, et diripiant alieni labores ejus.
        EN: Let a moneylender search out all his substance, and let strangers plunder his labors.

108:12  LA: Non sit illi adjutor, nec sit qui misereatur pupillis ejus.
        EN: Let him have no helper, and let there be no one to have mercy on his orphans.

108:13  LA: Fiant nati ejus in interitum ; in generatione una deleatur nomen ejus.
        EN: Let his descendants be given to destruction; in one generation let his name be erased.

108:14  LA: In memoriam redeat iniquitas patrum ejus in conspectu Domini, et peccatum matris ejus non deleatur.
        EN: Let his fathers' iniquity return to remembrance before the Lord, and let his mother's sin not be erased.

108:15  LA: Fiant contra Dominum semper, et dispereat de terra memoria eorum :
        EN: Let them be before the Lord always, and let their memory perish from the earth,

108:16  LA: pro eo quod non est recordatus facere misericordiam,
        EN: because he did not remember to show mercy, but persecuted the needy and destitute person, the one pierced in heart, to put him to death.

108:17  LA: Et dilexit maledictionem, et veniet ei ; et noluit benedictionem, et elongabitur ab eo. Et induit maledictionem sicut vestimentum ; et intravit sicut aqua in interiora ejus, et sicut oleum in ossibus ejus.
        EN: He loved a curse, and it will come to him; he did not want a blessing, and it will be far from him. He clothed himself in a curse like a garment, and it entered his inner parts like water and his bones like oil.

108:18  LA: Et dilexit maledictionem, et veniet ei ; et noluit benedictionem, et elongabitur ab eo. Et induit maledictionem sicut vestimentum ; et intravit sicut aqua in interiora ejus, et sicut oleum in ossibus ejus.
        EN: He loved a curse, and it will come to him; he did not want a blessing, and it will be far from him. He clothed himself in a curse like a garment, and it entered his inner parts like water and his bones like oil.

108:19  LA: Fiat ei sicut vestimentum quo operitur, et sicut zona qua semper præcingitur.
        EN: Let it be to him like the garment that covers him and like the belt with which he is always girded.

108:20  LA: Hoc opus eorum qui detrahunt mihi apud Dominum, et qui loquuntur mala adversus animam meam.
        EN: This is the work of those who slander me before the Lord and speak evil against my soul.

108:21  LA: Et tu, Domine, Domine, fac mecum propter nomen tuum, quia suavis est misericordia tua.
        EN: But You, Lord, Lord, act for me for Your name's sake, because Your mercy is pleasant.

108:22  LA: Libera me, quia egenus et pauper ego sum, et cor meum conturbatum est intra me.
        EN: Deliver me, for I am needy and poor, and my heart is troubled within me.

108:23  LA: Sicut umbra cum declinat ablatus sum, et excussus sum sicut locustæ.
        EN: I have been carried away like a declining shadow and shaken off like locusts.

108:24  LA: Genua mea infirmata sunt a jejunio, et caro mea immutata est propter oleum.
        EN: My knees have grown weak from fasting, and my flesh has changed because of oil.

108:25  LA: Et ego factus sum opprobrium illis ; viderunt me, et moverunt capita sua.
        EN: I have become a reproach to them; they saw me and shook their heads.

108:26  LA: Adjuva me, Domine Deus meus ; salvum me fac secundum misericordiam tuam.
        EN: Help me, Lord my God; save me according to Your mercy.

108:27  LA: Et sciant quia manus tua hæc, et tu, Domine, fecisti eam.
        EN: Let them know that this is Your hand and that You, Lord, have done it.

108:28  LA: Maledicent illi, et tu benedices : qui insurgunt in me confundantur ; servus autem tuus lætabitur.
        EN: They will curse, and You will bless. Let those who rise against me be confounded, but Your servant will rejoice.

108:29  LA: Induantur qui detrahunt mihi pudore, et operiantur sicut diploide confusione sua.
        EN: Let those who slander me be clothed in shame and wrapped in their disgrace as in a double cloak.

108:30  LA: Confitebor Domino nimis in ore meo, et in medio multorum laudabo eum :
        EN: I will give abundant thanks to the Lord with my mouth, and amid many I will praise Him,

108:31  LA: quia astitit a dextris pauperis, ut salvam faceret a persequentibus animam meam.]
        EN: because He has stood at the poor man's right hand to save my soul from those who pursue it.

PSALMS 109

109:1  LA: Psalmus David. [Dixit Dominus Domino meo : Sede a dextris meis, donec ponam inimicos tuos scabellum pedum tuorum.
       EN: A psalm of David. The Lord said to my lord, “Sit at My right hand until I make your enemies a footstool for your feet.”

109:2  LA: Virgam virtutis tuæ emittet Dominus ex Sion : dominare in medio inimicorum tuorum.
       EN: The Lord will send the rod of your power from Zion: rule in the midst of your enemies.

109:3  LA: Tecum principium in die virtutis tuæ in splendoribus sanctorum : ex utero, ante luciferum, genui te.
       EN: With you is the beginning on the day of your power, in the splendors of the holy ones; from the womb, before the morning star, I begot you.

109:4  LA: Juravit Dominus, et non pœnitebit eum : Tu es sacerdos in æternum secundum ordinem Melchisedech.
       EN: The Lord has sworn and will not repent: “You are a priest forever according to the order of Melchizedek.”

109:5  LA: Dominus a dextris tuis ; confregit in die iræ suæ reges.
       EN: The Lord is at your right hand; He has shattered kings on the day of His anger.

109:6  LA: Judicabit in nationibus, implebit ruinas ; conquassabit capita in terra multorum.
       EN: He will judge among the nations and fill them with ruins; He will crush heads in the land of many.

109:7  LA: De torrente in via bibet ; propterea exaltabit caput.]
       EN: He will drink from the torrent on the way; therefore he will lift his head.

PSALMS 110

110:1  LA: Alleluja . [Confitebor tibi, Domine, in toto corde meo, in consilio justorum, et congregatione.
       EN: Alleluia. I will give You thanks, Lord, with all my heart, in the council of the just and the assembly.

110:2  LA: Magna opera Domini : exquisita in omnes voluntates ejus.
       EN: Great are the Lord's works, sought out in all His purposes.

110:3  LA: Confessio et magnificentia opus ejus, et justitia ejus manet in sæculum sæculi.
       EN: His work is praise and magnificence, and His justice remains forever and ever.

110:4  LA: Memoriam fecit mirabilium suorum, misericors et miserator Dominus.
       EN: The Lord, merciful and compassionate, has made a remembrance of His wonders.

110:5  LA: Escam dedit timentibus se ; memor erit in sæculum testamenti sui.
       EN: He has given food to those who fear Him; He will remember His covenant forever.

110:6  LA: Virtutem operum suorum annuntiabit populo suo,
       EN: He will proclaim the power of His works to His people, to give them the inheritance of nations. The works of His hands are truth and judgment.

110:7  LA: Virtutem operum suorum annuntiabit populo suo,
       EN: He will proclaim the power of His works to His people, to give them the inheritance of nations. The works of His hands are truth and judgment.

110:8  LA: Fidelia omnia mandata ejus, confirmata in sæculum sæculi, facta in veritate et æquitate.
       EN: All His commandments are faithful, established forever and ever, made in truth and equity.

110:9  LA: Redemptionem misit populo suo ; mandavit in æternum testamentum suum. Sanctum et terribile nomen ejus.
       EN: He has sent redemption to His people; He has commanded His covenant forever. Holy and fearsome is His name.

110:10  LA: Initium sapientiæ timor Domini ; intellectus bonus omnibus facientibus eum : laudatio ejus manet in sæculum sæculi.]
        EN: The beginning of wisdom is fear of the Lord; there is good understanding for all who practice it. His praise remains forever and ever.

PSALMS 111

111:1  LA: Alleluja , reversionis Aggæi et Zachariæ. [Beatus vir qui timet Dominum : in mandatis ejus volet nimis.
       EN: Alleluia. Of the return of Haggai and Zechariah. Blessed is the man who fears the Lord; he will greatly delight in His commandments.

111:2  LA: Potens in terra erit semen ejus ; generatio rectorum benedicetur.
       EN: His offspring will be mighty upon the earth; the generation of the upright will be blessed.

111:3  LA: Gloria et divitiæ in domo ejus, et justitia ejus manet in sæculum sæculi.
       EN: Glory and riches are in his house, and his righteousness remains forever and ever.

111:4  LA: Exortum est in tenebris lumen rectis : misericors, et miserator, et justus.
       EN: Light has arisen in the darkness for the upright: merciful, compassionate, and just.

111:5  LA: Jucundus homo qui miseretur et commodat ; disponet sermones suos in judicio :
       EN: Pleasing is the man who shows mercy and lends; he will order his words with judgment,

111:6  LA: quia in æternum non commovebitur.
       EN: for he will never be shaken.

111:7  LA: In memoria æterna erit justus ; ab auditione mala non timebit. Paratum cor ejus sperare in Domino,
       EN: The just man will be remembered forever; he will not fear an evil report. His heart is prepared to hope in the Lord;

111:8  LA: confirmatum est cor ejus ; non commovebitur donec despiciat inimicos suos.
       EN: his heart is made firm. He will not be shaken until he looks down upon his enemies.

111:9  LA: Dispersit, dedit pauperibus ; justitia ejus manet in sæculum sæculi : cornu ejus exaltabitur in gloria.
       EN: He has distributed freely and given to the poor; his righteousness remains forever and ever. His horn will be exalted in glory.

111:10  LA: Peccator videbit, et irascetur ; dentibus suis fremet et tabescet : desiderium peccatorum peribit.]
        EN: The sinner will see and be angry; he will gnash his teeth and waste away. The desire of sinners will perish.

PSALMS 112

112:1  LA: Alleluja . [Laudate, pueri, Dominum ; laudate nomen Domini.
       EN: Alleluia. Praise the Lord, children; praise the Lord's name.

112:2  LA: Sit nomen Domini benedictum ex hoc nunc et usque in sæculum.
       EN: May the Lord's name be blessed from now and forever.

112:3  LA: A solis ortu usque ad occasum laudabile nomen Domini.
       EN: From the rising of the sun to its setting, the Lord's name is worthy of praise.

112:4  LA: Excelsus super omnes gentes Dominus, et super cælos gloria ejus.
       EN: The Lord is exalted above all nations, and His glory above the heavens.

112:5  LA: Quis sicut Dominus Deus noster, qui in altis habitat,
       EN: Who is like the Lord our God, who dwells on high

112:6  LA: et humilia respicit in cælo et in terra ?
       EN: and looks upon the lowly things in heaven and on earth?

112:7  LA: Suscitans a terra inopem, et de stercore erigens pauperem :
       EN: He raises the needy from the earth and lifts the poor from the dung heap,

112:8  LA: ut collocet eum cum principibus, cum principibus populi sui.
       EN: to seat him with princes, with the princes of His people.

112:9  LA: Qui habitare facit sterilem in domo, matrem filiorum lætantem.]
       EN: He makes the barren woman dwell in a house as a joyful mother of children.

PSALMS 113

113:1  LA: Alleluja . [In exitu Israël de Ægypto, domus Jacob de populo barbaro,
       EN: Alleluia. When Israel came out of Egypt, the house of Jacob from a people of foreign speech,

113:2  LA: facta est Judæa sanctificatio ejus ; Israël potestas ejus.
       EN: Judah became His consecrated place, Israel His dominion.

113:3  LA: Mare vidit, et fugit ; Jordanis conversus est retrorsum.
       EN: The sea saw and fled; the Jordan turned backward.

113:4  LA: Montes exsultaverunt ut arietes, et colles sicut agni ovium.
       EN: The mountains leaped like rams, and the hills like lambs of the flock.

113:5  LA: Quid est tibi, mare, quod fugisti ? et tu, Jordanis, quia conversus es retrorsum ?
       EN: What happened to you, sea, that you fled, and to you, Jordan, that you turned backward?

113:6  LA: montes, exsultastis sicut arietes ? et colles, sicut agni ovium ?
       EN: Mountains, why did you leap like rams, and hills like lambs of the flock?

113:7  LA: A facie Domini mota est terra, a facie Dei Jacob :
       EN: The earth shook before the Lord, before the God of Jacob,

113:8  LA: qui convertit petram in stagna aquarum, et rupem in fontes aquarum.
       EN: who turned the rock into pools of water and the crag into springs of water.

113:9  LA: Non nobis, Domine, non nobis, sed nomini tuo da gloriam :
       EN: Not to us, Lord, not to us, but to Your name give glory, for Your mercy and Your truth, lest the nations say: Where is their God?

113:10  LA: Non nobis, Domine, non nobis, sed nomini tuo da gloriam :
        EN: Not to us, Lord, not to us, but to Your name give glory, for Your mercy and Your truth, lest the nations say: Where is their God?

113:11  LA: Deus autem noster in cælo ; omnia quæcumque voluit fecit.
        EN: Our God is in heaven; He has done everything He desired.

113:12  LA: Simulacra gentium argentum et aurum, opera manuum hominum.
        EN: The idols of the nations are silver and gold, the work of human hands.

113:13  LA: Os habent, et non loquentur ; oculos habent, et non videbunt.
        EN: They have mouths and will not speak; they have eyes and will not see.

113:14  LA: Aures habent, et non audient ; nares habent, et non odorabunt.
        EN: They have ears and will not hear; they have noses and will not smell.

113:15  LA: Manus habent, et non palpabunt ; pedes habent, et non ambulabunt ; non clamabunt in gutture suo.
        EN: They have hands and will not feel; they have feet and will not walk; they will not cry out with their throats.

113:16  LA: Similes illis fiant qui faciunt ea, et omnes qui confidunt in eis.
        EN: May those who make them become like them, and all who trust in them.

113:17  LA: Domus Israël speravit in Domino ; adjutor eorum et protector eorum est.
        EN: The house of Israel has hoped in the Lord; He is their helper and protector.

113:18  LA: Domus Aaron speravit in Domino ; adjutor eorum et protector eorum est.
        EN: The house of Aaron has hoped in the Lord; He is their helper and protector.

113:19  LA: Qui timent Dominum speraverunt in Domino ; adjutor eorum et protector eorum est.
        EN: Those who fear the Lord have hoped in the Lord; He is their helper and protector.

113:20  LA: Dominus memor fuit nostri, et benedixit nobis. Benedixit domui Israël ; benedixit domui Aaron.
        EN: The Lord has remembered us and blessed us. He has blessed the house of Israel; He has blessed the house of Aaron.

113:21  LA: Benedixit omnibus qui timent Dominum, pusillis cum majoribus.
        EN: He has blessed all who fear the Lord, the small with the great.

113:22  LA: Adjiciat Dominus super vos, super vos et super filios vestros.
        EN: May the Lord increase you, you and your children.

113:23  LA: Benedicti vos a Domino, qui fecit cælum et terram.
        EN: You are blessed by the Lord, who made heaven and earth.

113:24  LA: Cælum cæli Domino ; terram autem dedit filiis hominum.
        EN: The heaven of heavens belongs to the Lord, but He has given the earth to the children of men.

113:25  LA: Non mortui laudabunt te, Domine, neque omnes qui descendunt in infernum :
        EN: The dead will not praise You, Lord, nor any who descend to the underworld.

113:26  LA: sed nos qui vivimus, benedicimus Domino, ex hoc nunc et usque in sæculum.]
        EN: But we who live bless the Lord, from now and forever.

PSALMS 114

114:1  LA: Alleluja . [Dilexi, quoniam exaudiet Dominus vocem orationis meæ.
       EN: Alleluia. I have loved, for the Lord will hear the voice of my prayer.

114:2  LA: Quia inclinavit aurem suam mihi, et in diebus meis invocabo.
       EN: For He has inclined His ear to me, and I will call upon Him all my days.

       EN: I will please the Lord in the land of the living.

PSALMS 115

115:1  LA: Alleluja . [Credidi, propter quod locutus sum ; ego autem humiliatus sum nimis.

PSALMS 117

117:1  LA: Alleluja . [Confitemini Domino, quoniam bonus, quoniam in sæculum misericordia ejus.
       EN: Alleluia. Give thanks to the Lord, for He is good, for His mercy endures forever.

117:2  LA: Dicat nunc Israël : Quoniam bonus, quoniam in sæculum misericordia ejus.
       EN: Let Israel now say: He is good, for His mercy endures forever.

117:3  LA: Dicat nunc domus Aaron : Quoniam in sæculum misericordia ejus.
       EN: Let the house of Aaron now say: His mercy endures forever.

117:4  LA: Dicant nunc qui timent Dominum : Quoniam in sæculum misericordia ejus.
       EN: Let those who fear the Lord now say: His mercy endures forever.

117:5  LA: De tribulatione invocavi Dominum, et exaudivit me in latitudine Dominus.
       EN: From tribulation I called upon the Lord, and the Lord answered me in a spacious place.

117:6  LA: Dominus mihi adjutor ; non timebo quid faciat mihi homo.
       EN: The Lord is my helper; I will not fear what a human being may do to me.

117:7  LA: Dominus mihi adjutor, et ego despiciam inimicos meos.
       EN: The Lord is my helper, and I will look down upon my enemies.

117:8  LA: Bonum est confidere in Domino, quam confidere in homine.
       EN: It is better to trust in the Lord than to trust in a human being.

117:9  LA: Bonum est sperare in Domino, quam sperare in principibus.
       EN: It is better to hope in the Lord than to hope in princes.

117:10  LA: Omnes gentes circuierunt me, et in nomine Domini, quia ultus sum in eos.
        EN: All nations surrounded me, and in the Lord's name I have taken vengeance upon them.

117:11  LA: Circumdantes circumdederunt me, et in nomine Domini, quia ultus sum in eos.
        EN: Surrounding me, they surrounded me, and in the Lord's name I have taken vengeance upon them.

117:12  LA: Circumdederunt me sicut apes, et exarserunt sicut ignis in spinis : et in nomine Domini, quia ultus sum in eos.
        EN: They surrounded me like bees and blazed like fire among thorns, and in the Lord's name I have taken vengeance upon them.

117:13  LA: Impulsus eversus sum, ut caderem, et Dominus suscepit me.
        EN: I was thrust and overturned so that I might fall, but the Lord upheld me.

117:14  LA: Fortitudo mea et laus mea Dominus, et factus est mihi in salutem.
        EN: The Lord is my strength and my praise, and He has become my salvation.

117:15  LA: Vox exsultationis et salutis in tabernaculis justorum.
        EN: The voice of exultation and salvation is in the tents of the just.

117:16  LA: Dextera Domini fecit virtutem ; dextera Domini exaltavit me : dextera Domini fecit virtutem.
        EN: The Lord's right hand has acted mightily; the Lord's right hand has exalted me; the Lord's right hand has acted mightily.

117:17  LA: Non moriar, sed vivam, et narrabo opera Domini.
        EN: I will not die, but live and recount the Lord's works.

117:18  LA: Castigans castigavit me Dominus, et morti non tradidit me.
        EN: The Lord has chastened me severely, but has not handed me over to death.

117:19  LA: Aperite mihi portas justitiæ : ingressus in eas confitebor Domino.
        EN: Open for me the gates of righteousness; I will enter them and give thanks to the Lord.

117:20  LA: Hæc porta Domini : justi intrabunt in eam.
        EN: This is the Lord's gate; the just will enter it.

117:21  LA: Confitebor tibi quoniam exaudisti me, et factus es mihi in salutem.
        EN: I will give thanks to You, for You have heard me and become my salvation.

117:22  LA: Lapidem quem reprobaverunt ædificantes, hic factus est in caput anguli.
        EN: The stone the builders rejected has become the cornerstone.

117:23  LA: A Domino factum est istud, et est mirabile in oculis nostris.
        EN: This has been done by the Lord, and it is wonderful in our eyes.

117:24  LA: Hæc est dies quam fecit Dominus ; exsultemus, et lætemur in ea.
        EN: This is the day the Lord has made; let us exult and rejoice in it.

117:25  LA: O Domine, salvum me fac ; o Domine, bene prosperare.
        EN: O Lord, save me; O Lord, grant good success.

117:26  LA: Benedictus qui venit in nomine Domini : benediximus vobis de domo Domini.
        EN: Blessed is the one who comes in the Lord's name. We have blessed you from the Lord's house.

117:27  LA: Deus Dominus, et illuxit nobis. Constituite diem solemnem in condensis, usque ad cornu altaris.
        EN: The Lord is God, and He has shone upon us. Establish a solemn feast with thick foliage, up to the horn of the altar.

117:28  LA: Deus meus es tu, et confitebor tibi ; Deus meus es tu, et exaltabo te. Confitebor tibi quoniam exaudisti me, et factus es mihi in salutem.
        EN: You are my God, and I will give thanks to You; You are my God, and I will exalt You. I will give thanks to You, for You have heard me and become my salvation.

117:29  LA: Confitemini Domino, quoniam bonus, quoniam in sæculum misericordia ejus.]
        EN: Give thanks to the Lord, for He is good, for His mercy endures forever.

PSALMS 118

118:1  LA: Alleluja . [<Aleph>Beati immaculati in via, qui ambulant in lege Domini.
       EN: Alleluia. [Aleph] Blessed are the blameless in the way, who walk in the Lord's law.

118:2  LA: Beati qui scrutantur testimonia ejus ; in toto corde exquirunt eum.
       EN: Blessed are those who search His testimonies; they seek Him with their whole heart.

118:3  LA: Non enim qui operantur iniquitatem in viis ejus ambulaverunt.
       EN: For those who practice iniquity have not walked in His ways.

118:4  LA: Tu mandasti mandata tua custodiri nimis.
       EN: You have commanded that Your commandments be kept diligently.

118:5  LA: Utinam dirigantur viæ meæ ad custodiendas justificationes tuas.
       EN: If only my ways were directed toward keeping Your statutes!

118:6  LA: Tunc non confundar, cum perspexero in omnibus mandatis tuis.
       EN: Then I will not be put to shame, when I look closely into all Your commandments.

118:7  LA: Confitebor tibi in directione cordis, in eo quod didici judicia justitiæ tuæ.
       EN: I will give thanks to You with an upright heart, because I have learned the judgments of Your righteousness.

118:8  LA: Justificationes tuas custodiam ; non me derelinquas usquequaque.
       EN: I will keep Your statutes; do not utterly abandon me.

118:9  LA: In quo corrigit adolescentior viam suam ? in custodiendo sermones tuos.
       EN: [Beth] How does a young man correct his way? By keeping Your sayings.

118:10  LA: In toto corde meo exquisivi te ; ne repellas me a mandatis tuis.
        EN: With my whole heart I have sought You; do not drive me away from Your commandments.

118:11  LA: In corde meo abscondi eloquia tua, ut non peccem tibi.
        EN: I have hidden Your utterances in my heart, so that I may not sin against You.

118:12  LA: Benedictus es, Domine ; doce me justificationes tuas.
        EN: Blessed are You, Lord; teach me Your statutes.

118:13  LA: In labiis meis pronuntiavi omnia judicia oris tui.
        EN: With my lips I have declared all the judgments of Your mouth.

118:14  LA: In via testimoniorum tuorum delectatus sum, sicut in omnibus divitiis.
        EN: I have delighted in the way of Your testimonies as in all riches.

118:15  LA: In mandatis tuis exercebor, et considerabo vias tuas.
        EN: I will occupy myself with Your commandments and consider Your ways.

118:16  LA: In justificationibus tuis meditabor : non obliviscar sermones tuos.
        EN: I will meditate on Your statutes; I will not forget Your sayings.

118:17  LA: Retribue servo tuo, vivifica me, et custodiam sermones tuos.
        EN: [Ghimel] Repay Your servant; give me life, and I will keep Your sayings.

118:18  LA: Revela oculos meos, et considerabo mirabilia de lege tua.
        EN: Uncover my eyes, and I will behold wonders from Your law.

118:19  LA: Incola ego sum in terra : non abscondas a me mandata tua.
        EN: I am a sojourner on the earth; do not hide Your commandments from me.

118:20  LA: Concupivit anima mea desiderare justificationes tuas in omni tempore.
        EN: My soul has longed to desire Your statutes at every moment.

118:21  LA: Increpasti superbos ; maledicti qui declinant a mandatis tuis.
        EN: You have rebuked the proud; cursed are those who turn aside from Your commandments.

118:22  LA: Aufer a me opprobrium et contemptum, quia testimonia tua exquisivi.
        EN: Take reproach and contempt from me, for I have sought Your testimonies.

118:23  LA: Etenim sederunt principes, et adversum me loquebantur ; servus autem tuus exercebatur in justificationibus tuis.
        EN: For princes sat and spoke against me, but Your servant occupied himself with Your statutes.

118:24  LA: Nam et testimonia tua meditatio mea est, et consilium meum justificationes tuæ.
        EN: For Your testimonies are my meditation, and Your statutes my counsel.

118:25  LA: Adhæsit pavimento anima mea : vivifica me secundum verbum tuum.
        EN: [Daleth] My soul has clung to the pavement; give me life according to Your word.

118:26  LA: Vias meas enuntiavi, et exaudisti me ; doce me justificationes tuas.
        EN: I have declared my ways, and You have heard me; teach me Your statutes.

118:27  LA: Viam justificationum tuarum instrue me, et exercebor in mirabilibus tuis.
        EN: Instruct me in the way of Your statutes, and I will occupy myself with Your wonders.

118:28  LA: Dormitavit anima mea præ tædio : confirma me in verbis tuis.
        EN: My soul has slumbered from weariness; strengthen me in Your words.

118:29  LA: Viam iniquitatis amove a me, et de lege tua miserere mei.
        EN: Remove the way of iniquity from me, and through Your law have mercy on me.

118:30  LA: Viam veritatis elegi ; judicia tua non sum oblitus.
        EN: I have chosen the way of truth; I have not forgotten Your judgments.

118:31  LA: Adhæsi testimoniis tuis, Domine ; noli me confundere.
        EN: I have clung to Your testimonies, Lord; do not put me to shame.

118:32  LA: Viam mandatorum tuorum cucurri, cum dilatasti cor meum.
        EN: I ran the way of Your commandments when You enlarged my heart.

118:33  LA: Legem pone mihi, Domine, viam justificationum tuarum, et exquiram eam semper.
        EN: [He] Set the way of Your statutes as a law for me, Lord, and I will seek it always.

118:34  LA: Da mihi intellectum, et scrutabor legem tuam, et custodiam illam in toto corde meo.
        EN: Give me understanding, and I will search Your law and keep it with my whole heart.

118:35  LA: Deduc me in semitam mandatorum tuorum, quia ipsam volui.
        EN: Lead me along the path of Your commandments, for I have desired it.

118:36  LA: Inclina cor meum in testimonia tua, et non in avaritiam.
        EN: Incline my heart toward Your testimonies and not toward greed.

118:37  LA: Averte oculos meos, ne videant vanitatem ; in via tua vivifica me.
        EN: Turn my eyes away from seeing vanity; give me life in Your way.

118:38  LA: Statue servo tuo eloquium tuum in timore tuo.
        EN: Establish Your utterance for Your servant in the fear of You.

118:39  LA: Amputa opprobrium meum quod suspicatus sum, quia judicia tua jucunda.
        EN: Cut away the reproach I have feared, for Your judgments are delightful.

118:40  LA: Ecce concupivi mandata tua : in æquitate tua vivifica me.
        EN: Behold, I have desired Your commandments; give me life in Your equity.

118:41  LA: Et veniat super me misericordia tua, Domine ; salutare tuum secundum eloquium tuum.
        EN: [Vau] Let Your mercy come upon me, Lord, Your salvation according to Your utterance.

118:42  LA: Et respondebo exprobrantibus mihi verbum, quia speravi in sermonibus tuis.
        EN: Then I will answer those who reproach me with a word, for I have hoped in Your sayings.

118:43  LA: Et ne auferas de ore meo verbum veritatis usquequaque, quia in judiciis tuis supersperavi.
        EN: Do not utterly take the word of truth from my mouth, for I have placed great hope in Your judgments.

118:44  LA: Et custodiam legem tuam semper, in sæculum et in sæculum sæculi.
        EN: I will keep Your law always, forever and ever.

118:45  LA: Et ambulabam in latitudine, quia mandata tua exquisivi.
        EN: I walked in a spacious place, for I sought Your commandments.

118:46  LA: Et loquebar in testimoniis tuis in conspectu regum, et non confundebar.
        EN: I spoke of Your testimonies in the sight of kings and was not put to shame.

118:47  LA: Et meditabar in mandatis tuis, quæ dilexi.
        EN: I meditated on Your commandments, which I loved.

118:48  LA: Et levavi manus meas ad mandata tua, quæ dilexi, et exercebar in justificationibus tuis.
        EN: I lifted my hands to Your commandments, which I loved, and occupied myself with Your statutes.

118:49  LA: Memor esto verbi tui servo tuo, in quo mihi spem dedisti.
        EN: [Zain] Remember Your word to Your servant, in which You gave me hope.
