118:50  LA: Hæc me consolata est in humilitate mea, quia eloquium tuum vivificavit me.
        EN: This has comforted me in my lowliness, for Your utterance has given me life.

118:51  LA: Superbi inique agebant usquequaque ; a lege autem tua non declinavi.
        EN: The proud acted most wickedly, but I did not turn aside from Your law.

118:52  LA: Memor fui judiciorum tuorum a sæculo, Domine, et consolatus sum.
        EN: I remembered Your judgments of old, Lord, and was comforted.

118:53  LA: Defectio tenuit me, pro peccatoribus derelinquentibus legem tuam.
        EN: Faintness seized me because of sinners who forsake Your law.

118:54  LA: Cantabiles mihi erant justificationes tuæ in loco peregrinationis meæ.
        EN: Your statutes were songs to me in the place of my pilgrimage.

118:55  LA: Memor fui nocte nominis tui, Domine, et custodivi legem tuam.
        EN: In the night I remembered Your name, Lord, and kept Your law.

118:56  LA: Hæc facta est mihi, quia justificationes tuas exquisivi.
        EN: This has come to me because I sought Your statutes.

118:57  LA: Portio mea, Domine, dixi custodire legem tuam.
        EN: [Heth] My portion, Lord, I have said, is to keep Your law.

118:58  LA: Deprecatus sum faciem tuam in toto corde meo ; miserere mei secundum eloquium tuum.
        EN: I have entreated Your face with my whole heart; have mercy on me according to Your utterance.

118:59  LA: Cogitavi vias meas, et converti pedes meos in testimonia tua.
        EN: I considered my ways and turned my feet toward Your testimonies.

118:60  LA: Paratus sum, et non sum turbatus, ut custodiam mandata tua.
        EN: I am ready and have not been troubled, so that I may keep Your commandments.

118:61  LA: Funes peccatorum circumplexi sunt me, et legem tuam non sum oblitus.
        EN: The cords of sinners have entwined me, but I have not forgotten Your law.

118:62  LA: Media nocte surgebam ad confitendum tibi, super judicia justificationis tuæ.
        EN: At midnight I would rise to give thanks to You for the judgments of Your righteousness.

118:63  LA: Particeps ego sum omnium timentium te, et custodientium mandata tua.
        EN: I am a companion of all who fear You and keep Your commandments.

118:64  LA: Misericordia tua, Domine, plena est terra ; justificationes tuas doce me.
        EN: The earth is full of Your mercy, Lord; teach me Your statutes.

118:65  LA: Bonitatem fecisti cum servo tuo, Domine, secundum verbum tuum.
        EN: [Teth] You have shown goodness to Your servant, Lord, according to Your word.

118:66  LA: Bonitatem, et disciplinam, et scientiam doce me, quia mandatis tuis credidi.
        EN: Teach me goodness, discipline, and knowledge, for I have believed Your commandments.

118:67  LA: Priusquam humiliarer ego deliqui : propterea eloquium tuum custodivi.
        EN: Before I was humbled, I sinned; therefore I have kept Your utterance.

118:68  LA: Bonus es tu, et in bonitate tua doce me justificationes tuas.
        EN: You are good, and in Your goodness teach me Your statutes.

118:69  LA: Multiplicata est super me iniquitas superborum ; ego autem in toto corde meo scrutabor mandata tua.
        EN: The iniquity of the proud has multiplied against me, but I will search Your commandments with my whole heart.

118:70  LA: Coagulatum est sicut lac cor eorum ; ego vero legem tuam meditatus sum.
        EN: Their heart has curdled like milk, but I have meditated on Your law.

118:71  LA: Bonum mihi quia humiliasti me, ut discam justificationes tuas.
        EN: It is good for me that You have humbled me, so that I may learn Your statutes.

118:72  LA: Bonum mihi lex oris tui, super millia auri et argenti.
        EN: The law of Your mouth is better for me than thousands of gold and silver pieces.

118:73  LA: Manus tuæ fecerunt me, et plasmaverunt me : da mihi intellectum, et discam mandata tua.
        EN: [Jod] Your hands have made me and formed me; give me understanding, and I will learn Your commandments.

118:74  LA: Qui timent te videbunt me et lætabuntur, quia in verba tua supersperavi.
        EN: Those who fear You will see me and rejoice, for I have placed great hope in Your words.

118:75  LA: Cognovi, Domine, quia æquitas judicia tua, et in veritate tua humiliasti me.
        EN: I know, Lord, that Your judgments are equity and that in Your truth You have humbled me.

118:76  LA: Fiat misericordia tua ut consoletur me, secundum eloquium tuum servo tuo.
        EN: Let Your mercy comfort me according to Your utterance to Your servant.

118:77  LA: Veniant mihi miserationes tuæ, et vivam, quia lex tua meditatio mea est.
        EN: Let Your compassions come to me, and I will live, for Your law is my meditation.

118:78  LA: Confundantur superbi, quia injuste iniquitatem fecerunt in me ; ego autem exercebor in mandatis tuis.
        EN: Let the proud be put to shame, for they have unjustly worked iniquity against me; but I will occupy myself with Your commandments.

118:79  LA: Convertantur mihi timentes te, et qui noverunt testimonia tua.
        EN: Let those who fear You turn toward me, and those who know Your testimonies.

118:80  LA: Fiat cor meum immaculatum in justificationibus tuis, ut non confundar.
        EN: Let my heart be blameless in Your statutes, so that I may not be put to shame.

118:81  LA: Defecit in salutare tuum anima mea, et in verbum tuum supersperavi.
        EN: [Caph] My soul has fainted for Your salvation, and I have placed great hope in Your word.

118:82  LA: Defecerunt oculi mei in eloquium tuum, dicentes : Quando consolaberis me ?
        EN: My eyes have failed for Your utterance, saying: When will You comfort me?

118:83  LA: Quia factus sum sicut uter in pruina ; justificationes tuas non sum oblitus.
        EN: For I have become like a wineskin in the frost; I have not forgotten Your statutes.

118:84  LA: Quot sunt dies servi tui ? quando facies de persequentibus me judicium ?
        EN: How many are Your servant's days? When will You pass judgment upon those who persecute me?

118:85  LA: Narraverunt mihi iniqui fabulationes, sed non ut lex tua.
        EN: The wicked have told me tales, but they are not like Your law.

118:86  LA: Omnia mandata tua veritas : inique persecuti sunt me, adjuva me.
        EN: All Your commandments are truth; they have persecuted me unjustly. Help me.

118:87  LA: Paulominus consummaverunt me in terra ; ego autem non dereliqui mandata tua.
        EN: They almost made an end of me on earth, but I did not forsake Your commandments.

118:88  LA: Secundum misericordiam tuam vivifica me, et custodiam testimonia oris tui.
        EN: Give me life according to Your mercy, and I will keep the testimonies of Your mouth.

118:89  LA: In æternum, Domine, verbum tuum permanet in cælo.
        EN: [Lamed] Forever, Lord, Your word remains in heaven.

118:90  LA: In generationem et generationem veritas tua ; fundasti terram, et permanet.
        EN: Your truth endures from generation to generation; You founded the earth, and it remains.

118:91  LA: Ordinatione tua perseverat dies, quoniam omnia serviunt tibi.
        EN: By Your ordering the day continues, for all things serve You.

118:92  LA: Nisi quod lex tua meditatio mea est, tunc forte periissem in humilitate mea.
        EN: Had Your law not been my meditation, I might then have perished in my lowliness.

118:93  LA: In æternum non obliviscar justificationes tuas, quia in ipsis vivificasti me.
        EN: I will never forget Your statutes, for through them You have given me life.

118:94  LA: Tuus sum ego ; salvum me fac : quoniam justificationes tuas exquisivi.
        EN: I am Yours; save me, for I have sought Your statutes.

118:95  LA: Me exspectaverunt peccatores ut perderent me ; testimonia tua intellexi.
        EN: Sinners have waited for me to destroy me; I have understood Your testimonies.

118:96  LA: Omnis consummationis vidi finem, latum mandatum tuum nimis.
        EN: I have seen the end of every perfection; Your commandment is exceedingly broad.

118:97  LA: Quomodo dilexi legem tuam, Domine ! tota die meditatio mea est.
        EN: [Mem] How I have loved Your law, Lord! It is my meditation all day long.

118:98  LA: Super inimicos meos prudentem me fecisti mandato tuo, quia in æternum mihi est.
        EN: By Your commandment You have made me wiser than my enemies, for it is mine forever.

118:99  LA: Super omnes docentes me intellexi, quia testimonia tua meditatio mea est.
        EN: I have understood more than all who teach me, for Your testimonies are my meditation.

118:100  LA: Super senes intellexi, quia mandata tua quæsivi.
         EN: I have understood more than the elders, for I have sought Your commandments.

118:101  LA: Ab omni via mala prohibui pedes meos, ut custodiam verba tua.
         EN: I have kept my feet from every evil way, so that I may keep Your words.

118:102  LA: A judiciis tuis non declinavi, quia tu legem posuisti mihi.
         EN: I have not turned aside from Your judgments, for You have laid down a law for me.

118:103  LA: Quam dulcia faucibus meis eloquia tua ! super mel ori meo.
         EN: How sweet Your utterances are to my palate, sweeter than honey to my mouth!

118:104  LA: A mandatis tuis intellexi ; propterea odivi omnem viam iniquitatis.
         EN: From Your commandments I have gained understanding; therefore I have hated every way of iniquity.

118:105  LA: Lucerna pedibus meis verbum tuum, et lumen semitis meis.
         EN: [Nun] Your word is a lamp for my feet and a light for my paths.

118:106  LA: Juravi et statui custodire judicia justitiæ tuæ.
         EN: I have sworn and resolved to keep the judgments of Your righteousness.

118:107  LA: Humiliatus sum usquequaque, Domine ; vivifica me secundum verbum tuum.
         EN: I am utterly humbled, Lord; give me life according to Your word.

118:108  LA: Voluntaria oris mei beneplacita fac, Domine, et judicia tua doce me.
         EN: Make the freewill offerings of my mouth pleasing, Lord, and teach me Your judgments.

118:109  LA: Anima mea in manibus meis semper, et legem tuam non sum oblitus.
         EN: My soul is always in my hands, and I have not forgotten Your law.

118:110  LA: Posuerunt peccatores laqueum mihi, et de mandatis tuis non erravi.
         EN: Sinners have set a snare for me, and I have not strayed from Your commandments.

118:111  LA: Hæreditate acquisivi testimonia tua in æternum, quia exsultatio cordis mei sunt.
         EN: I have acquired Your testimonies as an inheritance forever, for they are my heart's exultation.

118:112  LA: Inclinavi cor meum ad faciendas justificationes tuas in æternum, propter retributionem.
         EN: I have inclined my heart to carry out Your statutes forever, for the sake of the reward.

118:113  LA: Iniquos odio habui, et legem tuam dilexi.
         EN: [Samech] I have hated the wicked and loved Your law.

118:114  LA: Adjutor et susceptor meus es tu, et in verbum tuum supersperavi.
         EN: You are my helper and upholder, and I have placed great hope in Your word.

118:115  LA: Declinate a me, maligni, et scrutabor mandata Dei mei.
         EN: Turn away from me, evildoers, and I will search the commandments of my God.

118:116  LA: Suscipe me secundum eloquium tuum, et vivam, et non confundas me ab exspectatione mea.
         EN: Uphold me according to Your utterance, and I will live; do not put me to shame in my expectation.

118:117  LA: Adjuva me, et salvus ero, et meditabor in justificationibus tuis semper.
         EN: Help me, and I will be saved, and I will meditate on Your statutes always.

118:118  LA: Sprevisti omnes discedentes a judiciis tuis, quia injusta cogitatio eorum.
         EN: You have despised all who depart from Your judgments, for their thought is unjust.

118:119  LA: Prævaricantes reputavi omnes peccatores terræ ; ideo dilexi testimonia tua.
         EN: I have reckoned all the sinners of the earth as transgressors; therefore I have loved Your testimonies.

118:120  LA: Confige timore tuo carnes meas ; a judiciis enim tuis timui.
         EN: Pierce my flesh with the fear of You, for I have feared Your judgments.

118:121  LA: Feci judicium et justitiam : non tradas me calumniantibus me.
         EN: [Ain] I have practiced judgment and righteousness; do not hand me over to those who slander me.

118:122  LA: Suscipe servum tuum in bonum : non calumnientur me superbi.
         EN: Uphold Your servant for good; do not let the proud slander me.

118:123  LA: Oculi mei defecerunt in salutare tuum, et in eloquium justitiæ tuæ.
         EN: My eyes have failed for Your salvation and for the utterance of Your righteousness.

118:124  LA: Fac cum servo tuo secundum misericordiam tuam, et justificationes tuas doce me.
         EN: Deal with Your servant according to Your mercy, and teach me Your statutes.

118:125  LA: Servus tuus sum ego : da mihi intellectum, ut sciam testimonia tua.
         EN: I am Your servant; give me understanding, so that I may know Your testimonies.

118:126  LA: Tempus faciendi, Domine : dissipaverunt legem tuam.
         EN: It is time to act, Lord; they have torn apart Your law.

118:127  LA: Ideo dilexi mandata tua super aurum et topazion.
         EN: Therefore I have loved Your commandments more than gold and topaz.

118:128  LA: Propterea ad omnia mandata tua dirigebar ; omnem viam iniquam odio habui.
         EN: Therefore I was directed toward all Your commandments; I have hated every unjust way.

118:129  LA: Mirabilia testimonia tua : ideo scrutata est ea anima mea.
         EN: [Phe] Your testimonies are wonderful; therefore my soul has searched them.

118:130  LA: Declaratio sermonum tuorum illuminat, et intellectum dat parvulis.
         EN: The unfolding of Your sayings gives light and gives understanding to the little ones.

118:131  LA: Os meum aperui, et attraxi spiritum : quia mandata tua desiderabam.
         EN: I opened my mouth and drew in breath, for I desired Your commandments.

118:132  LA: Aspice in me, et miserere mei, secundum judicium diligentium nomen tuum.
         EN: Look upon me and have mercy on me, according to Your judgment for those who love Your name.

118:133  LA: Gressus meos dirige secundum eloquium tuum, et non dominetur mei omnis injustitia.
         EN: Direct my steps according to Your utterance, and let no injustice rule over me.

118:134  LA: Redime me a calumniis hominum ut custodiam mandata tua.
         EN: Redeem me from human slanders, so that I may keep Your commandments.

118:135  LA: Faciem tuam illumina super servum tuum, et doce me justificationes tuas.
         EN: Make Your face shine upon Your servant, and teach me Your statutes.

118:136  LA: Exitus aquarum deduxerunt oculi mei, quia non custodierunt legem tuam.
         EN: My eyes have poured out streams of water, because they have not kept Your law.

118:137  LA: Justus es, Domine, et rectum judicium tuum.
         EN: [Sade] You are just, Lord, and Your judgment is upright.

118:138  LA: Mandasti justitiam testimonia tua, et veritatem tuam nimis.
         EN: You have commanded Your testimonies in righteousness and in the abundance of Your truth.

118:139  LA: Tabescere me fecit zelus meus, quia obliti sunt verba tua inimici mei.
         EN: My zeal has made me waste away, for my enemies have forgotten Your words.

118:140  LA: Ignitum eloquium tuum vehementer, et servus tuus dilexit illud.
         EN: Your utterance is intensely tried by fire, and Your servant has loved it.

118:141  LA: Adolescentulus sum ego et contemptus ; justificationes tuas non sum oblitus.
         EN: I am young and despised; I have not forgotten Your statutes.

118:142  LA: Justitia tua, justitia in æternum, et lex tua veritas.
         EN: Your righteousness is righteousness forever, and Your law is truth.

118:143  LA: Tribulatio et angustia invenerunt me ; mandata tua meditatio mea est.
         EN: Tribulation and distress have found me; Your commandments are my meditation.

118:144  LA: Æquitas testimonia tua in æternum : intellectum da mihi, et vivam.
         EN: Your testimonies are equity forever; give me understanding, and I will live.

118:145  LA: Clamavi in toto corde meo : exaudi me, Domine ; justificationes tuas requiram.
         EN: [Coph] I have cried out with my whole heart; hear me, Lord. I will seek Your statutes.

118:146  LA: Clamavi ad te ; salvum me fac : ut custodiam mandata tua.
         EN: I have cried out to You; save me, so that I may keep Your commandments.

118:147  LA: Præveni in maturitate, et clamavi : quia in verba tua supersperavi.
         EN: I rose early and cried out, for I have placed great hope in Your words.

118:148  LA: Prævenerunt oculi mei ad te diluculo, ut meditarer eloquia tua.
         EN: My eyes turned early toward You at dawn, so that I might meditate on Your utterances.

118:149  LA: Vocem meam audi secundum misericordiam tuam, Domine, et secundum judicium tuum vivifica me.
         EN: Hear my voice according to Your mercy, Lord, and give me life according to Your judgment.

118:150  LA: Appropinquaverunt persequentes me iniquitati : a lege autem tua longe facti sunt.
         EN: Those who persecute me have drawn near to iniquity, but they have gone far from Your law.

118:151  LA: Prope es tu, Domine, et omnes viæ tuæ veritas.
         EN: You are near, Lord, and all Your ways are truth.

118:152  LA: Initio cognovi de testimoniis tuis, quia in æternum fundasti ea.
         EN: From the beginning I have known from Your testimonies that You founded them forever.

118:153  LA: Vide humilitatem meam, et eripe me, quia legem tuam non sum oblitus.
         EN: [Res] See my lowliness and rescue me, for I have not forgotten Your law.

118:154  LA: Judica judicium meum, et redime me : propter eloquium tuum vivifica me.
         EN: Judge my cause and redeem me; give me life for the sake of Your utterance.

118:155  LA: Longe a peccatoribus salus, quia justificationes tuas non exquisierunt.
         EN: Salvation is far from sinners, for they have not sought Your statutes.

118:156  LA: Misericordiæ tuæ multæ, Domine ; secundum judicium tuum vivifica me.
         EN: Your mercies are many, Lord; give me life according to Your judgment.

118:157  LA: Multi qui persequuntur me, et tribulant me ; a testimoniis tuis non declinavi.
         EN: Many persecute and afflict me; I have not turned aside from Your testimonies.

118:158  LA: Vidi prævaricantes et tabescebam, quia eloquia tua non custodierunt.
         EN: I saw transgressors and wasted away, because they did not keep Your utterances.

118:159  LA: Vide quoniam mandata tua dilexi, Domine : in misericordia tua vivifica me.
         EN: See that I have loved Your commandments, Lord; give me life in Your mercy.

118:160  LA: Principium verborum tuorum veritas ; in æternum omnia judicia justitiæ tuæ.
         EN: The beginning of Your words is truth; all the judgments of Your righteousness endure forever.

118:161  LA: Principes persecuti sunt me gratis, et a verbis tuis formidavit cor meum.
         EN: [Sin] Princes have persecuted me without cause, and my heart has trembled at Your words.

118:162  LA: Lætabor ego super eloquia tua, sicut qui invenit spolia multa.
         EN: I will rejoice over Your utterances like one who finds abundant spoils.

118:163  LA: Iniquitatem odio habui, et abominatus sum, legem autem tuam dilexi.
         EN: I have hated and abhorred iniquity, but I have loved Your law.

118:164  LA: Septies in die laudem dixi tibi, super judicia justitiæ tuæ.
         EN: Seven times a day I have spoken praise to You for the judgments of Your righteousness.

118:165  LA: Pax multa diligentibus legem tuam, et non est illis scandalum.
         EN: Those who love Your law have great peace, and there is no stumbling block for them.

118:166  LA: Exspectabam salutare tuum, Domine, et mandata tua dilexi.
         EN: I awaited Your salvation, Lord, and loved Your commandments.

118:167  LA: Custodivit anima mea testimonia tua, et dilexit ea vehementer.
         EN: My soul has kept Your testimonies and loved them exceedingly.

118:168  LA: Servavi mandata tua et testimonia tua, quia omnes viæ meæ in conspectu tuo.
         EN: I have observed Your commandments and testimonies, for all my ways are in Your sight.

118:169  LA: Appropinquet deprecatio mea in conspectu tuo, Domine ; juxta eloquium tuum da mihi intellectum.
         EN: [Tau] Let my prayer draw near in Your sight, Lord; give me understanding according to Your utterance.

118:170  LA: Intret postulatio mea in conspectu tuo ; secundum eloquium tuum eripe me.
         EN: Let my petition enter Your presence; rescue me according to Your utterance.

118:171  LA: Eructabunt labia mea hymnum, cum docueris me justificationes tuas.
         EN: My lips will pour forth a hymn when You have taught me Your statutes.

118:172  LA: Pronuntiabit lingua mea eloquium tuum, quia omnia mandata tua æquitas.
         EN: My tongue will proclaim Your utterance, for all Your commandments are equity.

118:173  LA: Fiat manus tua ut salvet me, quoniam mandata tua elegi.
         EN: May Your hand be there to save me, for I have chosen Your commandments.

118:174  LA: Concupivi salutare tuum, Domine, et lex tua meditatio mea est.
         EN: I have longed for Your salvation, Lord, and Your law is my meditation.

118:175  LA: Vivet anima mea, et laudabit te, et judicia tua adjuvabunt me.
         EN: My soul will live and praise You, and Your judgments will help me.

118:176  LA: Erravi sicut ovis quæ periit : quære servum tuum, quia mandata tua non sum oblitus.]
         EN: I have strayed like a sheep that is lost; seek Your servant, for I have not forgotten Your commandments.

PSALMS 119

119:1  LA: Canticum graduum. [Ad Dominum cum tribularer clamavi, et exaudivit me.
       EN: A song of ascents. When I was afflicted, I cried to the Lord, and He heard me.

119:2  LA: Domine, libera animam meam a labiis iniquis et a lingua dolosa.
       EN: Lord, deliver my soul from wicked lips and a deceitful tongue.

119:3  LA: Quid detur tibi, aut quid apponatur tibi ad linguam dolosam ?
       EN: What may be given to you, or added to you, for a deceitful tongue?

119:4  LA: Sagittæ potentis acutæ, cum carbonibus desolatoriis.
       EN: A mighty man's sharp arrows, with devastating coals.

119:5  LA: Heu mihi, quia incolatus meus prolongatus est ! habitavi cum habitantibus Cedar ;
       EN: Alas for me, for my sojourn has been prolonged! I have dwelt with the inhabitants of Kedar;

119:6  LA: multum incola fuit anima mea.
       EN: my soul has long been a sojourner.

119:7  LA: Cum his qui oderunt pacem eram pacificus ; cum loquebar illis, impugnabant me gratis.]
       EN: With those who hated peace I was peaceable; when I spoke to them, they attacked me without cause.

PSALMS 120

120:1  LA: Canticum graduum. [Levavi oculos meos in montes, unde veniet auxilium mihi.
       EN: A song of ascents. I have lifted my eyes to the mountains, from where help will come to me.

120:2  LA: Auxilium meum a Domino, qui fecit cælum et terram.
       EN: My help comes from the Lord, who made heaven and earth.

120:3  LA: Non det in commotionem pedem tuum, neque dormitet qui custodit te.
       EN: May He not let your foot be shaken, and may He who guards you not slumber.

120:4  LA: Ecce non dormitabit neque dormiet qui custodit Israël.
       EN: Behold, He who guards Israel will neither slumber nor sleep.

120:5  LA: Dominus custodit te ; Dominus protectio tua super manum dexteram tuam.
       EN: The Lord guards you; the Lord is your protection at your right hand.

120:6  LA: Per diem sol non uret te, neque luna per noctem.
       EN: By day the sun will not burn you, nor the moon by night.

120:7  LA: Dominus custodit te ab omni malo ; custodiat animam tuam Dominus.
       EN: The Lord guards you from every evil; may the Lord guard your soul.

120:8  LA: Dominus custodiat introitum tuum et exitum tuum, ex hoc nunc et usque in sæculum.]
       EN: May the Lord guard your coming in and your going out, from now and forever.

PSALMS 121

121:1  LA: Canticum graduum. [Lætatus sum in his quæ dicta sunt mihi : In domum Domini ibimus.
       EN: A song of ascents. I rejoiced at what was said to me: We will go into the Lord's house.

121:2  LA: Stantes erant pedes nostri in atriis tuis, Jerusalem.
       EN: Our feet stood in your courts, Jerusalem.

121:3  LA: Jerusalem, quæ ædificatur ut civitas, cujus participatio ejus in idipsum.
       EN: Jerusalem is built as a city whose fellowship is united in itself.

121:4  LA: Illuc enim ascenderunt tribus, tribus Domini : testimonium Israël, ad confitendum nomini Domini.
       EN: For there the tribes went up, the tribes of the Lord, a testimony to Israel, to give thanks to the Lord's name.

121:5  LA: Quia illic sederunt sedes in judicio, sedes super domum David.
       EN: For there thrones sat in judgment, thrones over the house of David.

121:6  LA: Rogate quæ ad pacem sunt Jerusalem, et abundantia diligentibus te.
       EN: Ask for the things that bring peace to Jerusalem, and abundance for those who love you.

121:7  LA: Fiat pax in virtute tua, et abundantia in turribus tuis.
       EN: May there be peace in your strength and abundance in your towers.

121:8  LA: Propter fratres meos et proximos meos, loquebar pacem de te.
       EN: For the sake of my brothers and neighbors, I spoke peace concerning you.

121:9  LA: Propter domum Domini Dei nostri, quæsivi bona tibi.]
       EN: For the sake of the house of the Lord our God, I have sought good things for you.

PSALMS 122

122:1  LA: Canticum graduum. [Ad te levavi oculos meos, qui habitas in cælis.
       EN: A song of ascents. I have lifted my eyes to You, who dwell in the heavens.

122:2  LA: Ecce sicut oculi servorum in manibus dominorum suorum ; sicut oculi ancillæ in manibus dominæ suæ : ita oculi nostri ad Dominum Deum nostrum, donec misereatur nostri.
       EN: Behold, as servants' eyes are upon their masters' hands, as a maidservant's eyes are upon her mistress's hands, so our eyes are upon the Lord our God until He has mercy on us.

122:3  LA: Miserere nostri, Domine, miserere nostri, quia multum repleti sumus despectione ;
       EN: Have mercy on us, Lord, have mercy on us, for we have been filled with much contempt.

122:4  LA: quia multum repleta est anima nostra opprobrium abundantibus, et despectio superbis.]
       EN: For our soul is filled to overflowing: a reproach to the prosperous and a contempt to the proud.

PSALMS 123

123:1  LA: Canticum graduum. [Nisi quia Dominus erat in nobis, dicat nunc Israël,
       EN: A song of ascents. Had the Lord not been among us, let Israel now say,

123:2  LA: nisi quia Dominus erat in nobis : cum exsurgerent homines in nos,
       EN: had the Lord not been among us, when men rose against us,

123:3  LA: forte vivos deglutissent nos ; cum irasceretur furor eorum in nos,
       EN: they might have swallowed us alive. When their fury raged against us,

123:4  LA: forsitan aqua absorbuisset nos ;
       EN: the water might have swallowed us. Our soul passed through a torrent; our soul might have passed through unbearable water.

123:5  LA: forsitan aqua absorbuisset nos ;
       EN: the water might have swallowed us. Our soul passed through a torrent; our soul might have passed through unbearable water.

123:6  LA: Benedictus Dominus, qui non dedit nos in captionem dentibus eorum.
       EN: Blessed is the Lord, who did not give us as prey to their teeth.

123:7  LA: Anima nostra sicut passer erepta est de laqueo venantium ; laqueus contritus est, et nos liberati sumus.
       EN: Our soul has been rescued like a sparrow from the hunters' snare. The snare has been broken, and we have been delivered.

123:8  LA: Adjutorium nostrum in nomine Domini, qui fecit cælum et terram.]
       EN: Our help is in the Lord's name, who made heaven and earth.

PSALMS 124

124:1  LA: Canticum graduum. [Qui confidunt in Domino, sicut mons Sion : non commovebitur in æternum, qui habitat
       EN: A song of ascents. Those who trust in the Lord are like Mount Zion. He will never be shaken, who dwells

124:2  LA: in Jerusalem. Montes in circuitu ejus ; et Dominus in circuitu populi sui, ex hoc nunc et usque in sæculum.
       EN: in Jerusalem. Mountains are around her, and the Lord is around His people, from now and forever.

124:3  LA: Quia non relinquet Dominus virgam peccatorum super sortem justorum : ut non extendant justi ad iniquitatem manus suas,
       EN: For the Lord will not leave the rod of sinners upon the lot of the just, lest the just stretch their hands toward iniquity.

124:4  LA: benefac, Domine, bonis, et rectis corde.
       EN: Do good, Lord, to the good and to the upright in heart.

124:5  LA: Declinantes autem in obligationes, adducet Dominus cum operantibus iniquitatem. Pax super Israël !]
       EN: But those who turn aside into entanglements the Lord will lead away with the workers of iniquity. Peace upon Israel!

PSALMS 125

125:1  LA: Canticum graduum. [In convertendo Dominus captivitatem Sion, facti sumus sicut consolati.
       EN: A song of ascents. When the Lord turned back Zion's captivity, we became like those comforted.

125:2  LA: Tunc repletum est gaudio os nostrum, et lingua nostra exsultatione. Tunc dicent inter gentes : Magnificavit Dominus facere cum eis.
       EN: Then our mouth was filled with joy and our tongue with exultation. Then they will say among the nations: The Lord has done great things for them.

125:3  LA: Magnificavit Dominus facere nobiscum ; facti sumus lætantes.
       EN: The Lord has done great things for us; we have become glad.

125:4  LA: Converte, Domine, captivitatem nostram, sicut torrens in austro.
       EN: Turn back our captivity, Lord, like a torrent in the south.

125:5  LA: Qui seminant in lacrimis, in exsultatione metent.
       EN: Those who sow in tears will reap in exultation.

125:6  LA: Euntes ibant et flebant, mittentes semina sua. Venientes autem venient cum exsultatione, portantes manipulos suos.]
       EN: They went on their way weeping, casting their seed. But they will surely come with exultation, carrying their sheaves.

PSALMS 126

126:1  LA: Canticum graduum Salomonis. [Nisi Dominus ædificaverit domum, in vanum laboraverunt qui ædificant eam. Nisi Dominus custodierit civitatem, frustra vigilat qui custodit eam.
       EN: A song of ascents. Of Solomon. Unless the Lord builds the house, those who build it have labored in vain. Unless the Lord guards the city, its guard keeps watch in vain.

126:2  LA: Vanum est vobis ante lucem surgere : surgite postquam sederitis, qui manducatis panem doloris. Cum dederit dilectis suis somnum,
       EN: It is vain for you to rise before daylight. Rise after you have sat down, you who eat the bread of sorrow. When He has given sleep to His beloved,

126:3  LA: ecce hæreditas Domini, filii ; merces, fructus ventris.
       EN: behold, children are the Lord's inheritance; the fruit of the womb is a reward.

126:4  LA: Sicut sagittæ in manu potentis, ita filii excussorum.
       EN: Like arrows in the hand of a mighty man, so are the sons of those driven out.

126:5  LA: Beatus vir qui implevit desiderium suum ex ipsis : non confundetur cum loquetur inimicis suis in porta.]
       EN: Blessed is the man who has satisfied his desire with them; he will not be put to shame when he speaks with his enemies at the gate.

PSALMS 127

127:1  LA: Canticum graduum. [Beati omnes qui timent Dominum, qui ambulant in viis ejus.
       EN: A song of ascents. Blessed are all who fear the Lord, who walk in His ways.

127:2  LA: Labores manuum tuarum quia manducabis : beatus es, et bene tibi erit.
       EN: For you will eat the labors of your hands; you are blessed, and it will be well with you.

127:3  LA: Uxor tua sicut vitis abundans in lateribus domus tuæ ; filii tui sicut novellæ olivarum in circuitu mensæ tuæ.
       EN: Your wife is like a fruitful vine on the sides of your house; your children are like young olive trees around your table.

127:4  LA: Ecce sic benedicetur homo qui timet Dominum.
       EN: Behold, thus will the man who fears the Lord be blessed.

127:5  LA: Benedicat tibi Dominus ex Sion, et videas bona Jerusalem omnibus diebus vitæ tuæ.
       EN: May the Lord bless you from Zion, and may you see Jerusalem's good things all the days of your life.

127:6  LA: Et videas filios filiorum tuorum : pacem super Israël.]
       EN: May you see your children's children, peace upon Israel.

PSALMS 128

128:1  LA: Canticum graduum. [Sæpe expugnaverunt me a juventute mea, dicat nunc Israël ;
       EN: A song of ascents. Often they have fought against me from my youth, let Israel now say.

128:2  LA: sæpe expugnaverunt me a juventute mea : etenim non potuerunt mihi.
       EN: Often they have fought against me from my youth, yet they have not prevailed against me.

128:3  LA: Supra dorsum meum fabricaverunt peccatores ; prolongaverunt iniquitatem suam.
       EN: Sinners have built upon my back; they have prolonged their iniquity.

128:4  LA: Dominus justus concidit cervices peccatorum.
       EN: The just Lord has cut through the necks of sinners.

128:5  LA: Confundantur, et convertantur retrorsum omnes qui oderunt Sion.
       EN: May all who hate Zion be put to shame and turned backward.

128:6  LA: Fiant sicut fœnum tectorum, quod priusquam evellatur exaruit :
       EN: May they become like grass upon the rooftops, which withers before it is pulled up,

128:7  LA: de quo non implevit manum suam qui metit, et sinum suum qui manipulos colligit.
       EN: with which the reaper has not filled his hand, nor the gatherer of sheaves his bosom.

128:8  LA: Et non dixerunt qui præteribant : Benedictio Domini super vos. Benediximus vobis in nomine Domini.]
       EN: Those passing by have not said: The Lord's blessing be upon you. We have blessed you in the Lord's name.

PSALMS 129

129:1  LA: Canticum graduum. [De profundis clamavi ad te, Domine ;
       EN: A song of ascents. From the depths I have cried to You, Lord.

129:2  LA: Domine, exaudi vocem meam. Fiant aures tuæ intendentes in vocem deprecationis meæ.
       EN: Lord, hear my voice. Let Your ears attend to the voice of my prayer.

129:3  LA: Si iniquitates observaveris, Domine, Domine, quis sustinebit ?
       EN: If You keep watch over iniquities, Lord, Lord, who will endure?

129:4  LA: Quia apud te propitiatio est ; et propter legem tuam sustinui te, Domine. Sustinuit anima mea in verbo ejus :
       EN: For with You is atonement, and for the sake of Your law I have waited for You, Lord. My soul has waited upon His word;

129:5  LA: Quia apud te propitiatio est ; et propter legem tuam sustinui te, Domine. Sustinuit anima mea in verbo ejus :
       EN: For with You is atonement, and for the sake of Your law I have waited for You, Lord. My soul has waited upon His word;

129:6  LA: speravit anima mea in Domino.
       EN: my soul has hoped in the Lord. From the morning watch until night, let Israel hope in the Lord.

129:7  LA: Quia apud Dominum misericordia, et copiosa apud eum redemptio.
       EN: For with the Lord is mercy, and with Him is abundant redemption.

129:8  LA: Et ipse redimet Israël ex omnibus iniquitatibus ejus.]
       EN: He Himself will redeem Israel from all its iniquities.

PSALMS 130

130:1  LA: Canticum graduum David. [Domine, non est exaltatum cor meum, neque elati sunt oculi mei, neque ambulavi in magnis, neque in mirabilibus super me.
       EN: A song of ascents. Of David. Lord, my heart is not exalted, nor are my eyes lifted high. I have not walked amid great things or wonders beyond me.

130:2  LA: Si non humiliter sentiebam, sed exaltavi animam meam : sicut ablactatus est super matre sua, ita retributio in anima mea.
       EN: If I did not think humbly, but exalted my soul, then as a weaned child upon its mother, so is the recompense in my soul.

130:3  LA: Speret Israël in Domino, ex hoc nunc et usque in sæculum.]
       EN: Let Israel hope in the Lord, from now and forever.

PSALMS 131

131:1  LA: Canticum graduum. [Memento, Domine, David, et omnis mansuetudinis ejus :
       EN: A song of ascents. Remember David, Lord, and all his meekness,

131:2  LA: sicut juravit Domino ; votum vovit Deo Jacob :
       EN: how he swore to the Lord and vowed a vow to the God of Jacob:

131:3  LA: Si introiero in tabernaculum domus meæ ; si ascendero in lectum strati mei ;
       EN: If I enter the tent of my house, if I climb into the bed where I lie,

131:4  LA: si dedero somnum oculis meis, et palpebris meis dormitationem,
       EN: if I give sleep to my eyes and slumber to my eyelids,

131:5  LA: et requiem temporibus meis, donec inveniam locum Domino, tabernaculum Deo Jacob.
       EN: and rest to my temples, until I find a place for the Lord, a tabernacle for the God of Jacob!

131:6  LA: Ecce audivimus eam in Ephrata ; invenimus eam in campis silvæ.
       EN: Behold, we heard of it in Ephrathah; we found it in the fields of the forest.

131:7  LA: Introibimus in tabernaculum ejus ; adorabimus in loco ubi steterunt pedes ejus.
       EN: We will enter His tabernacle; we will worship at the place where His feet have stood.

131:8  LA: Surge, Domine, in requiem tuam, tu et arca sanctificationis tuæ.
       EN: Rise, Lord, to Your rest, You and the ark of Your sanctification.

131:9  LA: Sacerdotes tui induantur justitiam, et sancti tui exsultent.
       EN: Let Your priests be clothed with righteousness, and let Your holy ones exult.

131:10  LA: Propter David servum tuum non avertas faciem christi tui.
        EN: For the sake of David Your servant, do not turn away the face of Your anointed one.

131:11  LA: Juravit Dominus David veritatem, et non frustrabitur eam : De fructu ventris tui ponam super sedem tuam.
        EN: The Lord has sworn truth to David, and He will not make it fail: I will place one from the fruit of your womb upon your throne.

131:12  LA: Si custodierint filii tui testamentum meum, et testimonia mea hæc quæ docebo eos, et filii eorum usque in sæculum sedebunt super sedem tuam.
        EN: If your sons keep My covenant and these testimonies of Mine that I will teach them, their sons too will sit upon your throne forever.

131:13  LA: Quoniam elegit Dominus Sion : elegit eam in habitationem sibi.
        EN: For the Lord has chosen Zion; He has chosen her as a dwelling for Himself.

131:14  LA: Hæc requies mea in sæculum sæculi ; hic habitabo, quoniam elegi eam.
        EN: This is My rest forever and ever; here I will dwell, for I have chosen her.

131:15  LA: Viduam ejus benedicens benedicam ; pauperes ejus saturabo panibus.
        EN: I will bless her widow abundantly; I will satisfy her poor with bread.

131:16  LA: Sacerdotes ejus induam salutari, et sancti ejus exsultatione exsultabunt.
        EN: I will clothe her priests with salvation, and her holy ones will exult with great exultation.

131:17  LA: Illuc producam cornu David ; paravi lucernam christo meo.
        EN: There I will bring forth a horn for David; I have prepared a lamp for My anointed one.

131:18  LA: Inimicos ejus induam confusione ; super ipsum autem efflorebit sanctificatio mea.]
        EN: I will clothe his enemies with shame, but upon him My sanctification will blossom.

PSALMS 132

132:1  LA: Canticum graduum David. [Ecce quam bonum et quam jucundum, habitare fratres in unum !
       EN: A song of ascents. Of David. Behold, how good and how delightful it is for brothers to dwell together!

132:2  LA: Sicut unguentum in capite, quod descendit in barbam, barbam Aaron, quod descendit in oram vestimenti ejus ;
       EN: It is like ointment upon the head, running down upon the beard, Aaron's beard, running down upon the edge of his garment;

132:3  LA: sicut ros Hermon, qui descendit in montem Sion. Quoniam illic mandavit Dominus benedictionem, et vitam usque in sæculum.]
       EN: like the dew of Hermon, descending upon Mount Zion. For there the Lord has commanded blessing and life forever.

PSALMS 133

133:1  LA: Canticum graduum. [Ecce nunc benedicite Dominum, omnes servi Domini : qui statis in domo Domini, in atriis domus Dei nostri.
       EN: A song of ascents. Behold, now bless the Lord, all servants of the Lord, who stand in the Lord's house, in the courts of our God's house.

133:2  LA: In noctibus extollite manus vestras in sancta, et benedicite Dominum.
       EN: At night lift your hands toward the holy places and bless the Lord.

133:3  LA: Benedicat te Dominus ex Sion, qui fecit cælum et terram.]
       EN: May the Lord bless you from Zion, He who made heaven and earth.

PSALMS 134

134:1  LA: Alleluja . [Laudate nomen Domini ; laudate, servi, Dominum :
       EN: Alleluia. Praise the Lord's name; praise the Lord, servants,

134:2  LA: qui statis in domo Domini, in atriis domus Dei nostri.
       EN: who stand in the Lord's house, in the courts of our God's house.

134:3  LA: Laudate Dominum, quia bonus Dominus ; psallite nomini ejus, quoniam suave.
       EN: Praise the Lord, for the Lord is good; sing to His name, for it is sweet.

134:4  LA: Quoniam Jacob elegit sibi Dominus ; Israël in possessionem sibi.
       EN: For the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself, Israel as His own possession.

134:5  LA: Quia ego cognovi quod magnus est Dominus, et Deus noster præ omnibus diis.
       EN: For I know that the Lord is great, and our God above all gods.

134:6  LA: Omnia quæcumque voluit Dominus fecit, in cælo, in terra, in mari et in omnibus abyssis.
       EN: The Lord has done everything He desired, in heaven, on earth, in the sea, and in all the depths.

134:7  LA: Educens nubes ab extremo terræ, fulgura in pluviam fecit ; qui producit ventos de thesauris suis.
       EN: He brings clouds from the end of the earth and has made lightning for rain; He brings the winds from His treasuries.

134:8  LA: Qui percussit primogenita Ægypti, ab homine usque ad pecus.
       EN: He struck down Egypt's firstborn, from human beings to cattle.

134:9  LA: Et misit signa et prodigia in medio tui, Ægypte : in Pharaonem, et in omnes servos ejus.
       EN: He sent signs and wonders into your midst, Egypt, against Pharaoh and all his servants.

134:10  LA: Qui percussit gentes multas, et occidit reges fortes :
        EN: He struck many nations and killed mighty kings:

134:11  LA: Sehon, regem Amorrhæorum, et Og, regem Basan, et omnia regna Chanaan :
        EN: Sihon king of the Amorites, Og king of Bashan, and all the kingdoms of Canaan.

134:12  LA: et dedit terram eorum hæreditatem, hæreditatem Israël populo suo.
        EN: He gave their land as an inheritance, an inheritance to Israel His people.

134:13  LA: Domine, nomen tuum in æternum ; Domine, memoriale tuum in generationem et generationem.
        EN: Lord, Your name endures forever; Lord, Your memorial from generation to generation.

134:14  LA: Quia judicabit Dominus populum suum, et in servis suis deprecabitur.
        EN: For the Lord will judge His people and will be entreated on behalf of His servants.

134:15  LA: Simulacra gentium argentum et aurum, opera manuum hominum.
        EN: The idols of the nations are silver and gold, the work of human hands.

134:16  LA: Os habent, et non loquentur ; oculos habent, et non videbunt.
        EN: They have mouths and will not speak; they have eyes and will not see.

134:17  LA: Aures habent, et non audient ; neque enim est spiritus in ore ipsorum.
        EN: They have ears and will not hear, for there is no breath in their mouths.

134:18  LA: Similes illis fiant qui faciunt ea, et omnes qui confidunt in eis.
        EN: May those who make them become like them, and all who trust in them.

134:19  LA: Domus Israël, benedicite Domino ; domus Aaron, benedicite Domino.
        EN: House of Israel, bless the Lord; house of Aaron, bless the Lord.

134:20  LA: Domus Levi, benedicite Domino ; qui timetis Dominum, benedicite Domino.
        EN: House of Levi, bless the Lord; you who fear the Lord, bless the Lord.

134:21  LA: Benedictus Dominus ex Sion, qui habitat in Jerusalem.]
        EN: Blessed is the Lord from Zion, who dwells in Jerusalem.

PSALMS 135

135:1  LA: Alleluja . [Confitemini Domino, quoniam bonus, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.
       EN: Alleluia. Give thanks to the Lord, for He is good, for His mercy endures forever.

135:2  LA: Confitemini Deo deorum, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.
       EN: Give thanks to the God of gods, for His mercy endures forever.

135:3  LA: Confitemini Domino dominorum, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.
       EN: Give thanks to the Lord of lords, for His mercy endures forever.

135:4  LA: Qui facit mirabilia magna solus, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.
       EN: To Him who alone performs great wonders, for His mercy endures forever.

135:5  LA: Qui fecit cælos in intellectu, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.
       EN: Who made the heavens with understanding, for His mercy endures forever.

135:6  LA: Qui firmavit terram super aquas, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.
       EN: Who established the earth upon the waters, for His mercy endures forever.

135:7  LA: Qui fecit luminaria magna, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus :
       EN: Who made the great lights, for His mercy endures forever:

135:8  LA: solem in potestatem diei, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus ;
       EN: the sun to rule the day, for His mercy endures forever;

135:9  LA: lunam et stellas in potestatem noctis, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.
       EN: the moon and stars to rule the night, for His mercy endures forever.

135:10  LA: Qui percussit Ægyptum cum primogenitis eorum, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.
        EN: Who struck Egypt with its firstborn, for His mercy endures forever.

135:11  LA: Qui eduxit Israël de medio eorum, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus,
        EN: Who brought Israel out from their midst, for His mercy endures forever,

135:12  LA: in manu potenti et brachio excelso, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.
        EN: with a mighty hand and an uplifted arm, for His mercy endures forever.

135:13  LA: Qui divisit mare Rubrum in divisiones, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus ;
        EN: Who divided the Red Sea into divisions, for His mercy endures forever,

135:14  LA: et eduxit Israël per medium ejus, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus ;
        EN: and brought Israel through its midst, for His mercy endures forever,

135:15  LA: et excussit Pharaonem et virtutem ejus in mari Rubro, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.
        EN: and shook Pharaoh and his army into the Red Sea, for His mercy endures forever.

135:16  LA: Qui traduxit populum suum per desertum, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.
        EN: Who led His people through the wilderness, for His mercy endures forever.

135:17  LA: Qui percussit reges magnos, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus ;
        EN: Who struck down great kings, for His mercy endures forever,

135:18  LA: et occidit reges fortes, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus :
        EN: and killed mighty kings, for His mercy endures forever:

135:19  LA: Sehon, regem Amorrhæorum, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus ;
        EN: Sihon king of the Amorites, for His mercy endures forever,

135:20  LA: et Og, regem Basan, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus :
        EN: and Og king of Bashan, for His mercy endures forever.

135:21  LA: et dedit terram eorum hæreditatem, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus ;
        EN: He gave their land as an inheritance, for His mercy endures forever,

135:22  LA: hæreditatem Israël, servo suo, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.
        EN: an inheritance to Israel His servant, for His mercy endures forever.

135:23  LA: Quia in humilitate nostra memor fuit nostri, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus ;
        EN: For He remembered us in our lowliness, for His mercy endures forever,

135:24  LA: et redemit nos ab inimicis nostris, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.
        EN: and redeemed us from our enemies, for His mercy endures forever.

135:25  LA: Qui dat escam omni carni, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.
        EN: Who gives food to all flesh, for His mercy endures forever.

135:26  LA: Confitemini Deo cæli, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus. Confitemini Domino dominorum, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus.]
        EN: Give thanks to the God of heaven, for His mercy endures forever. Give thanks to the Lord of lords, for His mercy endures forever.

PSALMS 136

136:1  LA: Psalmus David, Jeremiæ. [Super flumina Babylonis illic sedimus et flevimus, cum recordaremur Sion.
       EN: A psalm of David, of Jeremiah. By the rivers of Babylon, there we sat and wept as we remembered Zion.

136:2  LA: In salicibus in medio ejus suspendimus organa nostra :
       EN: Upon the willows in its midst we hung our instruments,

136:3  LA: quia illic interrogaverunt nos, qui captivos duxerunt nos, verba cantionum ; et qui abduxerunt nos : Hymnum cantate nobis de canticis Sion.
       EN: for there those who led us captive asked us for the words of songs, and those who carried us away said: Sing us a hymn from the songs of Zion.

136:4  LA: Quomodo cantabimus canticum Domini in terra aliena ?
       EN: How shall we sing the Lord's song in a foreign land?

136:5  LA: Si oblitus fuero tui, Jerusalem, oblivioni detur dextera mea.
       EN: If I forget you, Jerusalem, let my right hand be given over to forgetfulness.

136:6  LA: Adhæreat lingua mea faucibus meis, si non meminero tui ; si non proposuero Jerusalem in principio lætitiæ meæ.
       EN: Let my tongue cling to my palate if I do not remember you, if I do not place Jerusalem at the beginning of my joy.

136:7  LA: Memor esto, Domine, filiorum Edom, in die Jerusalem : qui dicunt : Exinanite, exinanite usque ad fundamentum in ea.
       EN: Remember, Lord, the sons of Edom on Jerusalem's day, who say: Empty it, empty it to its very foundation!

136:8  LA: Filia Babylonis misera ! beatus qui retribuet tibi retributionem tuam quam retribuisti nobis.
       EN: Wretched daughter of Babylon! Blessed is the one who repays you the repayment you have paid to us.

136:9  LA: Beatus qui tenebit, et allidet parvulos tuos ad petram.]
       EN: Blessed is the one who seizes your little ones and dashes them against the rock.

PSALMS 137

137:1  LA: Ipsi David. [Confitebor tibi, Domine, in toto corde meo, quoniam audisti verba oris mei. In conspectu angelorum psallam tibi ;
       EN: Of David himself. I will give thanks to You, Lord, with my whole heart, for You have heard the words of my mouth. In the sight of the angels I will sing to You.

137:2  LA: adorabo ad templum sanctum tuum, et confitebor nomini tuo : super misericordia tua et veritate tua ; quoniam magnificasti super omne, nomen sanctum tuum.
       EN: I will worship toward Your holy temple and give thanks to Your name for Your mercy and Your truth, for You have magnified Your holy name above everything.

137:3  LA: In quacumque die invocavero te, exaudi me ; multiplicabis in anima mea virtutem.
       EN: On whatever day I call upon You, hear me; You will increase strength in my soul.

137:4  LA: Confiteantur tibi, Domine, omnes reges terræ, quia audierunt omnia verba oris tui.
       EN: Let all the kings of the earth give thanks to You, Lord, for they have heard all the words of Your mouth.

137:5  LA: Et cantent in viis Domini, quoniam magna est gloria Domini ;
       EN: Let them sing in the Lord's ways, for great is the Lord's glory.

137:6  LA: quoniam excelsus Dominus, et humilia respicit, et alta a longe cognoscit.
       EN: For the Lord is exalted and looks upon the lowly, and knows the lofty from afar.

137:7  LA: Si ambulavero in medio tribulationis, vivificabis me ; et super iram inimicorum meorum extendisti manum tuam, et salvum me fecit dextera tua.
       EN: If I walk in the midst of tribulation, You will give me life. You have stretched out Your hand against my enemies' anger, and Your right hand has saved me.

137:8  LA: Dominus retribuet pro me. Domine, misericordia tua in sæculum ; opera manuum tuarum ne despicias.]
       EN: The Lord will repay on my behalf. Lord, Your mercy endures forever; do not despise the works of Your hands.

PSALMS 138

138:1  LA: In finem, psalmus David. [Domine, probasti me, et cognovisti me ;
       EN: For the end. A psalm of David. Lord, You have tested me and known me.

138:2  LA: tu cognovisti sessionem meam et resurrectionem meam.
       EN: You have known my sitting down and my rising up.

138:3  LA: Intellexisti cogitationes meas de longe ; semitam meam et funiculum meum investigasti :
       EN: You have understood my thoughts from afar; You have searched out my path and my measuring cord.

138:4  LA: et omnes vias meas prævidisti, quia non est sermo in lingua mea.
       EN: You have foreseen all my ways, for there is no word upon my tongue.

138:5  LA: Ecce, Domine, tu cognovisti omnia, novissima et antiqua. Tu formasti me, et posuisti super me manum tuam.
       EN: Behold, Lord, You have known everything, the latest and the ancient. You formed me and placed Your hand upon me.

138:6  LA: Mirabilis facta est scientia tua ex me ; confortata est, et non potero ad eam.
       EN: Your knowledge has become wonderful beyond me; it is mighty, and I cannot reach it.

138:7  LA: Quo ibo a spiritu tuo ? et quo a facie tua fugiam ?
       EN: Where shall I go from Your Spirit, and where shall I flee from Your face?

138:8  LA: Si ascendero in cælum, tu illic es ; si descendero in infernum, ades.
       EN: If I ascend into heaven, You are there; if I descend to the underworld, You are present.

138:9  LA: Si sumpsero pennas meas diluculo, et habitavero in extremis maris,
       EN: If I take up my wings at dawn and dwell at the farthest ends of the sea,

138:10  LA: etenim illuc manus tua deducet me, et tenebit me dextera tua.
        EN: even there Your hand will lead me, and Your right hand will hold me.

138:11  LA: Et dixi : Forsitan tenebræ conculcabunt me ; et nox illuminatio mea in deliciis meis.
        EN: I said: Perhaps darkness will trample me, and night will be my illumination in my delights.

138:12  LA: Quia tenebræ non obscurabuntur a te, et nox sicut dies illuminabitur : sicut tenebræ ejus, ita et lumen ejus.
        EN: For darkness will not be darkened before You, and night will be illumined like day; its darkness is as its light.

138:13  LA: Quia tu possedisti renes meos ; suscepisti me de utero matris meæ.
        EN: For You possessed my kidneys; You upheld me from my mother's womb.

138:14  LA: Confitebor tibi, quia terribiliter magnificatus es ; mirabilia opera tua, et anima mea cognoscit nimis.
        EN: I will give thanks to You, for You are fearfully magnified. Your works are wonderful, and my soul knows this exceedingly well.

138:15  LA: Non est occultatum os meum a te, quod fecisti in occulto ; et substantia mea in inferioribus terræ.
        EN: My bone, which You made in secret, is not hidden from You, nor my substance in the lower parts of the earth.

138:16  LA: Imperfectum meum viderunt oculi tui, et in libro tuo omnes scribentur. Dies formabuntur, et nemo in eis.
        EN: Your eyes saw my unformed being, and all will be written in Your book. Days will be formed, and no one is in them.

138:17  LA: Mihi autem nimis honorificati sunt amici tui, Deus ; nimis confortatus est principatus eorum.
        EN: To me Your friends are greatly honored, God; their rule is made exceedingly strong.

138:18  LA: Dinumerabo eos, et super arenam multiplicabuntur. Exsurrexi, et adhuc sum tecum.
        EN: I will count them, and they will be more numerous than the sand. I have risen, and I am still with You.

138:19  LA: Si occideris, Deus, peccatores, viri sanguinum, declinate a me :
        EN: If You kill sinners, God, then turn away from me, you men of blood,

138:20  LA: quia dicitis in cogitatione : Accipient in vanitate civitates tuas.
        EN: for you say in thought: They will take Your cities in vain.

138:21  LA: Nonne qui oderunt te, Domine, oderam, et super inimicos tuos tabescebam ?
        EN: Did I not hate those who hate You, Lord, and waste away because of Your enemies?

138:22  LA: Perfecto odio oderam illos, et inimici facti sunt mihi.
        EN: I hated them with a perfect hatred, and they became enemies to me.

138:23  LA: Proba me, Deus, et scito cor meum ; interroga me, et cognosce semitas meas.
        EN: Test me, God, and know my heart; question me and know my paths.

138:24  LA: Et vide si via iniquitatis in me est, et deduc me in via æterna.]
        EN: See whether a way of iniquity is in me, and lead me in the everlasting way.

PSALMS 139

139:1  LA: In finem. Psalmus David.
       EN: For the end. A psalm of David.

139:2  LA: [Eripe me, Domine, ab homine malo ; a viro iniquo eripe me.
       EN: Rescue me, Lord, from an evil man; rescue me from an unjust man.

139:3  LA: Qui cogitaverunt iniquitates in corde, tota die constituebant prælia.
       EN: They planned iniquities in their hearts; all day they prepared battles.

139:4  LA: Acuerunt linguas suas sicut serpentis ; venenum aspidum sub labiis eorum.
       EN: They sharpened their tongues like a serpent's; the venom of asps is beneath their lips.

139:5  LA: Custodi me, Domine, de manu peccatoris, et ab hominibus iniquis eripe me. Qui cogitaverunt supplantare gressus meos :
       EN: Guard me, Lord, from the sinner's hand, and rescue me from unjust men who have planned to trip my steps.

139:6  LA: absconderunt superbi laqueum mihi. Et funes extenderunt in laqueum ; juxta iter, scandalum posuerunt mihi.
       EN: The proud have hidden a snare for me and stretched out cords as a trap; beside the path they have placed a stumbling block for me.

139:7  LA: Dixi Domino : Deus meus es tu ; exaudi, Domine, vocem deprecationis meæ.
       EN: I said to the Lord: You are my God; hear the voice of my prayer, Lord.

139:8  LA: Domine, Domine, virtus salutis meæ, obumbrasti super caput meum in die belli.
       EN: Lord, Lord, the strength of my salvation, You have sheltered my head on the day of battle.

139:9  LA: Ne tradas me, Domine, a desiderio meo peccatori : cogitaverunt contra me ; ne derelinquas me, ne forte exaltentur.
       EN: Do not hand me over to the sinner against my desire, Lord. They have plotted against me; do not abandon me, lest they be exalted.

139:10  LA: Caput circuitus eorum : labor labiorum ipsorum operiet eos.
        EN: The head of their circle: the toil of their own lips will cover them.

139:11  LA: Cadent super eos carbones ; in ignem dejicies eos : in miseriis non subsistent.
        EN: Coals will fall upon them; You will cast them into fire. They will not stand amid their miseries.

139:12  LA: Vir linguosus non dirigetur in terra ; virum injustum mala capient in interitu.
        EN: A man of many words will not be established on earth; evils will catch the unjust man in destruction.

139:13  LA: Cognovi quia faciet Dominus judicium inopis, et vindictam pauperum.
        EN: I know that the Lord will secure judgment for the needy and vindication for the poor.

139:14  LA: Verumtamen justi confitebuntur nomini tuo, et habitabunt recti cum vultu tuo.]
        EN: Surely the just will give thanks to Your name, and the upright will dwell before Your face.

PSALMS 140

140:1  LA: Psalmus David. [Domine, clamavi ad te : exaudi me ; intende voci meæ, cum clamavero ad te.
       EN: A psalm of David. Lord, I have cried out to You; hear me. Attend to my voice when I cry out to You.

140:2  LA: Dirigatur oratio mea sicut incensum in conspectu tuo ; elevatio manuum mearum sacrificium vespertinum.
       EN: Let my prayer be directed like incense in Your sight, the lifting of my hands as an evening sacrifice.

140:3  LA: Pone, Domine, custodiam ori meo, et ostium circumstantiæ labiis meis.
       EN: Set a guard upon my mouth, Lord, and an enclosing door for my lips.

140:4  LA: Non declines cor meum in verba malitiæ, ad excusandas excusationes in peccatis ; cum hominibus operantibus iniquitatem, et non communicabo cum electis eorum.
       EN: Do not incline my heart toward words of malice, to offer excuses for sins with those who practice iniquity; I will not share with their chosen ones.

140:5  LA: Corripiet me justus in misericordia, et increpabit me : oleum autem peccatoris non impinguet caput meum. Quoniam adhuc et oratio mea in beneplacitis eorum :
       EN: The just man will correct me in mercy and rebuke me, but let the sinner's oil not fatten my head. For even now my prayer is amid the things that please them.

140:6  LA: absorpti sunt juncti petræ judices eorum. Audient verba mea, quoniam potuerunt.
       EN: Their judges, joined to the rock, have been swallowed up. They will hear my words, for they have proved strong.

140:7  LA: Sicut crassitudo terræ erupta est super terram, dissipata sunt ossa nostra secus infernum.
       EN: As the earth's rich soil is broken up upon the ground, our bones have been scattered beside the underworld.

140:8  LA: Quia ad te, Domine, Domine, oculi mei ; in te speravi, non auferas animam meam.
       EN: For my eyes are toward You, Lord, Lord; I have hoped in You. Do not take away my soul.

140:9  LA: Custodi me a laqueo quem statuerunt mihi, et a scandalis operantium iniquitatem.
       EN: Guard me from the snare they have set for me and from the stumbling blocks of those who practice iniquity.

140:10  LA: Cadent in retiaculo ejus peccatores : singulariter sum ego, donec transeam.]
        EN: Sinners will fall into its net; I am alone until I pass through.

PSALMS 141

141:1  LA: Intellectus David, cum esset in spelunca, oratio.
       EN: For understanding. Of David, when he was in the cave. A prayer.

141:2  LA: [Voce mea ad Dominum clamavi, voce mea ad Dominum deprecatus sum.
       EN: With my voice I cried to the Lord; with my voice I pleaded with the Lord.

141:3  LA: Effundo in conspectu ejus orationem meam, et tribulationem meam ante ipsum pronuntio :
       EN: I pour out my prayer in His sight, and declare my distress before Him,

141:4  LA: in deficiendo ex me spiritum meum, et tu cognovisti semitas meas. In via hac qua ambulabam absconderunt laqueum mihi.
       EN: as my spirit fails within me; and You have known my paths. On this way along which I was walking, they hid a snare for me.

141:5  LA: Considerabam ad dexteram, et videbam, et non erat qui cognosceret me : periit fuga a me, et non est qui requirat animam meam.
       EN: I looked to the right and saw that there was no one who knew me. Escape failed me, and there is no one who seeks my soul.

141:6  LA: Clamavi ad te, Domine ; dixi : Tu es spes mea, portio mea in terra viventium.
       EN: I cried to You, Lord; I said: You are my hope, my portion in the land of the living.

141:7  LA: Intende ad deprecationem meam, quia humiliatus sum nimis. Libera me a persequentibus me, quia confortati sunt super me.
       EN: Attend to my plea, for I have been brought very low. Deliver me from those who pursue me, for they have grown stronger than I.

141:8  LA: Educ de custodia animam meam ad confitendum nomini tuo ; me exspectant justi donec retribuas mihi.]
       EN: Bring my soul out of confinement, to give thanks to Your name. The just await me until You repay me.

PSALMS 142

142:1  LA: Psalmus David, quando persequebatur eum Absalom filius ejus. [Domine, exaudi orationem meam ; auribus percipe obsecrationem meam in veritate tua ; exaudi me in tua justitia.
       EN: A psalm of David, when his son Absalom was pursuing him. Lord, hear my prayer; give ear to my supplication in Your truth. Hear me in Your righteousness.

142:2  LA: Et non intres in judicium cum servo tuo, quia non justificabitur in conspectu tuo omnis vivens.
       EN: And do not enter into judgment with Your servant, for no one living will be justified in Your sight.

142:3  LA: Quia persecutus est inimicus animam meam ; humiliavit in terra vitam meam ; collocavit me in obscuris, sicut mortuos sæculi.
       EN: For the enemy has pursued my soul; he has brought my life low to the earth. He has placed me in darkness, like those long dead.

142:4  LA: Et anxiatus est super me spiritus meus ; in me turbatum est cor meum.
       EN: And my spirit has become anxious within me; my heart is troubled within me.

142:5  LA: Memor fui dierum antiquorum ; meditatus sum in omnibus operibus tuis : in factis manuum tuarum meditabar.
       EN: I remembered the days of old; I meditated on all Your works. I pondered the deeds of Your hands.

142:6  LA: Expandi manus meas ad te ; anima mea sicut terra sine aqua tibi.
       EN: I stretched out my hands to You; my soul is like a waterless land before You.

142:7  LA: Velociter exaudi me, Domine ; defecit spiritus meus. Non avertas faciem tuam a me, et similis ero descendentibus in lacum.
       EN: Hear me quickly, Lord; my spirit has failed. Do not turn Your face away from me, or I will be like those who go down into the pit.

142:8  LA: Auditam fac mihi mane misericordiam tuam, quia in te speravi. Notam fac mihi viam in qua ambulem, quia ad te levavi animam meam.
       EN: Let me hear Your mercy in the morning, for I have hoped in You. Make known to me the way in which I should walk, for I have lifted my soul to You.

142:9  LA: Eripe me de inimicis meis, Domine : ad te confugi.
       EN: Rescue me from my enemies, Lord; I have fled to You.

142:10  LA: Doce me facere voluntatem tuam, quia Deus meus es tu. Spiritus tuus bonus deducet me in terram rectam.
        EN: Teach me to do Your will, for You are my God. Your good Spirit will lead me into an upright land.

142:11  LA: Propter nomen tuum, Domine, vivificabis me : in æquitate tua, educes de tribulatione animam meam,
        EN: For Your name's sake, Lord, You will give me life. In Your equity You will bring my soul out of distress,

142:12  LA: et in misericordia tua disperdes inimicos meos, et perdes omnes qui tribulant animam meam, quoniam ego servus tuus sum.]
        EN: and in Your mercy You will wipe out my enemies and destroy all who afflict my soul, for I am Your servant.

PSALMS 143

143:1  LA: Psalmus David. Adversus Goliath. [Benedictus Dominus Deus meus, qui docet manus meas ad prælium, et digitos meos ad bellum.
       EN: A psalm of David. Against Goliath. Blessed be the Lord my God, who teaches my hands for battle and my fingers for war.

143:2  LA: Misericordia mea et refugium meum ; susceptor meus et liberator meus ; protector meus, et in ipso speravi, qui subdit populum meum sub me.
       EN: My mercy and my refuge, my support and my deliverer, my protector, in whom I have hoped, who subjects my people to me.

143:3  LA: Domine, quid est homo, quia innotuisti ei ? aut filius hominis, quia reputas eum ?
       EN: Lord, what is a human being, that You have made Yourself known to him, or a son of man, that You take account of him?

143:4  LA: Homo vanitati similis factus est ; dies ejus sicut umbra prætereunt.
       EN: A human being has become like vanity; his days pass away like a shadow.

143:5  LA: Domine, inclina cælos tuos, et descende ; tange montes, et fumigabunt.
       EN: Lord, bend down Your heavens and descend; touch the mountains, and they will smoke.

143:6  LA: Fulgura coruscationem, et dissipabis eos ; emitte sagittas tuas, et conturbabis eos.
       EN: Flash forth lightning, and You will scatter them; send out Your arrows, and You will throw them into confusion.

143:7  LA: Emitte manum tuam de alto : eripe me, et libera me de aquis multis, de manu filiorum alienorum :
       EN: Send out Your hand from on high; rescue me and deliver me from many waters, from the hand of foreigners,

143:8  LA: quorum os locutum est vanitatem, et dextera eorum dextera iniquitatis.
       EN: whose mouth has spoken vanity, and whose right hand is a right hand of iniquity.

143:9  LA: Deus, canticum novum cantabo tibi ; in psalterio decachordo psallam tibi.
       EN: God, I will sing a new song to You; on a ten-stringed harp I will sing praise to You.

143:10  LA: Qui das salutem regibus, qui redemisti David servum tuum de gladio maligno,
        EN: You who give salvation to kings, who have redeemed David Your servant from the evil sword,

143:11  LA: eripe me, et erue me de manu filiorum alienorum, quorum os locutum est vanitatem, et dextera eorum dextera iniquitatis.
        EN: rescue me and snatch me from the hand of foreigners, whose mouth has spoken vanity, and whose right hand is a right hand of iniquity.

143:12  LA: Quorum filii sicut novellæ plantationes in juventute sua ; filiæ eorum compositæ, circumornatæ ut similitudo templi.
        EN: Their sons are like young plantings in their youth; their daughters are adorned, decorated all around like a temple.

143:13  LA: Promptuaria eorum plena, eructantia ex hoc in illud ; oves eorum fœtosæ, abundantes in egressibus suis ;
        EN: Their storehouses are full, overflowing from one into another; their sheep are fruitful, abundant as they go out;

143:14  LA: boves eorum crassæ. Non est ruina maceriæ, neque transitus, neque clamor in plateis eorum.
        EN: their cattle are fat. There is no breach in the wall, nor passage through it, nor outcry in their streets.

143:15  LA: Beatum dixerunt populum cui hæc sunt ; beatus populus cujus Dominus Deus ejus.]
        EN: They have called blessed the people who have these things; blessed are the people whose God is the Lord.

PSALMS 144

144:1  LA: Laudatio ipsi David. [Exaltabo te, Deus meus rex, et benedicam nomini tuo in sæculum, et in sæculum sæculi.
       EN: A praise of David himself. I will exalt You, my God and King, and bless Your name forever and for ever and ever.

144:2  LA: Per singulos dies benedicam tibi, et laudabo nomen tuum in sæculum, et in sæculum sæculi.
       EN: Every single day I will bless You, and praise Your name forever and for ever and ever.

144:3  LA: Magnus Dominus, et laudabilis nimis, et magnitudinis ejus non est finis.
       EN: Great is the Lord and exceedingly worthy of praise, and there is no end to His greatness.

144:4  LA: Generatio et generatio laudabit opera tua, et potentiam tuam pronuntiabunt.
       EN: Generation after generation will praise Your works, and they will proclaim Your power.

144:5  LA: Magnificentiam gloriæ sanctitatis tuæ loquentur, et mirabilia tua narrabunt.
       EN: They will speak of the magnificence of the glory of Your holiness, and recount Your wonders.

144:6  LA: Et virtutem terribilium tuorum dicent, et magnitudinem tuam narrabunt.
       EN: And they will speak of the might of Your awesome deeds, and recount Your greatness.

144:7  LA: Memoriam abundantiæ suavitatis tuæ eructabunt, et justitia tua exsultabunt.
       EN: They will pour forth the memory of Your abundant sweetness, and exult in Your righteousness.

144:8  LA: Miserator et misericors Dominus : patiens, et multum misericors.
       EN: The Lord is compassionate and merciful, patient and abounding in mercy.

144:9  LA: Suavis Dominus universis, et miserationes ejus super omnia opera ejus.
       EN: The Lord is sweet to all, and His compassion is over all His works.

144:10  LA: Confiteantur tibi, Domine, omnia opera tua, et sancti tui benedicant tibi.
        EN: Let all Your works give thanks to You, Lord, and let Your holy ones bless You.

144:11  LA: Gloriam regni tui dicent, et potentiam tuam loquentur :
        EN: They will speak of the glory of Your kingdom, and tell of Your power,

144:12  LA: ut notam faciant filiis hominum potentiam tuam, et gloriam magnificentiæ regni tui.
        EN: to make Your power known to the children of men, and the glory of the magnificence of Your kingdom.

144:13  LA: Regnum tuum regnum omnium sæculorum ; et dominatio tua in omni generatione et generationem. Fidelis Dominus in omnibus verbis suis, et sanctus in omnibus operibus suis.
        EN: Your kingdom is a kingdom of all ages, and Your dominion endures through every generation. The Lord is faithful in all His words and holy in all His works.

144:13a  LA: Regnum tuum regnum omnium sæculorum ; et dominatio tua in omni generatione et generationem. Fidelis Dominus in omnibus verbis suis, et sanctus in omnibus operibus suis.
         EN: Your kingdom is a kingdom of all ages, and Your dominion endures through every generation. The Lord is faithful in all His words and holy in all His works.

144:14  LA: Allevat Dominus omnes qui corruunt, et erigit omnes elisos.
        EN: The Lord lifts up all who are falling, and raises up all who have been cast down.

144:15  LA: Oculi omnium in te sperant, Domine, et tu das escam illorum in tempore opportuno.
        EN: The eyes of all hope in You, Lord, and You give them their food at the proper time.

144:16  LA: Aperis tu manum tuam, et imples omne animal benedictione.
        EN: You open Your hand and fill every living creature with blessing.

144:17  LA: Justus Dominus in omnibus viis suis, et sanctus in omnibus operibus suis.
        EN: The Lord is just in all His ways, and holy in all His works.

144:18  LA: Prope est Dominus omnibus invocantibus eum, omnibus invocantibus eum in veritate.
        EN: The Lord is near to all who call upon Him, to all who call upon Him in truth.

144:19  LA: Voluntatem timentium se faciet, et deprecationem eorum exaudiet, et salvos faciet eos.
        EN: He will fulfill the will of those who fear Him, and hear their plea and save them.

144:20  LA: Custodit Dominus omnes diligentes se, et omnes peccatores disperdet.
        EN: The Lord guards all who love Him, and will destroy all sinners.

144:21  LA: Laudationem Domini loquetur os meum ; et benedicat omnis caro nomini sancto ejus in sæculum, et in sæculum sæculi.]
        EN: My mouth will speak the praise of the Lord; and let all flesh bless His holy name forever and for ever and ever.

146:9  LA: qui dat jumentis escam ipsorum, et pullis corvorum invocantibus eum.
