PSALMS 147

147:1  LA: Alleluja . [Lauda, Jerusalem, Dominum ; lauda Deum tuum, Sion.
       EN: Alleluia. Praise the Lord, Jerusalem; praise your God, Zion.

147:2  LA: Quoniam confortavit seras portarum tuarum ; benedixit filiis tuis in te.
       EN: For He has strengthened the bars of your gates; He has blessed your children within you.

147:3  LA: Qui posuit fines tuos pacem, et adipe frumenti satiat te.
       EN: He has made your borders peaceful, and fills you with the richness of wheat.

147:4  LA: Qui emittit eloquium suum terræ : velociter currit sermo ejus.
       EN: He sends out His utterance to the earth; His word runs swiftly.

147:5  LA: Qui dat nivem sicut lanam ; nebulam sicut cinerem spargit.
       EN: He gives snow like wool; He scatters mist like ashes.

147:6  LA: Mittit crystallum suam sicut buccellas : ante faciem frigoris ejus quis sustinebit ?
       EN: He sends His ice like morsels; who will endure before His cold?

147:7  LA: Emittet verbum suum, et liquefaciet ea ; flabit spiritus ejus, et fluent aquæ.
       EN: He will send out His word and melt them; His wind will blow, and the waters will flow.

147:8  LA: Qui annuntiat verbum suum Jacob, justitias et judicia sua Israël.
       EN: He declares His word to Jacob, His righteous ordinances and His judgments to Israel.

147:9  LA: Non fecit taliter omni nationi, et judicia sua non manifestavit eis. Alleluja.]
       EN: He has not dealt in this way with every nation, and He has not made His judgments known to them. Alleluia.

PSALMS 148

148:1  LA: Alleluja . [Laudate Dominum de cælis ; laudate eum in excelsis.
       EN: Alleluia. Praise the Lord from the heavens; praise Him in the heights.

148:2  LA: Laudate eum, omnes angeli ejus ; laudate eum, omnes virtutes ejus.
       EN: Praise Him, all His angels; praise Him, all His powers.

148:3  LA: Laudate eum, sol et luna ; laudate eum, omnes stellæ et lumen.
       EN: Praise Him, sun and moon; praise Him, all stars and light.

148:4  LA: Laudate eum, cæli cælorum ; et aquæ omnes quæ super cælos sunt,
       EN: Praise Him, heavens of heavens; and all waters that are above the heavens,

148:5  LA: laudent nomen Domini. Quia ipse dixit, et facta sunt ; ipse mandavit, et creata sunt.
       EN: let them praise the name of the Lord. For He spoke, and they came to be; He commanded, and they were created.

148:6  LA: Statuit ea in æternum, et in sæculum sæculi ; præceptum posuit, et non præteribit.
       EN: He established them forever and for ever and ever; He set a command, and it will not pass away.

148:7  LA: Laudate Dominum de terra, dracones et omnes abyssi ;
       EN: Praise the Lord from the earth, dragons and all depths;

148:8  LA: ignis, grando, nix, glacies, spiritus procellarum, quæ faciunt verbum ejus ;
       EN: fire, hail, snow, ice, storm winds that carry out His word;

148:9  LA: montes, et omnes colles ; ligna fructifera, et omnes cedri ;
       EN: mountains and all hills, fruit-bearing trees and all cedars;

148:10  LA: bestiæ, et universa pecora ; serpentes, et volucres pennatæ ;
        EN: wild beasts and all cattle, creeping things and winged birds;

148:11  LA: reges terræ et omnes populi ; principes et omnes judices terræ ;
        EN: kings of the earth and all peoples, rulers and all judges of the earth;

148:12  LA: juvenes et virgines ; senes cum junioribus, laudent nomen Domini :
        EN: young men and maidens, the old together with the young: let them praise the name of the Lord, for His name alone has been exalted.

148:13  LA: juvenes et virgines ; senes cum junioribus, laudent nomen Domini :
        EN: young men and maidens, the old together with the young: let them praise the name of the Lord, for His name alone has been exalted.

148:14  LA: Confessio ejus super cælum et terram ; et exaltavit cornu populi sui. Hymnus omnibus sanctis ejus ; filiis Israël, populo appropinquanti sibi. Alleluja.]
        EN: His praise is above heaven and earth, and He has exalted the horn of His people. A hymn for all His holy ones, for the children of Israel, a people who draw near to Him. Alleluia.

PSALMS 149

149:1  LA: Alleluja . [Cantate Domino canticum novum ; laus ejus in ecclesia sanctorum.
       EN: Alleluia. Sing a new song to the Lord; His praise is in the assembly of the holy ones.

149:2  LA: Lætetur Israël in eo qui fecit eum, et filii Sion exsultent in rege suo.
       EN: Let Israel rejoice in Him who made him, and let the children of Zion exult in their King.

149:3  LA: Laudent nomen ejus in choro ; in tympano et psalterio psallant ei.
       EN: Let them praise His name in the dance; let them sing praise to Him with tambourine and harp.

149:4  LA: Quia beneplacitum est Domino in populo suo, et exaltabit mansuetos in salutem.
       EN: For the Lord takes pleasure in His people, and will exalt the meek to salvation.

149:5  LA: Exsultabunt sancti in gloria ; lætabuntur in cubilibus suis.
       EN: The holy ones will exult in glory; they will rejoice upon their beds.

149:6  LA: Exaltationes Dei in gutture eorum, et gladii ancipites in manibus eorum :
       EN: The exaltations of God are in their throats, and double-edged swords in their hands,

149:7  LA: ad faciendam vindictam in nationibus, increpationes in populis ;
       EN: to carry out vengeance among the nations, rebukes among the peoples;

149:8  LA: ad alligandos reges eorum in compedibus, et nobiles eorum in manicis ferreis ;
       EN: to bind their kings in fetters, and their nobles in iron shackles;

149:9  LA: ut faciant in eis judicium conscriptum : gloria hæc est omnibus sanctis ejus. Alleluja.]
       EN: to carry out upon them the written judgment. This glory belongs to all His holy ones. Alleluia.

PSALMS 150

150:1  LA: Alleluja . [Laudate Dominum in sanctis ejus ; laudate eum in firmamento virtutis ejus.
       EN: Alleluia. Praise the Lord among His holy ones; praise Him in the firmament of His power.

150:2  LA: Laudate eum in virtutibus ejus ; laudate eum secundum multitudinem magnitudinis ejus.
       EN: Praise Him for His mighty deeds; praise Him according to the abundance of His greatness.

150:3  LA: Laudate eum in sono tubæ ; laudate eum in psalterio et cithara.
       EN: Praise Him with the sound of the trumpet; praise Him with harp and lyre.

150:4  LA: Laudate eum in tympano et choro ; laudate eum in chordis et organo.
       EN: Praise Him with tambourine and dance; praise Him with strings and pipe.

150:5  LA: Laudate eum in cymbalis benesonantibus ; laudate eum in cymbalis jubilationis.
       EN: Praise Him with resounding cymbals; praise Him with cymbals of jubilation.

150:6  LA: Omnis spiritus laudet Dominum ! Alleluja.]
       EN: Let every spirit praise the Lord! Alleluia.
